File: apps/client/src/components/BookCover.tsx
================
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react'
import { db, type Book } from '../db'

export function useCoverUrl(id: string, coverHash: string | null) {
  const [url, setUrl] = useState<string | null>(null)
  useEffect(() => {
    let alive = true
    let made: string | null = null
    setUrl(null)
    if (coverHash) {
      void db.covers.get(id).then((c) => {
        if (c && alive) {
          made = URL.createObjectURL(c.blob)
          setUrl(made)
        }
      })
    }
    return () => {
      alive = false
      if (made) URL.revokeObjectURL(made)
    }
  }, [id, coverHash])
  return url
}

export function BookCover({ book }: { book: Book }) {
  const url = useCoverUrl(book.id, book.cover_hash)
  if (url) return <img className="cover" src={url} alt="" loading="lazy" draggable={false} />
  const hue = parseInt(book.id.slice(0, 6), 16) % 360
  return (
    <div className="cover cover-fallback" style={{ background: `hsl(${hue} 32% 36%)` }}>
      <span className="cf-title">{book.title}</span>
      <span className="cf-author">{book.author}</span>
    </div>
  )
}

================
File: apps/client/src/components/ErrorBoundary.tsx
================
import { Component, type ErrorInfo, type ReactNode } from 'react';
import { AlertCircle, RefreshCw, Home, RotateCcw, ChevronDown } from 'lucide-react';
import { reportCrash } from '../lib/crashReporter';
import { Button } from '../design/components/Button';

interface Props {
  children: ReactNode;
  fallbackTitle?: string;
  onReset?: () => void;
}

interface State {
  hasError: boolean;
  error: Error | null;
  errorInfo: ErrorInfo | null;
  detailsOpen: boolean;
}

export class ErrorBoundary extends Component<Props, State> {
  public override state: State = {
    hasError: false,
    error: null,
    errorInfo: null,
    detailsOpen: false,
  };

  public static getDerivedStateFromError(error: Error): Partial<State> {
    return { hasError: true, error };
  }

  public override componentDidCatch(error: Error, errorInfo: ErrorInfo): void {
    this.setState({ errorInfo });
    reportCrash(error, {
      componentStack: errorInfo.componentStack,
    });
  }

  public handleReset = (): void => {
    this.setState({ hasError: false, error: null, errorInfo: null });
    this.props.onReset?.();
  };

  private handleReload = (): void => {
    window.location.reload();
  };

  private handleHome = (): void => {
    this.setState({ hasError: false, error: null, errorInfo: null });
    window.location.hash = '#/';
  };

  public override render(): ReactNode {
    if (this.state.hasError) {
      return (
        <div
          role="alert"
          aria-live="assertive"
          className="w-full h-full min-h-[300px] flex items-center justify-center p-6 bg-[var(--surface-base)] text-[var(--text-primary)]"
        >
          <div className="max-w-md w-full p-6 rounded-[var(--r-lg)] bg-[var(--surface-overlay)] border border-[var(--border-subtle)] shadow-xl flex flex-col gap-5 text-center items-center animate-in fade-in-50 duration-200">
            <div className="w-14 h-14 rounded-full bg-[var(--surface-subtle)] border border-[var(--border-subtle)] flex items-center justify-center text-[var(--accent)]">
              <AlertCircle className="w-8 h-8" />
            </div>

            <div className="flex flex-col gap-1.5">
              <h2 className="text-xl font-bold">
                {this.props.fallbackTitle || 'Something went wrong'}
              </h2>
              <p className="text-sm text-[var(--text-secondary)]">
                An unexpected error occurred while rendering this view. You can reload the view or
                return to your library.
              </p>
            </div>

            <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-3 justify-center w-full mt-2">
              <Button
                variant="accent"
                onClick={this.handleReload}
                className="flex items-center gap-2"
              >
                <RefreshCw className="w-4 h-4" />
                <span>Reload this view</span>
              </Button>
              <Button
                variant="standard"
                onClick={this.handleReset}
                className="flex items-center gap-2"
              >
                <RotateCcw className="w-4 h-4" />
                <span>Try again</span>
              </Button>
              <Button
                variant="subtle"
                onClick={this.handleHome}
                className="flex items-center gap-2"
              >
                <Home className="w-4 h-4" />
                <span>Return to Home</span>
              </Button>
            </div>

            {this.state.error && (
              <div className="w-full text-left mt-2 border-t border-[var(--border-subtle)] pt-4">
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => this.setState((s) => ({ detailsOpen: !s.detailsOpen }))}
                  className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs text-[var(--text-muted)] hover:text-[var(--text-primary)] cursor-pointer select-none"
                >
                  <ChevronDown
                    className={`w-3.5 h-3.5 transition-transform ${
                      this.state.detailsOpen ? 'rotate-180' : ''
                    }`}
                  />
                  <span>Technical details</span>
                </button>

                {this.state.detailsOpen && (
                  <div className="mt-2.5 p-3 rounded-[var(--r-sm)] bg-[var(--surface-base)] border border-[var(--border-subtle)] overflow-x-auto text-[11px] font-mono text-[var(--text-secondary)] max-h-40">
                    <p className="font-semibold text-red-400 mb-1">
                      {this.state.error.name}: {this.state.error.message}
                    </p>
                    {this.state.error.stack && (
                      <pre className="whitespace-pre-wrap">{this.state.error.stack}</pre>
                    )}
                  </div>
                )}
              </div>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>
      );
    }

    return this.props.children;
  }
}

================
File: apps/client/src/components/ImageViewer.tsx
================
import React, { useState, useRef } from 'react';
import { ZoomIn, ZoomOut, RotateCcw, X } from 'lucide-react';
import { IconButton } from '../design/components/IconButton';

export interface ImageViewerProps {
  src: string;
  alt?: string;
  onClose: () => void;
}

export function ImageViewer({ src, alt, onClose }: ImageViewerProps) {
  const [scale, setScale] = useState(1);
  const [position, setPosition] = useState({ x: 0, y: 0 });
  const [isDragging, setIsDragging] = useState(false);
  const dragStartRef = useRef({ x: 0, y: 0 });

  const zoomIn = () => setScale((s) => Math.min(4, s + 0.25));
  const zoomOut = () => setScale((s) => Math.max(0.5, s - 0.25));
  const resetZoom = () => {
    setScale(1);
    setPosition({ x: 0, y: 0 });
  };

  const handlePointerDown = (e: React.PointerEvent) => {
    setIsDragging(true);
    dragStartRef.current = { x: e.clientX - position.x, y: e.clientY - position.y };
  };

  const handlePointerMove = (e: React.PointerEvent) => {
    if (!isDragging) return;
    setPosition({
      x: e.clientX - dragStartRef.current.x,
      y: e.clientY - dragStartRef.current.y,
    });
  };

  const handlePointerUp = () => {
    setIsDragging(false);
  };

  return (
    <div
      role="dialog"
      aria-modal="true"
      aria-label="Image viewer"
      className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex flex-col items-center justify-center bg-black/85 backdrop-blur-md select-none touch-none animate-fade-in"
    >
      {/* Top Controls Toolbar */}
      <div className="absolute top-4 right-4 flex items-center gap-2 z-10 p-1.5 rounded-full bg-[var(--layer-solid)]/80 border border-[var(--stroke)] shadow-lg">
        <IconButton
          icon={<ZoomIn className="w-5 h-5 text-[var(--text-1)]" />}
          label="Zoom in"
          onClick={zoomIn}
        />
        <IconButton
          icon={<ZoomOut className="w-5 h-5 text-[var(--text-1)]" />}
          label="Zoom out"
          onClick={zoomOut}
        />
        <IconButton
          icon={<RotateCcw className="w-5 h-5 text-[var(--text-1)]" />}
          label="Reset zoom"
          onClick={resetZoom}
        />
        <div className="w-[1px] h-5 bg-[var(--stroke)] mx-0.5" />
        <IconButton
          icon={<X className="w-5 h-5 text-[var(--text-1)]" />}
          label="Close viewer"
          onClick={onClose}
        />
      </div>

      {/* Main Draggable/Zoomable Image Area */}
      <div
        className="w-full h-full flex items-center justify-center overflow-hidden cursor-grab active:cursor-grabbing p-4"
        onPointerDown={handlePointerDown}
        onPointerMove={handlePointerMove}
        onPointerUp={handlePointerUp}
        onPointerCancel={handlePointerUp}
      >
        <img
          src={src}
          alt={alt || 'Full screen preview'}
          className="max-w-[90vw] max-h-[85vh] object-contain transition-transform duration-75 select-none pointer-events-none rounded-[var(--r-md)] shadow-2xl"
          style={{
            transform: `translate(${position.x}px, ${position.y}px) scale(${scale})`,
          }}
          draggable={false}
        />
      </div>

      {/* Bottom Zoom Indicator */}
      <div className="absolute bottom-4 left-1/2 -translate-x-1/2 px-3 py-1 rounded-full bg-[var(--layer-solid)]/80 border border-[var(--stroke)] text-xs font-semibold text-[var(--text-2)]">
        {Math.round(scale * 100)}%
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

================
File: apps/client/src/components/OnboardingModal.tsx
================
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { BookOpen, Sparkles, Cloud, ArrowRight, Check, Upload, ShoppingBag } from 'lucide-react';
import { useTheme, ACCENT_PRESETS } from '../design/theme';
import { Button } from '../design/components/Button';
import { importOne } from '../lib/importBook';
import { t } from '../lib/i18n';

interface OnboardingModalProps {
  open: boolean;
  onComplete: () => void;
  onNavigateStore?: () => void;
  onOpenSignIn?: () => void;
}

export const OnboardingModal: React.FC<OnboardingModalProps> = ({
  open,
  onComplete,
  onNavigateStore,
  onOpenSignIn,
}) => {
  const [step, setStep] = useState<1 | 2 | 3>(1);
  const { settings, updateSettings } = useTheme();
  const [importedCount, setImportedCount] = useState(0);
  const [isDragging, setIsDragging] = useState(false);

  if (!open) return null;

  const handleDrop = async (e: React.DragEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setIsDragging(false);
    const files = Array.from(e.dataTransfer.files);
    let count = 0;
    for (const file of files) {
      const added = await importOne(file);
      if (added) count++;
    }
    setImportedCount((prev) => prev + count);
  };

  const handleFileInput = async (e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {
    const files = Array.from(e.target.files || []);
    let count = 0;
    for (const file of files) {
      const added = await importOne(file);
      if (added) count++;
    }
    setImportedCount((prev) => prev + count);
  };

  return (
    <div
      role="dialog"
      aria-modal="true"
      aria-label="Welcome Onboarding"
      className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center p-4 bg-black/60 backdrop-blur-md animate-in fade-in-0 duration-200"
    >
      <div className="w-full max-w-lg rounded-[var(--r-xl)] bg-[var(--surface-base)] border border-[var(--border-subtle)] shadow-2xl p-6 sm:p-8 flex flex-col gap-6 text-[var(--text-primary)]">
        {/* Step indicator */}
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between border-b border-[var(--border-subtle)] pb-4">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <div className="flex h-7 w-7 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-[var(--accent)] text-white text-xs font-bold shadow-sm">
              {step}
            </div>
            <span className="text-xs uppercase tracking-wider font-semibold text-[var(--text-muted)]">
              Step {step} of 3
            </span>
          </div>

          <div className="flex gap-1.5">
            {[1, 2, 3].map((s) => (
              <div
                key={s}
                className={`h-1.5 w-6 rounded-full transition-all ${
                  s === step
                    ? 'bg-[var(--accent)]'
                    : s < step
                      ? 'bg-[var(--accent-muted)]'
                      : 'bg-[var(--surface-subtle)]'
                }`}
              />
            ))}
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Step 1: Welcome & Theme */}
        {step === 1 && (
          <div className="flex flex-col gap-6 animate-in fade-in-50 duration-150">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
              <div className="p-3 rounded-2xl bg-[var(--accent-subtle)] text-[var(--accent)]">
                <Sparkles className="w-6 h-6" />
              </div>
              <div>
                <h2 className="text-xl font-bold tracking-tight">
                  {t('onboarding.welcomeTitle')}
                </h2>
                <p className="text-sm text-[var(--text-muted)]">
                  {t('onboarding.welcomeSubtitle')}
                </p>
              </div>
            </div>

            <div className="flex flex-col gap-3">
              <label className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-[var(--text-secondary)]">
                {t('settings.theme')}
              </label>
              <div className="grid grid-cols-3 gap-2">
                {(['system', 'light', 'dark'] as const).map((tMode) => (
                  <button
                    key={tMode}
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => updateSettings({ theme: tMode })}
                    className={`py-2 px-3 rounded-[var(--r-md)] border text-sm font-medium capitalize transition-all cursor-pointer ${
                      settings.theme === tMode
                        ? 'border-[var(--accent)] bg-[var(--accent-subtle)] text-[var(--accent)] font-semibold shadow-sm'
                        : 'border-[var(--border-subtle)] hover:bg-[var(--surface-subtle)] text-[var(--text-secondary)]'
                    }`}
                  >
                    {tMode}
                  </button>
                ))}
              </div>
            </div>

            <div className="flex flex-col gap-3">
              <label className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-[var(--text-secondary)]">
                {t('settings.accentColor')}
              </label>
              <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2.5">
                {Object.entries(ACCENT_PRESETS).map(([id, color]) => (
                  <button
                    key={id}
                    type="button"
                    title={id}
                    onClick={() => updateSettings({ accent: id })}
                    className={`w-9 h-9 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center transition-transform hover:scale-110 cursor-pointer shadow-sm ${
                      settings.accent === id ? 'ring-2 ring-offset-2 ring-[var(--accent)] scale-105' : ''
                    }`}
                    style={{ backgroundColor: color }}
                  >
                    {settings.accent === id && <Check className="w-4 h-4 text-white" />}
                  </button>
                ))}
              </div>
            </div>

            <div className="flex justify-end pt-2">
              <Button variant="accent" onClick={() => setStep(2)}>
                {t('onboarding.next')} <ArrowRight className="w-4 h-4 ml-1" />
              </Button>
            </div>
          </div>
        )}

        {/* Step 2: Add Books */}
        {step === 2 && (
          <div className="flex flex-col gap-6 animate-in fade-in-50 duration-150">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
              <div className="p-3 rounded-2xl bg-[var(--accent-subtle)] text-[var(--accent)]">
                <BookOpen className="w-6 h-6" />
              </div>
              <div>
                <h2 className="text-xl font-bold tracking-tight">
                  {t('onboarding.addBooksTitle')}
                </h2>
                <p className="text-sm text-[var(--text-muted)]">
                  {t('onboarding.addBooksSubtitle')}
                </p>
              </div>
            </div>

            <div
              onDragOver={(e) => {
                e.preventDefault();
                setIsDragging(true);
              }}
              onDragLeave={() => setIsDragging(false)}
              onDrop={handleDrop}
              className={`flex flex-col items-center justify-center border-2 border-dashed rounded-[var(--r-lg)] p-8 transition-colors text-center cursor-pointer ${
                isDragging
                  ? 'border-[var(--accent)] bg-[var(--accent-subtle)]'
                  : 'border-[var(--border-subtle)] hover:bg-[var(--surface-subtle)]'
              }`}
            >
              <Upload className="w-8 h-8 text-[var(--text-muted)] mb-2" />
              <p className="text-sm font-semibold">{t('onboarding.dropBooksHere')}</p>
              <p className="text-xs text-[var(--text-muted)] mt-1 mb-3">
                Supports EPUB, MOBI, AZW3, FB2, CBZ, CBR, PDF
              </p>

              <label className="cursor-pointer">
                <input
                  type="file"
                  multiple
                  accept=".epub,.mobi,.azw3,.azw,.fb2,.cbz,.cbr,.cb7,.pdf"
                  onChange={handleFileInput}
                  className="hidden"
                />
                <span className="text-xs font-semibold px-3 py-1.5 rounded-[var(--r-md)] bg-[var(--surface-subtle)] hover:bg-[var(--surface-overlay)] text-[var(--accent)] border border-[var(--border-subtle)] transition-colors">
                  {t('onboarding.browseFiles')}
                </span>
              </label>

              {importedCount > 0 && (
                <div className="mt-4 flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs text-[var(--accent)] font-semibold">
                  <Check className="w-4 h-4" /> {importedCount} book{importedCount > 1 ? 's' : ''} added to your library!
                </div>
              )}
            </div>

            <div className="flex items-center justify-between pt-2">
              <Button
                variant="standard"
                onClick={() => {
                  onNavigateStore?.();
                  setStep(3);
                }}
              >
                <ShoppingBag className="w-4 h-4 mr-1.5" /> {t('onboarding.exploreStore')}
              </Button>
              <Button variant="accent" onClick={() => setStep(3)}>
                {t('onboarding.next')} <ArrowRight className="w-4 h-4 ml-1" />
              </Button>
            </div>
          </div>
        )}

        {/* Step 3: Sync & Finish */}
        {step === 3 && (
          <div className="flex flex-col gap-6 animate-in fade-in-50 duration-150">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
              <div className="p-3 rounded-2xl bg-[var(--accent-subtle)] text-[var(--accent)]">
                <Cloud className="w-6 h-6" />
              </div>
              <div>
                <h2 className="text-xl font-bold tracking-tight">
                  {t('onboarding.syncTitle')}
                </h2>
                <p className="text-sm text-[var(--text-muted)]">
                  {t('onboarding.syncSubtitle')}
                </p>
              </div>
            </div>

            <div className="flex flex-col gap-3 p-4 rounded-[var(--r-lg)] bg-[var(--surface-subtle)] border border-[var(--border-subtle)]">
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                <div>
                  <h4 className="text-sm font-semibold">Self-hosted or Cloud Sync</h4>
                  <p className="text-xs text-[var(--text-muted)]">
                    Progress, highlights, bookmarks, and statistics stay in sync.
                  </p>
                </div>
                <Button
                  variant="standard"
                  size="sm"
                  onClick={() => {
                    onOpenSignIn?.();
                    onComplete();
                  }}
                >
                  {t('onboarding.signInNow')}
                </Button>
              </div>
            </div>

            <div className="flex justify-end gap-2 pt-2">
              <Button variant="accent" onClick={onComplete}>
                {t('onboarding.startReading')} <Check className="w-4 h-4 ml-1" />
              </Button>
            </div>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

================
File: apps/client/src/components/PullToRefresh.tsx
================
import React, { useState, useRef, useEffect, type ReactNode } from 'react';
import { triggerHaptic } from '../lib/mobileUx';

interface PullToRefreshProps {
  children: ReactNode;
  onRefresh: () => Promise<void>;
  disabled?: boolean;
  className?: string;
}

const THRESHOLD = 64;

export const PullToRefresh: React.FC<PullToRefreshProps> = ({
  children,
  onRefresh,
  disabled = false,
  className = '',
}) => {
  const containerRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const startY = useRef(0);
  const [pullDistance, setPullDistance] = useState(0);
  const [isRefreshing, setIsRefreshing] = useState(false);
  const hasTriggeredHaptic = useRef(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    const el = containerRef.current;
    if (!el || disabled) return;

    const onTouchStart = (e: TouchEvent) => {
      if (el.scrollTop <= 0 && !isRefreshing) {
        startY.current = e.touches[0]?.clientY ?? 0;
        hasTriggeredHaptic.current = false;
      } else {
        startY.current = 0;
      }
    };

    const onTouchMove = (e: TouchEvent) => {
      if (startY.current === 0 || isRefreshing) return;
      const currentY = e.touches[0]?.clientY ?? 0;
      const delta = currentY - startY.current;

      if (delta > 0 && el.scrollTop <= 0) {
        // Apply dampening
        const dist = Math.min(THRESHOLD * 1.5, delta * 0.45);
        setPullDistance(dist);

        if (dist >= THRESHOLD && !hasTriggeredHaptic.current) {
          triggerHaptic('light');
          hasTriggeredHaptic.current = true;
        }
      }
    };

    const onTouchEnd = async () => {
      if (startY.current === 0 || isRefreshing) return;
      startY.current = 0;

      if (pullDistance >= THRESHOLD) {
        setIsRefreshing(true);
        setPullDistance(THRESHOLD);
        try {
          await onRefresh();
        } finally {
          setIsRefreshing(false);
          setPullDistance(0);
        }
      } else {
        setPullDistance(0);
      }
    };

    el.addEventListener('touchstart', onTouchStart, { passive: true });
    el.addEventListener('touchmove', onTouchMove, { passive: true });
    el.addEventListener('touchend', onTouchEnd);

    return () => {
      el.removeEventListener('touchstart', onTouchStart);
      el.removeEventListener('touchmove', onTouchMove);
      el.removeEventListener('touchend', onTouchEnd);
    };
  }, [disabled, isRefreshing, onRefresh, pullDistance]);

  return (
    <div
      ref={containerRef}
      className={`relative overflow-y-auto ${className}`}
      data-testid="pull-to-refresh-container"
    >
      {/* Pull indicator */}
      <div
        className="pointer-events-none absolute left-0 right-0 top-0 flex items-center justify-center transition-transform duration-150 ease-out z-20"
        style={{
          transform: `translateY(${pullDistance - 40}px)`,
          opacity: pullDistance > 10 ? 1 : 0,
        }}
        aria-hidden="true"
      >
        <div className="flex h-10 w-10 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-[var(--surface-overlay)] text-[var(--text-primary)] shadow-md border border-[var(--border-subtle)]">
          {isRefreshing ? (
            <svg
              className="h-5 w-5 animate-spin text-[var(--accent)]"
              xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg"
              fill="none"
              viewBox="0 0 24 24"
            >
              <circle
                className="opacity-25"
                cx="12"
                cy="12"
                r="10"
                stroke="currentColor"
                strokeWidth="4"
              />
              <path
                className="opacity-75"
                fill="currentColor"
                d="M4 12a8 8 0 018-8V0C5.373 0 0 5.373 0 12h4zm2 5.291A7.962 7.962 0 014 12H0c0 3.042 1.135 5.824 3 7.938l3-2.647z"
              />
            </svg>
          ) : (
            <svg
              className="h-5 w-5 text-[var(--text-muted)] transition-transform duration-200"
              style={{
                transform: `rotate(${Math.min(180, (pullDistance / THRESHOLD) * 180)}deg)`,
              }}
              viewBox="0 0 24 24"
              fill="none"
              stroke="currentColor"
              strokeWidth="2"
              strokeLinecap="round"
              strokeLinejoin="round"
            >
              <line x1="12" y1="5" x2="12" y2="19" />
              <polyline points="19 12 12 19 5 12" />
            </svg>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>

      <div
        style={{
          transform: `translateY(${pullDistance}px)`,
          transition: isRefreshing ? 'transform 0.2s ease-out' : 'none',
        }}
      >
        {children}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

File: apps/client/src/components/ui.tsx
================
import { useEffect, type ReactNode } from 'react'
import { X } from 'lucide-react'

export function Modal({ title, onClose, children, width = 520 }: {
  title: string
  onClose: () => void
  children: ReactNode
  width?: number
}) {
  useEffect(() => {
    const onKey = (e: KeyboardEvent) => { if (e.key === 'Escape') onClose() }
    window.addEventListener('keydown', onKey)
    return () => window.removeEventListener('keydown', onKey)
  }, [onClose])

  return (
    <div className="modal-backdrop" onMouseDown={onClose}>
      <div className="modal" style={{ maxWidth: width }} onMouseDown={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}>
        <div className="modal-head">
          <h3>{title}</h3>
          <button className="icon-btn" onClick={onClose} aria-label="Close"><X size={18} /></button>
        </div>
        {children}
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}

export function Toast({ message, detail, onDone }: { message: string; detail?: string; onDone: () => void }) {
  useEffect(() => {
    const t = setTimeout(onDone, detail ? 9000 : 3500)
    return () => clearTimeout(t)
    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
  }, [])
  return (
    <div className="toast" onClick={onDone} role="status">
      <div>{message}</div>
      {detail && <pre>{detail}</pre>}
    </div>
  )
}

import React from 'react';
import {
  FolderPlus,
  Heart,
  CheckCircle,
  Download,
  Trash2,
  X,
  CheckSquare,
  Loader2,
} from 'lucide-react';
import { Button } from './Button';
import { IconButton } from './IconButton';

export interface BulkActionBarProps {
  selectedCount: number;
  totalCount: number;
  isAllSelected: boolean;
  onSelectAll: () => void;
  onClearSelection: () => void;
  onAddToCollection: () => void;
  onToggleFavorite: () => void;
  onToggleFinished: () => void;
  onExport: () => void;
  onDelete: () => void;
  exporting?: boolean;
}

export const BulkActionBar: React.FC<BulkActionBarProps> = ({
  selectedCount,
  totalCount,
  isAllSelected,
  onSelectAll,
  onClearSelection,
  onAddToCollection,
  onToggleFavorite,
  onToggleFinished,
  onExport,
  onDelete,
  exporting = false,
}) => {
  if (selectedCount === 0) return null;

  return (
    <div
      role="region"
      aria-label="Bulk actions"

          </button>
        </div>

        {/* Collection */}
        <Button
          variant="subtle"
          size="sm"
          onClick={onAddToCollection}
          title="Add to collection"
          className="gap-1.5 text-xs px-2 sm:px-2.5 h-8"
        >
          <FolderPlus className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-[var(--text-2)]" />

          <span className="hidden md:inline">Favourite</span>

File: apps/client/src/design/components/BulkCollectionDialog.tsx

import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { FolderPlus, Check } from 'lucide-react';
import { type Collection } from '../../db';
import { Dialog } from './Dialog';
import { Button } from './Button';

export interface BulkCollectionDialogProps {
  open: boolean;
  collections: Collection[];
  selectedCount: number;
  onClose: () => void;
  onAssign: (collectionId: string) => void;
  onCreateNewCollection: (name: string) => void;
}

export const BulkCollectionDialog: React.FC<BulkCollectionDialogProps> = ({
  open,
  collections,
  selectedCount,
  onClose,
  onAssign,
  onCreateNewCollection,
}) => {
  const [newColName, setNewColName] = useState('');
  const [selectedColId, setSelectedColId] = useState<string>(collections[0]?.id || '');

  if (!open) return null;

  return (
    <Dialog
      open={open}
      onClose={onClose}
      title="Add to collection"
      className="max-w-md"
    >
      <div className="space-y-4">
        <p className="text-xs text-[var(--text-2)]">
          Add {selectedCount} selected {selectedCount === 1 ? 'book' : 'books'} to a collection:
        </p>

        {/* Existing collections list */}
        <div className="space-y-1.5 max-h-48 overflow-y-auto p-1 rounded-md bg-[var(--layer-2)] border border-[var(--stroke)]">
          {collections.length === 0 ? (
            <p className="text-xs text-[var(--text-3)] p-3 text-center">
              No collections yet. Create one below.
            </p>
          ) : (
            collections.map((col) => {
              const isSelected = selectedColId === col.id;
              return (
                <button
                  key={col.id}
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setSelectedColId(col.id)}
                  className={`w-full flex items-center justify-between px-3 py-2 rounded-md text-xs font-medium transition-colors cursor-pointer text-left ${
                    isSelected
                      ? 'bg-[var(--accent)]/15 text-[var(--accent)] font-semibold'
                      : 'hover:bg-[var(--layer-3)] text-[var(--text-1)]'
                  }`}
                >
                  <span>{col.name}</span>
                  {isSelected && <Check className="w-4 h-4 stroke-[2.5]" />}
                </button>
              );
            })
          )}
        </div>

        {/* Create new collection inline */}
        <div className="pt-2 border-t border-[var(--stroke)]">
          <span className="block text-[11px] font-medium text-[var(--text-3)] mb-1">
            Or create a new collection:
          </span>
          <div className="flex gap-2">
            <input
              type="text"
              placeholder="New collection name…"
              value={newColName}
              onChange={(e) => setNewColName(e.target.value)}
              onKeyDown={(e) => {
                if (e.key === 'Enter' && newColName.trim()) {
                  onCreateNewCollection(newColName.trim());
                  setNewColName('');
                }
              }}
              className="flex-1 text-xs px-2.5 py-1.5 rounded-md bg-[var(--layer-2)] border border-[var(--stroke)] text-[var(--text-1)] outline-none focus:border-[var(--accent)]"
            />
            <Button
              type="button"
              variant="standard"
              size="sm"
              disabled={!newColName.trim()}
              onClick={() => {
                if (newColName.trim()) {
                  onCreateNewCollection(newColName.trim());
                  setNewColName('');
                }
              }}
              className="gap-1 text-xs"
            >
              <FolderPlus className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
              Create
            </Button>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Modal actions */}
        <div className="flex justify-end gap-2 pt-2 border-t border-[var(--stroke)]">
          <Button variant="subtle" size="sm" onClick={onClose}>
            Cancel
          </Button>
          <Button
            variant="accent"
            size="sm"
            disabled={!selectedColId}
            onClick={() => {
              if (selectedColId) {
                onAssign(selectedColId);
                onClose();
              }
            }}
          >
            Add books
          </Button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </Dialog>
  );
};

import React from 'react';
import * as RadixCheckbox from '@radix-ui/react-checkbox';
import { Check } from 'lucide-react';
import { clsx } from 'clsx';
import { twMerge } from 'tailwind-merge';

export interface CheckboxProps {
  checked: boolean | 'indeterminate';
  onCheckedChange: (checked: boolean | 'indeterminate') => void;
  label?: React.ReactNode;
  disabled?: boolean;
  className?: string;
}

export const Checkbox: React.FC<CheckboxProps> = ({
  checked,
  onCheckedChange,
  label,
  disabled,
  className,
}) => {
  const checkboxElement = (
    <RadixCheckbox.Root
      checked={checked}
      onCheckedChange={onCheckedChange}
      disabled={disabled}
      className={twMerge(
        clsx(
          'flex items-center justify-center w-[18px] h-[18px] rounded-[var(--r-sm)] border border-[var(--stroke-strong)] transition-colors cursor-pointer outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-[var(--accent)] shrink-0',
          checked === true ? 'bg-[var(--accent)] border-[var(--accent)] text-[var(--on-accent)]' : 'bg-[var(--layer-2)] text-transparent',
          disabled && 'opacity-40 pointer-events-none',
          className,
        ),
      )}
    >
      <RadixCheckbox.Indicator>
        <Check className="w-3.5 h-3.5 stroke-[2.5]" />
      </RadixCheckbox.Indicator>
    </RadixCheckbox.Root>
  );

  if (!label) return checkboxElement;

  return (
    <label className="flex items-center gap-2.5 cursor-pointer select-none text-sm text-[var(--text-1)]">
      {checkboxElement}
      <span>{label}</span>
    </label>
  );
};

import React, { type ReactNode } from 'react';

                    <span>{item.label}</span>
                  </div>
                  {item.shortcut && (
                    <span className="text-xs text-[var(--text-3)] font-mono ml-4">{item.shortcut}</span>
                  )}
                </RadixContextMenu.Item>
              ))}
              {secIdx < sections.length - 1 && (
                <RadixContextMenu.Separator className="h-[1px] bg-[var(--stroke)] my-1" />
              )}
            </React.Fragment>
          ))}
        </RadixContextMenu.Content>
      </RadixContextMenu.Portal>
    </RadixContextMenu.Root>
  );

import { Fragment, type FC, type ReactNode } from 'react';
import * as RadixDropdown from '@radix-ui/react-dropdown-menu';
import { Check } from 'lucide-react';
import { clsx } from 'clsx';
import { twMerge } from 'tailwind-merge';

export interface DropdownMenuItem {
  key: string;
  label: ReactNode;
  icon?: ReactNode;
  shortcut?: string;
  checked?: boolean;
  disabled?: boolean;
  danger?: boolean;
  onSelect?: () => void;
  subItems?: DropdownMenuItem[];
}

export interface DropdownMenuSection {
  title?: string;
  items: DropdownMenuItem[];
}

export interface DropdownProps {
  trigger: ReactNode;
  sections: DropdownMenuSection[];
  align?: 'start' | 'center' | 'end';
  side?: 'top' | 'right' | 'bottom' | 'left';
  className?: string;
}

export const Dropdown: FC<DropdownProps> = ({
  trigger,
  sections,
  align = 'start',
  side = 'bottom',
  className,
}) => {
  return (
    <RadixDropdown.Root>
      <RadixDropdown.Trigger asChild>{trigger}</RadixDropdown.Trigger>
      <RadixDropdown.Portal>
        <RadixDropdown.Content
          align={align}
          side={side}
          sideOffset={6}
          className={twMerge(
            clsx(
              'z-50 min-w-[180px] p-1.5 rounded-[var(--r-md)] bg-[var(--layer-solid)] text-[var(--text-1)] border border-[var(--stroke)] shadow-[var(--shadow-flyout)] outline-none animate-in fade-in-0 duration-150',
              className,
            ),
          )}
        >
          {sections.map((sec, secIdx) => (
            <Fragment key={sec.title ?? secIdx}>
              {sec.title && (
                <RadixDropdown.Label className="px-2.5 py-1 text-[11px] font-semibold text-[var(--text-3)] uppercase tracking-wider select-none">
                  {sec.title}
                </RadixDropdown.Label>
              )}
              {sec.items.map((item) => (
                <RadixDropdown.Item
                  key={item.key}
                  disabled={item.disabled}
                  onSelect={item.onSelect}
                  className={clsx(
                    'flex items-center justify-between gap-3 px-2.5 py-1.5 rounded-[var(--r-sm)] text-sm cursor-pointer select-none outline-none transition-colors',
                    item.disabled && 'opacity-40 pointer-events-none',
                    item.danger
                      ? 'text-[var(--danger)] hover:bg-[color-mix(in_oklab,var(--danger)_18%,transparent)]'
                      : 'text-[var(--text-1)] hover:bg-[var(--layer-2)] active:bg-[var(--layer-3)]',
                  )}
                >
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                    {item.checked !== undefined ? (
                      <span className="w-4 h-4 flex items-center justify-center">
                        {item.checked && <Check className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-[var(--accent)]" />}
                      </span>
                    ) : (
                      item.icon && <span className="w-4 h-4 flex items-center justify-center text-[var(--text-2)]">{item.icon}</span>
                    )}
                    <span>{item.label}</span>
                  </div>
                  {item.shortcut && (
                    <span className="text-xs text-[var(--text-3)] font-mono ml-4">{item.shortcut}</span>
                  )}
                </RadixDropdown.Item>
              ))}
              {secIdx < sections.length - 1 && (
                <RadixDropdown.Separator className="h-[1px] bg-[var(--stroke)] my-1" />
              )}
            </Fragment>
          ))}
        </RadixDropdown.Content>
      </RadixDropdown.Portal>
    </RadixDropdown.Root>
  );
};

export const DuplicateDialog: React.FC<DuplicateDialogProps> = ({

  existingBook,

    <Dialog
      open={open}
      onClose={() => onDecision('skip')}
      title="Duplicate Book Found"
      className="max-w-md"
    >
      <div className="space-y-4">
        <div className="flex items-start gap-3 p-3 rounded-lg bg-[var(--warning)]/10 border border-[var(--warning)]/30 text-[var(--text-1)]">
          <AlertTriangle className="w-5 h-5 text-[var(--warning)] shrink-0 mt-0.5" />
          <div className="text-xs space-y-1">
            <p className="font-semibold">
              A book with this title and author already exists in your library.
            </p>
            <p className="text-[var(--text-2)]">
              &ldquo;{existingBook.title}&rdquo; by {existingBook.author}
            </p>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Comparison card */}
        <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3 p-3 rounded-lg bg-[var(--layer-2)] border border-[var(--stroke)] text-xs">
          <div className="space-y-1">
            <span className="font-semibold text-[var(--text-3)] block uppercase tracking-wider text-[10px]">
              Existing Book
            </span>
            <p className="font-medium text-[var(--text-1)]">{existingBook.format}</p>
            <p className="text-[var(--text-2)]">{fmtBytes(existingBook.file_size)}</p>
          </div>
          <div className="space-y-1 border-l border-[var(--stroke)] pl-3">
            <span className="font-semibold text-[var(--accent)] block uppercase tracking-wider text-[10px]">
              Incoming File
            </span>
            <p className="font-medium text-[var(--text-1)]">
              {newFile.name.split('.').pop()?.toUpperCase() ?? 'FILE'}
            </p>
            <p className="text-[var(--text-2)]">{fmtBytes(newFile.size)}</p>
          </div>
        </div>

        <p className="text-xs text-[var(--text-2)]">
          Would you like to replace the existing book file (keeping existing reading position and notes), or keep both copies side by side?
        </p>

        {/* Decisions */}
        <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row justify-end gap-2 pt-2 border-t border-[var(--stroke)]">
          <Button
            type="button"
            variant="subtle"
            size="sm"
            onClick={() => onDecision('skip')}
            className="text-xs"
          >
            Skip file
          </Button>
          <Button
            type="button"
            variant="standard"
            size="sm"
            onClick={() => onDecision('keep-both')}
            className="text-xs"
          >
            Keep both
          </Button>
          <Button
            type="button"
            variant="accent"
            size="sm"
            onClick={() => onDecision('replace')}
            className="text-xs"
          >
            Replace existing
          </Button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </Dialog>
  );
};

================
File: apps/client/src/design/components/EditMetadataDialog.tsx

import React, { useState, useEffect, useRef } from 'react';
import { Star, Upload, Link as LinkIcon, X, Plus, Loader2 } from 'lucide-react';
import { db, dirty, type Book } from '../../db';
import { sha256 } from '../../lib/hash';
import { extractCoverColor, useTheme } from '../theme';
import { Dialog } from './Dialog';
import { Button } from './Button';
import { BookCover } from './BookCover';

export interface EditMetadataDialogProps {
  open: boolean;
  book: Book | null;
  onClose: () => void;
  onSaved?: (updatedBook: Book) => void;
}

async function resizeCoverBlob(blob: Blob): Promise<Blob> {
  try {
    const bmp = await createImageBitmap(blob);
    const scale = Math.min(1, 480 / bmp.width);
    const canvas = document.createElement('canvas');
    canvas.width = Math.round(bmp.width * scale);
    canvas.height = Math.round(bmp.height * scale);
    const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');
    if (!ctx) return blob;
    ctx.drawImage(bmp, 0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);
    bmp.close();
    return await new Promise((resolve) =>
      canvas.toBlob((b) => resolve(b ?? blob), 'image/jpeg', 0.85),
    );
  } catch {
    return blob;
  }
}

export const EditMetadataDialog: React.FC<EditMetadataDialogProps> = ({
  open,
  book,
  onClose,
  onSaved,
}) => {
  const { resolvedTheme } = useTheme();

  const [title, setTitle] = useState('');
  const [author, setAuthor] = useState('');
  const [series, setSeries] = useState('');
  const [seriesNumber, setSeriesNumber] = useState<string>('');
  const [language, setLanguage] = useState('');
  const [description, setDescription] = useState('');
  const [tags, setTags] = useState<string[]>([]);
  const [tagInput, setTagInput] = useState('');
  const [rating, setRating] = useState<number>(0);

  const [coverBlob, setCoverBlob] = useState<Blob | null>(null);
  const [coverPreviewUrl, setCoverPreviewUrl] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [coverUrlInput, setCoverUrlInput] = useState('');
  const [fetchingUrl, setFetchingUrl] = useState(false);
  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);

  const fileInputRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (book) {
      setTitle(book.title);
      setAuthor(book.author);
      setSeries(book.meta.series ?? '');
      setSeriesNumber(
        book.meta.series_number !== undefined ? String(book.meta.series_number) : '',
      );
      setLanguage(book.meta.language ?? '');
      setDescription(book.meta.description ?? '');
      setTags(book.meta.tags ? [...book.meta.tags] : []);
      setRating(book.meta.rating ?? 0);
      setCoverBlob(null);
      setCoverPreviewUrl(null);
      setCoverUrlInput('');
      setTagInput('');
    }
  }, [book, open]);

  useEffect(() => {
    return () => {
      if (coverPreviewUrl) {
        URL.revokeObjectURL(coverPreviewUrl);
      }
    };
  }, [coverPreviewUrl]);

  if (!book) return null;

  const handleAddTag = () => {
    const trimmed = tagInput.trim().replace(/^#/, '');
    if (trimmed && !tags.includes(trimmed)) {
      setTags([...tags, trimmed]);
      setTagInput('');
    }
  };

  const handleRemoveTag = (tagToRemove: string) => {
    setTags(tags.filter((t) => t !== tagToRemove));
  };

  const handleFileChange = async (e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {
    const file = e.target.files?.[0];
    if (!file) return;
    try {
      const resized = await resizeCoverBlob(file);
      setCoverBlob(resized);
      const url = URL.createObjectURL(resized);
      setCoverPreviewUrl(url);
    } catch {
      // Ignore invalid image
    }
  };

  const handleFetchCoverUrl = async () => {
    const url = coverUrlInput.trim();
    if (!url) return;
    setFetchingUrl(true);
    try {
      const res = await fetch(url);
      if (!res.ok) throw new Error('Failed to load image');
      const blob = await res.blob();
      const resized = await resizeCoverBlob(blob);
      setCoverBlob(resized);
      const objUrl = URL.createObjectURL(resized);
      setCoverPreviewUrl(objUrl);
      setCoverUrlInput('');
    } catch {
      alert('Could not fetch image from this URL.');
    } finally {
      setFetchingUrl(false);
    }
  };

  const handleSave = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!title.trim()) return;

    setSaving(true);
    try {
      let coverHash = book.cover_hash;
      let accent = book.meta.accent;

      if (coverBlob) {
        const buffer = await coverBlob.arrayBuffer();
        coverHash = await sha256(buffer);
        await db.covers.put({ id: book.id, blob: coverBlob });
        accent = await extractCoverColor(coverBlob, resolvedTheme === 'dark');
      }

      const parsedSeriesNum = seriesNumber.trim() ? parseFloat(seriesNumber) : undefined;

      const updatedMeta = {
        ...book.meta,
        series: series.trim() || undefined,
        series_number: !isNaN(parsedSeriesNum as number) ? parsedSeriesNum : undefined,
        language: language.trim() || undefined,
        description: description.trim() || undefined,
        tags: tags.length > 0 ? tags : undefined,
        rating: rating > 0 ? rating : undefined,
        accent,
      };

      const updates: Partial<Book> = {
        title: title.trim(),
        author: author.trim() || 'Unknown author',
        cover_hash: coverHash,
        meta: updatedMeta,
        ...dirty(),
      };

      await db.books.update(book.id, updates);

      const updatedBook: Book = {
        ...book,
        ...updates,
      };

      onSaved?.(updatedBook);
      onClose();
    } finally {
      setSaving(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <Dialog open={open} onClose={onClose} title="Edit metadata" className="max-w-xl">
      <form onSubmit={handleSave} className="space-y-4">
        {/* Top: Cover preview & Cover Replacement Controls */}
        <div className="flex gap-4 p-3 rounded-lg bg-[var(--layer-2)] border border-[var(--stroke)] items-center">
          <div className="w-20 shrink-0">
            {coverPreviewUrl ? (
              <div className="aspect-[2/3] w-full rounded-[var(--r-cover)] overflow-hidden shadow-xs border border-[var(--stroke)]">
                <img
                  src={coverPreviewUrl}
                  alt="New cover preview"
                  className="w-full h-full object-cover"
                />
              </div>
            ) : (
              <BookCover
                id={book.id}
                title={title || book.title}
                author={author || book.author}
                coverHash={book.cover_hash}
                className="w-20 pointer-events-none"
              />
            )}
          </div>

          <div className="flex-1 space-y-2">
            <span className="text-xs font-semibold text-[var(--text-1)] block">
              Book Cover
            </span>
            <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
              <input
                ref={fileInputRef}
                type="file"
                accept="image/*"
                onChange={handleFileChange}
                className="hidden"
              />
              <Button
                type="button"
                variant="standard"
                size="sm"
                onClick={() => fileInputRef.current?.click()}
                className="gap-1.5 text-xs"
              >
                <Upload className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-[var(--text-2)]" />
                Upload image
              </Button>
            </div>

            <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 mt-1">
              <input
                type="url"
                placeholder="Or paste image URL"
                value={coverUrlInput}
                onChange={(e) => setCoverUrlInput(e.target.value)}
                className="flex-1 text-xs px-2.5 py-1.5 rounded-md bg-[var(--layer-3)] border border-[var(--stroke)] text-[var(--text-1)] placeholder-[var(--text-3)] outline-none focus:border-[var(--accent)]"
              />
              <Button
                type="button"
                variant="standard"
                size="sm"
                onClick={handleFetchCoverUrl}
                disabled={!coverUrlInput.trim() || fetchingUrl}
                className="gap-1 text-xs shrink-0"
              >
                {fetchingUrl ? (
                  <Loader2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5 animate-spin" />
                ) : (
                  <LinkIcon className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-[var(--text-2)]" />
                )}
                Fetch
              </Button>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Title & Author */}
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-3">
          <div>
            <label
              htmlFor="meta-title"
              className="block text-xs font-medium text-[var(--text-2)] mb-1"
            >
              Title <span className="text-[var(--danger)]">*</span>
            </label>
            <input
              id="meta-title"
              type="text"
              required
              value={title}
              onChange={(e) => setTitle(e.target.value)}
              className="w-full text-xs px-2.5 py-2 rounded-md bg-[var(--layer-2)] border border-[var(--stroke)] text-[var(--text-1)] outline-none focus:border-[var(--accent)]"
            />
          </div>
          <div>
            <label
              htmlFor="meta-author"
              className="block text-xs font-medium text-[var(--text-2)] mb-1"
            >
              Author
            </label>
            <input
              id="meta-author"
              type="text"
              value={author}
              onChange={(e) => setAuthor(e.target.value)}
              className="w-full text-xs px-2.5 py-2 rounded-md bg-[var(--layer-2)] border border-[var(--stroke)] text-[var(--text-1)] outline-none focus:border-[var(--accent)]"
            />
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Series & Volume Number */}
        <div className="grid grid-cols-3 gap-3">
          <div className="col-span-2">
            <label
              htmlFor="meta-series"
              className="block text-xs font-medium text-[var(--text-2)] mb-1"
            >
              Series name
            </label>
            <input
              id="meta-series"
              type="text"
              placeholder="e.g. The Lord of the Rings"
              value={series}
              onChange={(e) => setSeries(e.target.value)}
              className="w-full text-xs px-2.5 py-2 rounded-md bg-[var(--layer-2)] border border-[var(--stroke)] text-[var(--text-1)] outline-none focus:border-[var(--accent)]"
            />
          </div>
          <div>
            <label
              htmlFor="meta-volume"
              className="block text-xs font-medium text-[var(--text-2)] mb-1"
            >
              Volume #
            </label>
            <input
              id="meta-volume"
              type="number"
              step="any"
              placeholder="e.g. 1"
              value={seriesNumber}
              onChange={(e) => setSeriesNumber(e.target.value)}
              className="w-full text-xs px-2.5 py-2 rounded-md bg-[var(--layer-2)] border border-[var(--stroke)] text-[var(--text-1)] outline-none focus:border-[var(--accent)]"
            />
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Rating & Language */}
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-3 items-center">
          <div>
            <span className="block text-xs font-medium text-[var(--text-2)] mb-1">
              Rating (0–5 stars)
            </span>
            <div className="flex items-center gap-1 py-1">
              {[1, 2, 3, 4, 5].map((star) => (
                <button
                  key={star}
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setRating(rating === star ? 0 : star)}
                  className="p-1 rounded hover:bg-[var(--layer-3)] transition-colors cursor-pointer text-[var(--text-3)] hover:text-amber-400"
                  aria-label={`Rate ${star} star`}
                >
                  <Star
                    className={`w-4 h-4 ${
                      star <= rating ? 'fill-amber-400 text-amber-400' : ''
                    }`}
                  />
                </button>
              ))}
              {rating > 0 && (
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setRating(0)}
                  className="text-[11px] text-[var(--text-3)] hover:text-[var(--text-1)] ml-2"
                >
                  Clear
                </button>
              )}
            </div>
          </div>
          <div>
            <label
              htmlFor="meta-lang"
              className="block text-xs font-medium text-[var(--text-2)] mb-1"
            >
              Language
            </label>
            <input
              id="meta-lang"

              placeholder="e.g. en, ja, fr"
              value={language}
              onChange={(e) => setLanguage(e.target.value)}
              className="w-full text-xs px-2.5 py-2 rounded-md bg-[var(--layer-2)] border border-[var(--stroke)] text-[var(--text-1)] outline-none focus:border-[var(--accent)]"

                >
                  <X className="w-3 h-3" />

                id="meta-tag-input"
                type="text"
                placeholder={tags.length === 0 ? 'Type tag and press Enter...' : 'Add tag...'}
                value={tagInput}
                onChange={(e) => setTagInput(e.target.value)}
                onKeyDown={(e) => {
                  if (e.key === 'Enter' || e.key === ',') {
                    e.preventDefault();
                    handleAddTag();
                  }
                }}
                className="w-full text-xs bg-transparent border-none outline-none text-[var(--text-1)] placeholder-[var(--text-3)] px-1"
              />
              {tagInput.trim() && (
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={handleAddTag}
                  className="p-1 text-[var(--accent)] hover:bg-[var(--layer-3)] rounded cursor-pointer"
                >
                  <Plus className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                </button>
              )}
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Description */}
        <div>
          <label
            htmlFor="meta-description"
            className="block text-xs font-medium text-[var(--text-2)] mb-1"
          >
            Description
          </label>
          <textarea
            id="meta-description"
            rows={3}
            placeholder="Book summary or synopsis..."
            value={description}
            onChange={(e) => setDescription(e.target.value)}
            className="w-full text-xs px-2.5 py-2 rounded-md bg-[var(--layer-2)] border border-[var(--stroke)] text-[var(--text-1)] outline-none focus:border-[var(--accent)] resize-y"
          />
        </div>

        {/* Actions */}
        <div className="flex justify-end gap-2 pt-2 border-t border-[var(--stroke)]">
          <Button type="button" variant="subtle" onClick={onClose} disabled={saving}>
            Cancel
          </Button>
          <Button type="submit" variant="accent" disabled={saving}>
            {saving ? <Loader2 className="w-4 h-4 animate-spin" /> : 'Save changes'}
          </Button>
        </div>
      </form>
    </Dialog>
  );
};

import React, { type ReactNode } from 'react';

export interface EmptyStateProps {

  title: ReactNode;
  description?: ReactNode;
  action?: ReactNode;
  className?: string;
}

export const EmptyState: React.FC<EmptyStateProps> = ({
  icon,
  title,
  description,
  action,
  className,
}) => {
  return (
    <div
      className={twMerge(
        clsx(
          'flex flex-col items-center justify-center text-center p-8 max-w-[420px] mx-auto select-none',

          {description}
        </p>
      )}

  );

File: apps/client/src/design/components/FocusTrap.tsx

import React, { useEffect, useRef } from 'react';

export interface FocusTrapProps {
  children: React.ReactNode;
  active?: boolean;
  onClose?: () => void;
  className?: string;
  autoFocus?: boolean;
  restoreFocus?: boolean;
}

const FOCUSABLE_SELECTOR =
  'a[href], button:not([disabled]), textarea:not([disabled]), input:not([disabled]), select:not([disabled]), [tabindex]:not([tabindex="-1"])';

function isFocusableVisible(el: HTMLElement): boolean {
  if (el.hasAttribute('disabled') || el.getAttribute('aria-hidden') === 'true' || el.hasAttribute('hidden')) {
    return false;
  }
  if (el.style.display === 'none' || el.style.visibility === 'hidden') {
    return false;
  }
  if (typeof el.offsetParent !== 'undefined' && el.offsetParent === null) {
    let cur: HTMLElement | null = el;
    while (cur) {
      if (cur.style.display === 'none' || cur.style.visibility === 'hidden' || cur.hasAttribute('hidden')) {
        return false;
      }
      cur = cur.parentElement;
    }
  }
  return true;
}

export function FocusTrap({
  children,
  active = true,
  onClose,
  className,
  autoFocus = true,
  restoreFocus = true,
}: FocusTrapProps) {
  const containerRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const previousActiveElementRef = useRef<HTMLElement | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!active) return;

    previousActiveElementRef.current = document.activeElement as HTMLElement | null;

    if (autoFocus && containerRef.current) {
      // Small timeout to allow children to mount and layout
      const timer = window.setTimeout(() => {
        if (!containerRef.current) return;
        const focusables = Array.from(
          containerRef.current.querySelectorAll<HTMLElement>(FOCUSABLE_SELECTOR),
        ).filter(isFocusableVisible);

        if (focusables.length > 0) {
          focusables[0].focus();
        } else {
          containerRef.current.focus();
        }
      }, 30);
      return () => window.clearTimeout(timer);
    }
  }, [active, autoFocus]);

  useEffect(() => {
    return () => {
      if (restoreFocus && previousActiveElementRef.current && typeof previousActiveElementRef.current.focus === 'function') {
        previousActiveElementRef.current.focus();
      }
    };
  }, [restoreFocus]);

  const handleKeyDown = (e: React.KeyboardEvent) => {
    if (!active || !containerRef.current) return;

    if (e.key === 'Escape' && onClose) {
      e.preventDefault();
      onClose();
      return;
    }

    if (e.key === 'Tab') {
      const focusables = Array.from(
        containerRef.current.querySelectorAll<HTMLElement>(FOCUSABLE_SELECTOR),
      ).filter(isFocusableVisible);

      if (focusables.length === 0) {
        e.preventDefault();
        return;
      }

      const first = focusables[0];
      const last = focusables[focusables.length - 1];

      if (e.shiftKey) {
        if (document.activeElement === first || !containerRef.current.contains(document.activeElement)) {
          e.preventDefault();
          last.focus();
        }
      } else {
        if (document.activeElement === last || !containerRef.current.contains(document.activeElement)) {
          e.preventDefault();
          first.focus();
        }
      }
    }
  };

  return (
    <div
      ref={containerRef}
      className={className}
      onKeyDown={handleKeyDown}
      tabIndex={-1}
      style={{ outline: 'none' }}
    >
      {children}
    </div>
  );
}

import React, { useState } from 'react';
import * as RadixDialog from '@radix-ui/react-dialog';
import { X, ZoomIn, ZoomOut, RotateCcw } from 'lucide-react';
import { IconButton } from './IconButton';
import { Slider } from './Slider';

export interface ImageViewerDialogProps {
  open: boolean;
  onOpenChange: (open: boolean) => void;
  src: string;
  alt?: string;
}

export const ImageViewerDialog: React.FC<ImageViewerDialogProps> = ({
  open,
  onOpenChange,
  src,
  alt = 'Image preview',
}) => {
  const [scale, setScale] = useState(1);

  const zoomIn = () => setScale((s) => Math.min(3, s + 0.25));
  const zoomOut = () => setScale((s) => Math.max(0.5, s - 0.25));
  const resetZoom = () => setScale(1);

  return (
    <RadixDialog.Root
      open={open}
      onOpenChange={(next) => {
        if (!next) resetZoom();
        onOpenChange(next);
      }}
    >
      <RadixDialog.Portal>
        <RadixDialog.Overlay className="fixed inset-0 z-50 bg-black/85 backdrop-blur-md animate-in fade-in-0 duration-150" />
        <RadixDialog.Content className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex flex-col items-center justify-between p-4 outline-none select-none text-[var(--text-1)]">
          {/* Header Controls */}
          <div className="w-full flex items-center justify-between max-w-4xl px-2 py-1">
            <span className="text-xs font-medium text-[var(--text-2)] truncate max-w-sm">
              {alt || 'Image zoom'}
            </span>
            <RadixDialog.Close asChild>
              <IconButton label="Close" icon={<X className="w-5 h-5" />} variant="subtle" />
            </RadixDialog.Close>
          </div>

          {/* Interactive Zoomable Viewport */}
          <div className="relative flex-1 w-full flex items-center justify-center overflow-auto p-4 cursor-grab active:cursor-grabbing">
            <img
              src={src}
              alt={alt}
              style={{
                transform: `scale(${scale})`,
                transition: 'transform 150ms cubic-bezier(0.1, 0.9, 0.2, 1)',
                maxHeight: '80vh',
                maxWidth: '90vw',
                objectFit: 'contain',
              }}
              className="rounded-[var(--r-md)] shadow-2xl pointer-events-auto"
              draggable={false}
            />
          </div>

          {/* Bottom Zoom Toolbar */}
          <div className="flex items-center gap-3 px-4 py-2 rounded-[var(--r-pill)] bg-[var(--layer-solid)] border border-[var(--stroke)] shadow-[var(--shadow-flyout)]">
            <IconButton
              label="Zoom out"
              icon={<ZoomOut className="w-4 h-4" />}
              size="sm"
              variant="subtle"
              onClick={zoomOut}
              disabled={scale <= 0.5}
            />
            <div className="w-28 flex items-center">
              <Slider
                value={[scale]}
                min={0.5}
                max={3}
                step={0.1}
                onValueChange={([val]) => setScale(val)}
              />
            </div>
            <IconButton
              label="Zoom in"
              icon={<ZoomIn className="w-4 h-4" />}
              size="sm"
              variant="subtle"
              onClick={zoomIn}
              disabled={scale >= 3}
            />
            <div className="w-[1px] h-4 bg-[var(--stroke)] mx-1" />
            <IconButton
              label="Reset zoom"
              icon={<RotateCcw className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />}
              size="sm"
              variant="subtle"
              onClick={resetZoom}
            />
            <span className="text-xs font-mono text-[var(--text-2)] min-w-[36px] text-right">
              {Math.round(scale * 100)}%
            </span>
          </div>
        </RadixDialog.Content>
      </RadixDialog.Portal>
    </RadixDialog.Root>
  );
};

File: apps/client/src/design/components/ImportProgressDialog.tsx
================
import React from 'react';
import { Loader2, CheckCircle2, AlertCircle, XCircle, Clock } from 'lucide-react';
import { Dialog } from './Dialog';
import { Button } from './Button';

export interface FileImportStatus {
  name: string;
  size: number;
  status: 'pending' | 'processing' | 'done' | 'skipped' | 'error';
  error?: string;
}

export interface ImportProgressDialogProps {
  open: boolean;
  files: FileImportStatus[];
  onCancel: () => void;
  onClose: () => void;
  isComplete: boolean;
}

export const ImportProgressDialog: React.FC<ImportProgressDialogProps> = ({
  open,
  files,
  onCancel,
  onClose,
  isComplete,
}) => {
  const completedCount = files.filter(
    (f) => f.status === 'done' || f.status === 'skipped' || f.status === 'error',
  ).length;
  const pct = files.length > 0 ? Math.round((completedCount / files.length) * 100) : 0;

  return (
    <Dialog
      open={open}
      onClose={isComplete ? onClose : onCancel}
      title={isComplete ? 'Import complete' : 'Importing books…'}
      className="max-w-lg"
    >
      <div className="space-y-4">
        {/* Progress bar */}
        <div className="space-y-1.5">
          <div className="flex justify-between text-xs text-[var(--text-2)] font-medium">
            <span>
              {completedCount} of {files.length} processed
            </span>
            <span className="text-[var(--accent)] font-semibold">{pct}%</span>
          </div>
          <div className="w-full h-2 rounded-full bg-[var(--layer-2)] overflow-hidden">
            <div
              className="h-full bg-[var(--accent)] rounded-full transition-all duration-[var(--d-fast)]"
              style={{ width: `${pct}%` }}
            />
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Per-file list */}
        <div className="max-h-60 overflow-y-auto space-y-1.5 p-1 rounded-md bg-[var(--layer-2)] border border-[var(--stroke)] text-xs">
          {files.map((file, idx) => (
            <div
              key={`${file.name}-${idx}`}
              className="flex items-center justify-between p-2 rounded hover:bg-[var(--layer-3)] transition-colors gap-2"
            >
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2 min-w-0">
                {file.status === 'pending' && (
                  <Clock className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-[var(--text-3)] shrink-0" />
                )}
                {file.status === 'processing' && (
                  <Loader2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-[var(--accent)] animate-spin shrink-0" />
                )}
                {file.status === 'done' && (
                  <CheckCircle2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-[var(--success)] shrink-0" />
                )}
                {file.status === 'skipped' && (
                  <AlertCircle className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-[var(--warning)] shrink-0" />
                )}
                {file.status === 'error' && (
                  <XCircle className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-[var(--danger)] shrink-0" />
                )}
                <span className="truncate text-[var(--text-1)] font-medium">
                  {file.name}
                </span>
              </div>

              <div className="shrink-0 text-[11px]">
                {file.status === 'pending' && (
                  <span className="text-[var(--text-3)]">Queued</span>
                )}
                {file.status === 'processing' && (
                  <span className="text-[var(--accent)]">Processing</span>
                )}
                {file.status === 'done' && (
                  <span className="text-[var(--success)] font-medium">Added</span>
                )}
                {file.status === 'skipped' && (
                  <span className="text-[var(--text-3)]">Skipped</span>
                )}
                {file.status === 'error' && (
                  <span className="text-[var(--danger)]" title={file.error}>
                    Failed
                  </span>
                )}
              </div>
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>

        {/* Dialog Actions */}
        <div className="flex justify-end gap-2 pt-2 border-t border-[var(--stroke)]">
          {!isComplete ? (
            <Button variant="subtle" size="sm" onClick={onCancel}>
              Cancel remaining
            </Button>
          ) : (
            <Button variant="accent" size="sm" onClick={onClose}>
              Done
            </Button>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
    </Dialog>
  );
};

import React, { type ReactNode } from 'react';
import { Info, CheckCircle2, AlertTriangle, AlertCircle, X } from 'lucide-react';
import { clsx } from 'clsx';
import { twMerge } from 'tailwind-merge';

export type InfoBarSeverity = 'info' | 'success' | 'warning' | 'error';

export interface InfoBarProps {
  severity?: InfoBarSeverity;
  title?: ReactNode;
  message: ReactNode;
  action?: ReactNode;
  onClose?: () => void;
  className?: string;
}

export const InfoBar: React.FC<InfoBarProps> = ({
  severity = 'info',
  title,
  message,
  action,
  onClose,
  className,
}) => {
  const icons = {
    info: <Info className="w-4 h-4 text-[var(--accent)] shrink-0" />,
    success: <CheckCircle2 className="w-4 h-4 text-[var(--success)] shrink-0" />,
    warning: <AlertTriangle className="w-4 h-4 text-[var(--warning)] shrink-0" />,
    error: <AlertCircle className="w-4 h-4 text-[var(--danger)] shrink-0" />,
  };

  const borders = {
    info: 'border-[var(--stroke)] bg-[var(--layer-2)]',
    success: 'border-[color-mix(in_oklab,var(--success)_30%,transparent)] bg-[color-mix(in_oklab,var(--success)_10%,transparent)]',
    warning: 'border-[color-mix(in_oklab,var(--warning)_30%,transparent)] bg-[color-mix(in_oklab,var(--warning)_10%,transparent)]',
    error: 'border-[color-mix(in_oklab,var(--danger)_30%,transparent)] bg-[color-mix(in_oklab,var(--danger)_10%,transparent)]',
  };

  return (
    <div
      role="alert"
      className={twMerge(
        clsx(
          'flex items-start justify-between gap-3 p-3 rounded-[var(--r-md)] border text-sm text-[var(--text-1)] shadow-xs animate-in fade-in-0 duration-150',
          borders[severity],
          className,
        ),
      )}
    >
      <div className="flex items-start gap-2.5 grow">
        <span className="mt-0.5">{icons[severity]}</span>
        <div className="grow">
          {title && <div className="font-semibold text-sm mb-0.5">{title}</div>}
          <div className="text-xs text-[var(--text-2)]">{message}</div>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="flex items-center gap-2 shrink-0">
        {action}
        {onClose && (
          <button
            type="button"
            aria-label="Dismiss alert"
            onClick={onClose}
            className="p-1 text-[var(--text-3)] hover:text-[var(--text-1)] rounded-[var(--r-sm)] cursor-pointer outline-none"
          >
            <X className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
          </button>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

import React, { type ReactNode } from 'react';
import * as RadixPopover from '@radix-ui/react-popover';
import { clsx } from 'clsx';
import { twMerge } from 'tailwind-merge';

export interface PopoverProps {
  open?: boolean;
  onOpenChange?: (open: boolean) => void;
  trigger: ReactNode;
  children: ReactNode;
  align?: 'start' | 'center' | 'end';
  side?: 'top' | 'right' | 'bottom' | 'left';
  sideOffset?: number;
  className?: string;
}

export const Popover: React.FC<PopoverProps> = ({
  open,
  onOpenChange,
  trigger,
  children,
  align = 'center',
  side = 'bottom',
  sideOffset = 6,
  className,
}) => {
  return (
    <RadixPopover.Root open={open} onOpenChange={onOpenChange}>
      <RadixPopover.Trigger asChild>{trigger}</RadixPopover.Trigger>
      <RadixPopover.Portal>
        <RadixPopover.Content
          align={align}
          side={side}
          sideOffset={sideOffset}
          className={twMerge(
            clsx(
              'z-50 rounded-[var(--r-lg)] bg-[var(--layer-solid)] p-3 text-[var(--text-1)] shadow-[var(--shadow-flyout)] border border-[var(--stroke)] outline-none',
              'animate-in fade-in-0 duration-150 data-[side=bottom]:slide-in-from-top-1.5 data-[side=top]:slide-in-from-bottom-1.5 data-[side=left]:slide-in-from-right-1.5 data-[side=right]:slide-in-from-left-1.5',
              className,
            ),
          )}
        >
          {children}
        </RadixPopover.Content>
      </RadixPopover.Portal>
    </RadixPopover.Root>
  );
};
