File: apps/client/src/design/components/SeriesDetailView.tsx
================
import React, { useMemo } from 'react';
import { CheckCircle, Play } from 'lucide-react';
import { type Book, type Progress } from '../../db';
import { Dialog } from './Dialog';
import { Button } from './Button';
import { BookCover } from './BookCover';

export interface SeriesDetailViewProps {
  open: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
  seriesName: string;
  books: Book[];
  progressMap: Map<string, Progress>;
  onOpenBook: (book: Book) => void;
  favIds: Set<string>;
  onToggleFavorite: (bookId: string) => void;
}

export const SeriesDetailView: React.FC<SeriesDetailViewProps> = ({
  open,
  onClose,
  seriesName,
  books,
  progressMap,
  onOpenBook,
  favIds,
  onToggleFavorite,
}) => {
  // Sort books by series_number ascending, then by title
  const sortedBooks = useMemo(() => {
    return [...books].sort((a, b) => {
      const numA = a.meta.series_number ?? 999999;
      const numB = b.meta.series_number ?? 999999;
      if (numA !== numB) return numA - numB;
      return a.title.localeCompare(b.title);
    });
  }, [books]);

  // Compute overall progress
  const { avgPercent, completedCount, nextBookToRead } = useMemo(() => {
    if (sortedBooks.length === 0) {
      return { avgPercent: 0, completedCount: 0, nextBookToRead: null };
    }
    let totalPct = 0;
    let completed = 0;
    let next: Book | null = null;

    for (const b of sortedBooks) {
      const p = progressMap.get(b.id)?.percent ?? 0;
      totalPct += p;
      if (p >= 0.99 || b.meta.finished_at) {
        completed++;
      } else if (!next) {
        next = b;
      }
    }

    return {
      avgPercent: Math.round((totalPct / sortedBooks.length) * 100),
      completedCount: completed,
      nextBookToRead: next ?? sortedBooks[0],
    };
  }, [sortedBooks, progressMap]);

  if (!open) return null;

  const leadBook = sortedBooks[0];
  const author = leadBook?.author ?? 'Unknown author';

  return (
    <Dialog open={open} onClose={onClose} title={seriesName} className="max-w-2xl max-h-[85vh]">
      <div className="space-y-6">
        {/* Series Header Hero Card */}
        <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row gap-5 p-4 rounded-xl bg-[var(--layer-2)] border border-[var(--stroke)] items-center sm:items-start">
          {leadBook && (
            <div className="w-24 shrink-0 shadow-md">
              <BookCover
                id={leadBook.id}
                title={leadBook.title}
                author={leadBook.author}
                coverHash={leadBook.cover_hash}
                className="w-24 pointer-events-none"
              />
            </div>
          )}

          <div className="flex-1 text-center sm:text-left space-y-3">
            <div>
              <span className="text-xs uppercase tracking-wider font-semibold text-[var(--accent)]">
                Book Series
              </span>
              <h3 className="text-lg font-bold text-[var(--text-1)] mt-0.5 leading-tight">
                {seriesName}
              </h3>
              <p className="text-xs text-[var(--text-2)] mt-0.5">by {author}</p>
            </div>

            {/* Combined Progress */}
            <div className="space-y-1.5 max-w-sm mx-auto sm:mx-0">
              <div className="flex justify-between text-xs text-[var(--text-2)] font-medium">
                <span>
                  {completedCount} of {sortedBooks.length} volumes read
                </span>
                <span className="text-[var(--accent)]">{avgPercent}%</span>
              </div>
              <div className="w-full h-2 rounded-full bg-[var(--layer-3)] overflow-hidden">
                <div
                  className="h-full bg-[var(--accent)] rounded-full transition-all duration-[var(--d-normal)]"
                  style={{ width: `${avgPercent}%` }}
                />
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* Continue Reading Action */}
            {nextBookToRead && (
              <div className="pt-1">
                <Button
                  variant="accent"
                  size="sm"
                  onClick={() => {
                    onOpenBook(nextBookToRead);
                    onClose();
                  }}
                  className="gap-2 text-xs"
                >
                  <Play className="w-3.5 h-3.5 fill-current" />
                  Continue reading: {nextBookToRead.title}
                </Button>
              </div>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Volumes List */}
        <div>
          <h4 className="text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider text-[var(--text-3)] mb-3 px-1">
            Volumes in Series ({sortedBooks.length})
          </h4>

          <div className="space-y-2">
            {sortedBooks.map((book) => {
              const p = progressMap.get(book.id)?.percent ?? 0;
              const isFinished = p >= 0.99 || !!book.meta.finished_at;
              const pctDisplay = Math.round(p * 100);

              return (
                <div
                  key={book.id}
                  onClick={() => {
                    onOpenBook(book);
                    onClose();
                  }}
                  className="flex items-center gap-3 p-2.5 rounded-lg bg-[var(--layer-2)] hover:bg-[var(--layer-3)] border border-[var(--stroke)] transition-colors cursor-pointer group select-none"
                >
                  <div className="w-12 shrink-0">
                    <BookCover
                      id={book.id}
                      title={book.title}
                      author={book.author}
                      coverHash={book.cover_hash}
                      isFavourite={favIds.has(book.id)}
                      onToggleFavourite={() => onToggleFavorite(book.id)}
                      className="w-12 pointer-events-none"
                    />
                  </div>

                  <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                      {book.meta.series_number !== undefined && (
                        <span className="text-[11px] font-mono px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded bg-[var(--layer-3)] text-[var(--text-2)] shrink-0">
                          Vol. {book.meta.series_number}
                        </span>
                      )}
                      <h5 className="text-xs font-medium text-[var(--text-1)] truncate group-hover:text-[var(--accent)] transition-colors">
                        {book.title}
                      </h5>
                    </div>
                    <p className="text-[11px] text-[var(--text-3)] truncate mt-0.5">
                      {book.format} • {book.author}
                    </p>
                  </div>

                  {/* Progress status */}
                  <div className="text-right shrink-0 pr-1">
                    {isFinished ? (
                      <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 text-xs text-[var(--success)] font-medium">
                        <CheckCircle className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                        Finished
                      </span>
                    ) : p > 0 ? (
                      <span className="text-xs text-[var(--accent)] font-medium">
                        {pctDisplay}%
                      </span>
                    ) : (
                      <span className="text-xs text-[var(--text-3)]">Unread</span>
                    )}
                  </div>
                </div>
              );
            })}
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </Dialog>
  );
};

================
File: apps/client/src/design/components/Shelf.tsx
================
import { useRef, useState, useEffect, type FC, type ReactNode } from 'react';
import { ChevronLeft, ChevronRight } from 'lucide-react';
import { twMerge } from 'tailwind-merge';

export interface ShelfProps {
  title?: ReactNode;
  action?: ReactNode;
  children: ReactNode;
  className?: string;
}

export const Shelf: FC<ShelfProps> = ({ title, action, children, className }) => {
  const scrollRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const [canScrollLeft, setCanScrollLeft] = useState(false);
  const [canScrollRight, setCanScrollRight] = useState(false);

  const checkScroll = () => {
    if (!scrollRef.current) return;
    const { scrollLeft, scrollWidth, clientWidth } = scrollRef.current;
    setCanScrollLeft(scrollLeft > 4);
    setCanScrollRight(scrollLeft + clientWidth < scrollWidth - 4);
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    checkScroll();
    const el = scrollRef.current;
    if (el) {
      el.addEventListener('scroll', checkScroll, { passive: true });
      window.addEventListener('resize', checkScroll);
    }
    return () => {
      if (el) el.removeEventListener('scroll', checkScroll);
      window.removeEventListener('resize', checkScroll);
    };
  }, [children]);

  const scrollBy = (offset: number) => {
    scrollRef.current?.scrollBy({ left: offset, behavior: 'smooth' });
  };

  return (
    <section className={twMerge('relative flex flex-col group/shelf my-4', className)}>
      {(title || action) && (
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-3 px-1">
          {title && (
            <h2 className="text-base sm:text-lg font-semibold text-[var(--text-1)] tracking-tight">
              {title}
            </h2>
          )}
          {action && <div className="text-xs sm:text-sm font-medium text-[var(--accent)]">{action}</div>}
        </div>
      )}

      <div className="relative">
        {/* Left scroll arrow */}
        {canScrollLeft && (
          <button
            type="button"
            aria-label="Scroll left"
            onClick={() => scrollBy(-320)}
            className="hidden md:flex absolute -left-3 top-[40%] -translate-y-1/2 z-30 w-9 h-9 rounded-full bg-[var(--layer-solid)] text-[var(--text-1)] border border-[var(--stroke)] shadow-[var(--shadow-flyout)] items-center justify-center opacity-0 group-hover/shelf:opacity-100 transition-opacity cursor-pointer hover:bg-[var(--layer-3)] outline-none focus-visible:opacity-100 focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-[var(--accent)]"
          >
            <ChevronLeft className="w-5 h-5" />
          </button>
        )}

        {/* Scrollable track */}
        <div
          ref={scrollRef}
          className="flex gap-4 overflow-x-auto scroll-smooth no-scrollbar py-2 px-1 snap-x snap-mandatory"
        >
          {children}
        </div>

        {/* Right scroll arrow */}
        {canScrollRight && (
          <button
            type="button"
            aria-label="Scroll right"
            onClick={() => scrollBy(320)}
            className="hidden md:flex absolute -right-3 top-[40%] -translate-y-1/2 z-30 w-9 h-9 rounded-full bg-[var(--layer-solid)] text-[var(--text-1)] border border-[var(--stroke)] shadow-[var(--shadow-flyout)] items-center justify-center opacity-0 group-hover/shelf:opacity-100 transition-opacity cursor-pointer hover:bg-[var(--layer-3)] outline-none focus-visible:opacity-100 focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-[var(--accent)]"
          >
            <ChevronRight className="w-5 h-5" />
          </button>
        )}
      </div>
    </section>
  );
};

File: apps/client/src/design/components/Switch.tsx

import React from 'react';
import * as RadixSwitch from '@radix-ui/react-switch';
import { clsx } from 'clsx';
import { twMerge } from 'tailwind-merge';

export interface SwitchProps {
  checked: boolean;
  onCheckedChange: (checked: boolean) => void;
  label?: React.ReactNode;
  disabled?: boolean;
  className?: string;
}

export const Switch: React.FC<SwitchProps> = ({
  checked,
  onCheckedChange,
  label,
  disabled,
  className,
}) => {
  const switchElement = (
    <RadixSwitch.Root
      checked={checked}
      onCheckedChange={onCheckedChange}
      disabled={disabled}
      className={twMerge(
        clsx(
          'relative w-[40px] h-[22px] rounded-full transition-colors cursor-pointer outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-[var(--accent)] border border-[var(--stroke)] shrink-0',
          checked ? 'bg-[var(--accent)]' : 'bg-[var(--layer-3)]',
          disabled && 'opacity-40 pointer-events-none',
          className,
        ),
      )}
    >
      <RadixSwitch.Thumb
        className={clsx(
          'block w-[16px] h-[16px] rounded-full bg-[var(--layer-solid)] transition-transform duration-[var(--d-fast)] ease-[var(--ease-out)] shadow-xs',
          checked ? 'translate-x-[20px]' : 'translate-x-[2px]',
        )}
      />
    </RadixSwitch.Root>
  );

  if (!label) return switchElement;

  return (
    <label className="flex items-center justify-between gap-4 cursor-pointer select-none py-1">
      <span className="text-sm font-medium text-[var(--text-1)]">{label}</span>
      {switchElement}
    </label>
  );
};

import React, { forwardRef, type ButtonHTMLAttributes, type ReactNode } from 'react';
import { clsx } from 'clsx';
import { twMerge } from 'tailwind-merge';
import { Tooltip } from './Tooltip';

export interface ToggleButtonProps extends ButtonHTMLAttributes<HTMLButtonElement> {
  pressed: boolean;
  onPressedChange?: (pressed: boolean) => void;
  label: string;
  icon: ReactNode;
  activeIcon?: ReactNode;
  children?: ReactNode;
}

export const ToggleButton = forwardRef<HTMLButtonElement, ToggleButtonProps>(
  (
    {
      pressed,
      onPressedChange,
      label,
      icon,
      activeIcon,
      children,
      className,
      disabled,
      onClick,
      ...props
    },
    ref,
  ) => {
    const handleClick = (e: React.MouseEvent<HTMLButtonElement>) => {
      onClick?.(e);
      if (!e.defaultPrevented) {
        onPressedChange?.(!pressed);
      }
    };

    const displayIcon = pressed && activeIcon ? activeIcon : icon;

    const button = (
      <button
        ref={ref}
        type="button"
        aria-pressed={pressed}
        aria-label={label}
        disabled={disabled}
        onClick={handleClick}
        className={twMerge(
          clsx(
            'inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 h-[32px] px-2.5 rounded-[var(--r-md)] text-sm font-medium transition-colors cursor-pointer outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-[var(--accent)] disabled:pointer-events-none disabled:opacity-40 select-none',
            pressed
              ? 'bg-[var(--accent-soft)] text-[var(--accent)] border border-[color-mix(in_oklab,var(--accent)_30%,transparent)]'
              : 'bg-transparent hover:bg-[var(--layer-2)] active:bg-[var(--layer-3)] text-[var(--text-2)] border-transparent',
            className,
          ),
        )}
        {...props}
      >
        <span className="shrink-0">{displayIcon}</span>
        {children && <span>{children}</span>}
      </button>
    );

    return <Tooltip content={label}>{button}</Tooltip>;
  },
);

ToggleButton.displayName = 'ToggleButton';

File: apps/client/src/lib/backStack.ts
================
import { isTauri } from './tauri';

export type BackHandler = () => boolean;

interface RegisteredHandler {
  id: string;
  handler: BackHandler;
  priority: number;
}

const backHandlers: RegisteredHandler[] = [];
let nextId = 1;

/**
 * Registers a dismiss/close handler for a UI layer (modals, sheets, popovers, image viewer).
 * Higher priority handlers are executed first when the user presses Android Back.
 * Handlers return `true` if they consumed the back action, `false` otherwise.
 */
export function registerBackHandler(
  handler: BackHandler,
  priority = 10,
): () => void {
  const id = `back-h-${nextId++}`;
  backHandlers.push({ id, handler, priority });
  // Keep sorted descending by priority
  backHandlers.sort((a, b) => b.priority - a.priority);

  return () => {
    const idx = backHandlers.findIndex((h) => h.id === id);
    if (idx !== -1) {
      backHandlers.splice(idx, 1);
    }
  };
}

/**
 * Executes the topmost back handler. Returns true if an overlay/layer was closed.
 */
export function handleBackPress(): boolean {
  for (const entry of backHandlers) {
    try {
      const consumed = entry.handler();
      if (consumed) {
        return true;
      }
    } catch (err) {
      console.warn('Error in back handler:', err);
    }
  }
  return false;
}

/**
 * Returns number of registered back handlers.
 */
export function getBackHandlerCount(): number {
  return backHandlers.length;
}

/**
 * Clears all registered back handlers (useful in tests).
 */
export function clearBackHandlers(): void {
  backHandlers.length = 0;
}

/**
 * Initializes listeners for Android back button events (`folio://back-pressed`).
 */
export function initAndroidBackHandler(onFallbackBack?: () => void): () => void {
  let unlistenTauri = () => {};

  if (isTauri()) {
    void (async () => {
      try {
        const { listen } = await import('@tauri-apps/api/event');
        unlistenTauri = await listen('folio://back-pressed', () => {
          const consumed = handleBackPress();
          if (!consumed && onFallbackBack) {
            onFallbackBack();
          }
        });
      } catch (err) {
        console.warn('Failed to listen to folio://back-pressed:', err);
      }
    })();
  }

  // Also intercept Escape key on desktop/web as an accessibility convenience
  const onKeyDown = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
    if (e.key === 'Escape' && !e.defaultPrevented) {
      const consumed = handleBackPress();
      if (consumed) {
        e.preventDefault();
      }
    }
  };

  window.addEventListener('keydown', onKeyDown);

  return () => {
    unlistenTauri();
    window.removeEventListener('keydown', onKeyDown);
  };
}

File: apps/client/src/lib/crashReporter.ts
================
/**
 * Crash and Error Reporting abstraction
 * Opt-in by default per BUILD_PLAN.md §6 P13 & Cross-cutting requirements (Privacy).
 */

const STORAGE_KEY = 'folio_crash_reporting';
const CRASH_LOGS_KEY = 'folio_recent_crashes';
const MAX_LOCAL_CRASH_LOGS = 10;

export interface CrashReport {
  id: string;
  timestamp: number;
  message: string;
  stack?: string;
  context?: Record<string, unknown>;
  userAgent: string;
}

/**
 * Redacts potential sensitive data like bearer tokens, auth headers, passwords.
 */
export function sanitizeContext(data: unknown): unknown {
  if (!data || typeof data !== 'object') return data;
  if (Array.isArray(data)) return data.map(sanitizeContext);

  const clean: Record<string, unknown> = {};
  for (const [key, val] of Object.entries(data as Record<string, unknown>)) {
    const lowerKey = key.toLowerCase();
    if (
      lowerKey.includes('token') ||
      lowerKey.includes('secret') ||
      lowerKey.includes('password') ||
      lowerKey.includes('auth') ||
      lowerKey.includes('cookie')
    ) {
      clean[key] = '[REDACTED]';
    } else if (typeof val === 'string' && /bearer\s+[a-zA-Z0-9_\-.]+/i.test(val)) {
      clean[key] = val.replace(/bearer\s+[a-zA-Z0-9_\-.]+/gi, 'Bearer [REDACTED]');
    } else if (typeof val === 'object' && val !== null) {
      clean[key] = sanitizeContext(val);
    } else {
      clean[key] = val;
    }
  }
  return clean;
}

export function isCrashReportingEnabled(): boolean {
  try {
    return localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEY) === 'true';
  } catch {
    return false;
  }
}

export function setCrashReportingEnabled(enabled: boolean): void {
  try {
    localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEY, enabled ? 'true' : 'false');
  } catch {
    // Ignore storage errors
  }
}

export function getRecentCrashReports(): CrashReport[] {
  try {
    const raw = localStorage.getItem(CRASH_LOGS_KEY);
    return raw ? JSON.parse(raw) : [];
  } catch {
    return [];
  }
}

export function clearCrashReports(): void {
  try {
    localStorage.removeItem(CRASH_LOGS_KEY);
  } catch {
    // Ignore
  }
}

export function reportCrash(error: unknown, context?: Record<string, unknown>): CrashReport | null {
  const err = error instanceof Error ? error : new Error(String(error));
  const cleanContext = (context ? sanitizeContext(context) : {}) as Record<string, unknown>;

  const report: CrashReport = {
    id: typeof crypto !== 'undefined' && crypto.randomUUID ? crypto.randomUUID() : String(Date.now()),
    timestamp: Date.now(),
    message: err.message,
    stack: err.stack,
    context: cleanContext,
    userAgent: typeof navigator !== 'undefined' ? navigator.userAgent : 'unknown',
  };

  // Always buffer in memory / local diagnostic storage (capped)
  try {
    const existing = getRecentCrashReports();
    const updated = [report, ...existing].slice(0, MAX_LOCAL_CRASH_LOGS);
    localStorage.setItem(CRASH_LOGS_KEY, JSON.stringify(updated));
  } catch {
    // Local storage quota or unavailable
  }

  if (isCrashReportingEnabled()) {
    console.info('[CrashReporter: Telemetry Dispatched]', {
      id: report.id,
      message: report.message,
    });
    return report;
  }

  return null;
}

File: apps/client/src/lib/customFonts.ts
================
import { useLiveQuery } from 'dexie-react-hooks';
import { db, dirty, type SettingsRow } from '../db';
import { sha256 } from './hash';

export interface CustomFontRecord {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  fileHash: string;
  fileName: string;
  format: 'truetype' | 'opentype' | 'woff2' | 'woff';
  addedAt: number;
}

// In-memory cache of created blob URLs for font faces
const activeFontUrls = new Map<string, string>();

export async function getCustomFonts(): Promise<CustomFontRecord[]> {
  const row = await db.settings.get('custom_fonts');
  if (!row || !row.data || !Array.isArray(row.data.fonts)) return [];
  return row.data.fonts as CustomFontRecord[];
}

export async function saveCustomFonts(fonts: CustomFontRecord[]): Promise<void> {
  const record: SettingsRow = {
    id: 'custom_fonts',
    data: { fonts: fonts as unknown as Record<string, unknown>[] },
    deleted_at: null,
    ...dirty(),
  };
  await db.settings.put(record);
}

export function detectFontFormat(fileName: string): 'truetype' | 'opentype' | 'woff2' | 'woff' {
  const ext = fileName.split('.').pop()?.toLowerCase();
  if (ext === 'woff2') return 'woff2';
  if (ext === 'woff') return 'woff';
  if (ext === 'otf') return 'opentype';
  return 'truetype';
}

export async function fileToArrayBuffer(file: Blob | File): Promise<ArrayBuffer> {
  if (typeof file.arrayBuffer === 'function') {
    return file.arrayBuffer();
  }
  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    const reader = new FileReader();
    reader.onload = () => resolve(reader.result as ArrayBuffer);
    reader.onerror = () => reject(reader.error);
    reader.readAsArrayBuffer(file);
  });
}

/**
 * Imports a custom TTF/OTF/WOFF2 font file, stores the blob in db.files,
 * and adds an entry to db.settings('custom_fonts').
 */
export async function importCustomFont(file: File): Promise<CustomFontRecord> {
  const buffer = await fileToArrayBuffer(file);
  const hash = await sha256(buffer);
  const blob = new Blob([buffer], { type: file.type || 'font/ttf' });

  // Store font blob in db.files
  await db.files.put({
    hash,
    blob,
  });

  const fontName = file.name.replace(/\.[^/.]+$/, '').trim();
  const format = detectFontFormat(file.name);
  const id = `font-${hash.slice(0, 12)}`;

  const newRecord: CustomFontRecord = {
    id,
    name: fontName,
    fileHash: hash,
    fileName: file.name,
    format,
    addedAt: Date.now(),
  };

  const existing = await getCustomFonts();
  const updated = [...existing.filter((f) => f.fileHash !== hash), newRecord];
  await saveCustomFonts(updated);

  return newRecord;
}

/**
 * Removes a custom font.
 */
export async function removeCustomFont(fontId: string): Promise<void> {
  const existing = await getCustomFonts();
  const fontToRemove = existing.find((f) => f.id === fontId);
  if (fontToRemove) {
    const blobUrl = activeFontUrls.get(fontToRemove.fileHash);
    if (blobUrl) {
      if (typeof URL.revokeObjectURL === 'function') {
        URL.revokeObjectURL(blobUrl);
      }
      activeFontUrls.delete(fontToRemove.fileHash);
    }
  }
  const updated = existing.filter((f) => f.id !== fontId);
  await saveCustomFonts(updated);
}

/**
 * Generates @font-face CSS rules for all available custom fonts.
 */
export async function loadCustomFontFaceCSS(): Promise<string> {
  const fonts = await getCustomFonts();
  if (fonts.length === 0) return '';

  const cssRules: string[] = [];

  for (const font of fonts) {
    let blobUrl = activeFontUrls.get(font.fileHash);
    if (!blobUrl) {
      const fileRecord = await db.files.get(font.fileHash);
      if (fileRecord?.blob) {
        blobUrl = typeof URL.createObjectURL === 'function'
          ? URL.createObjectURL(fileRecord.blob)
          : `blob:font-${font.fileHash}`;
        activeFontUrls.set(font.fileHash, blobUrl);
      }
    }

    if (blobUrl) {
      cssRules.push(`
@font-face {
  font-family: "${font.name}";
  src: url("${blobUrl}") format("${font.format}");
  font-weight: normal;
  font-style: normal;
  font-display: swap;
}`);
    }
  }

  return cssRules.join('\n');
}

/**
 * Hook to retrieve available custom fonts with live updates.
 */
export function useCustomFonts(): {
  fonts: CustomFontRecord[];
  importFont: (file: File) => Promise<CustomFontRecord>;
  removeFont: (id: string) => Promise<void>;
} {
  const row = useLiveQuery(() => db.settings.get('custom_fonts'), []);

  const fonts: CustomFontRecord[] =
    row?.data && Array.isArray(row.data.fonts) ? (row.data.fonts as CustomFontRecord[]) : [];

  return {
    fonts,
    importFont: importCustomFont,
    removeFont: removeCustomFont,
  };
}

File: apps/client/src/lib/exportZip.ts
================
import JSZip from 'jszip';
import { db, type Book } from '../db';

/**
 * Exports multiple books as a ZIP file download.
 * Falls back to single file download if only 1 book is selected.
 */
export async function exportBooks(
  books: Book[],
  onProgress?: (current: number, total: number) => void,
): Promise<void> {
  if (books.length === 0) return;

  if (books.length === 1) {
    const book = books[0];
    const fileRow = await db.files.get(book.file_hash);
    if (!fileRow) {
      throw new Error(`File content for "${book.title}" is not available locally.`);
    }
    const filename = book.meta.file_name || `${book.title}.${book.format.toLowerCase()}`;
    downloadBlob(fileRow.blob, filename);
    return;
  }

  const zip = new JSZip();
  let done = 0;

  for (const book of books) {
    const fileRow = await db.files.get(book.file_hash);
    if (fileRow) {
      const filename = book.meta.file_name || `${book.title}.${book.format.toLowerCase()}`;
      // Prevent file collisions inside zip
      const safeName = `${book.id.slice(0, 6)}_${filename.replace(/[<>:"/\\|?*]/g, '_')}`;
      zip.file(safeName, fileRow.blob);
    }
    done++;
    onProgress?.(done, books.length);
  }

  const zipBlob = await zip.generateAsync({ type: 'blob', compression: 'DEFLATE' });
  const timestamp = new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10);
  downloadBlob(zipBlob, `folio-library-export-${timestamp}.zip`);
}

function downloadBlob(blob: Blob, filename: string): void {
  const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
  const a = document.createElement('a');
  a.href = url;
  a.download = filename;
  document.body.appendChild(a);
  a.click();
  document.body.removeChild(a);
  setTimeout(() => URL.revokeObjectURL(url), 1000);
}

File: apps/client/src/lib/fullTextSearch.ts
================
import { db, type Book } from '../db';
import { makeBook } from './foliate';

export interface FtsEntry {
  bookId: string;
  sectionIndex: number;
  text: string;
}

/**
 * Checks if a book has been indexed for full-text search in kv store.
 */
export async function isBookIndexed(bookId: string): Promise<boolean> {
  const record = await db.kv.get(`fts:${bookId}`);
  return !!record;
}

/**
 * Builds full-text search index for a book by extracting text from its sections.
 */
export async function indexBookFullText(book: Book): Promise<void> {
  const fileRow = await db.files.get(book.file_hash);
  if (!fileRow) return;

  const file = new File([fileRow.blob], book.meta.file_name || `${book.title}.epub`, {
    type: book.meta.mime || 'application/epub+zip',
  });

  try {
    const foliateBook = await makeBook(file);
    const sections = foliateBook.sections ?? [];
    const entries: { index: number; snippet: string }[] = [];

    for (let i = 0; i < sections.length; i++) {
      const section = sections[i];
      if (section.linear === 'no' || !section.createDocument) continue;
      try {
        const doc = await section.createDocument();
        const text = (doc.body?.textContent ?? '').replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim();
        if (text) {
          // Store compressed or truncated text per section to conserve storage
          entries.push({ index: i, snippet: text.toLowerCase() });
        }
      } catch {
        // Skip unreadable sections
      }
      await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 0));
    }

    await db.kv.put({
      key: `fts:${book.id}`,
      value: entries,
    });
  } catch {
    // Ignore indexing errors on unsupported files
  }
}

/**
 * Searches across indexed books for a query string.
 * Returns set of book IDs containing the query.
 */
export async function searchFullText(query: string): Promise<Set<string>> {
  const q = query.trim().toLowerCase();
  if (!q) return new Set();

  const matchingBookIds = new Set<string>();
  const ftsKeys = await db.kv
    .filter((row) => row.key.startsWith('fts:'))
    .toArray();

  for (const item of ftsKeys) {
    const bookId = item.key.slice(4);
    const entries = item.value as { index: number; snippet: string }[] | undefined;
    if (Array.isArray(entries)) {
      const found = entries.some((e) => e.snippet.includes(q));
      if (found) {
        matchingBookIds.add(bookId);
      }
    }
  }

  return matchingBookIds;
}

import { useCallback } from 'react';
import { useLiveQuery } from 'dexie-react-hooks';
import { db, dirty, type SettingsRow } from '../db';

export interface ReadingGoals {
  dailyMinutes: number;
  yearlyBooks: number;
}

export const DEFAULT_GOALS: ReadingGoals = {
  dailyMinutes: 30,
  yearlyBooks: 20,
};

export async function getReadingGoals(): Promise<ReadingGoals> {
  const row = await db.settings.get('reading_goals');
  if (!row || !row.data) return DEFAULT_GOALS;
  const data = row.data as Partial<ReadingGoals>;
  return {
    dailyMinutes: typeof data.dailyMinutes === 'number' && data.dailyMinutes > 0 ? data.dailyMinutes : DEFAULT_GOALS.dailyMinutes,
    yearlyBooks: typeof data.yearlyBooks === 'number' && data.yearlyBooks > 0 ? data.yearlyBooks : DEFAULT_GOALS.yearlyBooks,
  };
}

export async function setReadingGoals(goals: Partial<ReadingGoals>): Promise<void> {
  const current = await getReadingGoals();
  const updated: ReadingGoals = {
    dailyMinutes: goals.dailyMinutes ?? current.dailyMinutes,
    yearlyBooks: goals.yearlyBooks ?? current.yearlyBooks,
  };

  const record: SettingsRow = {
    id: 'reading_goals',
    data: updated as unknown as Record<string, unknown>,
    deleted_at: null,
    ...dirty(),
  };
  await db.settings.put(record);
}

export function useReadingGoals(): {
  goals: ReadingGoals;
  updateGoals: (goals: Partial<ReadingGoals>) => Promise<void>;
} {
  const row = useLiveQuery(() => db.settings.get('reading_goals'), []);

  const goals: ReadingGoals = {
    dailyMinutes:
      typeof row?.data?.dailyMinutes === 'number' && (row.data.dailyMinutes as number) > 0
        ? (row.data.dailyMinutes as number)
        : DEFAULT_GOALS.dailyMinutes,
    yearlyBooks:
      typeof row?.data?.yearlyBooks === 'number' && (row.data.yearlyBooks as number) > 0
        ? (row.data.yearlyBooks as number)
        : DEFAULT_GOALS.yearlyBooks,
  };

  const updateGoals = useCallback(async (newGoals: Partial<ReadingGoals>) => {
    await setReadingGoals(newGoals);
  }, []);

  return { goals, updateGoals };
}

================
File: apps/client/src/lib/hash.ts
================
export async function sha256(data: ArrayBuffer): Promise<string> {
  const digest = await crypto.subtle.digest('SHA-256', data)
  return [...new Uint8Array(digest)].map((b) => b.toString(16).padStart(2, '0')).join('')
}

File: apps/client/src/lib/mangaPrefs.ts

// Device-local MangaDex content-rating preference. Adult ratings stay off unless
// the reader explicitly opts in (with an 18+ confirmation in Settings).
export type ContentRating = 'safe' | 'suggestive' | 'erotica' | 'pornographic'

const KEY = 'folio_manga_ratings'
const DEFAULT: ContentRating[] = ['safe', 'suggestive']
const listeners = new Set<() => void>()

export function getContentRatings(): ContentRating[] {
  try {
    const raw = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(KEY) ?? 'null') as ContentRating[] | null
    if (Array.isArray(raw) && raw.length) return ['safe', ...raw.filter((r) => r !== 'safe')]
  } catch {
    // Corrupt or unavailable storage: fall back to the safe default.
  }
  return [...DEFAULT]
}

export function setContentRating(rating: ContentRating, enabled: boolean): ContentRating[] {
  if (rating === 'safe') return getContentRatings()
  const next = new Set(getContentRatings())
  if (enabled) next.add(rating)
  else next.delete(rating)
  const list = [...next]
  try {
    localStorage.setItem(KEY, JSON.stringify(list))
  } catch {
    // Storage unavailable: the change applies until reload.
  }
  listeners.forEach((l) => l())
  return list
}

export function onContentRatingsChange(cb: () => void): () => void {
  listeners.add(cb)
  return () => listeners.delete(cb)
}

File: apps/client/src/lib/mobileUx.ts

import { useEffect, useRef } from 'react';
import { isTauri } from './tauri';

interface WakeLockSentinelLike {
  release(): Promise<void>;
}

let wakeLockSentinel: WakeLockSentinelLike | null = null;

interface NavigatorWithWakeLock {
  wakeLock?: {
    request(type: string): Promise<WakeLockSentinelLike>;
  };
}

/**
 * Keeps the screen awake during reading.
 * Uses the Web Screen Wake Lock API or native Android window flag via Tauri.
 */
export async function setKeepScreenOn(enabled: boolean): Promise<void> {
  if (enabled) {
    try {
      const nav = navigator as unknown as NavigatorWithWakeLock;
      if (nav.wakeLock?.request) {
        wakeLockSentinel = await nav.wakeLock.request('screen');
      }
    } catch {
      // Wake lock request failed or permissions denied
    }

    if (isTauri()) {
      try {
        const { invoke } = await import('@tauri-apps/api/core');
        await invoke('plugin:folio_tts|setKeepScreenOn', { enabled: true });
      } catch {
        // Ignored
      }
    }
  } else {
    try {
      if (wakeLockSentinel) {
        await wakeLockSentinel.release();
        wakeLockSentinel = null;
      }
    } catch {
      // Ignored
    }

    if (isTauri()) {
      try {
        const { invoke } = await import('@tauri-apps/api/core');
        await invoke('plugin:folio_tts|setKeepScreenOn', { enabled: false });
      } catch {
        // Ignored
      }
    }
  }
}

/**
 * Triggers subtle haptic feedback on page turn or interaction.
 */
export function triggerHaptic(type: 'light' | 'medium' | 'heavy' = 'light'): void {
  if (typeof navigator !== 'undefined' && 'vibrate' in navigator && typeof navigator.vibrate === 'function') {
    try {
      const ms = type === 'heavy' ? 40 : type === 'medium' ? 25 : 12;
      navigator.vibrate(ms);
    } catch {
      // Vibration not permitted or supported
    }
  }
}

/**
 * React hook to listen for hardware volume keys to turn pages in the reader (P11.5).
 */
export function useVolumeKeys(
  onNext: () => void,
  onPrev: () => void,
  enabled: boolean,
): void {
  const nextRef = useRef(onNext);
  const prevRef = useRef(onPrev);
  nextRef.current = onNext;
  prevRef.current = onPrev;

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!enabled) return;

    const onKeyDown = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
      // Android WebView sends VolumeUp (keyCode 24) and VolumeDown (keyCode 25)
      // or key "VolumeUp" / "VolumeDown" / "AudioVolumeUp" / "AudioVolumeDown"
      const keyCode = 'keyCode' in e ? (e as { keyCode: number }).keyCode : undefined;
      if (e.key === 'VolumeUp' || e.code === 'AudioVolumeUp' || keyCode === 24) {
        e.preventDefault();
        e.stopPropagation();
        prevRef.current();
      } else if (e.key === 'VolumeDown' || e.code === 'AudioVolumeDown' || keyCode === 25) {
        e.preventDefault();
        e.stopPropagation();
        nextRef.current();
      }
    };

    window.addEventListener('keydown', onKeyDown, { capture: true });
    return () => {
      window.removeEventListener('keydown', onKeyDown, { capture: true });
    };
  }, [enabled]);
}

export interface PullToRefreshState {
  isPulling: boolean;
  isRefreshing: boolean;
  pullDistance: number;
}

================
File: apps/client/src/lib/safeHtml.tsx
================
import React, { type ReactNode } from 'react';

const ALLOWED_TAGS = new Set([
  'p', 'b', 'i', 'strong', 'em', 'br', 'span', 'ul', 'ol', 'li',
  'blockquote', 'a', 'h1', 'h2', 'h3', 'h4', 'h5', 'h6', 'div',
]);

function domNodeToReact(node: Node, key: string): ReactNode {
  if (node.nodeType === Node.TEXT_NODE) {
    return node.textContent;
  }

  if (node.nodeType !== Node.ELEMENT_NODE) {
    return null;
  }

  const el = node as Element;
  const tagName = el.tagName.toLowerCase();

  if (!ALLOWED_TAGS.has(tagName)) {
    // Render text content of disallowed wrapper tags without dangerous markup
    return Array.from(el.childNodes).map((child, idx) =>
      domNodeToReact(child, `${key}-${idx}`),
    );
  }

  const children = Array.from(el.childNodes).map((child, idx) =>
    domNodeToReact(child, `${key}-${idx}`),
  );

  if (tagName === 'br') {
    return <br key={key} />;
  }

  if (tagName === 'a') {
    const rawHref = el.getAttribute('href') || '';
    let safeHref = '';
    try {
      const parsed = new URL(rawHref, 'http://localhost');
      if (['http:', 'https:', 'mailto:'].includes(parsed.protocol)) {
        safeHref = rawHref;
      }
    } catch {
      // Invalid URL
    }

    return (
      <a
        key={key}
        href={safeHref || undefined}
        target="_blank"
        rel="noopener noreferrer"
        className="text-[var(--accent)] underline hover:opacity-80 transition-opacity"
      >
        {children}
      </a>
    );
  }

  return React.createElement(tagName, { key }, children);
}

/**
 * Safely parse external HTML using DOMParser and convert to React elements.
 * Adheres to rule 5: Never use dangerouslySetInnerHTML.
 */
export function safeHtml(html: string | undefined | null): ReactNode {
  if (!html) return null;

  try {
    const parser = new DOMParser();
    const doc = parser.parseFromString(html, 'text/html');
    const nodes = Array.from(doc.body.childNodes);
    return nodes.map((n, idx) => domNodeToReact(n, `safe-${idx}`));
  } catch {
    return html;
  }
}

================
File: apps/client/src/lib/stats-engine.ts
================
import type { ReadingSession, Book, Series } from '../db';

export type StatPeriod = 'week' | 'month' | 'year' | 'all';

export interface DailyBarItem {
  date: string; // 'YYYY-MM-DD'
  label: string; // e.g. 'Mon', '12 Oct'
  totalMinutes: number;
  bookMinutes: number;
  mangaMinutes: number;
}

export interface HeatmapDay {
  date: string; // 'YYYY-MM-DD'
  minutes: number;
  level: 0 | 1 | 2 | 3 | 4;
  dayOfWeek: number; // 0 = Sunday, 1 = Monday, etc.
}

export interface TimeOfDaySegment {
  key: 'morning' | 'afternoon' | 'evening' | 'night';
  label: string;
  hours: string;
  durationSec: number;
  percentage: number;
}

export interface TopItem {
  id: string;
  title: string;
  authorOrMeta?: string;
  coverHash?: string | null;
  coverUrl?: string | null;
  kind: 'book' | 'manga';
  durationSec: number;
  unitsRead: number; // words for book, chapters/pages for manga
  percentageOfTotal: number;
}

export interface StatsSummary {
  period: StatPeriod;
  totalDurationSec: number;
  totalDurationFormatted: string;
  dailyAverageMinutes: number;
  longestSessionSec: number;
  longestSessionFormatted: string;
  bookSpeedWpm: number;
  mangaSpeedPpm: number;
  booksFinishedCount: number;
  chaptersReadCount: number;
  currentStreak: number;
  longestStreak: number;
  dailyBarChart: DailyBarItem[];
  heatmap: HeatmapDay[];
  timeOfDay: TimeOfDaySegment[];
  topItems: TopItem[];
}

export interface StatsEngineOptions {
  timeZone?: string;
  now?: number;
}

/**
 * Formats a duration in seconds to a human-readable string like "3h 45m" or "42m".
 */
export function formatDuration(seconds: number): string {
  if (seconds <= 0) return '0m';
  const hrs = Math.floor(seconds / 3600);
  const mins = Math.round((seconds % 3600) / 60);

  if (hrs === 0) return `${Math.max(1, mins)}m`;
  if (mins === 0) return `${hrs}h`;
  return `${hrs}h ${mins}m`;
}

const dateKeyFormatterCache = new Map<string, Intl.DateTimeFormat>();
const hourFormatterCache = new Map<string, Intl.DateTimeFormat>();

function getDateFormatter(timeZone: string): Intl.DateTimeFormat {
  let f = dateKeyFormatterCache.get(timeZone);
  if (!f) {
    f = new Intl.DateTimeFormat('en-CA', {
      timeZone,
      year: 'numeric',
      month: '2-digit',
      day: '2-digit',
    });
    dateKeyFormatterCache.set(timeZone, f);
  }
  return f;
}

function getHourFormatter(timeZone: string): Intl.DateTimeFormat {
  let f = hourFormatterCache.get(timeZone);
  if (!f) {
    f = new Intl.DateTimeFormat('en-US', {
      timeZone,
      hour: 'numeric',
      hour12: false,
    });
    hourFormatterCache.set(timeZone, f);
  }
  return f;
}

/**
 * Formats a timestamp into YYYY-MM-DD string in a specified time zone.
 */
export function getDateKey(timestampMs: number, timeZone: string = 'Asia/Kolkata'): string {
  try {
    return getDateFormatter(timeZone).format(new Date(timestampMs));
  } catch {
    const d = new Date(timestampMs);
    const yr = d.getFullYear();
    const mo = String(d.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, '0');
    const dy = String(d.getDate()).padStart(2, '0');
    return `${yr}-${mo}-${dy}`;
  }
}

/**
 * Gets the hour of the day (0..23) for a timestamp in a specified time zone.
 */
export function getHourInTimezone(timestampMs: number, timeZone: string = 'Asia/Kolkata'): number {
  try {
    const parts = getHourFormatter(timeZone).formatToParts(new Date(timestampMs));
    const hrPart = parts.find((p) => p.type === 'hour');
    return hrPart ? parseInt(hrPart.value, 10) % 24 : new Date(timestampMs).getHours();
  } catch {
    return new Date(timestampMs).getHours();
  }
}

/**
 * Splits a reading session that crosses local midnight into single-day segments.
 */
export interface SplitSessionSegment {
  dateKey: string;
  startHour: number;
  durationSec: number;
  kind: 'book' | 'manga';
  bookId: string;
  wordsRead: number;
}

export function splitSessionByMidnight(
  session: ReadingSession,
  timeZone: string = 'Asia/Kolkata',
): SplitSessionSegment[] {
  if (session.duration_sec <= 0) return [];
  const kind: 'book' | 'manga' = session.kind === 'manga' ? 'manga' : 'book';
  const startKey = getDateKey(session.started_at, timeZone);
  const endKey = getDateKey(session.ended_at, timeZone);

  if (startKey === endKey) {
    return [
      {
        dateKey: startKey,
        startHour: getHourInTimezone(session.started_at, timeZone),
        durationSec: session.duration_sec,
        kind,
        bookId: session.book_id,
        wordsRead: session.words_read || 0,
      },
    ];
  }

  // Crosses midnight: calculate proportion based on midnight boundary
  // Estimate end of start day
  const startDate = new Date(session.started_at);
  const segments: SplitSessionSegment[] = [];

  // Approximate midnight timestamp
  // Let's find milliseconds remaining until next day in target timezone
  const startHour = getHourInTimezone(session.started_at, timeZone);
  const endHour = getHourInTimezone(session.ended_at, timeZone);

  // Time in ms until midnight of start day approx
  // Fraction of session in day 1 vs day 2
  const totalElapsedMs = Math.max(1, session.ended_at - session.started_at);
  // Estimate day 1 portion
  const approxMsToMidnight = (24 - startHour) * 3600 * 1000 - startDate.getMinutes() * 60000;
  const day1Ratio = Math.max(0.05, Math.min(0.95, approxMsToMidnight / totalElapsedMs));

  const day1Sec = Math.round(session.duration_sec * day1Ratio);
  const day2Sec = Math.max(0, session.duration_sec - day1Sec);

  const day1Words = Math.round((session.words_read || 0) * day1Ratio);
  const day2Words = Math.max(0, (session.words_read || 0) - day1Words);

  if (day1Sec > 0) {
    segments.push({
      dateKey: startKey,
      startHour,
      durationSec: day1Sec,
      kind,
      bookId: session.book_id,
      wordsRead: day1Words,
    });
  }
  if (day2Sec > 0) {
    segments.push({
      dateKey: endKey,
      startHour: endHour,
      durationSec: day2Sec,
      kind,
      bookId: session.book_id,
      wordsRead: day2Words,
    });
  }

  return segments;
}

/**
 * Calculates streaks (current streak and longest streak) across all session dates.
 */
export function calculateStreaks(
  dateKeysWithReading: Set<string>,
  referenceDateKey: string,
): { currentStreak: number; longestStreak: number } {
  if (dateKeysWithReading.size === 0) {
    return { currentStreak: 0, longestStreak: 0 };
  }

  // Helper to add/sub days from YYYY-MM-DD
  const shiftDay = (dateStr: string, offsetDays: number): string => {
    const [y, m, d] = dateStr.split('-').map(Number);
    const date = new Date(Date.UTC(y, m - 1, d));
    date.setUTCDate(date.getUTCDate() + offsetDays);
    const ny = date.getUTCFullYear();
    const nm = String(date.getUTCMonth() + 1).padStart(2, '0');
    const nd = String(date.getUTCDate()).padStart(2, '0');
    return `${ny}-${nm}-${nd}`;
  };

  // Calculate current streak
  let currentStreak = 0;
  let checkKey = referenceDateKey;

  // If user hasn't read today, check if yesterday was read to keep streak alive
  if (!dateKeysWithReading.has(checkKey)) {
    checkKey = shiftDay(checkKey, -1);
  }

  while (dateKeysWithReading.has(checkKey)) {
    currentStreak++;
    checkKey = shiftDay(checkKey, -1);
  }

  // Calculate longest streak across sorted list of all active days
  const sortedDays = Array.from(dateKeysWithReading).sort();
  let longestStreak = 0;
  let currentStreakCount = 0;
  let prevDay: string | null = null;

  for (const day of sortedDays) {
    if (!prevDay) {
      currentStreakCount = 1;
    } else {
      const expectedNext = shiftDay(prevDay, 1);
      if (day === expectedNext) {
        currentStreakCount++;
      } else {
        currentStreakCount = 1;
      }
    }
    prevDay = day;
    if (currentStreakCount > longestStreak) {
      longestStreak = currentStreakCount;
    }
  }

  return { currentStreak, longestStreak };
}

/**
 * Main statistics computation engine.
 */
export function computeReadingStats(
  sessions: ReadingSession[],
  period: StatPeriod = 'week',
  options?: StatsEngineOptions & {
    books?: Book[];
    series?: Series[];
    finishedBookIds?: Set<string>;
  },
): StatsSummary {
  const timeZone = options?.timeZone || Intl.DateTimeFormat().resolvedOptions().timeZone || 'Asia/Kolkata';
  const nowMs = options?.now || Date.now();
  const todayKey = getDateKey(nowMs, timeZone);

  const booksMap = new Map<string, Book>();
  if (options?.books) {
    for (const b of options.books) booksMap.set(b.id, b);
  }

  const seriesMap = new Map<string, Series>();
  if (options?.series) {
    for (const s of options.series) seriesMap.set(s.id, s);
  }

  // 1. Filter out deleted sessions and find bounds
  const validSessions = sessions.filter((s) => !s.deleted_at && s.duration_sec >= 20);

  // 2. Determine date filter range based on period
  const DAY_MS = 864e5;
  let minTimestamp: number;
  let numPeriodDays: number;

  if (period === 'week') {
    numPeriodDays = 7;
    minTimestamp = nowMs - 7 * DAY_MS;
  } else if (period === 'month') {
    numPeriodDays = 30;
    minTimestamp = nowMs - 30 * DAY_MS;
  } else if (period === 'year') {
    numPeriodDays = 365;
    minTimestamp = nowMs - 365 * DAY_MS;
  } else {
    // 'all'
    minTimestamp = 0;
    numPeriodDays = 365;
  }

  // 3. Process sessions: all-time for streak & heatmap, filtered for period summary
  const allTimeActiveDays = new Set<string>();
  const allTimeHeatmapMap = new Map<string, number>(); // YYYY-MM-DD -> total minutes

  let totalDurationSec = 0;
  let longestSessionSec = 0;
  let totalBookSec = 0;
  let totalBookWords = 0;
  let totalMangaSec = 0;
  let totalMangaPages = 0;

  const itemDurationMap = new Map<string, { durationSec: number; units: number; kind: 'book' | 'manga' }>();
  const periodDailyMap = new Map<string, { bookSec: number; mangaSec: number }>();
  const timeOfDayBuckets: Record<'morning' | 'afternoon' | 'evening' | 'night', number> = {
    morning: 0,
    afternoon: 0,
    evening: 0,
    night: 0,
  };

  for (const session of validSessions) {
    // Track active day for streak
    const sessionDateKey = getDateKey(session.started_at, timeZone);
    allTimeActiveDays.add(sessionDateKey);

    // Heatmap tracking (last 365 days)
    const sessionMinutes = session.duration_sec / 60;
    allTimeHeatmapMap.set(sessionDateKey, (allTimeHeatmapMap.get(sessionDateKey) ?? 0) + sessionMinutes);

    // Filter for current period
    if (session.started_at >= minTimestamp) {
      totalDurationSec += session.duration_sec;
      if (session.duration_sec > longestSessionSec) {
        longestSessionSec = session.duration_sec;
      }

      // Midnight splitting for accurate daily breakdown & time-of-day
      const splitSegments = splitSessionByMidnight(session, timeZone);
      for (const seg of splitSegments) {
        // Daily chart data
        const currentDaily = periodDailyMap.get(seg.dateKey) ?? { bookSec: 0, mangaSec: 0 };
        if (seg.kind === 'manga') {
          currentDaily.mangaSec += seg.durationSec;
        } else {
          currentDaily.bookSec += seg.durationSec;
        }
        periodDailyMap.set(seg.dateKey, currentDaily);

        // Time of day bucket
        const hr = seg.startHour;
        if (hr >= 6 && hr < 12) {
          timeOfDayBuckets.morning += seg.durationSec;
        } else if (hr >= 12 && hr < 17) {
          timeOfDayBuckets.afternoon += seg.durationSec;
        } else if (hr >= 17 && hr < 22) {
          timeOfDayBuckets.evening += seg.durationSec;
        } else {
          timeOfDayBuckets.night += seg.durationSec;
        }
      }

      // Aggregate reading speeds
      if (session.kind === 'manga') {
        totalMangaSec += session.duration_sec;
        totalMangaPages += session.words_read || 0;
      } else {
        totalBookSec += session.duration_sec;
        totalBookWords += session.words_read || 0;
      }

      // Top items
      const sessionKind: 'book' | 'manga' = session.kind === 'manga' ? 'manga' : 'book';
      const existingItem = itemDurationMap.get(session.book_id) ?? {
        durationSec: 0,
        units: 0,
        kind: sessionKind,
      };
      existingItem.durationSec += session.duration_sec;
      existingItem.units += session.words_read || 0;
      existingItem.kind = sessionKind;
      itemDurationMap.set(session.book_id, existingItem);
    }
  }

  // 4. Calculate streaks
  const { currentStreak, longestStreak } = calculateStreaks(allTimeActiveDays, todayKey);

  // 5. Build Daily Bar Chart items
  const dailyBarChart: DailyBarItem[] = [];
  const shiftDateKey = (baseDateKey: string, offset: number): string => {
    const [y, m, d] = baseDateKey.split('-').map(Number);
    const date = new Date(Date.UTC(y, m - 1, d));
    date.setUTCDate(date.getUTCDate() + offset);
    const ny = date.getUTCFullYear();
    const nm = String(date.getUTCMonth() + 1).padStart(2, '0');
    const nd = String(date.getUTCDate()).padStart(2, '0');
    return `${ny}-${nm}-${nd}`;
  };

  const dayLabelFormatter = (dKey: string): string => {
    const [y, m, d] = dKey.split('-').map(Number);
    const dt = new Date(Date.UTC(y, m - 1, d));
    if (period === 'week') {
      return dt.toLocaleDateString('en-US', { weekday: 'short', timeZone: 'UTC' });
    }
    return `${dt.getUTCDate()} ${dt.toLocaleDateString('en-US', { month: 'short', timeZone: 'UTC' })}`;
  };

  const chartDaysCount = period === 'week' ? 7 : period === 'month' ? 30 : 14;
  for (let i = chartDaysCount - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
    const dKey = shiftDateKey(todayKey, -i);
    const entry = periodDailyMap.get(dKey) ?? { bookSec: 0, mangaSec: 0 };
    const bMin = Math.round(entry.bookSec / 60);
    const mMin = Math.round(entry.mangaSec / 60);
    dailyBarChart.push({
      date: dKey,
      label: dayLabelFormatter(dKey),
      totalMinutes: bMin + mMin,
      bookMinutes: bMin,
      mangaMinutes: mMin,
    });
  }

  // 6. Build Calendar Heatmap (past 364 days, 52 weeks of 7 days)
  const heatmap: HeatmapDay[] = [];
  const totalHeatmapDays = 52 * 7;
  for (let i = totalHeatmapDays - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
    const dKey = shiftDateKey(todayKey, -i);
    const [y, m, d] = dKey.split('-').map(Number);
    const dt = new Date(Date.UTC(y, m - 1, d));
    const dayOfWeek = dt.getUTCDay(); // 0 = Sun, 1 = Mon ...
    const minutes = Math.round(allTimeHeatmapMap.get(dKey) ?? 0);

    let level: 0 | 1 | 2 | 3 | 4 = 0;
    if (minutes > 60) level = 4;
    else if (minutes >= 30) level = 3;
    else if (minutes >= 15) level = 2;
    else if (minutes > 0) level = 1;

    heatmap.push({
      date: dKey,
      minutes,
      level,
      dayOfWeek,
    });
  }

  // 7. Time of day distribution
  const totalTodSec = Math.max(
    1,
    timeOfDayBuckets.morning + timeOfDayBuckets.afternoon + timeOfDayBuckets.evening + timeOfDayBuckets.night,
  );
  const timeOfDay: TimeOfDaySegment[] = [
    {
      key: 'morning',
      label: 'Morning',
      hours: '06:00 – 12:00',
      durationSec: timeOfDayBuckets.morning,
      percentage: Math.round((timeOfDayBuckets.morning / totalTodSec) * 100),
    },
    {
      key: 'afternoon',
      label: 'Afternoon',
      hours: '12:00 – 17:00',
      durationSec: timeOfDayBuckets.afternoon,
      percentage: Math.round((timeOfDayBuckets.afternoon / totalTodSec) * 100),
    },
    {
      key: 'evening',
      label: 'Evening',
      hours: '17:00 – 22:00',
      durationSec: timeOfDayBuckets.evening,
      percentage: Math.round((timeOfDayBuckets.evening / totalTodSec) * 100),
    },
    {
      key: 'night',
      label: 'Night',
      hours: '22:00 – 06:00',
      durationSec: timeOfDayBuckets.night,
      percentage: Math.round((timeOfDayBuckets.night / totalTodSec) * 100),
    },
  ];

  // 8. Reading speeds
  const bookSpeedWpm = totalBookSec > 0 ? Math.round((totalBookWords / totalBookSec) * 60) : 0;
  const mangaSpeedPpm = totalMangaSec > 0 ? Number(((totalMangaPages / totalMangaSec) * 60).toFixed(1)) : 0;

  // 9. Top Items
  const topItems: TopItem[] = Array.from(itemDurationMap.entries())
    .map(([id, info]) => {
      const book = booksMap.get(id);
      const series = seriesMap.get(id);
      const title = book?.title || series?.title || id;
      const authorOrMeta = book?.author || series?.author || '';
      const coverHash = book?.cover_hash || null;
      const coverUrl = series?.cover_url || null;
      const percentageOfTotal =
        totalDurationSec > 0 ? Math.round((info.durationSec / totalDurationSec) * 100) : 0;

      return {
        id,
        title,
        authorOrMeta,
        coverHash,
        coverUrl,
        kind: info.kind,
        durationSec: info.durationSec,
        unitsRead: info.units,
        percentageOfTotal,
      };
    })
    .sort((a, b) => b.durationSec - a.durationSec)
    .slice(0, 10);

  // 10. Summary numbers
  const dailyAverageMinutes = Math.round(totalDurationSec / Math.max(1, numPeriodDays) / 60);

  // Books finished
  let booksFinishedCount = 0;
  if (options?.finishedBookIds) {
    booksFinishedCount = options.finishedBookIds.size;
  } else if (options?.books) {
    booksFinishedCount = options.books.filter((b) => !!b.meta?.finished_at && !b.deleted_at).length;
  }

  // Chapters read
  let chaptersReadCount = 0;
  for (const session of validSessions) {
    if (session.kind === 'manga' && session.started_at >= minTimestamp) {
      chaptersReadCount += session.words_read > 0 ? 1 : 0;
    }
  }

  return {
    period,
    totalDurationSec,
    totalDurationFormatted: formatDuration(totalDurationSec),
    dailyAverageMinutes,
    longestSessionSec,
    longestSessionFormatted: formatDuration(longestSessionSec),
    bookSpeedWpm,
    mangaSpeedPpm,
    booksFinishedCount,
    chaptersReadCount,
    currentStreak,
    longestStreak,
    dailyBarChart,
    heatmap,
    timeOfDay,
    topItems,
  };
}

/**
 * Generates RFC 4180 compliant CSV string for export.
 */
export function exportSessionsToCsv(
  sessions: ReadingSession[],
  booksMap: Map<string, Book>,
  seriesMap: Map<string, Series>,
  timeZone: string = 'Asia/Kolkata',
): string {
  const headers = [
    'Session ID',
    'Item ID',
    'Title',
    'Kind',
    'Started At',
    'Ended At',
    'Duration (min)',
    'Duration (sec)',
    'Start %',
    'End %',
    'Words/Pages Read',
    'Device ID',
  ];

  const escapeField = (val: string | number | undefined | null): string => {
    if (val == null) return '';
    const str = String(val);
    if (str.includes(',') || str.includes('"') || str.includes('\n')) {
      return `"${str.replace(/"/g, '""')}"`;
    }
    return str;
  };

  const rows: string[] = [headers.join(',')];

  const formatDate = (ts: number): string => {
    try {
      return new Intl.DateTimeFormat('en-GB', {
        timeZone,
        year: 'numeric',
        month: '2-digit',
        day: '2-digit',
        hour: '2-digit',
        minute: '2-digit',
        second: '2-digit',
        hour12: false,
      }).format(new Date(ts));
    } catch {
      return new Date(ts).toISOString();
    }
  };

  for (const s of sessions) {
    if (s.deleted_at) continue;
    const title =
      s.kind === 'manga'
        ? seriesMap.get(s.book_id)?.title || s.book_id
        : booksMap.get(s.book_id)?.title || s.book_id;

    const row = [
      escapeField(s.id),
      escapeField(s.book_id),
      escapeField(title),
      escapeField(s.kind || 'book'),
      escapeField(formatDate(s.started_at)),
      escapeField(formatDate(s.ended_at)),
      escapeField((s.duration_sec / 60).toFixed(1)),
      escapeField(s.duration_sec),
      escapeField(Math.round(s.start_percent * 100)),
      escapeField(Math.round(s.end_percent * 100)),
      escapeField(s.words_read),
      escapeField(s.device_id),
    ];
    rows.push(row.join(','));
  }

  return rows.join('\r\n');
}

/**
 * Triggers a file download in the browser.
 */
export function downloadCsvFile(content: string, filename: string = 'folio-stats.csv') {
  const blob = new Blob([content], { type: 'text/csv;charset=utf-8;' });
  const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
  const link = document.createElement('a');
  link.setAttribute('href', url);
  link.setAttribute('download', filename);
  document.body.appendChild(link);
  link.click();
  document.body.removeChild(link);
  URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
}

File: apps/client/src/lib/tauriIntegration.ts

import { isTauri, listenFileOpen, listenDeepLink } from './tauri';
import { importOne } from './importBook';
import { db } from '../db';

export const SUPPORTED_DESKTOP_EXTENSIONS = [
  '.epub',
  '.mobi',
  '.azw3',
  '.azw',
  '.fb2',
  '.fbz',
  '.cbz',
  '.cbr',
  '.cb7',
  '.pdf',
];

export function isSupportedBookFile(filePath: string): boolean {
  const lower = filePath.toLowerCase();
  return SUPPORTED_DESKTOP_EXTENSIONS.some((ext) => lower.endsWith(ext));
}

/**
 * Handles an incoming file path from single instance CLI arguments or file association double-clicks.
 */
export async function handleOpenedFile(
  filePath: string,
  onOpenBook?: (bookId: string) => void,
): Promise<string | null> {
  if (!isSupportedBookFile(filePath)) return null;

  try {
    let file: File;
    if (isTauri()) {
      const { readFile } = await import('@tauri-apps/plugin-fs');
      const bytes = await readFile(filePath);
      const fileName = filePath.replace(/\\/g, '/').split('/').pop() || 'book.epub';
      file = new File([bytes], fileName);
    } else {
      return null;
    }

    // Import the book into database
    await importOne(file);
    const { sha256 } = await import('./hash');
    const bookId = await sha256(await file.arrayBuffer());

    // Call callback or navigate
    if (onOpenBook && bookId) {
      onOpenBook(bookId);
    }

    return bookId;
  } catch (err) {
    console.error('Failed to open file association:', filePath, err);
    return null;
  }
}

/**
 * Parses deep link URL and routes appropriately.
 */
export async function handleDeepLinkUrl(
  urlStr: string,
  onNavigate?: (path: string) => void,
): Promise<void> {
  try {
    const parsed = new URL(urlStr);
    if (parsed.protocol !== 'folio:') return;

    // In WHATWG URL parsing for folio://reader/123:
    // parsed.host is 'reader', parsed.pathname is '/123'
    // For folio:///reader/123:
    // parsed.host is '', parsed.pathname is '/reader/123'
    const fullPath = (parsed.host ? `/${parsed.host}` : '') + parsed.pathname;

    if (fullPath.startsWith('/reader/')) {
      const bookId = fullPath.replace(/^\/reader\//, '');
      if (bookId) {
        onNavigate?.(`/reader/${bookId}`);
        return;
      }
    }

    if (fullPath.startsWith('/book/')) {
      const bookId = fullPath.replace(/^\/book\//, '');
      if (bookId) {
        onNavigate?.(`/book/${bookId}`);
        return;
      }
    }

    const idParam = parsed.searchParams.get('id');
    if (idParam) {
      onNavigate?.(`/reader/${idParam}`);
      return;
    }

    const hashParam = parsed.searchParams.get('hash');
    if (hashParam) {
      const book = await db.books.where('file_hash').equals(hashParam).first();
      if (book) {
        onNavigate?.(`/reader/${book.id}`);
      }
    }
  } catch (err) {
    console.warn('Failed to parse deep link URL:', urlStr, err);
  }
}

/**
 * Initializes Tauri desktop listeners for file associations and deep links.
 */
export function initTauriDesktopIntegration(
  onNavigate: (path: string) => void,
): () => void {
  if (!isTauri()) return () => {};

  let cleanupFileOpen = () => {};
  let cleanupDeepLink = () => {};

  void listenFileOpen((filePath) => {
    void handleOpenedFile(filePath, (bookId) => {
      onNavigate(`/reader/${bookId}`);
    });
  }).then((cleanup) => {
    cleanupFileOpen = cleanup;
  });

  void listenDeepLink((url) => {
    void handleDeepLinkUrl(url, onNavigate);
  }).then((cleanup) => {
    cleanupDeepLink = cleanup;
  });

  return () => {
    cleanupFileOpen();
    cleanupDeepLink();
  };
}

================
File: apps/client/src/lib/ttsEngine.ts
================
import { isTauri } from './tauri';

export interface TtsVoice {
  voiceURI: string;
  name: string;
  lang: string;
  default?: boolean;
}

export interface TtsSpeakOptions {
  rate?: number;
  pitch?: number;
  voiceURI?: string;
  lang?: string;
  onRangeStart?: (charIndex: number, charLength: number) => void;
  onDone?: () => void;
  onError?: (err: unknown) => void;
}

export interface TtsEngine {
  isSupported(): boolean;
  speak(text: string, utteranceId: string, options?: TtsSpeakOptions): Promise<void>;
  stop(): Promise<void>;
  pause(): Promise<void>;
  resume(): Promise<void>;
  getVoices(): Promise<TtsVoice[]>;
  setMetadata?(title: string, chapter: string): Promise<void>;
}

/**
 * Web Speech API implementation for modern browsers and WebView fallback.
 */
export class WebSpeechEngine implements TtsEngine {
  isSupported(): boolean {
    return typeof window !== 'undefined' && 'speechSynthesis' in window;
  }

  async speak(text: string, _utteranceId: string, options?: TtsSpeakOptions): Promise<void> {
    if (!this.isSupported()) {
      options?.onError?.(new Error('SpeechSynthesis not supported'));
      return;
    }

    window.speechSynthesis.cancel();
    const u = new SpeechSynthesisUtterance(text);

    if (options?.rate) u.rate = options.rate;
    if (options?.pitch) u.pitch = options.pitch;

    if (options?.voiceURI) {
      const matched = window.speechSynthesis.getVoices().find((v) => v.voiceURI === options.voiceURI);
      if (matched) u.voice = matched;
    }
    if (options?.lang && !u.voice) {
      u.lang = options.lang;
    }

    u.onboundary = (e) => {
      // charIndex from SpeechSynthesisEvent
      const idx = e.charIndex ?? 0;
      const len = e.charLength ?? 0;
      options?.onRangeStart?.(idx, len);
    };

    u.onend = () => {
      options?.onDone?.();
    };

    u.onerror = (e) => {
      if (e.error === 'interrupted' || e.error === 'canceled') return;
      options?.onError?.(e);
    };

    window.speechSynthesis.speak(u);
  }

  async stop(): Promise<void> {
    if (this.isSupported()) {
      window.speechSynthesis.cancel();
    }
  }

  async pause(): Promise<void> {
    if (this.isSupported()) {
      window.speechSynthesis.pause();
    }
  }

  async resume(): Promise<void> {
    if (this.isSupported()) {
      window.speechSynthesis.resume();
    }
  }

  async getVoices(): Promise<TtsVoice[]> {
    if (!this.isSupported()) return [];
    return window.speechSynthesis.getVoices().map((v) => ({
      voiceURI: v.voiceURI,
      name: v.name,
      lang: v.lang,
      default: v.default,
    }));
  }
}

/**
 * Native Android TTS engine integrating with Kotlin TextToSpeech & Foreground Media Service.
 */
export class NativeAndroidTtsEngine implements TtsEngine {
  private activeUtteranceId: string | null = null;
  private currentOptions: TtsSpeakOptions | null = null;
  private unlistenRange: (() => void) | null = null;
  private unlistenDone: (() => void) | null = null;

  isSupported(): boolean {
    // True if in Tauri environment
    return isTauri();
  }

  private async setupListeners() {
    if (this.unlistenRange && this.unlistenDone) return;
    try {
      const { listen } = await import('@tauri-apps/api/event');
      const unRange = await listen<{ utteranceId: string; start: number; end: number }>(
        'folio://tts-range-start',
        (event) => {
          if (event.payload && event.payload.utteranceId === this.activeUtteranceId) {
            const start = event.payload.start;
            const len = Math.max(0, event.payload.end - start);
            this.currentOptions?.onRangeStart?.(start, len);
          }
        },
      );
      const unDone = await listen<{ utteranceId: string }>('folio://tts-done', (event) => {
        if (event.payload && event.payload.utteranceId === this.activeUtteranceId) {
          this.activeUtteranceId = null;
          this.currentOptions?.onDone?.();
        }
      });
      this.unlistenRange = unRange;
      this.unlistenDone = unDone;
    } catch {
      // Ignored outside Tauri
    }
  }

  async speak(text: string, utteranceId: string, options?: TtsSpeakOptions): Promise<void> {
    this.activeUtteranceId = utteranceId;
    this.currentOptions = options ?? null;
    await this.setupListeners();

    try {
      const { invoke } = await import('@tauri-apps/api/core');
      await invoke('plugin:folio_tts|speak', {
        text,
        utteranceId,
        rate: options?.rate ?? 1.0,
        pitch: options?.pitch ?? 1.0,
        voice: options?.voiceURI,
      });
    } catch (err) {
      console.warn('Native Android TTS invocation failed, falling back to WebSpeech:', err);
      const fallback = new WebSpeechEngine();
      return fallback.speak(text, utteranceId, options);
    }
  }

  async stop(): Promise<void> {
    this.activeUtteranceId = null;
    this.currentOptions = null;
    try {
      const { invoke } = await import('@tauri-apps/api/core');
      await invoke('plugin:folio_tts|stop');
    } catch {
      window.speechSynthesis?.cancel();
    }
  }

  async pause(): Promise<void> {
    try {
      const { invoke } = await import('@tauri-apps/api/core');
      await invoke('plugin:folio_tts|pause');
    } catch {
      window.speechSynthesis?.pause();
    }
  }

  async resume(): Promise<void> {
    try {
      const { invoke } = await import('@tauri-apps/api/core');
      await invoke('plugin:folio_tts|resume');
    } catch {
      window.speechSynthesis?.resume();
    }
  }

  async getVoices(): Promise<TtsVoice[]> {
    try {
      const { invoke } = await import('@tauri-apps/api/core');
      const res = await invoke<{ voices: { name: string; locale: string }[] }>('plugin:folio_tts|listVoices');
      if (res?.voices && Array.isArray(res.voices)) {
        return res.voices.map((v) => ({
          voiceURI: v.name,
          name: v.name,
          lang: v.locale,
        }));
      }
    } catch {
      // Fall back to web voices
    }
    const fallback = new WebSpeechEngine();
    return fallback.getVoices();
  }

  async setMetadata(title: string, chapter: string): Promise<void> {
    try {
      const { invoke } = await import('@tauri-apps/api/core');
      await invoke('plugin:folio_tts|setMetadata', { title, chapter });
    } catch {
      // No-op
    }
  }
}

let activeEngine: TtsEngine | null = null;

export function getTtsEngine(): TtsEngine {
  if (!activeEngine) {
    if (isTauri()) {
      activeEngine = new NativeAndroidTtsEngine();
    } else {
      activeEngine = new WebSpeechEngine();
    }
  }
  return activeEngine;
}

================
File: apps/client/src/lib/useAppFullscreen.ts

import { useSyncExternalStore } from 'react'
import { isTauri, onWindowResized, toggleTauriFullscreen } from './tauri'

// One shared fullscreen state for the title bar and both readers. In the desktop
// app the *window* must go fullscreen: the DOM Fullscreen API only fills the
// WebView, which already fills the window, so it visibly did nothing.
let fullscreen = false
let started = false
const subscribers = new Set<() => void>()

async function refresh(): Promise<void> {
  let next = typeof document !== 'undefined' && !!document.fullscreenElement
  if (isTauri()) {
    try {
      const { getCurrentWindow } = await import('@tauri-apps/api/window')
      next = await getCurrentWindow().isFullscreen()
    } catch {
      // Keep the DOM answer.
    }
  }
  if (next !== fullscreen) {
    fullscreen = next
    subscribers.forEach((s) => s())
  }
}

function start(): void {
  if (started || typeof document === 'undefined') return
  started = true
  document.addEventListener('fullscreenchange', () => void refresh())
  void onWindowResized(() => void refresh())
  void refresh()
}

export function isAppFullscreen(): boolean {
  return fullscreen
}

export async function toggleAppFullscreen(): Promise<void> {
  await toggleTauriFullscreen()
  // The resize event usually reports the change; this covers platforms that don't.
  setTimeout(() => void refresh(), 200)
}

export async function exitAppFullscreen(): Promise<void> {
  if (fullscreen) await toggleAppFullscreen()
}

export function useAppFullscreen(): boolean {
  start()
  return useSyncExternalStore(
    (cb) => {
      subscribers.add(cb)
      return () => subscribers.delete(cb)
    },
    () => fullscreen,
    () => false,
  )
}
