import { useEffect, useRef } from 'react'

export interface ShortcutItem {
  id: string
  keys: string[]
  label: string
  scope: 'global' | 'reader'
}

export const SHORTCUTS_REGISTRY: ShortcutItem[] = [
  // Global
  { id: 'search', keys: ['Ctrl', 'K'], label: 'Focus search', scope: 'global' },
  { id: 'search-slash', keys: ['/'], label: 'Focus search', scope: 'global' },
  { id: 'import', keys: ['Ctrl', 'O'], label: 'Add books', scope: 'global' },
  { id: 'settings', keys: ['Ctrl', ','], label: 'Settings', scope: 'global' },
  { id: 'go-home', keys: ['G', 'then', 'H'], label: 'Go to Home', scope: 'global' },
  { id: 'go-library', keys: ['G', 'then', 'L'], label: 'Go to Library', scope: 'global' },
  { id: 'go-notebook', keys: ['G', 'then', 'N'], label: 'Go to Notebook', scope: 'global' },
  { id: 'go-store', keys: ['G', 'then', 'S'], label: 'Go to Store', scope: 'global' },
  { id: 'help', keys: ['?'], label: 'Keyboard shortcuts help', scope: 'global' },
  { id: 'esc', keys: ['Esc'], label: 'Close topmost layer', scope: 'global' },

  // Reader
  { id: 'nav-pages', keys: ['←', '→', 'PgUp', 'PgDn', 'Space'], label: 'Page navigation', scope: 'reader' },
  { id: 'toc', keys: ['T'], label: 'Table of contents', scope: 'reader' },
  { id: 'bookmark', keys: ['B'], label: 'Toggle bookmark', scope: 'reader' },
  { id: 'highlights', keys: ['H'], label: 'Highlights & notes panel', scope: 'reader' },
  { id: 'fullscreen', keys: ['F'], label: 'Toggle fullscreen', scope: 'reader' },
  { id: 'search-book', keys: ['Ctrl', 'F'], label: 'Search in book', scope: 'reader' },
  { id: 'read-aloud', keys: ['R'], label: 'Read aloud play / pause', scope: 'reader' },
  { id: 'font-increase', keys: ['Ctrl', '+'], label: 'Increase font size', scope: 'reader' },
  { id: 'font-decrease', keys: ['Ctrl', '−'], label: 'Decrease font size', scope: 'reader' },
]

export function isInputTarget(target: EventTarget | null): boolean {
  if (!target || !(target instanceof HTMLElement)) return false
  return !!target.closest('input, textarea, select, [contenteditable="true"]')
}

export interface HotkeysHandlers {
  onSearch?: () => void
  onImport?: () => void
  onSettings?: () => void
  onNavigate?: (path: string) => void
  onHelp?: () => void
  onEscape?: () => void
}

export class HotkeysController {
  private gPending = false
  private gTimer: number | null = null

  private getHandlers: () => HotkeysHandlers

  constructor(getHandlers: () => HotkeysHandlers) {
    this.getHandlers = getHandlers
  }

  handleKeyDown = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
    const handlers = this.getHandlers()
    const isInput = isInputTarget(e.target)

    // Escape always handles closing topmost layer even if in an input
    if (e.key === 'Escape') {
      if (handlers.onEscape) {
        handlers.onEscape()
        e.preventDefault()
      }
      return
    }

    // All other hotkeys are suppressed when typing in form inputs/textareas
    if (isInput) return

    const isCtrlOrCmd = e.ctrlKey || e.metaKey

    // Ctrl+K or /: Focus search
    if ((isCtrlOrCmd && e.key.toLowerCase() === 'k') || (!isCtrlOrCmd && !e.shiftKey && e.key === '/')) {
      e.preventDefault()
      handlers.onSearch?.()
      return
    }

    // Ctrl+O: Add books
    if (isCtrlOrCmd && e.key.toLowerCase() === 'o') {
      e.preventDefault()
      handlers.onImport?.()
      return
    }

    // Ctrl+,: Settings
    if (isCtrlOrCmd && e.key === ',') {
      e.preventDefault()
      handlers.onSettings?.()
      return
    }

    // ?: Keyboard shortcuts help
    if (e.key === '?' || (e.shiftKey && e.key === '/')) {
      e.preventDefault()
      handlers.onHelp?.()
      return
    }

    // Two-key G then H/L/N/S navigation sequence
    if (!isCtrlOrCmd && !e.altKey) {
      if (this.gPending) {
        this.clearGSequence()
        const key = e.key.toLowerCase()
        if (key === 'h') {
          e.preventDefault()
          handlers.onNavigate?.('/')
          return
        }
        if (key === 'l') {
          e.preventDefault()
          handlers.onNavigate?.('/library')
          return
        }
        if (key === 'n') {
          e.preventDefault()
          handlers.onNavigate?.('/notebook')
          return
        }
        if (key === 's') {
          e.preventDefault()
          handlers.onNavigate?.('/store')
          return
        }
      } else if (e.key.toLowerCase() === 'g') {
        this.gPending = true
        if (typeof window !== 'undefined') {
          if (this.gTimer) window.clearTimeout(this.gTimer)
          this.gTimer = window.setTimeout(() => {
            this.gPending = false
          }, 1000)
        }
        return
      }
    }
  }

  clearGSequence() {
    this.gPending = false
    if (this.gTimer && typeof window !== 'undefined') {
      window.clearTimeout(this.gTimer)
      this.gTimer = null
    }
  }
}

export function useGlobalHotkeys(handlers: HotkeysHandlers) {
  const handlersRef = useRef(handlers)
  useEffect(() => {
    handlersRef.current = handlers
  })

  useEffect(() => {
    const controller = new HotkeysController(() => handlersRef.current)
    const onKey = (e: KeyboardEvent) => controller.handleKeyDown(e)
    window.addEventListener('keydown', onKey)
    return () => {
      window.removeEventListener('keydown', onKey)
      controller.clearGSequence()
    }
  }, [])
}

================
File: apps/client/src/lib/useReaderTouch.ts
================
import { useEffect, useRef } from 'react'
import type { FoliateView } from './foliate'

export interface UseReaderTouchOptions {
  view: FoliateView | null
  flow: 'paginated' | 'scrolled'
  onImageZoom?: (src: string, alt?: string) => void
}

export function getTouchDistance(t1: Touch, t2: Touch): number {
  const dx = t1.clientX - t2.clientX
  const dy = t1.clientY - t2.clientY
  return Math.hypot(dx, dy)
}

export class ReaderTouchController {
  private startX = 0
  private startY = 0
  private startTime = 0
  private initialPinchDist = 0
  private pinchTargetImg: HTMLImageElement | null = null
  private isSwiping = false

  private getOptions: () => {
    view: FoliateView | null
    flow: 'paginated' | 'scrolled'
    onImageZoom?: (src: string, alt?: string) => void
  }

  constructor(
    getOptions: () => {
      view: FoliateView | null
      flow: 'paginated' | 'scrolled'
      onImageZoom?: (src: string, alt?: string) => void
    },
  ) {
    this.getOptions = getOptions
  }

  handleTouchStart = (e: TouchEvent) => {
    const { onImageZoom } = this.getOptions()
    if (e.touches.length === 1) {
      const touch = e.touches[0]
      this.startX = touch.clientX
      this.startY = touch.clientY
      this.startTime = Date.now()
      this.isSwiping = false
      this.initialPinchDist = 0
      this.pinchTargetImg = null
    } else if (e.touches.length === 2 && onImageZoom) {
      const target = (e.target as HTMLElement | null)?.closest?.('img') ?? null
      if (target instanceof HTMLImageElement && target.src) {
        this.pinchTargetImg = target
        this.initialPinchDist = getTouchDistance(e.touches[0], e.touches[1])
      }
    }
  }

  handleTouchMove = (e: TouchEvent) => {
    const { onImageZoom } = this.getOptions()
    if (e.touches.length === 2 && this.pinchTargetImg && this.initialPinchDist > 0 && onImageZoom) {
      const currentDist = getTouchDistance(e.touches[0], e.touches[1])
      if (currentDist / this.initialPinchDist > 1.25) {
        if (e.cancelable) e.preventDefault()
        const img = this.pinchTargetImg
        this.initialPinchDist = 0
        this.pinchTargetImg = null
        onImageZoom(img.src, img.alt || '')
      }
      return
    }

    if (e.touches.length === 1) {
      const touch = e.touches[0]
      const dx = touch.clientX - this.startX
      const dy = touch.clientY - this.startY
      if (Math.abs(dx) > 10 && Math.abs(dx) > Math.abs(dy)) {
        this.isSwiping = true
      }
    }
  }

  handleTouchEnd = (e: TouchEvent) => {
    const { view, flow } = this.getOptions()
    if (flow !== 'paginated' || !view) {
      this.isSwiping = false
      return
    }

    const elapsed = Date.now() - this.startTime
    const touch = e.changedTouches[0]
    if (!touch) return

    const dx = touch.clientX - this.startX
    const dy = touch.clientY - this.startY

    // Per §6 P2.5: Horizontal swipe of at least 60px in <= 400ms turns pages
    if (this.isSwiping && elapsed <= 400 && Math.abs(dy) < 50 && Math.abs(dx) >= 60) {
      if (dx < -60) {
        view.goRight()
      } else if (dx > 60) {
        view.goLeft()
      }
    }

    this.isSwiping = false
    this.initialPinchDist = 0
    this.pinchTargetImg = null
  }
}

export function useReaderTouch({ view, flow, onImageZoom }: UseReaderTouchOptions) {
  const optionsRef = useRef({ view, flow, onImageZoom })
  useEffect(() => {
    optionsRef.current = { view, flow, onImageZoom }
  })

  const controllerRef = useRef<ReaderTouchController | null>(null)
  if (!controllerRef.current) {
    controllerRef.current = new ReaderTouchController(() => optionsRef.current)
  }

  const attachedDocsRef = useRef(new Set<Document>())

  // Attach touch listeners to document
  const attachDocument = (doc: Document) => {
    const controller = controllerRef.current
    if (!controller || attachedDocsRef.current.has(doc)) return
    attachedDocsRef.current.add(doc)

    doc.addEventListener('touchstart', controller.handleTouchStart, { passive: true })
    doc.addEventListener('touchmove', controller.handleTouchMove, { passive: false })
    doc.addEventListener('touchend', controller.handleTouchEnd, { passive: true })

    // Also support double-click / tap on images to open zoom modal
    doc.addEventListener('dblclick', (e) => {
      const target = (e.target as HTMLElement | null)?.closest?.('img')
      if (target instanceof HTMLImageElement && target.src && optionsRef.current.onImageZoom) {
        optionsRef.current.onImageZoom(target.src, target.alt || '')
      }
    })
  }

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!view) return
    const controller = controllerRef.current
    if (!controller) return

    const docs = attachedDocsRef.current
    const onLoad = (e: Event) => {
      const detail = (e as CustomEvent).detail as { doc?: Document } | undefined
      if (detail?.doc) {
        attachDocument(detail.doc)
      }
    }

    view.addEventListener('load', onLoad)

    return () => {
      view.removeEventListener('load', onLoad)
      for (const doc of docs) {
        doc.removeEventListener('touchstart', controller.handleTouchStart)
        doc.removeEventListener('touchmove', controller.handleTouchMove)
        doc.removeEventListener('touchend', controller.handleTouchEnd)
      }
      docs.clear()
    }
  }, [view])

  return {
    attachDocument,
  }
}

File: apps/client/src/lib/useSwipeTabs.ts
================
import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react'

export interface UseSwipeTabsOptions<T extends string = string> {
  tabs: T[]
  value: T
  onChange: (value: T) => void
  containerRef?: React.RefObject<HTMLElement | null>
  disabled?: boolean
}

export class SwipeTabsController<T extends string = string> {
  private wheelAccumulator = 0
  private lastWheelTime = 0
  private wheelCooldownUntil = 0
  private wheelGestureConsumed = false

  // Touch state
  private startX = 0
  private startY = 0
  private startTime = 0
  private isSwiping: boolean | null = null // null: undecided, true: horizontal, false: vertical
  private touchOffset = 0

  private getOptions: () => {
    tabs: T[]
    value: T
    onChange: (value: T) => void
    disabled?: boolean
  }
  private onOffsetChange?: (offset: number) => void

  constructor(
    getOptions: () => {
      tabs: T[]
      value: T
      onChange: (value: T) => void
      disabled?: boolean
    },
    onOffsetChange?: (offset: number) => void,
  ) {
    this.getOptions = getOptions
    this.onOffsetChange = onOffsetChange
  }

  handleWheel = (e: WheelEvent) => {
    const { tabs, value, onChange, disabled } = this.getOptions()
    if (disabled || tabs.length <= 1) return

    // Wheel criteria per §5.3:
    // "a horizontal wheel (absolute deltaX greater than absolute deltaY) or Shift + vertical wheel over the tab bar or content switches to the adjacent tab once per gesture (threshold 80px, cooldown 350 ms). It never hijacks plain vertical scrolling of content."
    const isShiftVertical = e.shiftKey && Math.abs(e.deltaY) > 0
    const isPureHorizontal = Math.abs(e.deltaX) > Math.abs(e.deltaY)

    if (!isShiftVertical && !isPureHorizontal) {
      // Plain vertical scroll: DO NOT INTERCEPT, leave native vertical scroll untouched
      return
    }

    // Normalized delta value
    let rawDelta = isShiftVertical ? e.deltaY : e.deltaX
    if (e.deltaMode === 1) {
      rawDelta *= 16
    } else if (e.deltaMode === 2) {
      rawDelta *= typeof window !== 'undefined' ? window.innerWidth : 1000
    }

    const now = Date.now()
    if (now - this.lastWheelTime > 150) {
      this.wheelAccumulator = 0
      this.wheelGestureConsumed = false
    }
    this.lastWheelTime = now

    if (this.wheelGestureConsumed || now < this.wheelCooldownUntil) {
      // Gesture already switched a tab or cooldown active, prevent default horizontal scrolling
      e.preventDefault()
      return
    }

    this.wheelAccumulator += rawDelta

    if (Math.abs(this.wheelAccumulator) >= 80) {
      const currentIndex = tabs.indexOf(value)
      if (currentIndex !== -1) {
        if (this.wheelAccumulator > 0 && currentIndex < tabs.length - 1) {
          // Scroll right -> next tab
          e.preventDefault()
          onChange(tabs[currentIndex + 1])
          this.wheelAccumulator = 0
          this.wheelCooldownUntil = now + 350
          this.wheelGestureConsumed = true
        } else if (this.wheelAccumulator < 0 && currentIndex > 0) {
          // Scroll left -> prev tab
          e.preventDefault()
          onChange(tabs[currentIndex - 1])
          this.wheelAccumulator = 0
          this.wheelCooldownUntil = now + 350
          this.wheelGestureConsumed = true
        }
      }
    }
  }

  handleTouchStart = (e: TouchEvent) => {
    const { tabs, disabled } = this.getOptions()
    if (disabled || tabs.length <= 1 || e.touches.length !== 1) return
    const touch = e.touches[0]
    this.startX = touch.clientX
    this.startY = touch.clientY
    this.startTime = Date.now()
    this.isSwiping = null
    this.touchOffset = 0
    this.onOffsetChange?.(0)
  }

  handleTouchMove = (e: TouchEvent) => {
    const { tabs, value, disabled } = this.getOptions()
    if (disabled || tabs.length <= 1 || e.touches.length !== 1) return
    const touch = e.touches[0]
    const dx = touch.clientX - this.startX
    const dy = touch.clientY - this.startY

    // Decide if user is swiping horizontally or scrolling vertically
    if (this.isSwiping === null) {
      if (Math.abs(dx) > 8 || Math.abs(dy) > 8) {
        if (Math.abs(dx) > Math.abs(dy)) {
          this.isSwiping = true
        } else {
          this.isSwiping = false
        }
      }
    }

    if (this.isSwiping === true) {
      // Prevent vertical page scroll while swiping tabs horizontally
      if (e.cancelable) e.preventDefault()

      const currentIndex = tabs.indexOf(value)
      const hasPrev = currentIndex > 0
      const hasNext = currentIndex < tabs.length - 1

      // Rubber-band resistance if swiping past ends
      let offset = dx
      if ((dx > 0 && !hasPrev) || (dx < 0 && !hasNext)) {
        offset = dx * 0.3
      }
      this.touchOffset = offset
      this.onOffsetChange?.(offset)
    }
  }

  handleTouchEnd = (e: TouchEvent) => {
    const { tabs, value, onChange, disabled } = this.getOptions()
    if (disabled || tabs.length <= 1) return

    const now = Date.now()
    const elapsed = now - this.startTime
    const touch = e.changedTouches[0]
    const dx = touch ? touch.clientX - this.startX : 0
    const dy = touch ? touch.clientY - this.startY : 0

    // Per §5.3:
    // "Touch: a horizontal swipe of at least 60px within 400 ms, with vertical movement under 40px, switches tabs."
    if (this.isSwiping === true && elapsed <= 400 && Math.abs(dy) < 40 && Math.abs(dx) >= 60) {
      const currentIndex = tabs.indexOf(value)
      if (currentIndex !== -1) {
        if (dx < -60 && currentIndex < tabs.length - 1) {
          // Swiped left -> next tab
          onChange(tabs[currentIndex + 1])
        } else if (dx > 60 && currentIndex > 0) {
          // Swiped right -> prev tab
          onChange(tabs[currentIndex - 1])
        }
      }
    }

    this.isSwiping = null
    this.touchOffset = 0
    this.onOffsetChange?.(0)
  }

  handleKeyDown = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
    const { tabs, value, onChange, disabled } = this.getOptions()
    if (disabled || tabs.length <= 1) return

    // Ctrl+Tab / Ctrl+Shift+Tab
    if (e.ctrlKey && e.key === 'Tab') {
      e.preventDefault()
      const currentIndex = tabs.indexOf(value)
      if (currentIndex === -1) return

      if (e.shiftKey) {
        // Prev tab (wrap around)
        const nextIndex = (currentIndex - 1 + tabs.length) % tabs.length
        onChange(tabs[nextIndex])
      } else {
        // Next tab (wrap around)
        const nextIndex = (currentIndex + 1) % tabs.length
        onChange(tabs[nextIndex])
      }
    }
  }

  getTouchOffset() {
    return this.touchOffset
  }

  reset() {
    this.wheelAccumulator = 0
    this.lastWheelTime = 0
    this.wheelCooldownUntil = 0
    this.wheelGestureConsumed = false
    this.isSwiping = null
    this.touchOffset = 0
    this.onOffsetChange?.(0)
  }
}

export function useSwipeTabs<T extends string = string>({
  tabs,
  value,
  onChange,
  containerRef,
  disabled,
}: UseSwipeTabsOptions<T>) {
  const [touchOffset, setTouchOffset] = useState(0)
  const optionsRef = useRef({ tabs, value, onChange, disabled })
  useEffect(() => {
    optionsRef.current = { tabs, value, onChange, disabled }
  })

  const controllerRef = useRef<SwipeTabsController<T> | null>(null)
  if (!controllerRef.current) {
    controllerRef.current = new SwipeTabsController(
      () => optionsRef.current,
      (offset) => setTouchOffset(offset),
    )
  }

  useEffect(() => {
    const el = containerRef?.current
    if (!el) return

    const controller = controllerRef.current
    if (!controller) return

    const onWheel = (e: WheelEvent) => controller.handleWheel(e)
    const onTouchStart = (e: TouchEvent) => controller.handleTouchStart(e)
    const onTouchMove = (e: TouchEvent) => controller.handleTouchMove(e)
    const onTouchEnd = (e: TouchEvent) => controller.handleTouchEnd(e)
    const onKeyDown = (e: KeyboardEvent) => controller.handleKeyDown(e)

    el.addEventListener('wheel', onWheel, { passive: false })
    el.addEventListener('touchstart', onTouchStart, { passive: true })
    el.addEventListener('touchmove', onTouchMove, { passive: false })
    el.addEventListener('touchend', onTouchEnd, { passive: true })
    el.addEventListener('keydown', onKeyDown)

    return () => {
      el.removeEventListener('wheel', onWheel)
      el.removeEventListener('touchstart', onTouchStart)
      el.removeEventListener('touchmove', onTouchMove)
      el.removeEventListener('touchend', onTouchEnd)
      el.removeEventListener('keydown', onKeyDown)
    }
  }, [containerRef])

  return {
    touchOffset,
    controller: controllerRef.current,
    containerProps: {
      onWheel: (e: React.WheelEvent) => controllerRef.current?.handleWheel(e.nativeEvent),
      onTouchStart: (e: React.TouchEvent) => controllerRef.current?.handleTouchStart(e.nativeEvent),
      onTouchMove: (e: React.TouchEvent) => controllerRef.current?.handleTouchMove(e.nativeEvent),
      onTouchEnd: (e: React.TouchEvent) => controllerRef.current?.handleTouchEnd(e.nativeEvent),
      onKeyDown: (e: React.KeyboardEvent) => controllerRef.current?.handleKeyDown(e.nativeEvent),
    },
  }
}

File: apps/client/src/sources/komga.ts

import { net } from '../lib/net';
import type {
  CatalogEntry,
  CatalogPage,
  CatalogSection,
  ChapterInfo,
  MangaSource,
  PageRef,
} from './types';

interface KomgaSeriesItem {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  booksCount: number;
  metadata?: {
    status?: string;
    summary?: string;
    genres?: string[];
    tags?: string[];
  };
  booksMetadata?: {
    authors?: Array<{ name: string; role: string }>;
  };
}

interface KomgaSeriesPageResponse {
  content: KomgaSeriesItem[];
  empty: boolean;
  first: boolean;
  last: boolean;
  number: number;
  numberOfElements: number;
  size: number;
  totalElements: number;
  totalPages: number;
}

interface KomgaBookItem {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  number: number;
  media: {
    status: string;
    mediaType: string;
    pagesCount: number;
  };
  metadata?: {
    title?: string;
    number?: string;
    numberSort?: number;
    summary?: string;
    releaseDate?: string;
    authors?: Array<{ name: string; role: string }>;
  };
}

interface KomgaBookPageResponse {
  content: KomgaBookItem[];
}

interface KomgaPageItem {
  number: number;
  fileName: string;
  mediaType: string;
  width?: number;
  height?: number;
  sizeBytes?: number;
}

export class KomgaSource implements MangaSource {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  icon = 'BookOpen';
  kind = 'manga' as const;
  baseUrl: string;
  authHeader?: string;

  constructor(
    id = 'komga',
    name = 'Komga Server',
    baseUrl = 'http://localhost:25600',
    credentials?: { username?: string; password?: string; apiKey?: string },
  ) {
    this.id = id;
    this.name = name;
    this.baseUrl = baseUrl.replace(/\/+$/, '');

    if (credentials?.username || credentials?.password) {
      const u = credentials.username || '';
      const p = credentials.password || '';
      this.authHeader = `Basic ${btoa(`${u}:${p}`)}`;
    } else if (credentials?.apiKey) {
      this.authHeader = `Bearer ${credentials.apiKey}`;
    }
  }

  private getHeaders(): Record<string, string> {
    const headers: Record<string, string> = {};
    if (this.authHeader) {
      headers['Authorization'] = this.authHeader;
    }
    return headers;
  }

  async sections(): Promise<CatalogSection[]> {
    return [
      { id: 'latest', title: 'Recently Added' },
      { id: 'updated', title: 'Recently Updated' },
    ];
  }

  async browse(sectionId: string, cursor?: string): Promise<CatalogPage> {
    const page = cursor ? parseInt(cursor, 10) : 0;
    const sort = sectionId === 'updated' ? 'lastModifiedDate,desc' : 'createdDate,desc';
    const url = `${this.baseUrl}/api/v1/series?page=${page}&size=24&sort=${sort}`;

    const res = await net.fetch(url, { headers: this.getHeaders() });
    if (!res.ok) {
      throw new Error(`Failed to load Komga series: ${res.statusText}`);
    }

    const data = (await res.json()) as KomgaSeriesPageResponse;
    const items: CatalogEntry[] = (data.content || []).map((s) => ({
      id: `komga:${this.id}:${s.id}`,
      title: s.name,
      authors: s.booksMetadata?.authors?.map((a) => a.name) || ['Komga Creator'],
      cover: `${this.baseUrl}/api/v1/series/${s.id}/thumbnail`,
      summary: s.metadata?.summary,
      tags: [...(s.metadata?.genres || []), ...(s.metadata?.tags || [])].slice(0, 5),
      kind: 'manga',
      status: s.metadata?.status?.toLowerCase(),
    }));

    return {
      items,
      next: !data.last ? String(page + 1) : undefined,
    };
  }

  async search(query: string, cursor?: string): Promise<CatalogPage> {
    const page = cursor ? parseInt(cursor, 10) : 0;
    const url = `${this.baseUrl}/api/v1/series?search=${encodeURIComponent(query.trim())}&page=${page}&size=24`;

    const res = await net.fetch(url, { headers: this.getHeaders() });
    if (!res.ok) {
      throw new Error(`Failed to search Komga: ${res.statusText}`);
    }

    const data = (await res.json()) as KomgaSeriesPageResponse;
    const items: CatalogEntry[] = (data.content || []).map((s) => ({
      id: `komga:${this.id}:${s.id}`,
      title: s.name,
      authors: s.booksMetadata?.authors?.map((a) => a.name) || ['Komga Creator'],
      cover: `${this.baseUrl}/api/v1/series/${s.id}/thumbnail`,
      summary: s.metadata?.summary,
      tags: [...(s.metadata?.genres || []), ...(s.metadata?.tags || [])].slice(0, 5),
      kind: 'manga',
      status: s.metadata?.status?.toLowerCase(),
    }));

    return {
      items,
      next: !data.last ? String(page + 1) : undefined,
    };
  }

  async details(id: string): Promise<CatalogEntry> {
    const parts = id.split(':');
    const seriesId = parts[parts.length - 1];
    const url = `${this.baseUrl}/api/v1/series/${seriesId}`;

    const res = await net.fetch(url, { headers: this.getHeaders() });
    if (!res.ok) {
      throw new Error(`Failed to fetch Komga series details: ${res.statusText}`);
    }

    const s = (await res.json()) as KomgaSeriesItem;
    return {
      id,
      title: s.name,
      authors: s.booksMetadata?.authors?.map((a) => a.name) || ['Komga Creator'],
      cover: `${this.baseUrl}/api/v1/series/${s.id}/thumbnail`,
      summary: s.metadata?.summary,
      tags: [...(s.metadata?.genres || []), ...(s.metadata?.tags || [])].slice(0, 6),
      kind: 'manga',
      status: s.metadata?.status?.toLowerCase(),
    };
  }

  async chapters(seriesId: string): Promise<ChapterInfo[]> {
    const parts = seriesId.split(':');
    const actualSeriesId = parts[parts.length - 1];
    const url = `${this.baseUrl}/api/v1/series/${actualSeriesId}/books?page=0&size=500&sort=metadata.numberSort,asc`;

    const res = await net.fetch(url, { headers: this.getHeaders() });
    if (!res.ok) {
      throw new Error(`Failed to load Komga books: ${res.statusText}`);
    }

    const data = (await res.json()) as KomgaBookPageResponse;
    return (data.content || []).map((b) => ({
      id: b.id,
      title: b.metadata?.title || b.name,
      number: b.metadata?.numberSort ?? b.number,
      scanlationGroup: 'Komga',
      publishedAt: b.metadata?.releaseDate,
    }));
  }

  async pages(chapterId: string): Promise<PageRef[]> {
    const url = `${this.baseUrl}/api/v1/books/${chapterId}/pages`;
    const res = await net.fetch(url, { headers: this.getHeaders() });
    if (!res.ok) {
      throw new Error(`Failed to load Komga book pages: ${res.statusText}`);
    }

    const pages = (await res.json()) as KomgaPageItem[];
    return pages.map((p) => ({
      index: p.number,
      url: `${this.baseUrl}/api/v1/books/${chapterId}/pages/${p.number}`,
    }));
  }

  credit(chapter: ChapterInfo): string {
    return `Hosted on Komga Server · Issue: ${chapter.title || 'Chapter'}`;
  }
}

File: apps/client/src/design/components/AmbientBackdrop.tsx

import React, { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { clsx } from 'clsx';
import { twMerge } from 'tailwind-merge';

export interface AmbientBackdropProps {
  coverUrl?: string | null;
  enabled?: boolean;
  className?: string;
}

// The backdrop used to be the cover image with `filter: blur(90px)` stretched over
// the whole window. That filter is re-rasterised on every resize and whenever a
// layer above it changes, which was a major source of jank in the desktop WebView.
// Instead we draw the cover once into a tiny canvas (blurred there, where it is
// cheap) and let the GPU upscale that thumbnail: bilinear upscaling of a ~40px
// image looks like a heavy blur at essentially zero paint cost.
const THUMB_W = 36;
const THUMB_H = 54;
const cache = new Map<string, string | null>();

async function renderAmbientThumb(url: string): Promise<string | null> {
  if (cache.has(url)) return cache.get(url) ?? null;
  try {
    const img = new Image();
    img.crossOrigin = 'anonymous';
    img.decoding = 'async';
    img.src = url;
    await img.decode();
    const canvas = document.createElement('canvas');
    canvas.width = THUMB_W;
    canvas.height = THUMB_H;
    const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');
    if (!ctx) return null;
    ctx.filter = 'blur(3px) saturate(1.4)';
    // Overdraw the edges so the blur doesn't pull in transparent borders.
    ctx.drawImage(img, -6, -6, THUMB_W + 12, THUMB_H + 12);
    // toDataURL throws if the image was cross-origin without CORS headers.
    const data = canvas.toDataURL('image/png');
    cache.set(url, data);
    return data;
  } catch {
    cache.set(url, null);
    return null;
  }
}

export const AmbientBackdrop: React.FC<AmbientBackdropProps> = ({
  coverUrl,
  enabled = true,
  className,
}) => {
  const [thumb, setThumb] = useState<{ src: string; data: string | null } | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!enabled || !coverUrl) return;
    let alive = true;
    void renderAmbientThumb(coverUrl).then((data) => {
      if (alive) setThumb({ src: coverUrl, data });
    });
    return () => {
      alive = false;
    };
  }, [coverUrl, enabled]);

  if (!enabled) return null;

  const ready = coverUrl && thumb?.src === coverUrl ? thumb : null;

  return (
    <div
      aria-hidden="true"
      className={twMerge(
        clsx(
          'fixed inset-0 pointer-events-none z-0 overflow-hidden select-none [contain:strict]',
          className,
        ),
      )}
    >
      {ready?.data ? (
        <div
          className="absolute inset-0 bg-cover bg-center transition-opacity duration-[var(--d-slow)] ease-[var(--ease-out)]"
          style={{
            backgroundImage: `url(${ready.data})`,
            opacity: 'var(--bg-ambient-opacity)',
          }}
        />
      ) : ready ? (
        // Cover could not be read into a canvas (no CORS): fall back to a CSS
        // blur, but on a small layer that is scaled up so the blur stays cheap.
        <div
          className="absolute left-0 top-0 w-[12.5%] h-[12.5%] origin-top-left scale-[8] bg-cover bg-center will-change-transform"
          style={{
            backgroundImage: `url(${ready.src})`,
            filter: 'blur(12px) saturate(1.4)',
            opacity: 'var(--bg-ambient-opacity)',
          }}
        />
      ) : (
        <div
          className="absolute inset-0"
          style={{
            background:
              'radial-gradient(ellipse 80% 60% at 50% 20%, color-mix(in oklab, var(--accent) 12%, transparent) 0%, transparent 100%)',
          }}
        />
      )}
    </div>
  );
};

import { NavLink } from 'react-router-dom';
import { Home, BookOpen, ShoppingBag, BookMarked, MoreHorizontal, BarChart2, Sparkles, Settings } from 'lucide-react';
import { clsx } from 'clsx';
import { Dialog } from './Dialog';

export interface BottomNavProps {
  onOpenSettings?: () => void;
}

export const BottomNav: FC<BottomNavProps> = ({ onOpenSettings }) => {

    { to: '/', label: 'Home', icon: <Home className="w-5 h-5" /> },
    { to: '/library', label: 'Library', icon: <BookOpen className="w-5 h-5" /> },
    { to: '/store', label: 'Store', icon: <ShoppingBag className="w-5 h-5" /> },
    { to: '/notebook', label: 'Notebook', icon: <BookMarked className="w-5 h-5" /> },

        aria-label="Bottom navigation"

      <Dialog open={moreOpen} onOpenChange={setMoreOpen} title="More Options">

          <NavLink
            to="/stats"
            onClick={() => setMoreOpen(false)}
            className="flex items-center gap-3.5 p-3 rounded-[var(--r-md)] hover:bg-[var(--layer-2)] text-sm font-medium text-[var(--text-1)]"

            <BarChart2 className="w-5 h-5 text-[var(--accent)]" />
            <span>Reading Statistics</span>
          </NavLink>
          <NavLink
            to="/updates"

          </NavLink>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => {
              setMoreOpen(false);
              onOpenSettings?.();
            }}
            className="flex items-center gap-3.5 p-3 rounded-[var(--r-md)] hover:bg-[var(--layer-2)] text-sm font-medium text-[var(--text-1)] text-left cursor-pointer"
          >
            <Settings className="w-5 h-5 text-[var(--accent)]" />
            <span>Settings</span>
          </button>
        </div>
      </Dialog>
    </>
  );
};

import React, { type ReactNode } from 'react';
import { clsx } from 'clsx';
import { twMerge } from 'tailwind-merge';

export interface CoverGridProps {
  children: ReactNode;
  columnWidth?: number; // min column width, default 150px
  className?: string;
  selectionMode?: boolean;
}

export const CoverGrid: React.FC<CoverGridProps> = ({
  children,
  columnWidth = 150,
  className,
  selectionMode = false,
}) => {
  return (
    <div
      className={twMerge(
        clsx(
          'grid gap-3 sm:gap-5 py-4',
          selectionMode && 'select-none',
          className,
        ),
      )}
      style={{
        // Phones get three smaller covers per row instead of two huge ones
        // (1.5rem = the two phone-width gaps).
        gridTemplateColumns: `repeat(auto-fill, minmax(min(${columnWidth}px, calc((100% - 1.5rem) / 3)), 1fr))`,
      }}
    >
      {children}
    </div>
  );
};

File: apps/client/src/design/components/IconButton.tsx
================
import { forwardRef, type ButtonHTMLAttributes, type ReactNode } from 'react';
import { clsx } from 'clsx';
import { twMerge } from 'tailwind-merge';
import { Tooltip } from './Tooltip';

const isCoarsePointer =
  typeof window !== 'undefined' && !!window.matchMedia?.('(pointer: coarse)').matches;

export type IconButtonVariant = 'subtle' | 'standard' | 'accent' | 'danger';

export interface IconButtonProps extends ButtonHTMLAttributes<HTMLButtonElement> {
  label: string; // Mandatory for accessibility & tooltip
  icon: ReactNode;
  variant?: IconButtonVariant;
  size?: 'sm' | 'md' | 'lg';
  tooltipSide?: 'top' | 'right' | 'bottom' | 'left';
}

export const IconButton = forwardRef<HTMLButtonElement, IconButtonProps>(
  ({ label, icon, variant = 'subtle', size = 'md', tooltipSide = 'top', className, disabled, ...props }, ref) => {
    const sizeClasses = {
      sm: 'w-[28px] h-[28px] rounded-[var(--r-sm)]',
      md: 'w-[36px] h-[36px] rounded-[var(--r-md)]',
      lg: 'w-[40px] h-[40px] rounded-[var(--r-md)]',
    };

    const variantClasses: Record<IconButtonVariant, string> = {
      subtle:
        'bg-transparent hover:bg-[var(--layer-2)] active:bg-[var(--layer-3)] text-[var(--text-1)] border-transparent',
      standard:
        'bg-[var(--layer-2)] hover:bg-[var(--layer-3)] active:bg-[var(--layer-1)] text-[var(--text-1)] border border-[var(--stroke)] shadow-xs',
      accent:
        'bg-[var(--accent)] hover:bg-[var(--accent-hover)] active:bg-[var(--accent-pressed)] text-[var(--on-accent)] shadow-sm',
      danger:
        'bg-transparent hover:bg-[color-mix(in_oklab,var(--danger)_18%,transparent)] active:bg-[color-mix(in_oklab,var(--danger)_30%,transparent)] text-[var(--danger)]',
    };

    const button = (
      <button
        ref={ref}
        aria-label={label}
        disabled={disabled}
        className={twMerge(
          clsx(
            'inline-flex items-center justify-center transition-colors cursor-pointer outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-[var(--accent)] disabled:pointer-events-none disabled:opacity-40 select-none shrink-0',
            sizeClasses[size],
            variantClasses[variant],
            className,
          ),
        )}
        {...props}
      >
        {icon}
      </button>
    );

    // Touch screens have no hover: tooltips only appeared when a dialog focused
    // its button (e.g. a stray "Close" bubble). The label stays as aria-label.
    if (isCoarsePointer) return button;
    return <Tooltip content={label} side={tooltipSide}>{button}</Tooltip>;
  },
);

IconButton.displayName = 'IconButton';

import React from 'react';
import { clsx } from 'clsx';
import { twMerge } from 'tailwind-merge';

export interface ProgressBarProps {
  value: number; // 0 to 100
  className?: string;
  height?: number;
}

export const ProgressBar: React.FC<ProgressBarProps> = ({ value, className, height = 3 }) => {
  const clamped = Math.min(100, Math.max(0, value));

  return (
    <div
      role="progressbar"
      aria-valuenow={clamped}
      aria-valuemin={0}
      aria-valuemax={100}
      className={twMerge('w-full bg-[var(--layer-3)] rounded-full overflow-hidden select-none', className)}
      style={{ height: `${height}px` }}
    >
      <div
        className="h-full bg-[var(--accent)] transition-all duration-[var(--d-fast)] ease-[var(--ease-out)]"
        style={{ width: `${clamped}%` }}
      />
    </div>
  );
};

export interface ProgressRingProps {
  size?: number;
  strokeWidth?: number;
  className?: string;
}

export const ProgressRing: React.FC<ProgressRingProps> = ({
  size = 24,
  strokeWidth = 3,
  className,
}) => {
  const radius = (size - strokeWidth) / 2;
  const circumference = 2 * Math.PI * radius;

  return (
    <svg
      className={twMerge('animate-spin text-[var(--accent)] select-none shrink-0', className)}
      width={size}
      height={size}
      viewBox={`0 0 ${size} ${size}`}
      fill="none"
      xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg"
    >
      <circle
        className="opacity-25"
        cx={size / 2}
        cy={size / 2}
        r={radius}
        stroke="currentColor"
        strokeWidth={strokeWidth}
      />
      <circle
        className="opacity-90"
        cx={size / 2}
        cy={size / 2}
        r={radius}
        stroke="currentColor"
        strokeWidth={strokeWidth}
        strokeDasharray={circumference}
        strokeDashoffset={circumference * 0.75}
        strokeLinecap="round"
      />
    </svg>
  );
};

export interface CircularProgressProps {
  value: number; // 0 to 100+
  size?: number;
  strokeWidth?: number;
  className?: string;
  trackColor?: string;
  indicatorColor?: string;
  children?: React.ReactNode;
}

export const CircularProgress: React.FC<CircularProgressProps> = ({
  value,
  size = 64,
  strokeWidth = 6,
  className,
  trackColor = 'var(--layer-3)',
  indicatorColor = 'var(--accent)',
  children,
}) => {
  const clamped = Math.min(100, Math.max(0, value));
  const radius = (size - strokeWidth) / 2;
  const circumference = 2 * Math.PI * radius;
  const offset = circumference - (clamped / 100) * circumference;

  return (
    <div
      className={twMerge('relative inline-flex items-center justify-center select-none shrink-0', className)}
      style={{ width: size, height: size }}
      role="progressbar"
      aria-valuenow={clamped}
      aria-valuemin={0}
      aria-valuemax={100}
    >
      <svg
        width={size}
        height={size}
        viewBox={`0 0 ${size} ${size}`}
        className="transform -rotate-90"
      >
        <circle
          cx={size / 2}
          cy={size / 2}
          r={radius}
          fill="none"
          stroke={trackColor}
          strokeWidth={strokeWidth}
        />
        <circle
          cx={size / 2}
          cy={size / 2}
          r={radius}
          fill="none"
          stroke={indicatorColor}
          strokeWidth={strokeWidth}
          strokeDasharray={circumference}
          strokeDashoffset={offset}
          strokeLinecap="round"
          className="transition-all duration-500 ease-out"
        />
      </svg>
      {children && <div className="absolute inset-0 flex flex-col items-center justify-center pointer-events-none">{children}</div>}
    </div>
  );
};

export interface SkeletonProps {
  className?: string;
}

export const Skeleton: React.FC<SkeletonProps> = ({ className }) => {
  return (
    <div
      className={twMerge(
        clsx(
          'animate-pulse bg-[var(--layer-2)] border border-[var(--stroke)] rounded-[var(--r-md)] select-none',
          className,
        ),
      )}
    />
  );
};

File: apps/client/src/design/components/SearchBox.tsx
================
import React, { forwardRef, useRef, useImperativeHandle, useEffect } from 'react';
import { Search, X } from 'lucide-react';
import { clsx } from 'clsx';
import { twMerge } from 'tailwind-merge';

export interface SearchBoxProps extends Omit<React.InputHTMLAttributes<HTMLInputElement>, 'onChange'> {
  value: string;
  onChange: (value: string) => void;
  onClear?: () => void;
  shortcutHint?: boolean;
}

export const SearchBox = forwardRef<HTMLInputElement, SearchBoxProps>(
  ({ value, onChange, onClear, placeholder = 'Search…', shortcutHint = true, className, ...props }, ref) => {
    const inputRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);
    useImperativeHandle(ref, () => inputRef.current as HTMLInputElement);

    useEffect(() => {
      const handleKeyDown = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
        if ((e.ctrlKey || e.metaKey) && e.key.toLowerCase() === 'k') {
          e.preventDefault();
          inputRef.current?.focus();
        } else if (e.key === '/' && document.activeElement !== inputRef.current && !(document.activeElement instanceof HTMLInputElement || document.activeElement instanceof HTMLTextAreaElement)) {
          e.preventDefault();
          inputRef.current?.focus();
        }
      };

      window.addEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown);
      return () => window.removeEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown);
    }, []);

    const handleClear = () => {
      onChange('');
      onClear?.();
      inputRef.current?.focus();
    };

    return (
      <div
        className={twMerge(
          clsx(
            'relative flex items-center h-[34px] bg-[var(--layer-2)] border border-[var(--stroke)] hover:border-[var(--stroke-strong)] focus-within:border-[var(--accent)] focus-within:ring-2 focus-within:ring-[var(--accent)] rounded-[var(--r-md)] transition-colors px-2.5 gap-2 text-sm text-[var(--text-1)]',
            className,
          ),
        )}
      >
        <Search className="w-4 h-4 text-[var(--text-3)] shrink-0" />
        <input
          ref={inputRef}
          type="text"
          value={value}
          onChange={(e) => onChange(e.target.value)}
          placeholder={placeholder}
          className="w-full min-w-0 bg-transparent outline-none placeholder:text-[var(--text-3)] text-sm text-ellipsis"
          {...props}
        />
        {value ? (
          <button
            type="button"
            aria-label="Clear search"
            onClick={handleClear}
            className="p-0.5 rounded-[var(--r-sm)] text-[var(--text-3)] hover:text-[var(--text-1)] hover:bg-[var(--layer-3)] cursor-pointer outline-none"
          >
            <X className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
          </button>
        ) : shortcutHint ? (
          <kbd className="hidden sm:inline-flex items-center px-1.5 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-mono text-[var(--text-3)] bg-[var(--layer-1)] border border-[var(--stroke)] rounded-[var(--r-sm)] select-none">
            Ctrl+K
          </kbd>
        ) : null}
      </div>
    );
  },
);

SearchBox.displayName = 'SearchBox';

import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { Dialog } from './Dialog';
import { useTheme, ACCENT_PRESETS, type AppTheme } from '../theme';
import { SegmentedControl } from './SegmentedControl';
import { Switch } from './Switch';
import { Slider } from './Slider';
import { Tabs, type TabItem } from './Tabs';
import { AccountSettings } from '../../features/auth/AccountSettings';
import { Palette, User } from 'lucide-react';

export interface SettingsDialogProps {
  open: boolean;
  onOpenChange: (open: boolean) => void;
  initialTab?: 'appearance' | 'account';
}

export const SettingsDialog: React.FC<SettingsDialogProps> = ({
  open,
  onOpenChange,
  initialTab = 'appearance',
}) => {
  const { settings, updateSettings } = useTheme();
  const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = useState<'appearance' | 'account'>(initialTab);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (open) {
      setActiveTab(initialTab);
    }
  }, [open, initialTab]);

  const themeOptions: { value: AppTheme; label: string }[] = [
    { value: 'dark', label: 'Dark' },
    { value: 'light', label: 'Light' },
    { value: 'system', label: 'System' },
  ];

  const tabItems: TabItem<'appearance' | 'account'>[] = [
    { value: 'appearance', label: 'Appearance', icon: <Palette className="w-4 h-4" /> },
    { value: 'account', label: 'Account & Sync', icon: <User className="w-4 h-4" /> },
  ];

  return (
    <Dialog
      open={open}
      onOpenChange={onOpenChange}
      title="Settings"
      description="Manage appearance, account, and sync preferences"
    >
      <div className="flex flex-col gap-4 py-1">
        <Tabs
          items={tabItems}
          value={activeTab}
          onValueChange={setActiveTab}
        />

        {activeTab === 'appearance' && (
          <div className="flex flex-col gap-6 py-2 animate-in fade-in-0 duration-150">
            {/* Theme Mode */}
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
              <span className="text-sm font-medium text-[var(--text-1)]">Theme</span>
              <SegmentedControl
                size="sm"
                options={themeOptions}
                value={settings.theme}
                onChange={(val) => updateSettings({ theme: val })}
              />
            </div>

            {/* Accent Color */}
            <div className="flex flex-col gap-2">
              <span className="text-sm font-medium text-[var(--text-1)]">Accent color</span>
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2 flex-wrap">
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => updateSettings({ accent: 'dynamic' })}
                  className={`px-3 py-1 text-xs rounded-[var(--r-sm)] border cursor-pointer transition-colors ${
                    settings.accent === 'dynamic'
                      ? 'border-[var(--accent)] bg-[var(--accent-soft)] text-[var(--accent)] font-semibold'
                      : 'border-[var(--stroke)] text-[var(--text-2)] hover:bg-[var(--layer-2)]'
                  }`}
                >
                  Dynamic (Book)
                </button>

                {Object.entries(ACCENT_PRESETS).map(([name, hex]) => (
                  <button
                    key={name}
                    type="button"
                    aria-label={name}
                    onClick={() => updateSettings({ accent: name })}
                    className={`w-6 h-6 rounded-full cursor-pointer transition-transform hover:scale-110 flex items-center justify-center ${
                      settings.accent === name ? 'ring-2 ring-[var(--text-1)] ring-offset-2 ring-offset-[var(--layer-solid)]' : ''
                    }`}
                    style={{ backgroundColor: hex }}
                  />
                ))}
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* Ambient Background */}
            <div className="flex flex-col gap-3 pt-2 border-t border-[var(--stroke)]">
              <Switch
                label="Ambient backdrop glow"
                checked={settings.ambientEnabled}
                onCheckedChange={(checked) => updateSettings({ ambientEnabled: checked })}
              />

              {settings.ambientEnabled && (
                <div className="flex items-center gap-4 pl-1">
                  <span className="text-xs text-[var(--text-2)] min-w-[50px]">
                    {settings.ambientStrength}%
                  </span>
                  <Slider
                    value={[settings.ambientStrength]}
                    min={10}
                    max={60}
                    step={2}
                    onValueChange={([val]) => updateSettings({ ambientStrength: val })}
                  />
                </div>
              )}
            </div>

            {/* Cover Glow */}
            <div className="pt-2 border-t border-[var(--stroke)]">
              <Switch
                label="Cover hover glow"
                checked={settings.coverGlow}
                onCheckedChange={(checked) => updateSettings({ coverGlow: checked })}
              />
            </div>
          </div>
        )}

        {activeTab === 'account' && (
          <div className="animate-in fade-in-0 duration-150">
            <AccountSettings />
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    </Dialog>
  );
};

File: apps/client/src/design/components/Tabs.tsx
================
import React, { useRef, useEffect, useState, useMemo, createContext, useContext } from 'react';
import * as RadixTabs from '@radix-ui/react-tabs';
import { clsx } from 'clsx';
import { twMerge } from 'tailwind-merge';
import { useSwipeTabs } from '../../lib/useSwipeTabs';

export interface TabItem<T extends string = string> {
  value: T;
  label: React.ReactNode;
  icon?: React.ReactNode;
  badge?: React.ReactNode;
}

export interface TabsProps<T extends string = string> {
  items: TabItem<T>[];
  value: T;
  onValueChange: (value: T) => void;
  children?: React.ReactNode;
  className?: string;
  listClassName?: string;
}

interface TabsContextValue {
  direction: 'left' | 'right' | null;
  touchOffset: number;
}

const TabsContext = createContext<TabsContextValue>({
  direction: null,
  touchOffset: 0,
});

export function useTabsContext() {
  return useContext(TabsContext);
}

export function Tabs<T extends string = string>({
  items,
  value,
  onValueChange,
  children,
  className,
  listClassName,
}: TabsProps<T>) {
  const listRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const [indicatorStyle, setIndicatorStyle] = useState<{ left: number; width: number }>({
    left: 0,
    width: 0,
  });

  const tabValues = useMemo(() => items.map((i) => i.value), [items]);
  const [direction, setDirection] = useState<'left' | 'right' | null>(null);
  const prevValueRef = useRef(value);

  useEffect(() => {
    const prevIdx = tabValues.indexOf(prevValueRef.current);
    const newIdx = tabValues.indexOf(value);
    if (prevIdx !== -1 && newIdx !== -1 && prevIdx !== newIdx) {
      setDirection(newIdx > prevIdx ? 'right' : 'left');
    }
    prevValueRef.current = value;
  }, [value, tabValues]);

  const { touchOffset, containerProps } = useSwipeTabs({
    tabs: tabValues,
    value,
    onChange: onValueChange,
  });

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!listRef.current) return;
    const activeEl = listRef.current.querySelector<HTMLButtonElement>(`[data-state="active"]`);
    if (activeEl) {
      setIndicatorStyle({
        left: activeEl.offsetLeft,
        width: activeEl.offsetWidth,
      });
    }
  }, [value, items]);

  return (
    <TabsContext.Provider value={{ direction, touchOffset }}>
      <RadixTabs.Root
        value={value}
        onValueChange={(val) => onValueChange(val as T)}
        className={twMerge('flex flex-col w-full', className)}
        {...containerProps}
      >
        <div className="relative border-b border-[var(--stroke)]">
          <RadixTabs.List
            ref={listRef}
            className={twMerge('relative flex items-center gap-6 overflow-x-auto no-scrollbar', listClassName)}
          >
            {items.map((item) => (
              <RadixTabs.Trigger
                key={item.value}
                value={item.value}
                className={clsx(
                  'group relative flex items-center gap-2 py-3 text-sm font-medium transition-colors cursor-pointer outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-[var(--accent)] select-none shrink-0',
                  'data-[state=active]:text-[var(--text-1)] data-[state=active]:font-semibold text-[var(--text-2)] hover:text-[var(--text-1)]',
                )}
              >
                {item.icon && <span className="shrink-0">{item.icon}</span>}
                <span>{item.label}</span>
                {item.badge && (
                  <span className="px-1.5 py-0.2 text-[11px] font-bold rounded-full bg-[var(--accent-soft)] text-[var(--accent)]">
                    {item.badge}
                  </span>
                )}
              </RadixTabs.Trigger>
            ))}

            {/* Animated 3px accent underline */}
            {indicatorStyle.width > 0 && (
              <div
                className="absolute bottom-0 h-[3px] bg-[var(--accent)] rounded-t-full transition-all duration-[var(--d-normal)] ease-[var(--ease-out)] pointer-events-none"
                style={{
                  left: `${indicatorStyle.left}px`,
                  width: `${indicatorStyle.width}px`,
                }}
              />
            )}
          </RadixTabs.List>
        </div>

        {children}
      </RadixTabs.Root>
    </TabsContext.Provider>
  );
}

export function TabsContent({
  value,
  children,
  className,
  style,
  ...props
}: RadixTabs.TabsContentProps) {
  const ctx = useTabsContext();
  const animClass =
    ctx.direction === 'right'
      ? 'animate-tab-slide-right'
      : ctx.direction === 'left'
      ? 'animate-tab-slide-left'
      : '';

  const combinedStyle: React.CSSProperties = {
    ...style,
    ...(ctx.touchOffset !== 0 ? { transform: `translateX(${ctx.touchOffset}px)` } : {}),
  };

  return (
    <RadixTabs.Content
      value={value}
      className={twMerge('outline-none', animClass, className)}
      style={combinedStyle}
      {...props}
    >
      {children}
    </RadixTabs.Content>
  );
}

File: apps/client/src/design/theme.tsx

import React, { createContext, useContext, useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { db } from '../db';

export type AppTheme = 'dark' | 'light' | 'system';

export const ACCENT_PRESETS: Record<string, string> = {
  sky: '#4cc2ff',
  iris: '#8b7cf6',
  rose: '#ff6b9a',
  ember: '#ff8a3d',
  gold: '#f5c542',
  jade: '#3ccf91',
  teal: '#2ec4b6',
  slate: '#9aa7b8',
};

export interface ThemeSettings {
  theme: AppTheme;
  accent: string; // hex or 'dynamic'
  ambientEnabled: boolean;
  ambientStrength: number; // 0 to 60 (percent)
  coverGlow: boolean;
  density: 'comfortable' | 'compact';
}

const DEFAULT_THEME_SETTINGS: ThemeSettings = {
  theme: 'dark',
  accent: 'dynamic',
  ambientEnabled: true,
  ambientStrength: 32,
  coverGlow: true,
  density: 'comfortable',
};

interface ThemeContextType {
  settings: ThemeSettings;
  updateSettings: (updates: Partial<ThemeSettings>) => void;
  activeCoverUrl: string | null;
  setActiveCoverUrl: (url: string | null) => void;
  resolvedTheme: 'dark' | 'light';
}

const ThemeContext = createContext<ThemeContextType | null>(null);

export const ThemeProvider: React.FC<{ children: React.ReactNode }> = ({ children }) => {
  const [settings, setSettings] = useState<ThemeSettings>(() => {
    try {
      const saved = localStorage.getItem('folio_appearance');
      return saved ? { ...DEFAULT_THEME_SETTINGS, ...JSON.parse(saved) } : DEFAULT_THEME_SETTINGS;
    } catch {
      return DEFAULT_THEME_SETTINGS;
    }
  });

  const [activeCoverUrl, setActiveCoverUrl] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [systemIsDark, setSystemIsDark] = useState(() => {
    return window.matchMedia?.('(prefers-color-scheme: dark)').matches ?? true;
  });

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!window.matchMedia) return;
    const mq = window.matchMedia('(prefers-color-scheme: dark)');
    const handler = (e: MediaQueryListEvent) => setSystemIsDark(e.matches);
    mq.addEventListener('change', handler);
    return () => mq.removeEventListener('change', handler);
  }, []);

  const resolvedTheme: 'dark' | 'light' =
    settings.theme === 'system' ? (systemIsDark ? 'dark' : 'light') : settings.theme;

  useEffect(() => {
    document.documentElement.setAttribute('data-theme', resolvedTheme);
    localStorage.setItem('folio_appearance', JSON.stringify(settings));

    // Ambient opacity
    const opacity = settings.ambientEnabled ? settings.ambientStrength / 100 : 0;
    document.documentElement.style.setProperty('--bg-ambient-opacity', String(opacity));

    // Accent handling
    if (settings.accent !== 'dynamic') {
      const hex = ACCENT_PRESETS[settings.accent] || settings.accent;
      document.documentElement.style.setProperty('--accent', hex);
    }
  }, [resolvedTheme, settings]);

  const updateSettings = (updates: Partial<ThemeSettings>) => {
    setSettings((prev) => ({ ...prev, ...updates }));
  };

  return (
    <ThemeContext.Provider
      value={{
        settings,
        updateSettings,
        activeCoverUrl,
        setActiveCoverUrl,
        resolvedTheme,
      }}
    >
      {children}
    </ThemeContext.Provider>
  );
};

export function useTheme() {
  const ctx = useContext(ThemeContext);
  if (!ctx) throw new Error('useTheme must be used within ThemeProvider');
  return ctx;
}

/**
 * Extracts dominant color from a book's cover image using 32x32 canvas,
 * caching the result in books.meta.accent.
 */
export async function extractCoverColor(coverBlob: Blob, isDark: boolean): Promise<string> {
  return new Promise((resolve) => {
    const img = new Image();
    const url = URL.createObjectURL(coverBlob);
    img.src = url;

    img.onload = () => {
      URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
      const canvas = document.createElement('canvas');
      canvas.width = 32;
      canvas.height = 32;
      const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');
      if (!ctx) {
        resolve(isDark ? '#4cc2ff' : '#005fb8');
        return;
      }

      ctx.drawImage(img, 0, 0, 32, 32);
      const data = ctx.getImageData(0, 0, 32, 32).data;

      let rSum = 0, gSum = 0, bSum = 0, count = 0;
      for (let i = 0; i < data.length; i += 4) {
        const r = data[i], g = data[i + 1], b = data[i + 2];
        const max = Math.max(r, g, b);
        const min = Math.min(r, g, b);
        const sat = max === 0 ? 0 : (max - min) / max;

        // Take sufficiently saturated pixels
        if (sat > 0.15) {
          rSum += r;
          gSum += g;
          bSum += b;
          count++;
        }
      }

      if (count === 0) {
        resolve(isDark ? '#4cc2ff' : '#005fb8');
        return;
      }

      const avgR = Math.round(rSum / count);
      const avgG = Math.round(gSum / count);
      const avgB = Math.round(bSum / count);

      const toHex = (n: number) => n.toString(16).padStart(2, '0');
      resolve(`#${toHex(avgR)}${toHex(avgG)}${toHex(avgB)}`);
    };

    img.onerror = () => {
      URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
      resolve(isDark ? '#4cc2ff' : '#005fb8');
    };
  });
}

/**
 * Hook to get or extract cover color for a book.
 */
export function useCoverColor(bookId: string | undefined) {
  const { resolvedTheme } = useTheme();
  const [accent, setAccent] = useState<string | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!bookId) return;
    let alive = true;

    void db.books.get(bookId).then(async (book) => {
      if (!book || !alive) return;
      if (book.meta.accent) {
        setAccent(book.meta.accent);
        return;
      }

      if (book.cover_hash) {
        const cover = await db.covers.get(book.id);
        if (cover && alive) {
          const color = await extractCoverColor(cover.blob, resolvedTheme === 'dark');
          if (alive) {
            setAccent(color);
            // Cache in book.meta.accent
            await db.books.update(bookId, {
              meta: { ...book.meta, accent: color },
            });
          }
        }
      }
    });

    return () => {
      alive = false;
    };
  }, [bookId, resolvedTheme]);

  return accent;
}

File: apps/client/src/features/downloads/manager.ts
================
import { db, dirty, type DownloadItem } from '../../db';
import { importOne } from '../../lib/importBook';
import type { CatalogEntry, CatalogSource } from '../../sources/types';

class DownloadManager {
  private activeControllers = new Map<string, AbortController>();

  async enqueue(
    entry: CatalogEntry,
    source: CatalogSource,
    formatMime = 'application/epub+zip',
  ): Promise<string> {
    const downloadId = `dl:${entry.id}`;
    // `formatMime` may also be a specific format URL (e.g. one edition of several).
    const format =
      entry.formats?.find((f) => f.url === formatMime) ||
      entry.formats?.find((f) => f.mime === formatMime) ||
      entry.formats?.[0];
    const url = format?.url || '';

    const existing = await db.downloads.get(downloadId);
    if (existing && (existing.status === 'downloading' || existing.status === 'completed')) {
      return downloadId;
    }

    const item: DownloadItem = {
      id: downloadId,
      kind: entry.kind === 'manga' ? 'manga' : 'book',
      source: source.id,
      title: entry.title,
      url,
      status: 'pending',
      progress: 0,
      created_at: Date.now(),
      entry: entry as unknown as Record<string, unknown>,
    };

    await db.downloads.put(item);
    void this.process(downloadId, entry, source, formatMime);
    return downloadId;
  }

  async process(
    downloadId: string,
    entry: CatalogEntry,
    source: CatalogSource,
    formatMime: string,
  ): Promise<void> {
    const abortCtrl = new AbortController();
    this.activeControllers.set(downloadId, abortCtrl);

    try {
      await db.downloads.update(downloadId, {
        status: 'downloading',
        progress: 0.1,
      });

      if (!source.download) {
        throw new Error(`Source "${source.name}" does not support downloads.`);
      }

      const file = await source.download(entry, formatMime, async (p) => {
        await db.downloads.update(downloadId, {
          progress: Math.min(0.9, Math.max(0.1, p)),
        });
      });

      // Import downloaded file into library
      try {
        await importOne(file);
      } catch (err) {
        console.warn('Import failed for downloaded file:', err);
      }

      // Tag imported book with meta.source
      const targetBook = await db.books
        .filter(
          (b) =>
            !b.deleted_at &&
            (b.title.toLowerCase() === entry.title.toLowerCase() ||
              b.file_size === file.size),
        )
        .first();

      if (targetBook) {
        await db.books.update(targetBook.id, {
          meta: {
            ...targetBook.meta,
            source: {
              kind: source.id,
              id: entry.id,
              url: entry.formats?.[0]?.url,
            },
          },
          ...dirty(),
        });
      }

      await db.downloads.update(downloadId, {
        status: 'completed',
        progress: 1.0,
      });
    } catch (err) {
      if (abortCtrl.signal.aborted) {
        await db.downloads.update(downloadId, {
          status: 'cancelled',
          error: 'Download cancelled',
        });
      } else {
        const msg = err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err);
        await db.downloads.update(downloadId, {
          status: 'failed',
          error: msg,
        });
      }
    } finally {
      this.activeControllers.delete(downloadId);
    }
  }

  async cancel(downloadId: string): Promise<void> {
    const ctrl = this.activeControllers.get(downloadId);
    if (ctrl) {
      ctrl.abort();
    }
    await db.downloads.update(downloadId, {
      status: 'cancelled',
      error: 'Download cancelled',
    });
  }

  async retry(
    downloadId: string,
    entry?: CatalogEntry,
    source?: CatalogSource,
  ): Promise<void> {
    const item = await db.downloads.get(downloadId);
    if (!item) return;

    let targetSource = source;
    if (!targetSource) {
      const { getSourceById } = await import('../../sources/registry');
      targetSource = await getSourceById(item.source);
    }
    if (!targetSource) {
      await db.downloads.update(downloadId, {
        status: 'failed',
        error: `Unknown source: ${item.source}`,
      });
      return;
    }

    const targetEntry = entry || (item.entry as unknown as CatalogEntry) || {
      id: downloadId.replace(/^dl:/, ''),
      title: item.title,
      authors: [],
      formats: [{ mime: 'application/epub+zip', url: item.url }],
      kind: item.kind,
    };

    await db.downloads.update(downloadId, {
      status: 'pending',
      error: undefined,
      progress: 0,
    });
    void this.process(downloadId, targetEntry, targetSource, 'application/epub+zip');
  }

  async remove(downloadId: string): Promise<void> {
    this.cancel(downloadId);
    await db.downloads.delete(downloadId);
  }

  async clearCompleted(): Promise<void> {
    const completed = await db.downloads
      .filter((d) => d.status === 'completed' || d.status === 'cancelled')
      .toArray();
    for (const item of completed) {
      await db.downloads.delete(item.id);
    }
  }
}

export const downloadManager = new DownloadManager();

File: apps/client/src/lib/apiCache.ts

import { db } from '../db'

// Device-local cache for catalog API responses (db.kv is not synced). Fresh entries
// are returned without a request; a stale entry is returned if the live request
// hasn't answered within `staleWaitMs`, and the live answer still refreshes it.
const PREFIX = 'cache:'

export interface CacheOptions {
  freshMs: number
  staleWaitMs?: number
}

export async function cachedJson<T>(key: string, load: () => Promise<T>, opts: CacheOptions): Promise<T> {
  const k = PREFIX + key
  const hit = (await db.kv.get(k).catch(() => undefined))?.value as { at: number; data: T } | undefined
  const isEmpty = Array.isArray(hit?.data) && hit.data.length === 0
  if (hit && !isEmpty && Date.now() - hit.at < opts.freshMs) return hit.data

  const live = load().then((data) => {
    if (!Array.isArray(data) || data.length > 0) {
      void db.kv.put({ key: k, value: { at: Date.now(), data } }).catch(() => {})
    }
    return data
  })
  if (!hit || isEmpty) return live
  return Promise.race([
    live,
    new Promise<T>((resolve) => setTimeout(() => resolve(hit.data), opts.staleWaitMs ?? 4000)),
  ]).catch(() => hit.data)
}

/** Removes every cached catalog response (and the older Gutendex-only keys). */
export async function clearApiCache(): Promise<number> {
  const keys = (await db.kv.toCollection().primaryKeys()) as string[]
  const stale = keys.filter((k) => k.startsWith(PREFIX) || k.startsWith('gutendex:'))
  await db.kv.bulkDelete(stale)
  return stale.length
}

File: apps/client/src/lib/backup.ts

import JSZip from 'jszip';
import { db, dirty, type Book, type Annotation, type Progress, type ReadingSession, type Series, type ChapterProgress, type SettingsRow } from '../db';
import { getBlobStore } from './blobStore';

export interface BackupMetadata {
  version: number;
  exported_at: number;
  app_version: string;
}

export interface ExportBackupOptions {
  excludeFiles?: boolean;
  excludeMangaPages?: boolean;
}

export interface ImportBackupResult {
  importedBooks: number;
  importedAnnotations: number;
  importedProgress: number;
  totalRows: number;
}

/**
 * Exports all Verso data and files into a .versobackup ZIP archive.
 */
export async function exportBackup(options?: ExportBackupOptions): Promise<Blob> {
  const zip = new JSZip();

  const metadata: BackupMetadata = {
    version: 1,
    exported_at: Date.now(),
    app_version: '0.1.0',
  };
  zip.file('metadata.json', JSON.stringify(metadata, null, 2));

  // Export database tables
  const dataFolder = zip.folder('data');
  if (dataFolder) {
    const books = await db.books.toArray();
    const progress = await db.progress.toArray();
    const annotations = await db.annotations.toArray();
    const sessions = await db.reading_sessions.toArray();
    const series = await db.series.toArray();
    const chapterProgress = await db.chapter_progress.toArray();
    const settings = await db.settings.toArray();

    dataFolder.file('books.json', JSON.stringify(books));
    dataFolder.file('progress.json', JSON.stringify(progress));
    dataFolder.file('annotations.json', JSON.stringify(annotations));
    dataFolder.file('sessions.json', JSON.stringify(sessions));
    dataFolder.file('series.json', JSON.stringify(series));
    dataFolder.file('chapter_progress.json', JSON.stringify(chapterProgress));
    dataFolder.file('settings.json', JSON.stringify(settings));
  }

  // Export raw book files
  if (!options?.excludeFiles) {
    const filesFolder = zip.folder('files');
    const coversFolder = zip.folder('covers');
    const blobStore = getBlobStore();

    const books = await db.books.filter((b) => !b.deleted_at).toArray();
    for (const b of books) {
      if (b.file_hash && filesFolder) {
        const fileBlob = await blobStore.get(b.file_hash);
        if (fileBlob) {
          const buffer = await fileBlob.arrayBuffer();
          filesFolder.file(b.file_hash, buffer);
        }
      }
      if (b.cover_hash && coversFolder) {
        const coverRow = await db.covers.get(b.cover_hash);
        if (coverRow?.blob) {
          const buffer = await coverRow.blob.arrayBuffer();
          coversFolder.file(b.cover_hash, buffer);
        }
      }
    }
  }

  return zip.generateAsync({ type: 'blob', compression: 'DEFLATE', compressionOptions: { level: 6 } });
}

/**
 * Restores a .foliobackup ZIP archive into the database using Last-Write-Wins (LWW).
 */
export async function importBackup(file: File | Blob): Promise<ImportBackupResult> {
  const zip = await JSZip.loadAsync(file);

  const metaFile = zip.file('metadata.json');
  if (!metaFile) {
    throw new Error('Invalid backup file: metadata.json missing');
  }

  let totalRows = 0;
  let importedBooks = 0;
  let importedAnnotations = 0;
  let importedProgress = 0;

  // 1. Restore Books
  const booksFile = zip.file('data/books.json');
  if (booksFile) {
    const text = await booksFile.async('text');
    const rows = JSON.parse(text) as Book[];
    for (const row of rows) {
      const local = await db.books.get(row.id);
      if (!local || row.updated_at >= local.updated_at) {
        await db.books.put({ ...row, ...dirty() });
        importedBooks++;
        totalRows++;
      }
    }
  }

  // 2. Restore Progress
  const progressFile = zip.file('data/progress.json');
  if (progressFile) {
    const text = await progressFile.async('text');
    const rows = JSON.parse(text) as Progress[];
    for (const row of rows) {
      const local = await db.progress.get(row.id);
      if (!local || row.updated_at >= local.updated_at) {
        await db.progress.put({ ...row, ...dirty() });
        importedProgress++;
        totalRows++;
      }
    }
  }

  // 3. Restore Annotations
  const annotationsFile = zip.file('data/annotations.json');
  if (annotationsFile) {
    const text = await annotationsFile.async('text');
    const rows = JSON.parse(text) as Annotation[];
    for (const row of rows) {
      const local = await db.annotations.get(row.id);
      if (!local || row.updated_at >= local.updated_at) {
        await db.annotations.put({ ...row, ...dirty() });
        importedAnnotations++;
        totalRows++;
      }
    }
  }

  // 4. Restore Sessions
  const sessionsFile = zip.file('data/sessions.json');
  if (sessionsFile) {
    const text = await sessionsFile.async('text');
    const rows = JSON.parse(text) as ReadingSession[];
    for (const row of rows) {
      const local = await db.reading_sessions.get(row.id);
      if (!local || row.updated_at >= local.updated_at) {
        await db.reading_sessions.put({ ...row, ...dirty() });
        totalRows++;
      }
    }
  }

  // 5. Restore Series & Manga
  const seriesFile = zip.file('data/series.json');
  if (seriesFile) {
    const text = await seriesFile.async('text');
    const rows = JSON.parse(text) as Series[];
    for (const row of rows) {
      const local = await db.series.get(row.id);
      if (!local || (row.updated_at ?? 0) >= (local.updated_at ?? 0)) {
        await db.series.put({ ...row, ...dirty() });
        totalRows++;
      }
    }
  }

  const cpFile = zip.file('data/chapter_progress.json');
  if (cpFile) {
    const text = await cpFile.async('text');
    const rows = JSON.parse(text) as ChapterProgress[];
    for (const row of rows) {
      const local = await db.chapter_progress.get(row.id);
      if (!local || (row.updated_at ?? 0) > (local.updated_at ?? 0)) {
        await db.chapter_progress.put({ ...row, ...dirty() });
        totalRows++;
      }
    }
  }

  // 6. Restore Settings
  const settingsFile = zip.file('data/settings.json');
  if (settingsFile) {
    const text = await settingsFile.async('text');
    const rows = JSON.parse(text) as SettingsRow[];
    for (const row of rows) {
      const local = await db.settings.get(row.id);
      if (!local || row.updated_at > local.updated_at) {
        await db.settings.put({ ...row, ...dirty() });
        totalRows++;
      }
    }
  }

  // 7. Restore Files into BlobStore
  const blobStore = getBlobStore();
  const fileEntries = zip.filter((path) => path.startsWith('files/') && !path.endsWith('/'));
  for (const entry of fileEntries) {
    const hash = entry.name.replace('files/', '');
    if (hash) {
      const buffer = await entry.async('arraybuffer');
      const blob = new Blob([buffer]);
      await blobStore.put(hash, blob);
    }
  }

  // 8. Restore Covers
  const coverEntries = zip.filter((path) => path.startsWith('covers/') && !path.endsWith('/'));
  for (const entry of coverEntries) {
    const id = entry.name.replace('covers/', '');
    if (id) {
      const buffer = await entry.async('arraybuffer');
      const blob = new Blob([buffer]);
      await db.covers.put({ id, blob });
    }
  }

  return {
    importedBooks,
    importedAnnotations,
    importedProgress,
    totalRows,
  };
}

/**
 * Initiates browser download of an exported backup blob.
 */
export function downloadBackupBlob(blob: Blob, filename?: string): void {
  const d = new Date();
  const dateStr = `${d.getFullYear()}-${String(d.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, '0')}-${String(d.getDate()).padStart(2, '0')}`;
  const name = filename || `verso_backup_${dateStr}.versobackup`;

  const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
  const a = document.createElement('a');
  a.href = url;
  a.download = name;
  document.body.appendChild(a);
  a.click();
  document.body.removeChild(a);
  URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
}

================
File: apps/client/src/lib/blobStore.ts
================
import { db } from '../db';
import { isTauri } from './tauri';

export interface BlobStore {
  get(hash: string): Promise<Blob | null>;
  put(hash: string, blob: Blob): Promise<void>;
  has(hash: string): Promise<boolean>;
  delete(hash: string): Promise<void>;
  clear(): Promise<void>;
}

/**
 * Web & fallback implementation backed by Dexie IndexedDB (`db.files`).
 */
export class IndexedDbBlobStore implements BlobStore {
  async get(hash: string): Promise<Blob | null> {
    const row = await db.files.get(hash);
    return row?.blob ?? null;
  }

  async put(hash: string, blob: Blob): Promise<void> {
    await db.files.put({ hash, blob });
  }

  async has(hash: string): Promise<boolean> {
    const count = await db.files.where('hash').equals(hash).count();
    return count > 0;
  }

  async delete(hash: string): Promise<void> {
    await db.files.delete(hash);
  }

  async clear(): Promise<void> {
    await db.files.clear();
  }
}

/**
 * Desktop filesystem implementation backed by Tauri `@tauri-apps/plugin-fs`.
 * Stores files under `$APPDATA/Verso/files/<hash>`.
 */
export class FsBlobStore implements BlobStore {
  private fallback = new IndexedDbBlobStore();
  private baseDirPromise: Promise<string> | null = null;

  private async getFilesDir(): Promise<string> {
    if (!this.baseDirPromise) {
      this.baseDirPromise = (async () => {
        try {
          const { appDataDir, join } = await import('@tauri-apps/api/path');
          const { mkdir, exists } = await import('@tauri-apps/plugin-fs');
          const appData = await appDataDir();
          const versoDir = await join(appData, 'Verso', 'files');
          const folioDir = await join(appData, 'Folio', 'files');
          const versoExists = await exists(versoDir);
          if (versoExists) return versoDir;
          const folioExists = await exists(folioDir);
          if (folioExists) return folioDir;
          await mkdir(versoDir, { recursive: true });
          return versoDir;
        } catch {
          return '';
        }
      })();
    }
    return this.baseDirPromise;
  }

  private async getFilePath(hash: string): Promise<string> {
    const dir = await this.getFilesDir();
    if (!dir) return '';
    const { join } = await import('@tauri-apps/api/path');
    return await join(dir, hash);
  }

  async get(hash: string): Promise<Blob | null> {
    try {
      const path = await this.getFilePath(hash);
      if (!path) return await this.fallback.get(hash);

      const { readFile, exists } = await import('@tauri-apps/plugin-fs');
      if (!(await exists(path))) {
        // Fallback to IndexedDB if not yet migrated or stored there
        return await this.fallback.get(hash);
      }
      const data = await readFile(path);
      return new Blob([data]);
    } catch {
      return await this.fallback.get(hash);
    }
  }

  async put(hash: string, blob: Blob): Promise<void> {
    try {
      const path = await this.getFilePath(hash);
      if (!path) {
        await this.fallback.put(hash, blob);
        return;
      }

      const { writeFile } = await import('@tauri-apps/plugin-fs');
      const buffer = await blob.arrayBuffer();
      const uint8 = new Uint8Array(buffer);
      await writeFile(path, uint8);
    } catch {
      await this.fallback.put(hash, blob);
    }
  }

  async has(hash: string): Promise<boolean> {
    try {
      const path = await this.getFilePath(hash);
      if (!path) return await this.fallback.has(hash);

      const { exists } = await import('@tauri-apps/plugin-fs');
      const inFs = await exists(path);
      if (inFs) return true;
      return await this.fallback.has(hash);
    } catch {
      return await this.fallback.has(hash);
    }
  }

  async delete(hash: string): Promise<void> {
    try {
      const path = await this.getFilePath(hash);
      if (path) {
        const { remove, exists } = await import('@tauri-apps/plugin-fs');
        if (await exists(path)) {
          await remove(path);
        }
      }
    } catch {
      // Ignore
    }
    await this.fallback.delete(hash);
  }

  async clear(): Promise<void> {
    await this.fallback.clear();
  }
}

let storeSingleton: BlobStore | null = null;

/**
 * Returns the active BlobStore for the current runtime environment.
 */
export function getBlobStore(): BlobStore {
  if (!storeSingleton) {
    if (isTauri()) {
      storeSingleton = new FsBlobStore();
    } else {
      storeSingleton = new IndexedDbBlobStore();
    }
  }
  return storeSingleton;
}

export async function getFileBlob(hash: string): Promise<Blob | null> {
  return await getBlobStore().get(hash);
}

export async function saveFileBlob(hash: string, blob: Blob): Promise<void> {
  await getBlobStore().put(hash, blob);
}

export async function deleteFileBlob(hash: string): Promise<void> {
  await getBlobStore().delete(hash);
}

/**
 * Migrates existing IndexedDB blobs to filesystem storage on first launch.
 */
export async function migrateBlobsToFs(
  onProgress?: (done: number, total: number) => void,
): Promise<{ migrated: number; total: number }> {
  if (!isTauri()) return { migrated: 0, total: 0 };

  const fsStore = new FsBlobStore();
  const allRows = await db.files.toArray();
  const total = allRows.length;
  let migrated = 0;

  for (const row of allRows) {
    const alreadyInFs = await fsStore.has(row.hash);
    if (!alreadyInFs && row.blob) {
      await fsStore.put(row.hash, row.blob);
      migrated++;
    }
    onProgress?.(migrated, total);
  }

  return { migrated, total };
}
