import { useMemo } from 'react'
import { useLiveQuery } from 'dexie-react-hooks'
import { db, dirty, type CollectionBook } from '../db'

export const FAVORITES = 'favorites'

export function useFavoriteIds(): Set<string> {
  const rows = useLiveQuery(
    () => db.collection_books.where('collection_id').equals(FAVORITES).filter((r) => !r.deleted_at).toArray(),
    [],
    [] as CollectionBook[],
  )
  return useMemo(() => new Set(rows.map((r) => r.book_id)), [rows])
}

export async function setFavorite(bookId: string, favorite: boolean) {
  await db.collection_books.put({
    id: `fav:${bookId}`,
    collection_id: FAVORITES,
    book_id: bookId,
    deleted_at: favorite ? null : Date.now(),
    ...dirty(),
  })
}

export async function toggleFavorite(bookId: string, current?: boolean) {
  if (current !== undefined) {
    await setFavorite(bookId, !current)
  } else {
    const existing = await db.collection_books.get(`fav:${bookId}`)
    const isFav = !!existing && !existing.deleted_at
    await setFavorite(bookId, !isFav)
  }
}

File: apps/client/src/lib/format.ts

export function fmtBytes(n: number): string {
  if (n < 1024) return `${n} B`
  if (n < 1024 ** 2) return `${(n / 1024).toFixed(0)} KB`
  return `${(n / 1024 ** 2).toFixed(1)} MB`
}

export function fmtMinutes(min: number): string {
  const m = Math.round(min)
  if (m < 60) return `${m} min`
  const h = Math.floor(m / 60)
  return `${h} h ${m % 60} min`
}

export function fmtDate(ms: number): string {
  return new Date(ms).toLocaleDateString(undefined, { day: 'numeric', month: 'short', year: 'numeric' })
}

export function fmtRelativeTime(ms: number | null): string {
  if (!ms) return 'Never'
  const diffSec = Math.floor((Date.now() - ms) / 1000)
  if (diffSec < 10) return 'Just now'
  if (diffSec < 60) return `${diffSec}s ago`
  const diffMin = Math.floor(diffSec / 60)
  if (diffMin < 60) return `${diffMin}m ago`
  const diffHr = Math.floor(diffMin / 60)
  if (diffHr < 24) return `${diffHr}h ago`
  return fmtDate(ms)
}

  return RTL_LANGUAGES.some((code) => lang.toLowerCase().startsWith(code));

export function updateDocumentDirection(lang: string): void {
  if (typeof document === 'undefined') return;
  const isRtl = isRtlLanguage(lang);
  document.documentElement.dir = isRtl ? 'rtl' : 'ltr';
  document.documentElement.lang = lang;
}

export interface DictSense { html: string; examples: string[] }
export interface DictGroup { partOfSpeech: string; senses: DictSense[] }
export interface DictResult {
  word: string
  phonetic?: string
  audio?: string
  groups: DictGroup[]
  source: 'Wiktionary' | 'Free Dictionary'
}
export interface WikiResult { title: string; extract: string; thumbnail?: string; url: string }

export const LANGUAGES: [string, string][] = [
  ['hi', 'Hindi'], ['en', 'English'], ['bn', 'Bengali'], ['ta', 'Tamil'], ['te', 'Telugu'],
  ['mr', 'Marathi'], ['gu', 'Gujarati'], ['kn', 'Kannada'], ['ml', 'Malayalam'], ['pa', 'Punjabi'],
  ['ur', 'Urdu'], ['es', 'Spanish'], ['fr', 'French'], ['de', 'German'], ['it', 'Italian'],
  ['pt', 'Portuguese'], ['ru', 'Russian'], ['ja', 'Japanese'], ['ko', 'Korean'],
  ['zh-CN', 'Chinese (Simplified)'], ['ar', 'Arabic'],
]

export const langName = (code: string) => LANGUAGES.find(([c]) => c === code)?.[1] ?? code.toUpperCase()
export const baseLang = (lang?: string) => (lang || 'en').toLowerCase().split(/[-_]/)[0] || 'en'
export const cleanWord = (s: string) => s.trim().replace(/^[^\p{L}\p{N}]+|[^\p{L}\p{N}]+$/gu, '')

const cache = new Map<string, Promise<unknown>>()
function cached<T>(key: string, fn: () => Promise<T>): Promise<T> {
  if (!cache.has(key)) cache.set(key, fn().catch((e) => { cache.delete(key); throw e }))
  return cache.get(key) as Promise<T>
}

/** fetch + JSON with a hard timeout, so a hung service fails fast instead of spinning forever. */
async function getJson<T>(url: string, timeoutMs: number): Promise<{ ok: boolean; status: number; data?: T }> {
  const r = await fetch(url, { signal: AbortSignal.timeout(timeoutMs) })
  if (!r.ok) return { ok: false, status: r.status }
  return { ok: true, status: r.status, data: (await r.json()) as T }
}

const escapeHtml = (s: string) => s.replace(/&/g, '&amp;').replace(/</g, '&lt;').replace(/>/g, '&gt;')

type WiktionaryResponse = Record<string, {
  partOfSpeech: string
  definitions: { definition: string; examples?: string[] }[]
}[]>

async function wiktionary(word: string, lang: string): Promise<DictResult | null> {
  // A capitalised word (e.g. at the start of a sentence) usually only has an entry in
  // lower case. Ask for both spellings at once instead of waiting for a 404 first.
  const terms = [...new Set([word, word.toLowerCase()])]
  const responses = await Promise.all(
    terms.map((term) =>
      getJson<WiktionaryResponse>(
        `https://en.wiktionary.org/api/rest_v1/page/definition/${encodeURIComponent(term)}`,
        8000,
      ).catch(() => null),
    ),
  )
  for (const [i, term] of terms.entries()) {
    const data = responses[i]?.data
    if (!data) continue
    const list = data[lang] ?? data.en ?? Object.values(data)[0]
    if (!list?.length) continue
    const groups = list
      .map((g) => ({
        partOfSpeech: g.partOfSpeech,
        senses: g.definitions
          .filter((d) => d.definition?.trim())
          .slice(0, 6)
          .map((d) => ({ html: d.definition, examples: (d.examples ?? []).slice(0, 1) })),
      }))
      .filter((g) => g.senses.length)
    if (groups.length) return { word: term, groups, source: 'Wiktionary' }
  }
  return null
}

// dictionaryapi.dev regularly goes down by hanging ~20s and then returning 522. After
// one timeout/5xx, skip it for a while instead of making every lookup wait on it again.
const FREE_DICT_COOLDOWN_MS = 10 * 60_000
let freeDictDownUntil = 0

interface FreeDictEntry {
  word: string
  phonetic?: string
  phonetics?: { text?: string; audio?: string }[]
  meanings?: { partOfSpeech: string; definitions: { definition: string; example?: string }[] }[]
}

async function freeDictionary(word: string): Promise<DictResult | null> {
  if (Date.now() < freeDictDownUntil) return null
  let res: Awaited<ReturnType<typeof getJson<FreeDictEntry[]>>>
  try {
    res = await getJson<FreeDictEntry[]>(
      `https://api.dictionaryapi.dev/api/v2/entries/en/${encodeURIComponent(word.toLowerCase())}`,
      5000,
    )
  } catch {
    freeDictDownUntil = Date.now() + FREE_DICT_COOLDOWN_MS
    return null
  }
  if (res.status >= 500) freeDictDownUntil = Date.now() + FREE_DICT_COOLDOWN_MS
  if (!res.data) return null
  const data = res.data
  let phonetic: string | undefined
  let audio: string | undefined
  const groups: DictGroup[] = []
  for (const e of data) {
    phonetic ??= e.phonetic || e.phonetics?.find((p) => p.text)?.text
    audio ??= e.phonetics?.find((p) => p.audio)?.audio || undefined
    for (const m of e.meanings ?? []) {
      groups.push({
        partOfSpeech: m.partOfSpeech,
        senses: m.definitions.slice(0, 5).map((d) => ({
          html: escapeHtml(d.definition),
          examples: d.example ? [escapeHtml(d.example)] : [],
        })),
      })
    }
  }
  return groups.length ? { word: data[0]?.word ?? word, phonetic, audio, groups, source: 'Free Dictionary' } : null
}

const freeDictCached = (w: string) =>
  cached(`fd:${w.toLowerCase()}`, () => freeDictionary(w).catch(() => null))

/**
 * Definitions. Resolves as soon as Wiktionary answers; it no longer waits for the
 * Free Dictionary request (which only adds pronunciation and can take ~20s when that
 * service is struggling). Free Dictionary is awaited only as a fallback when
 * Wiktionary has nothing. Use `pronunciation()` to fill in phonetics/audio later.
 */
export function define(word: string, lang?: string): Promise<DictResult | null> {
  const w = cleanWord(word)
  const l = baseLang(lang)
  return cached(`d:${l}:${w}`, async () => {
    const fd = l === 'en' ? freeDictCached(w) : Promise.resolve(null)
    const wk = await wiktionary(w, l).catch(() => null)
    if (wk) return wk
    return fd
  })
}

/** Phonetic spelling and audio for an English word (may resolve after the definition). */
export function pronunciation(word: string, lang?: string): Promise<{ phonetic?: string; audio?: string } | null> {
  if (baseLang(lang) !== 'en') return Promise.resolve(null)
  return freeDictCached(cleanWord(word)).then((fd) =>
    fd && (fd.phonetic || fd.audio) ? { phonetic: fd.phonetic, audio: fd.audio } : null,
  )
}

export function wikipedia(term: string, lang?: string): Promise<WikiResult | null> {
  const l = baseLang(lang)
  const title = cleanWord(term).replace(/\s+/g, '_')
  return cached(`w:${l}:${title}`, async () => {
    const { data: d } = await getJson<{
      title: string
      extract?: string
      thumbnail?: { source: string }
      content_urls?: { desktop?: { page?: string } }
    }>(`https://${l}.wikipedia.org/api/rest_v1/page/summary/${encodeURIComponent(title)}?redirect=true`, 8000)
    if (!d) return null
    if (!d.extract) return null
    return {
      title: d.title,
      extract: d.extract,
      thumbnail: d.thumbnail?.source,
      url: d.content_urls?.desktop?.page ?? `https://${l}.wikipedia.org/wiki/${encodeURIComponent(title)}`,
    }
  })
}

function decodeEntities(s: string) {
  const t = document.createElement('textarea')
  t.innerHTML = s
  return t.value
}

export function translate(text: string, from: string, to: string): Promise<string> {
  const src = baseLang(from)
  if (src === baseLang(to)) return Promise.resolve(text)
  const q = text.trim().slice(0, 500)
  return cached(`t:${src}:${to}:${q}`, async () => {
    const r = await fetch(
      `https://api.mymemory.translated.net/get?q=${encodeURIComponent(q)}&langpair=${encodeURIComponent(`${src}|${to}`)}`,
      { signal: AbortSignal.timeout(10000) },
    )
    if (!r.ok) throw new Error(`Translation service error (${r.status})`)
    const d = await r.json()
    const out: string = d?.responseData?.translatedText ?? ''
    if (String(d?.responseStatus) !== '200' || /MYMEMORY WARNING|LIMIT/i.test(out)) {
      throw new Error(out ? decodeEntities(out) : 'Translation is unavailable right now.')
    }
    return decodeEntities(out)
  })
}

================
File: apps/client/src/lib/series.ts
================
import { db, dirty, now, type Series } from '../db'
import type { CatalogEntry } from '../sources/types'

/**
 * A series row means "Verso knows this series" (it was followed or read). Following
 * is `meta.followed`; rows written before that flag existed were only ever created
 * by following, so a missing flag counts as followed.
 */
export function isFollowedSeries(s: Series | null | undefined): boolean {
  return !!s && !s.deleted_at && s.meta?.followed !== false
}

/**
 * Records that a series was read just now, creating its row (not followed) if
 * needed, so it shows up in reading history even when it isn't followed.
 */
export async function recordSeriesRead(
  seriesId: string,
  source: string,
  loadEntry: () => Promise<CatalogEntry | null>,
): Promise<void> {
  const existing = await db.series.get(seriesId)
  if (existing) {
    await db.series.update(seriesId, {
      last_read_at: now(),
      // A previously unfollowed (soft-deleted) series comes back as history only.
      ...(existing.deleted_at ? { deleted_at: null, meta: { ...existing.meta, followed: false } } : {}),
      ...dirty(),
    })
    return
  }
  const entry = await loadEntry().catch(() => null)
  const row: Series = {
    id: seriesId,
    source,
    source_id: seriesId.replace(/^[^:]+:/, ''),
    title: entry?.title ?? 'Unknown series',
    author: entry?.authors.join(', ') || '',
    cover_url: entry?.cover,
    meta: { followed: false },
    added_at: now(),
    last_read_at: now(),
    deleted_at: null,
    ...dirty(),
  }
  // Another write may have created it while details were loading.
  if (!(await db.series.get(seriesId))) await db.series.put(row)
}

/** Follow or unfollow, keeping the row (and so the reading history) either way. */
export async function setSeriesFollowed(
  seriesId: string,
  source: string,
  entry: CatalogEntry,
  followed: boolean,
): Promise<void> {
  const existing = await db.series.get(seriesId)
  const details = {
    title: entry.title,
    author: entry.authors.join(', ') || 'Unknown',
    cover_url: entry.cover,
  }
  if (existing) {
    await db.series.update(seriesId, {
      ...details,
      deleted_at: null,
      meta: { ...existing.meta, followed },
      ...(followed ? { last_checked_at: now() } : {}),
      ...dirty(),
    })
    return
  }
  await db.series.put({
    id: seriesId,
    source,
    source_id: seriesId.replace(/^[^:]+:/, ''),
    ...details,
    meta: { followed },
    added_at: now(),
    last_checked_at: now(),
    deleted_at: null,
    ...dirty(),
  })
}

export interface MangaBookmark {
  chapterId: string
  chapterTitle: string
  page: number
  createdAt: number
}

export function getMangaBookmarks(s: Series | null | undefined): MangaBookmark[] {
  const list = s?.meta?.bookmarks
  return Array.isArray(list) ? (list as MangaBookmark[]) : []
}

export function isMangaPageBookmarked(s: Series | null | undefined, chapterId: string, page: number): boolean {
  return getMangaBookmarks(s).some((b) => b.chapterId === chapterId && b.page === page)
}

/** Adds or removes a page bookmark. Bookmarks sync with the series row. */
export async function toggleMangaBookmark(
  seriesId: string,
  source: string,
  mark: Omit<MangaBookmark, 'createdAt'>,
): Promise<void> {
  // A bookmark implies the series is in history; make sure the row exists.
  if (!(await db.series.get(seriesId))) await recordSeriesRead(seriesId, source, async () => null)
  const row = await db.series.get(seriesId)
  if (!row) return
  const list = getMangaBookmarks(row)
  const exists = list.some((b) => b.chapterId === mark.chapterId && b.page === mark.page)
  const next = exists
    ? list.filter((b) => !(b.chapterId === mark.chapterId && b.page === mark.page))
    : [...list, { ...mark, createdAt: now() }]
  await db.series.update(seriesId, { meta: { ...row.meta, bookmarks: next }, ...dirty() })
}

/**
 * Clears manga reading history: series that were only read (not in the library)
 * are removed, library series keep their place but lose their "last read" time.
 * Chapter read marks are kept so unread counts stay correct.
 */
export async function clearMangaHistory(): Promise<number> {
  const rows = await db.series.filter((s) => !s.deleted_at && !!s.last_read_at).toArray()
  await db.transaction('rw', db.series, async () => {
    for (const s of rows) {
      if (isFollowedSeries(s)) await db.series.update(s.id, { last_read_at: undefined, ...dirty() })
      else await db.series.update(s.id, { deleted_at: now(), ...dirty() })
    }
  })
  return rows.length
}

/** Removes one series from reading history (a library series stays in the library). */
export async function removeFromMangaHistory(seriesId: string): Promise<void> {
  const s = await db.series.get(seriesId)
  if (!s) return
  if (isFollowedSeries(s)) await db.series.update(seriesId, { last_read_at: undefined, ...dirty() })
  else await db.series.update(seriesId, { deleted_at: now(), ...dirty() })
}

File: apps/client/src/lib/session.ts
================
import { useEffect, useRef, useCallback } from 'react';
import { db, deviceId, dirty, uid, type ReadingSession } from '../db';

export const IDLE_TIMEOUT_MS = 5 * 60 * 1000; // 5 minutes inactivity
export const MIN_SESSION_DURATION_SEC = 20; // Sessions < 20s are discarded
export const MAX_SESSION_DURATION_SEC = 4 * 3600; // Cap at 4 hours

export interface ReadingSessionOptions {
  kind?: 'book' | 'manga';
  pagesReadRef?: { current: number };
  idleTimeoutMs?: number;
}

export function useReadingSession(
  bookId: string,
  active: boolean,
  fractionRef: { current: number },
  wordCount?: number,
  options?: ReadingSessionOptions,
) {
  const kind = options?.kind ?? 'book';
  const idleTimeout = options?.idleTimeoutMs ?? IDLE_TIMEOUT_MS;
  const lastActiveRef = useRef<number>(0);
  const isIdleRef = useRef<boolean>(false);
  const startRef = useRef<number>(0);
  const startFracRef = useRef<number>(0);
  const startPagesRef = useRef<number>(0);

  const saveSession = useCallback(
    (startTime: number, endTime: number, startFrac: number, endFrac: number, pagesCount: number) => {
      const seconds = Math.round((endTime - startTime) / 1000);
      if (seconds < MIN_SESSION_DURATION_SEC || !bookId) return;

      const durationSec = Math.min(seconds, MAX_SESSION_DURATION_SEC);
      const wordsRead =
        kind === 'book'
          ? Math.max(0, Math.round((endFrac - startFrac) * (wordCount ?? 0)))
          : pagesCount;

      const session: ReadingSession = {
        id: uid(),
        book_id: bookId,
        started_at: startTime,
        ended_at: endTime,
        duration_sec: durationSec,
        start_percent: startFrac,
        end_percent: endFrac,
        words_read: wordsRead,
        device_id: deviceId(),
        kind,
        deleted_at: null,
        ...dirty(),
      };

      void db.reading_sessions.put(session);
    },
    [bookId, kind, wordCount],
  );

  const flush = useCallback(() => {
    if (startRef.current <= 0 || isIdleRef.current) return;
    const now = Date.now();
    const effectiveEndTime = Math.min(now, lastActiveRef.current > 0 ? lastActiveRef.current : now);
    const endFrac = fractionRef.current;
    const currentPages = options?.pagesReadRef?.current ?? 0;
    const pagesRead = Math.max(0, currentPages - startPagesRef.current);

    saveSession(startRef.current, effectiveEndTime, startFracRef.current, endFrac, pagesRead);

    // Reset session start tracking
    startRef.current = 0;
    isIdleRef.current = false;
  }, [fractionRef, options?.pagesReadRef, saveSession]);

  const recordActivity = useCallback(() => {
    const now = Date.now();
    lastActiveRef.current = now;

    if (isIdleRef.current || startRef.current === 0) {
      // User was idle or starting a new session block
      startRef.current = now;
      startFracRef.current = fractionRef.current;
      startPagesRef.current = options?.pagesReadRef?.current ?? 0;
      isIdleRef.current = false;
    }
  }, [fractionRef, options?.pagesReadRef]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!active) return;

    const now = Date.now();
    startRef.current = now;
    lastActiveRef.current = now;
    startFracRef.current = fractionRef.current;
    startPagesRef.current = options?.pagesReadRef?.current ?? 0;
    isIdleRef.current = false;

    // Check for idle every 15 seconds
    const checkInterval = window.setInterval(() => {
      if (isIdleRef.current || startRef.current === 0) return;
      const idleTime = Date.now() - lastActiveRef.current;
      if (idleTime >= idleTimeout) {
        // 5 min inactivity reached: end session at last activity time
        const endFrac = fractionRef.current;
        const currentPages = options?.pagesReadRef?.current ?? 0;
        const pagesRead = Math.max(0, currentPages - startPagesRef.current);

        saveSession(startRef.current, lastActiveRef.current, startFracRef.current, endFrac, pagesRead);

        isIdleRef.current = true;
        startRef.current = 0;
      }
    }, 15000);

    // Activity event listeners
    const handleActivity = () => {
      recordActivity();
    };

    window.addEventListener('pointerdown', handleActivity, { passive: true });
    window.addEventListener('keydown', handleActivity, { passive: true });
    window.addEventListener('wheel', handleActivity, { passive: true });
    window.addEventListener('touchstart', handleActivity, { passive: true });

    const onVisibility = () => {
      if (document.visibilityState === 'hidden') {
        flush();
      } else {
        recordActivity();
      }
    };

    document.addEventListener('visibilitychange', onVisibility);
    window.addEventListener('pagehide', flush);

    return () => {
      window.clearInterval(checkInterval);
      window.removeEventListener('pointerdown', handleActivity);
      window.removeEventListener('keydown', handleActivity);
      window.removeEventListener('wheel', handleActivity);
      window.removeEventListener('touchstart', handleActivity);
      document.removeEventListener('visibilitychange', onVisibility);
      window.removeEventListener('pagehide', flush);
      flush();
    };
  }, [active, bookId, flush, idleTimeout, options?.pagesReadRef, recordActivity, saveSession, fractionRef]);

  return { recordActivity, flush };
}

================
File: apps/client/src/lib/useWheelPaging.ts
================
import { useEffect, useRef } from 'react'
import type { FoliateView } from './foliate'
import type { ReaderSettings } from './readerSettings'

/** True if the wheel event is over app UI (not the book) that can scroll itself. */
function overScrollableUi(e: WheelEvent): boolean {
  const target = e.target as Element | null
  // Events from inside the book's iframe belong to the page itself.
  if (!target || target.ownerDocument !== document) return false
  for (let el: Element | null = target; el && el !== document.body; el = el.parentElement) {
    if (el.tagName === 'FOLIATE-VIEW') return false
    const cs = getComputedStyle(el)
    if (/(auto|scroll)/.test(cs.overflowY) && el.scrollHeight > el.clientHeight + 1) return true
    if (/(auto|scroll)/.test(cs.overflowX) && el.scrollWidth > el.clientWidth + 1 && Math.abs(e.deltaX) > Math.abs(e.deltaY)) return true
  }
  return false
}

export interface WheelPagingOptions {
  view: FoliateView | null
  settings: Pick<ReaderSettings, 'flow' | 'wheelPaging' | 'wheelInvert' | 'fontSize'>
  onFontSizeChange?: (newSize: number) => void
}

export function normalizeWheel(e: WheelEvent): { dx: number; dy: number } {
  let dx = e.deltaX
  let dy = e.deltaY
  if (e.deltaMode === 1) {
    // DOM_DELTA_LINE: typically ~16px per line
    dx *= 16
    dy *= 16
  } else if (e.deltaMode === 2) {
    // DOM_DELTA_PAGE: viewport height / width
    const vh = typeof window !== 'undefined' ? window.innerHeight || 800 : 800
    const vw = typeof window !== 'undefined' ? window.innerWidth || 1200 : 1200
    dx *= vw
    dy *= vh
  }
  return { dx, dy }
}

export class WheelPagingController {
  private accumulator = 0
  private lastEventTime = 0
  private cooldownUntil = 0
  private gestureConsumed = false
  private getOptions: () => {
    view: FoliateView | null
    settings: Pick<ReaderSettings, 'flow' | 'wheelPaging' | 'wheelInvert' | 'fontSize'>
    onFontSizeChange?: (newSize: number) => void
  }

  constructor(
    getOptions: () => {
      view: FoliateView | null
      settings: Pick<ReaderSettings, 'flow' | 'wheelPaging' | 'wheelInvert' | 'fontSize'>
      onFontSizeChange?: (newSize: number) => void
    },
  ) {
    this.getOptions = getOptions
  }

  handleWheel = (e: WheelEvent) => {
    const { view, settings, onFontSizeChange } = this.getOptions()

    // Ctrl + wheel: font size step ±1 (clamped 12–36), prevents browser zoom
    if (e.ctrlKey || e.metaKey) {
      e.preventDefault()
      if (onFontSizeChange) {
        const step = e.deltaY < 0 ? 1 : -1
        const next = Math.min(36, Math.max(12, settings.fontSize + step))
        if (next !== settings.fontSize) {
          onFontSizeChange(next)
        }
      }
      return
    }

    // Scrolled mode: native scrolling, no interception
    if (settings.flow !== 'paginated') {
      return
    }

    // The listener is on the whole window, so wheel over the dictionary card, the
    // contents panel or any other scrollable UI used to turn the page instead of
    // scrolling it. Leave those to native scrolling.
    if (overScrollableUi(e)) {
      return
    }

    // If wheel paging is disabled, leave native behavior
    if (!settings.wheelPaging) {
      return
    }

    // Paginated mode: prevent default scrolling / bouncing
    e.preventDefault()

    if (!view) return

    const now = Date.now()

    // Trackpad inertia / gesture gap: treat a gap of > 120 ms as a new gesture
    if (now - this.lastEventTime > 120) {
      this.accumulator = 0
      this.gestureConsumed = false
    }
    this.lastEventTime = now

    // If the current gesture has already turned a page, ignore trailing inertia events
    if (this.gestureConsumed) {
      return
    }

    // During cooldown period (280 ms), ignore events
    if (now < this.cooldownUntil) {
      return
    }

    const { dx, dy } = normalizeWheel(e)
    const effectiveDelta = (settings.wheelInvert ? -1 : 1) * (dy + dx)

    this.accumulator += effectiveDelta

    if (Math.abs(this.accumulator) >= 60) {
      if (this.accumulator > 0) {
        view.goRight()
      } else {
        view.goLeft()
      }
      this.accumulator = 0
      this.cooldownUntil = now + 280
      this.gestureConsumed = true
    }
  }

  reset() {
    this.accumulator = 0
    this.lastEventTime = 0
    this.cooldownUntil = 0
    this.gestureConsumed = false
  }
}

export function useWheelPaging({ view, settings, onFontSizeChange }: WheelPagingOptions) {
  const optionsRef = useRef({ view, settings, onFontSizeChange })
  useEffect(() => {
    optionsRef.current = { view, settings, onFontSizeChange }
  })

  const controllerRef = useRef<WheelPagingController | null>(null)
  if (!controllerRef.current) {
    controllerRef.current = new WheelPagingController(() => optionsRef.current)
  }

  const attachedDocsRef = useRef(new Set<Document>())

  // Attach to window
  useEffect(() => {
    const controller = controllerRef.current
    if (!controller) return

    const handleWheel = controller.handleWheel
    window.addEventListener('wheel', handleWheel, { passive: false })

    return () => {
      window.removeEventListener('wheel', handleWheel)
    }
  }, [])

  // Listen to foliate-view 'load' event to attach to each loaded section document
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!view) return
    const controller = controllerRef.current
    if (!controller) return

    const handleWheel = controller.handleWheel
    const docs = attachedDocsRef.current

    const attachDoc = (doc: Document) => {
      if (docs.has(doc)) return
      docs.add(doc)
      doc.addEventListener('wheel', handleWheel, { passive: false })
    }

    const onLoad = (e: Event) => {
      const detail = (e as CustomEvent).detail as { doc?: Document } | undefined
      if (detail?.doc) {
        attachDoc(detail.doc)
      }
    }

    view.addEventListener('load', onLoad)

    return () => {
      view.removeEventListener('load', onLoad)
      for (const doc of docs) {
        doc.removeEventListener('wheel', handleWheel)
      }
      docs.clear()
    }
  }, [view])

  return {
    attachDocument: (doc: Document) => {
      const controller = controllerRef.current
      if (controller && !attachedDocsRef.current.has(doc)) {
        attachedDocsRef.current.add(doc)
        doc.addEventListener('wheel', controller.handleWheel, { passive: false })
      }
    },
  }
}

import { useState, useMemo } from 'react';
import { useLiveQuery } from 'dexie-react-hooks';
import {
  BarChart2,
  Calendar,
  Clock,
  Download,
  Flame,
  Moon,
  Sun,
  Sunrise,
  Sunset,
  Target,
  Trophy,
  Zap,
  BookOpen,
  CheckCircle2,
  Sliders,
} from 'lucide-react';
import { db, type Book, type Series, type ReadingSession } from '../db';
import {
  computeReadingStats,
  exportSessionsToCsv,
  downloadCsvFile,
  type StatPeriod,
  type DailyBarItem,
  type HeatmapDay,
} from '../lib/stats-engine';
import { useReadingGoals } from '../lib/goals';
import { Tabs, TabsContent, type TabItem } from '../design/components/Tabs';
import { Button } from '../design/components/Button';
import { Dialog } from '../design/components/Dialog';
import { BookCover } from '../design/components/BookCover';
import { CircularProgress } from '../design/components/Progress';

const PERIOD_TABS: TabItem<StatPeriod>[] = [
  { value: 'week', label: 'Week' },
  { value: 'month', label: 'Month' },
  { value: 'year', label: 'Year' },
  { value: 'all', label: 'All time' },
];

export default function Stats() {
  const [period, setPeriod] = useState<StatPeriod>('week');
  const [showGoalsDialog, setShowGoalsDialog] = useState(false);
  const [hoveredBar, setHoveredBar] = useState<DailyBarItem | null>(null);
  const [hoveredHeatmap, setHoveredHeatmap] = useState<HeatmapDay | null>(null);

  // Queries
  const sessions = useLiveQuery(() => db.reading_sessions.toArray(), [], [] as ReadingSession[]);
  const books = useLiveQuery(() => db.books.filter((b) => !b.deleted_at).toArray(), [], [] as Book[]);
  const series = useLiveQuery(() => db.series.filter((s) => !s.deleted_at).toArray(), [], [] as Series[]);

  const { goals, updateGoals } = useReadingGoals();
  const [editDaily, setEditDaily] = useState<number>(goals.dailyMinutes);
  const [editYearly, setEditYearly] = useState<number>(goals.yearlyBooks);

  // Compute finished books
  const finishedBooks = useMemo(() => {
    return books.filter((b) => !!b.meta?.finished_at);
  }, [books]);

  const finishedBookIds = useMemo(() => {
    return new Set(finishedBooks.map((b) => b.id));
  }, [finishedBooks]);

  // Compute reading statistics
  const stats = useMemo(() => {
    return computeReadingStats(sessions, period, {
      books,
      series,
      finishedBookIds,
    });
  }, [sessions, period, books, series, finishedBookIds]);

  // Compute today's minutes for daily goal
  const todayStats = useMemo(() => {
    return computeReadingStats(sessions, 'week', { books, series });
  }, [sessions, books, series]);

  const todayMinutes = useMemo(() => {
    const todayBar = todayStats.dailyBarChart[todayStats.dailyBarChart.length - 1];
    return todayBar?.totalMinutes ?? 0;
  }, [todayStats]);

  const dailyGoalPercent = Math.min(100, Math.round((todayMinutes / Math.max(1, goals.dailyMinutes)) * 100));
  const yearlyGoalPercent = Math.min(
    100,
    Math.round((finishedBooks.length / Math.max(1, goals.yearlyBooks)) * 100),
  );

  // CSV export handler
  const handleExportCsv = () => {
    const booksMap = new Map(books.map((b) => [b.id, b]));
    const seriesMap = new Map(series.map((s) => [s.id, s]));
    const csv = exportSessionsToCsv(sessions, booksMap, seriesMap);
    const dateStr = new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0];
    downloadCsvFile(csv, `folio-reading-sessions-${dateStr}.csv`);
  };

  const handleSaveGoals = async () => {
    await updateGoals({ dailyMinutes: editDaily, yearlyBooks: editYearly });
    setShowGoalsDialog(false);
  };

  // Bar chart max height scale
  const maxBarMinutes = useMemo(() => {
    const max = Math.max(...stats.dailyBarChart.map((b) => b.totalMinutes), 1);
    return Math.ceil(max / 15) * 15 || 60;
  }, [stats.dailyBarChart]);

  return (
    <div className="flex-1 min-h-0 overflow-y-auto overflow-x-hidden">
      <div className="w-full max-w-6xl mx-auto px-4 sm:px-6 py-6 space-y-6 select-none">
        {/* Top Header & Actions */}
        <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-4">

            <h1 className="text-2xl sm:text-3xl font-display font-bold text-[var(--text-1)]">
              Reading Statistics
            </h1>
            <p className="text-sm text-[var(--text-2)] mt-0.5">
              Your reading habits, pace, streaks, and personal milestones
            </p>
          </div>

          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <Button
              variant="standard"
              size="sm"
              icon={<Sliders className="w-4 h-4" />}
              onClick={() => {
                setEditDaily(goals.dailyMinutes);
                setEditYearly(goals.yearlyBooks);
                setShowGoalsDialog(true);
              }}
            >
              Goals
            </Button>

            <Button
              variant="standard"
              size="sm"
              icon={<Download className="w-4 h-4" />}
              onClick={handleExportCsv}
            >
              Export CSV
            </Button>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Reading Goals Overview Card */}
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-4">
          {/* Daily Goal Card */}
          <div className="flex items-center gap-4 p-4 rounded-[var(--r-lg)] bg-[var(--layer-2)] border border-[var(--stroke)] shadow-xs">
            <CircularProgress
              value={dailyGoalPercent}
              size={56}
              strokeWidth={5}
              indicatorColor="var(--accent)"
            >
              <span className="text-xs font-bold text-[var(--text-1)]">{dailyGoalPercent}%</span>
            </CircularProgress>
            <div className="grow min-w-0">
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                <span className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-[var(--accent)] flex items-center gap-1.5">
                  <Target className="w-3.5 h-3.5" /> Daily Goal
                </span>
                <span className="text-xs text-[var(--text-3)] font-medium">
                  {todayMinutes} / {goals.dailyMinutes} min
                </span>
              </div>
              <p className="text-sm font-semibold text-[var(--text-1)] mt-1 truncate">
                {todayMinutes >= goals.dailyMinutes
                  ? 'Goal achieved today! 🎉'
                  : `${goals.dailyMinutes - todayMinutes} minutes left today`}
              </p>
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Yearly Goal Card */}
          <div className="flex items-center gap-4 p-4 rounded-[var(--r-lg)] bg-[var(--layer-2)] border border-[var(--stroke)] shadow-xs">
            <CircularProgress
              value={yearlyGoalPercent}
              size={56}
              strokeWidth={5}
              indicatorColor="var(--success)"
            >
              <span className="text-xs font-bold text-[var(--text-1)]">{yearlyGoalPercent}%</span>
            </CircularProgress>
            <div className="grow min-w-0">
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                <span className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-[var(--success)] flex items-center gap-1.5">
                  <Trophy className="w-3.5 h-3.5" /> Yearly Books Goal
                </span>

                  {finishedBooks.length} / {goals.yearlyBooks} books

                {finishedBooks.length >= goals.yearlyBooks

                  : `${goals.yearlyBooks - finishedBooks.length} books remaining this year`}
              </p>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Period Tabs */}
        <Tabs
          items={PERIOD_TABS}
          value={period}
          onValueChange={(val) => setPeriod(val)}
          className="w-full"
        >
          <TabsContent value={period} className="space-y-6 pt-4">
            {/* Key Metric Highlights Grid */}
            <div className="grid grid-cols-2 sm:grid-cols-3 lg:grid-cols-6 gap-3">
              {/* Total Time */}
              <div className="p-3.5 rounded-[var(--r-lg)] bg-[var(--layer-2)] border border-[var(--stroke)] shadow-xs">
                <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-[var(--accent)] text-xs font-medium">
                  <Clock className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                  <span>Reading Time</span>
                </div>
                <div className="text-xl sm:text-2xl font-bold text-[var(--text-1)] mt-1.5">
                  {stats.totalDurationFormatted}
                </div>
                <div className="text-[11px] text-[var(--text-3)] mt-0.5">Total duration</div>
              </div>

              {/* Daily Average */}
              <div className="p-3.5 rounded-[var(--r-lg)] bg-[var(--layer-2)] border border-[var(--stroke)] shadow-xs">
                <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-[var(--accent)] text-xs font-medium">
                  <Calendar className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                  <span>Daily Average</span>
                </div>

                  {stats.dailyAverageMinutes}m
                </div>
                <div className="text-[11px] text-[var(--text-3)] mt-0.5">Per day in period</div>
              </div>

              {/* Longest Session */}
              <div className="p-3.5 rounded-[var(--r-lg)] bg-[var(--layer-2)] border border-[var(--stroke)] shadow-xs">
                <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-[var(--accent)] text-xs font-medium">
                  <Zap className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                  <span>Longest Session</span>
                </div>
                <div className="text-xl sm:text-2xl font-bold text-[var(--text-1)] mt-1.5">
                  {stats.longestSessionFormatted}
                </div>
                <div className="text-[11px] text-[var(--text-3)] mt-0.5">Single session max</div>
              </div>

              {/* Reading Speed */}
              <div className="p-3.5 rounded-[var(--r-lg)] bg-[var(--layer-2)] border border-[var(--stroke)] shadow-xs">
                <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-[var(--accent)] text-xs font-medium">
                  <BookOpen className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                  <span>Reading Speed</span>
                </div>
                <div className="text-xl sm:text-2xl font-bold text-[var(--text-1)] mt-1.5">
                  {stats.bookSpeedWpm > 0 ? `${stats.bookSpeedWpm} wpm` : '—'}
                </div>
                <div className="text-[11px] text-[var(--text-3)] mt-0.5">
                  {stats.mangaSpeedPpm > 0 ? `${stats.mangaSpeedPpm} manga ppm` : 'Words / minute'}
                </div>
              </div>

              {/* Streaks */}
              <div className="p-3.5 rounded-[var(--r-lg)] bg-[var(--layer-2)] border border-[var(--stroke)] shadow-xs">
                <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-[var(--warning)] text-xs font-medium">
                  <Flame className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                  <span>Reading Streak</span>
                </div>
                <div className="text-xl sm:text-2xl font-bold text-[var(--text-1)] mt-1.5">
                  {stats.currentStreak} {stats.currentStreak === 1 ? 'day' : 'days'}
                </div>
                <div className="text-[11px] text-[var(--text-3)] mt-0.5">
                  Best: {stats.longestStreak} days
                </div>
              </div>

              {/* Books & Chapters Finished */}
              <div className="p-3.5 rounded-[var(--r-lg)] bg-[var(--layer-2)] border border-[var(--stroke)] shadow-xs">
                <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-[var(--success)] text-xs font-medium">
                  <CheckCircle2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                  <span>Finished</span>
                </div>
                <div className="text-xl sm:text-2xl font-bold text-[var(--text-1)] mt-1.5">
                  {stats.booksFinishedCount} books
                </div>
                <div className="text-[11px] text-[var(--text-3)] mt-0.5">
                  {stats.chaptersReadCount} manga chapters
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* Daily Reading Bar Chart */}
            <div className="p-5 rounded-[var(--r-xl)] bg-[var(--layer-2)] border border-[var(--stroke)] shadow-xs">
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-4">
                <div>
                  <h3 className="text-base font-bold text-[var(--text-1)] flex items-center gap-2">
                    <BarChart2 className="w-4 h-4 text-[var(--accent)]" />
                    Daily Reading Time
                  </h3>
                  <p className="text-xs text-[var(--text-3)] mt-0.5">
                    Minutes read each day across books and manga
                  </p>
                </div>

                {hoveredBar && (
                  <div className="text-xs font-medium px-2.5 py-1 rounded-[var(--r-md)] bg-[var(--layer-3)] border border-[var(--stroke)] text-[var(--text-1)]">
                    <span className="font-bold text-[var(--accent)]">{hoveredBar.totalMinutes} min</span> on{' '}
                    {hoveredBar.date}
                    {hoveredBar.mangaMinutes > 0 && (
                      <span className="text-[var(--text-3)] ml-1">
                        ({hoveredBar.bookMinutes}m books, {hoveredBar.mangaMinutes}m manga)
                      </span>
                    )}
                  </div>
                )}
              </div>

              {/* Responsive SVG Bar Chart */}
              <div className="w-full h-48 sm:h-56 relative flex items-end justify-between gap-1 pt-6 pb-6 px-1">
                {/* Horizontal Reference Lines */}
                <div className="absolute inset-0 top-6 bottom-6 flex flex-col justify-between pointer-events-none opacity-20">
                  <div className="border-b border-[var(--stroke)] w-full" />
                  <div className="border-b border-[var(--stroke)] w-full" />
                  <div className="border-b border-[var(--stroke)] w-full" />
                </div>

                {stats.dailyBarChart.map((bar) => {
                  const heightPct = Math.min(100, Math.round((bar.totalMinutes / maxBarMinutes) * 100));
                  const isHovered = hoveredBar?.date === bar.date;

                  return (
                    <div
                      key={bar.date}
                      className="flex-1 flex flex-col items-center h-full justify-end group cursor-pointer relative"
                      onMouseEnter={() => setHoveredBar(bar)}
                      onMouseLeave={() => setHoveredBar(null)}
                    >
                      {/* Bar Pill */}
                      <div className="w-full max-w-[32px] flex flex-col justify-end flex-1 min-h-0">
                        <div
                          className={`w-full rounded-t-md transition-all duration-200 ${
                            isHovered
                              ? 'bg-[var(--accent-hover)] shadow-sm'
                              : bar.totalMinutes > 0
                                ? 'bg-[var(--accent)]'
                                : 'bg-[var(--layer-3)]'
                          }`}
                          style={{
                            height: `${Math.max(4, heightPct)}%`,
                          }}
                        />
                      </div>

                      {/* X-axis Label */}
                      <span
                        className={`text-[10px] leading-3 mt-2 shrink-0 max-w-full truncate transition-colors ${
                          isHovered ? 'text-[var(--accent)] font-bold' : 'text-[var(--text-3)]'
                        }`}
                      >
                        {bar.label}
                      </span>
                    </div>
                  );
                })}
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* GitHub-style Calendar Heatmap */}
            <div className="p-5 rounded-[var(--r-xl)] bg-[var(--layer-2)] border border-[var(--stroke)] shadow-xs overflow-hidden">
              <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-2 mb-4">
                <div>
                  <h3 className="text-base font-bold text-[var(--text-1)] flex items-center gap-2">
                    <Calendar className="w-4 h-4 text-[var(--accent)]" />
                    Reading Activity Heatmap
                  </h3>
                  <p className="text-xs text-[var(--text-3)] mt-0.5">
                    Consistent reading across the past 52 weeks ({stats.currentStreak}-day streak)
                  </p>
                </div>

                <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs text-[var(--text-3)]">
                  <span>Less</span>
                  <div className="w-3 h-3 rounded-xs bg-[var(--layer-3)]" />
                  <div className="w-3 h-3 rounded-xs bg-[var(--accent-soft)]" />
                  <div className="w-3 h-3 rounded-xs bg-[var(--accent)] opacity-60" />
                  <div className="w-3 h-3 rounded-xs bg-[var(--accent)] opacity-80" />
                  <div className="w-3 h-3 rounded-xs bg-[var(--accent)]" />
                  <span>More</span>
                </div>
              </div>

              {/* Heatmap Grid */}
              <div className="overflow-x-auto pb-2 no-scrollbar">
                <div className="inline-grid grid-rows-7 grid-flow-col gap-1 min-w-[700px]">
                  {stats.heatmap.map((day) => {
                    let bgStyle = 'bg-[var(--layer-3)]';
                    if (day.level === 1) bgStyle = 'bg-[var(--accent-soft)]';
                    if (day.level === 2) bgStyle = 'bg-[var(--accent)] opacity-60';
                    if (day.level === 3) bgStyle = 'bg-[var(--accent)] opacity-80';
                    if (day.level === 4) bgStyle = 'bg-[var(--accent)]';

                    return (
                      <div
                        key={day.date}
                        className={`w-3 h-3 rounded-xs cursor-pointer transition-all hover:ring-2 hover:ring-[var(--accent)] ${bgStyle}`}
                        title={`${day.date}: ${day.minutes} minutes`}
                        onMouseEnter={() => setHoveredHeatmap(day)}
                        onMouseLeave={() => setHoveredHeatmap(null)}
                      />
                    );
                  })}
                </div>
              </div>

              {hoveredHeatmap && (
                <div className="text-xs text-[var(--text-2)] mt-2">
                  <span className="font-semibold text-[var(--text-1)]">{hoveredHeatmap.date}</span>: {hoveredHeatmap.minutes} minutes read
                </div>
              )}
            </div>

            {/* Time of Day Distribution & Top Items Grid */}
            <div className="grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-2 gap-6">
              {/* Time of Day Distribution */}
              <div className="p-5 rounded-[var(--r-xl)] bg-[var(--layer-2)] border border-[var(--stroke)] shadow-xs">
                <h3 className="text-base font-bold text-[var(--text-1)] mb-1">
                  Time of Day Distribution
                </h3>
                <p className="text-xs text-[var(--text-3)] mb-4">
                  When you prefer to read throughout the day
                </p>

                <div className="space-y-3.5">
                  {stats.timeOfDay.map((tod) => {
                    const icon =
                      tod.key === 'morning' ? (
                        <Sunrise className="w-4 h-4 text-[var(--warning)]" />
                      ) : tod.key === 'afternoon' ? (
                        <Sun className="w-4 h-4 text-[var(--warning)]" />
                      ) : tod.key === 'evening' ? (
                        <Sunset className="w-4 h-4 text-[var(--accent)]" />
                      ) : (
                        <Moon className="w-4 h-4 text-[var(--accent)]" />
                      );

                    return (
                      <div key={tod.key} className="space-y-1">
                        <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-xs">
                          <span className="font-medium text-[var(--text-1)] flex items-center gap-2">
                            {icon}
                            {tod.label} <span className="text-[var(--text-3)] text-[11px]">({tod.hours})</span>
                          </span>
                          <span className="text-[var(--text-2)] font-semibold">
                            {tod.percentage}% ({Math.round(tod.durationSec / 60)}m)
                          </span>
                        </div>
                        <div className="w-full h-2 rounded-full bg-[var(--layer-3)] overflow-hidden">
                          <div
                            className="h-full bg-[var(--accent)] rounded-full transition-all duration-300"
                            style={{ width: `${tod.percentage}%` }}
                          />
                        </div>
                      </div>
                    );
                  })}
                </div>
              </div>

              {/* Top Books & Series by Time */}
              <div className="p-5 rounded-[var(--r-xl)] bg-[var(--layer-2)] border border-[var(--stroke)] shadow-xs flex flex-col justify-between">
                <div>
                  <h3 className="text-base font-bold text-[var(--text-1)] mb-1">
                    Top Titles by Reading Time
                  </h3>
                  <p className="text-xs text-[var(--text-3)] mb-4">
                    Books and manga series you spent the most time reading
                  </p>

                  {stats.topItems.length > 0 ? (
                    <div className="space-y-3">
                      {stats.topItems.slice(0, 5).map((item, idx) => (
                        <div
                          key={item.id}
                          className="flex items-center justify-between gap-3 text-xs p-2 rounded-[var(--r-md)] bg-[var(--layer-1)] border border-[var(--stroke)]"
                        >
                          <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5 min-w-0">
                            <span className="text-xs font-bold text-[var(--text-3)] w-4 text-center">
                              #{idx + 1}
                            </span>
                            <div className="min-w-0">
                              <p className="font-semibold text-[var(--text-1)] truncate">
                                {item.title}
                              </p>
                              <p className="text-[11px] text-[var(--text-3)] truncate">
                                {item.kind === 'manga' ? 'Manga Series' : item.authorOrMeta || 'Book'}
                              </p>
                            </div>
                          </div>

                          <div className="text-right shrink-0">
                            <span className="font-bold text-[var(--text-1)]">
                              {Math.round(item.durationSec / 60)}m
                            </span>
                            <div className="text-[10px] text-[var(--accent)] font-medium">
                              {item.percentageOfTotal}%
                            </div>
                          </div>
                        </div>
                      ))}
                    </div>
                  ) : (
                    <div className="text-center py-8 text-xs text-[var(--text-3)]">
                      No reading recorded in this period yet.
                    </div>
                  )}
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* Finished Books Wall */}
            {finishedBooks.length > 0 && (
              <div className="p-5 rounded-[var(--r-xl)] bg-[var(--layer-2)] border border-[var(--stroke)] shadow-xs">
                <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-4">
                  <div>
                    <h3 className="text-base font-bold text-[var(--text-1)] flex items-center gap-2">
                      <Trophy className="w-4 h-4 text-[var(--success)]" />
                      Finished Books Wall
                    </h3>
                    <p className="text-xs text-[var(--text-3)] mt-0.5">
                      {finishedBooks.length} completed books in your personal library
                    </p>
                  </div>
                </div>

                <div className="grid grid-cols-2 sm:grid-cols-4 md:grid-cols-6 lg:grid-cols-8 gap-3">
                  {finishedBooks.map((b) => (
                    <div key={b.id} className="flex flex-col items-center text-center group">
                      <div className="w-full aspect-[2/3] max-w-[100px]">
                        <BookCover
                          id={b.id}
                          title={b.title}
                          author={b.author}
                          coverHash={b.cover_hash}
                          progressPercent={100}
                        />
                      </div>
                      <span className="text-xs font-medium text-[var(--text-1)] mt-1.5 line-clamp-1">
                        {b.title}
                      </span>
                      <span className="text-[10px] text-[var(--text-3)] line-clamp-1">
                        {b.author}
                      </span>
                    </div>
                  ))}
                </div>
              </div>
            )}
          </TabsContent>
        </Tabs>

        {/* Reading Goals Setup / Edit Dialog */}
        {showGoalsDialog && (
          <Dialog
            open={true}
            onOpenChange={(open) => !open && setShowGoalsDialog(false)}
            title="Reading Goals"
            description="Set your daily reading target and yearly book completion milestones."
            footer={
              <div className="flex items-center justify-end gap-2 w-full">
                <Button variant="standard" onClick={() => setShowGoalsDialog(false)}>
                  Cancel
                </Button>
                <Button variant="accent" onClick={handleSaveGoals}>
                  Save Goals
                </Button>
              </div>
            }
          >
            <div className="space-y-4 py-2">
              <div>
                <label
                  htmlFor="daily-reading-goal-input"
                  className="text-xs font-semibold text-[var(--text-2)] uppercase tracking-wider block mb-1.5"
                >
                  Daily Reading Goal (Minutes)
                </label>
                <input
                  id="daily-reading-goal-input"
                  type="number"
                  min={5}
                  max={480}
                  value={editDaily}
                  onChange={(e) => setEditDaily(Math.max(5, parseInt(e.target.value, 10) || 5))}
                  className="w-full px-3 py-2 text-sm rounded-[var(--r-md)] bg-[var(--layer-3)] border border-[var(--stroke)] text-[var(--text-1)] outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-[var(--accent)]"
                />
                <p className="text-[11px] text-[var(--text-3)] mt-1">
                  Suggested targets: 15, 30, 45, or 60 minutes
                </p>
              </div>

              <div>
                <label
                  htmlFor="yearly-reading-goal-input"
                  className="text-xs font-semibold text-[var(--text-2)] uppercase tracking-wider block mb-1.5"
                >
                  Yearly Finished Books Goal
                </label>
                <input
                  id="yearly-reading-goal-input"
                  type="number"
                  min={1}
                  max={365}
                  value={editYearly}
                  onChange={(e) => setEditYearly(Math.max(1, parseInt(e.target.value, 10) || 1))}
                  className="w-full px-3 py-2 text-sm rounded-[var(--r-md)] bg-[var(--layer-3)] border border-[var(--stroke)] text-[var(--text-1)] outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-[var(--accent)]"
                />
                <p className="text-[11px] text-[var(--text-3)] mt-1">
                  Suggested targets: 12, 20, 30, or 50 books per year
                </p>
              </div>
            </div>
          </Dialog>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

================
File: apps/client/src/sources/archiveorg.ts
