import { net } from '../lib/net';
import { cachedJson } from '../lib/apiCache';
import type {
  CatalogEntry,
  CatalogFormat,
  CatalogPage,
  CatalogSection,
  CatalogSource,
} from './types';

interface ArchiveOrgDoc {
  identifier: string;
  title?: string;
  creator?: string | string[];
  description?: string | string[];
  year?: string | number;
  downloads?: number;
}

interface ArchiveOrgSearchResponse {
  response: {
    numFound: number;
    start: number;
    docs: ArchiveOrgDoc[];
  };
}

interface ArchiveOrgFile {
  name: string;
  format?: string;
  size?: string;
}

interface ArchiveOrgMetadataResponse {
  result?: ArchiveOrgFile[];
  files?: ArchiveOrgFile[];
  metadata?: {
    title?: string;
    creator?: string;
    description?: string;
    subject?: string | string[];
  };
}

function parseArchiveOrgDoc(doc: ArchiveOrgDoc): CatalogEntry {
  const authors: string[] = [];
  if (Array.isArray(doc.creator)) {
    authors.push(...doc.creator);
  } else if (doc.creator) {
    authors.push(doc.creator);
  } else {
    authors.push('Internet Archive Contributor');
  }

  let summary = '';
  if (Array.isArray(doc.description)) {
    summary = doc.description.join(' ');
  } else if (doc.description) {
    summary = doc.description;
  }

  const isPeriodical =
    (doc.title && /magazine|periodical|issue|journal|comic/i.test(doc.title)) ||
    (doc.identifier && /magazine|comic/i.test(doc.identifier));

  const coverUrl = `https://archive.org/services/img/${doc.identifier}`;
  const formats: CatalogFormat[] = [];

  if (isPeriodical) {
    formats.push({
      mime: 'application/pdf',
      url: `https://archive.org/download/${doc.identifier}/${doc.identifier}.pdf`,
      label: 'Scanned Magazine (PDF with images)',
    });
    formats.push({
      mime: 'application/epub+zip',
      url: `https://archive.org/download/${doc.identifier}/${doc.identifier}.epub`,
      label: 'Text Only (OCR EPUB)',
    });
  } else {
    formats.push({
      mime: 'application/epub+zip',
      url: `https://archive.org/download/${doc.identifier}/${doc.identifier}.epub`,
      label: 'Free EPUB',
    });
    formats.push({
      mime: 'application/pdf',
      url: `https://archive.org/download/${doc.identifier}/${doc.identifier}.pdf`,
      label: 'Scanned Book (PDF with images)',
    });
  }

  return {
    id: `archiveorg:${doc.identifier}`,
    title: doc.title || doc.identifier,
    authors,
    cover: coverUrl,
    summary,
    formats,
    kind: isPeriodical ? 'comic' : 'book',
    year: typeof doc.year === 'number' ? doc.year : doc.year ? parseInt(String(doc.year), 10) : undefined,
  };
}

export class ArchiveOrgSource implements CatalogSource {
  id = 'archiveorg';
  name = 'Internet Archive';
  icon = 'BookOpen';
  kind = 'books' as const;

  async sections(): Promise<CatalogSection[]> {
    return [
      { id: 'popular', title: 'Most Popular' },
      { id: 'magazines', title: 'Magazines & Periodicals' },
      { id: 'comics', title: 'Comics & Graphic Novels' },
      { id: 'gutenberg', title: 'Gutenberg Archive' },
      { id: 'folkscanomy', title: 'Community Library' },
      { id: 'fiction', title: 'Fiction' },
      { id: 'history', title: 'History & Biographies' },
    ];
  }

  async browse(sectionId: string, cursor?: string): Promise<CatalogPage> {
    const limit = 24;
    const page = cursor ? parseInt(cursor, 10) : 1;

    let filter = 'mediatype:texts AND -mediatype:collection AND (format:EPUB OR format:PDF OR format:CBZ)';
    if (sectionId === 'magazines') {
      filter = 'mediatype:texts AND -mediatype:collection AND (collection:magazine_rack OR collection:computermagazines OR subject:magazine OR subject:periodicals)';
    } else if (sectionId === 'comics') {
      filter = 'mediatype:texts AND -mediatype:collection AND (collection:comics OR collection:comicbooks OR subject:comics)';
    } else if (sectionId === 'gutenberg') {
      filter += ' AND collection:gutenberg';
    } else if (sectionId === 'folkscanomy') {
      filter += ' AND collection:folkscanomy';
    } else if (sectionId === 'fiction') {
      filter += ' AND subject:fiction';
    } else if (sectionId === 'history') {
      filter += ' AND subject:history';
    }

    const params = new URLSearchParams();
    params.set('q', filter);
    params.append('fl[]', 'identifier');
    params.append('fl[]', 'title');
    params.append('fl[]', 'creator');
    params.append('fl[]', 'description');
    params.append('fl[]', 'year');
    params.append('fl[]', 'downloads');
    params.set('sort[]', 'downloads desc');
    params.set('rows', String(limit));
    params.set('page', String(page));
    params.set('output', 'json');

    const url = `https://archive.org/advancedsearch.php?${params.toString()}`;
    const data = await cachedJson<ArchiveOrgSearchResponse>(
      url,
      async () => {
        const res = await net.fetch(url);
        if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`Archive.org error: ${res.statusText}`);
        return (await res.json()) as ArchiveOrgSearchResponse;
      },
      { freshMs: 60 * 60_000 },
    );

    const items = (data.response?.docs || []).map(parseArchiveOrgDoc);
    const hasMore = page * limit < (data.response?.numFound || 0);
    const next = hasMore ? String(page + 1) : undefined;

    return { items, next };
  }

  async search(query: string, cursor?: string): Promise<CatalogPage> {
    const limit = 24;
    const page = cursor ? parseInt(cursor, 10) : 1;

    const safeQuery = query.replace(/[^\w\s-]/g, ' ').trim();
    const filter = `mediatype:texts AND -mediatype:collection AND (format:EPUB OR format:PDF OR format:CBZ) AND (title:(${safeQuery}) OR creator:(${safeQuery}) OR description:(${safeQuery}))`;

    const params = new URLSearchParams();
    params.set('q', filter);
    params.append('fl[]', 'identifier');
    params.append('fl[]', 'title');
    params.append('fl[]', 'creator');
    params.append('fl[]', 'description');
    params.append('fl[]', 'year');
    params.append('fl[]', 'downloads');
    params.set('sort[]', 'downloads desc');
    params.set('rows', String(limit));
    params.set('page', String(page));
    params.set('output', 'json');

    const url = `https://archive.org/advancedsearch.php?${params.toString()}`;
    const data = await cachedJson<ArchiveOrgSearchResponse>(
      url,
      async () => {
        const res = await net.fetch(url);
        if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`Archive.org search error: ${res.statusText}`);
        return (await res.json()) as ArchiveOrgSearchResponse;
      },
      { freshMs: 30 * 60_000 },
    );

    const items = (data.response?.docs || []).map(parseArchiveOrgDoc);
    const hasMore = page * limit < (data.response?.numFound || 0);
    const next = hasMore ? String(page + 1) : undefined;

    return { items, next };
  }

  async details(id: string): Promise<CatalogEntry> {
    const identifier = id.replace(/^archiveorg:/, '');
    const url = `https://archive.org/metadata/${identifier}`;

    const data = await cachedJson<ArchiveOrgMetadataResponse>(
      url,
      async () => {
        const res = await net.fetch(url);
        if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`Archive.org details error: ${res.statusText}`);
        return (await res.json()) as ArchiveOrgMetadataResponse;
      },
      { freshMs: 6 * 60 * 60_000 },
    );

    const meta = data.metadata || {};
    const files = data.files || data.result || [];

    // 1. PDF files (scanned magazines/books with images)
    const pdfFiles = files.filter(
      (f) =>
        f.name.toLowerCase().endsWith('.pdf') &&
        !f.name.toLowerCase().endsWith('_lcp.pdf') &&
        !f.name.toLowerCase().endsWith('_chocr.pdf'),
    );
    // Find best visual PDF (prefer clean non-_text and non-_bw PDF)
    const visualPdf =
      pdfFiles.find(
        (f) =>
          !f.name.toLowerCase().endsWith('_text.pdf') &&
          !f.name.toLowerCase().endsWith('_bw.pdf') &&
          !f.name.toLowerCase().endsWith('_daisy.pdf'),
      ) || pdfFiles[0];

    // 2. CBZ / Comic files
    const cbzFile = files.find(
      (f) =>
        f.name.toLowerCase().endsWith('.cbz') ||
        f.name.toLowerCase().endsWith('.cbr'),
    );

    // 3. Clean EPUB files (excluding DRM-locked _lcp.epub)
    const epubFile = files.find(
      (f) =>
        f.name.toLowerCase().endsWith('.epub') &&
        !f.name.toLowerCase().endsWith('_lcp.epub') &&
        !f.name.toLowerCase().endsWith('_daisy.zip'),
    );

    const isPeriodical =
      meta.subject?.toString().toLowerCase().includes('magazine') ||
      meta.subject?.toString().toLowerCase().includes('comic') ||
      meta.subject?.toString().toLowerCase().includes('periodical') ||
      meta.title?.toString().toLowerCase().includes('magazine') ||
      meta.description?.toString().toLowerCase().includes('magazine') ||
      Boolean(cbzFile);

    const formats: CatalogFormat[] = [];

    // For magazines & comics: prioritize visual scans with images (PDF / CBZ)
    if (isPeriodical || !epubFile) {
      if (visualPdf) {
        formats.push({
          mime: 'application/pdf',
          url: `https://archive.org/download/${identifier}/${visualPdf.name}`,
          label: 'Full Magazine Scan (PDF with images)',
          size: visualPdf.size ? parseInt(visualPdf.size, 10) : undefined,
        });
      }
      if (cbzFile) {
        formats.push({
          mime: 'application/vnd.comicbook+zip',
          url: `https://archive.org/download/${identifier}/${cbzFile.name}`,
          label: 'Comic / Scanned Pages (CBZ)',
          size: cbzFile.size ? parseInt(cbzFile.size, 10) : undefined,
        });
      }
      if (epubFile) {
        formats.push({
          mime: 'application/epub+zip',
          url: `https://archive.org/download/${identifier}/${epubFile.name}`,
          label: 'Text Only (OCR EPUB)',
          size: epubFile.size ? parseInt(epubFile.size, 10) : undefined,
        });
      }
    } else {
      // For general books: offer reflowable EPUB first, then original page scan PDF
      if (epubFile) {
        formats.push({
          mime: 'application/epub+zip',
          url: `https://archive.org/download/${identifier}/${epubFile.name}`,
          label: 'Free EPUB (Reflowable text)',
          size: epubFile.size ? parseInt(epubFile.size, 10) : undefined,
        });
      }
      if (visualPdf) {
        formats.push({
          mime: 'application/pdf',
          url: `https://archive.org/download/${identifier}/${visualPdf.name}`,
          label: 'Original Book Scan (PDF with images)',
          size: visualPdf.size ? parseInt(visualPdf.size, 10) : undefined,
        });
      }
      if (cbzFile) {
        formats.push({
          mime: 'application/vnd.comicbook+zip',
          url: `https://archive.org/download/${identifier}/${cbzFile.name}`,
          label: 'Comic / Scanned Pages (CBZ)',
          size: cbzFile.size ? parseInt(cbzFile.size, 10) : undefined,
        });
      }
    }

    if (formats.length === 0) {
      formats.push({
        mime: 'application/pdf',
        url: `https://archive.org/download/${identifier}/${identifier}.pdf`,
        label: 'Scanned PDF with images',
      });
    }

    const tags: string[] = [];
    if (Array.isArray(meta.subject)) {
      tags.push(...meta.subject.slice(0, 6));
    } else if (meta.subject) {
      tags.push(meta.subject);
    }

    return {
      id: `archiveorg:${identifier}`,
      title: meta.title || identifier,
      authors: meta.creator ? [meta.creator] : ['Internet Archive Contributor'],
      cover: `https://archive.org/services/img/${identifier}`,
      summary: meta.description,
      tags,
      formats,
      kind: isPeriodical ? 'comic' : 'book',
    };
  }

  async download(
    entry: CatalogEntry,
    formatUrl: string,
    onProgress?: (p: number) => void,
  ): Promise<File> {
    const identifier = entry.id.replace(/^archiveorg:/, '');
    let chosenUrl = formatUrl;
    let chosenMime = 'application/epub+zip';
    let chosenExt = 'epub';

    const matchedFormat = entry.formats?.find((f) => f.url === formatUrl);
    if (matchedFormat) {
      chosenUrl = matchedFormat.url;
      chosenMime = matchedFormat.mime;
      if (chosenMime === 'application/pdf' || chosenUrl.toLowerCase().endsWith('.pdf')) {
        chosenExt = 'pdf';
        chosenMime = 'application/pdf';
      } else if (
        chosenMime.includes('comic') ||
        chosenUrl.toLowerCase().endsWith('.cbz')
      ) {
        chosenExt = 'cbz';
        chosenMime = 'application/vnd.comicbook+zip';
      }
    } else if (!chosenUrl) {
      const first = entry.formats?.[0];
      if (first) {
        chosenUrl = first.url;
        chosenMime = first.mime;
        if (chosenMime === 'application/pdf' || chosenUrl.toLowerCase().endsWith('.pdf')) {
          chosenExt = 'pdf';
          chosenMime = 'application/pdf';
        } else if (chosenMime.includes('comic') || chosenUrl.toLowerCase().endsWith('.cbz')) {
          chosenExt = 'cbz';
          chosenMime = 'application/vnd.comicbook+zip';
        }
      }
    }

    // If chosenUrl has no extension or is generic, resolve actual filename via metadata
    if (
      !chosenUrl ||
      (!chosenUrl.endsWith('.pdf') && !chosenUrl.endsWith('.epub') && !chosenUrl.endsWith('.cbz'))
    ) {
      try {
        const metaUrl = `https://archive.org/metadata/${identifier}`;
        const res = await net.fetch(metaUrl);
        if (res.ok) {
          const json = (await res.json()) as ArchiveOrgMetadataResponse;
          const files = json.files || json.result || [];
          const pdf = files.find(
            (f) =>
              f.name.toLowerCase().endsWith('.pdf') &&
              !f.name.toLowerCase().endsWith('_text.pdf'),
          );
          const epub = files.find((f) => f.name.toLowerCase().endsWith('.epub'));

          if (pdf && (!epub || chosenExt === 'pdf' || entry.title.toLowerCase().includes('magazine'))) {
            chosenUrl = `https://archive.org/download/${identifier}/${pdf.name}`;
            chosenExt = 'pdf';
            chosenMime = 'application/pdf';
          } else if (epub) {
            chosenUrl = `https://archive.org/download/${identifier}/${epub.name}`;
            chosenExt = 'epub';
            chosenMime = 'application/epub+zip';
          }
        }
      } catch {
        // Fallback
      }
    }

    chosenUrl = chosenUrl || `https://archive.org/download/${identifier}/${identifier}.${chosenExt}`;

    onProgress?.(0.05);
    const blob = await net.download(chosenUrl, {
      onProgress: (p) => onProgress?.(0.05 + p * 0.9),
    });
    onProgress?.(1.0);

    const safeTitle = entry.title.replace(/[<>:"/\\|?*]/g, '_').slice(0, 80);
    return new File([blob], `${safeTitle}.${chosenExt}`, {
      type: chosenMime,
    });
  }
}

export const archiveOrgSource = new ArchiveOrgSource();

================
File: apps/client/src/sources/mangapill.ts
================
import { net, proxyImageUrl } from '../lib/net';
import { cachedJson } from '../lib/apiCache';
import type {
  CatalogEntry,
  CatalogPage,
  CatalogSection,
  ChapterInfo,
  MangaSource,
  PageRef,
} from './types';

function parseMangaPillCards(html: string): CatalogEntry[] {
  const items: CatalogEntry[] = [];
  const seen = new Set<string>();

  // Matches <a ... href="/manga/(\d+)/([^"/]+)" ...>
  const linkRegex = /<a\b[^>]*\bhref="\/manga\/(\d+)\/([^"/]+)"[^>]*>([\s\S]*?)<\/a>/gi;
  let match: RegExpExecArray | null;

  while ((match = linkRegex.exec(html)) !== null) {
    const id = match[1];
    const slug = match[2];
    if (seen.has(id)) continue;

    const inner = match[3];
    const imgMatch = /(?:data-src|src)="([^"]+)"/i.exec(inner);
    const titleMatch = /<div[^>]*>([^<]+)<\/div>/i.exec(inner);

    const title = (titleMatch?.[1] || '')
      .replace(/&amp;/g, '&')
      .replace(/&#039;/g, "'")
      .trim() || slug.replace(/-/g, ' ').replace(/\b\w/g, (c) => c.toUpperCase());

    const rawCover = imgMatch?.[1] || `https://cdn.readdetectiveconan.com/file/mangapill/i/${id}.jpeg`;

    seen.add(id);
    items.push({
      id: `mangapill:${id}:${slug}`,
      title,
      authors: ['Manga Artist'],
      cover: proxyImageUrl(rawCover),
      kind: 'manga',
    });
  }

  return items;
}

export class MangaPillSource implements MangaSource {
  id = 'mangapill';
  name = 'MangaPill';
  icon = 'BookOpen';
  kind = 'manga' as const;

  async sections(): Promise<CatalogSection[]> {
    return [
      { id: 'popular', title: 'Popular' },
      { id: 'new', title: 'New Manga' },
      { id: 'recent', title: 'Latest Updates' },
    ];
  }

  async browse(sectionId: string, cursor?: string): Promise<CatalogPage> {
    const page = cursor ? parseInt(cursor, 10) : 1;
    let url = `https://mangapill.com/search?type=manga&sort=popularity&page=${page}`;

    if (sectionId === 'new') {
      url = `https://mangapill.com/mangas/new?page=${page}`;
    } else if (sectionId === 'recent') {
      url = `https://mangapill.com/chapters?page=${page}`;
    }

    const html = await cachedJson<string>(
      url,
      async () => {
        const res = await net.fetch(url, {
          headers: {
            'User-Agent': 'Mozilla/5.0 (Windows NT 10.0; Win64; x64) AppleWebKit/537.36 (KHTML, like Gecko) Chrome/120.0.0.0 Safari/537.36',
          },
        });
        if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`MangaPill browse error: ${res.statusText}`);
        return res.text();
      },
      { freshMs: 30 * 60_000 },
    );

    const items = parseMangaPillCards(html);
    const hasMore = items.length >= 10;
    const next = hasMore ? String(page + 1) : undefined;

    return { items, next };
  }

  async search(query: string, cursor?: string): Promise<CatalogPage> {
    const page = cursor ? parseInt(cursor, 10) : 1;
    const trimmed = query.trim();
    const url = page === 1 && trimmed.length < 20
      ? `https://mangapill.com/quick-search?q=${encodeURIComponent(trimmed)}`
      : `https://mangapill.com/search?q=${encodeURIComponent(trimmed)}&page=${page}`;

    const html = await cachedJson<string>(
      url,
      async () => {
        const res = await net.fetch(url, {
          headers: {
            'User-Agent': 'Mozilla/5.0 (Windows NT 10.0; Win64; x64) AppleWebKit/537.36 (KHTML, like Gecko) Chrome/120.0.0.0 Safari/537.36',
          },
        });
        if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`MangaPill search error: ${res.statusText}`);
        return res.text();
      },
      { freshMs: 30 * 60_000 },
    );

    const items = parseMangaPillCards(html);
    const hasMore = items.length >= 10;
    const next = hasMore ? String(page + 1) : undefined;

    return { items, next };
  }

  async details(id: string): Promise<CatalogEntry> {
    const parts = id.replace(/^mangapill:/, '').split(':');
    const mangaId = parts[0];
    const slug = parts[1] || '';
    const url = `https://mangapill.com/manga/${mangaId}/${slug}`;

    const html = await cachedJson<string>(
      url,
      async () => {
        const res = await net.fetch(url, {
          headers: {
            'User-Agent': 'Mozilla/5.0 (Windows NT 10.0; Win64; x64) AppleWebKit/537.36 (KHTML, like Gecko) Chrome/120.0.0.0 Safari/537.36',
          },
        });
        if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`MangaPill details error: ${res.statusText}`);
        return res.text();
      },
      { freshMs: 6 * 60 * 60_000 },
    );

    // Title
    const titleMatch = /<h1[^>]*>([\s\S]*?)<\/h1>/i.exec(html);
    const title = titleMatch ? titleMatch[1].replace(/<[^>]+>/g, '').trim() : slug.replace(/-/g, ' ');

    // Cover
    const coverMatch = /<img\b[^>]*\b(?:data-src|src)="([^"]+)"[^>]*\balt="[^"]*"/i.exec(html) ||
                       /(?:data-src|src)="([^"]*\/mangapill\/i\/[^"]+)"/i.exec(html);
    const rawCover = coverMatch ? coverMatch[1] : `https://cdn.readdetectiveconan.com/file/mangapill/i/${mangaId}.jpeg`;

    // Synopsis
    const descMatch = /<p[^>]*class="[^"]*text-sm[^"]*text-secondary[^"]*"[^>]*>([\s\S]*?)<\/p>/i.exec(html) ||
                      /<div[^>]*class="[^"]*description[^"]*"[^>]*>([\s\S]*?)<\/div>/i.exec(html);
    const summary = descMatch ? descMatch[1].replace(/<[^>]+>/g, '').trim() : '';

    // Tags/Genres
    const tags: string[] = [];
    const genreRegex = /<a\b[^>]*\bhref="\/search\?genre=[^"]*"[^>]*>([\s\S]*?)<\/a>/gi;
    let gm: RegExpExecArray | null;
    while ((gm = genreRegex.exec(html)) !== null) {
      tags.push(gm[1].replace(/<[^>]+>/g, '').trim());
    }

    // Status
    const statusMatch = /Status[\s\S]*?<div[^>]*>([^<]+)<\/div>/i.exec(html);
    const status = statusMatch ? statusMatch[1].trim().toLowerCase() : undefined;

    return {
      id,
      title,
      authors: ['Manga Creator'],
      cover: proxyImageUrl(rawCover),
      summary,
      tags: tags.slice(0, 8),
      kind: 'manga',
      status,
    };
  }

  async chapters(seriesId: string): Promise<ChapterInfo[]> {
    const parts = seriesId.replace(/^mangapill:/, '').split(':');
    const mangaId = parts[0];
    const slug = parts[1] || '';
    const url = `https://mangapill.com/manga/${mangaId}/${slug}`;

    const html = await cachedJson<string>(
      `mangapill:chapters:${mangaId}`,
      async () => {
        const res = await net.fetch(url, {
          headers: {
            'User-Agent': 'Mozilla/5.0 (Windows NT 10.0; Win64; x64) AppleWebKit/537.36 (KHTML, like Gecko) Chrome/120.0.0.0 Safari/537.36',
          },
        });
        if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`MangaPill chapters error: ${res.statusText}`);
        return res.text();
      },
      { freshMs: 30 * 60_000 },
    );

    const chapters: ChapterInfo[] = [];
    const chapterRegex = /<a\b[^>]*\bhref="\/chapters\/(\d+-\d+\/[^"]+)"[^>]*>([\s\S]*?)<\/a>/gi;
    let match: RegExpExecArray | null;

    while ((match = chapterRegex.exec(html)) !== null) {
      const chId = match[1];
      const chTitle = match[2].replace(/<[^>]+>/g, '').trim();
      const numMatch = /Chapter\s+(\d+(?:\.\d+)?)/i.exec(chTitle);
      const num = numMatch ? parseFloat(numMatch[1]) : undefined;

      chapters.push({
        id: chId,
        title: chTitle,
        number: num,
        scanlationGroup: 'MangaPill',
      });
    }

    return chapters;
  }

  async pages(chapterId: string): Promise<PageRef[]> {
    const url = `https://mangapill.com/chapters/${chapterId}`;
    const res = await net.fetch(url, {
      headers: {
        'User-Agent': 'Mozilla/5.0 (Windows NT 10.0; Win64; x64) AppleWebKit/537.36 (KHTML, like Gecko) Chrome/120.0.0.0 Safari/537.36',
      },
    });

    if (!res.ok) {
      throw new Error(`Failed to load MangaPill chapter pages: ${res.statusText}`);
    }

    const html = await res.text();
    const pages: PageRef[] = [];
    const imgRegex = /(?:data-src|src)="([^"]+)"/gi;
    let match: RegExpExecArray | null;
    let idx = 1;
    const seen = new Set<string>();

    while ((match = imgRegex.exec(html)) !== null) {
      const imgUrl = match[1];
      if (
        (imgUrl.includes('.jpeg') || imgUrl.includes('.jpg') || imgUrl.includes('.png') || imgUrl.includes('.webp')) &&
        !imgUrl.includes('logo') && !imgUrl.includes('icon') && !imgUrl.includes('avatar') &&
        !seen.has(imgUrl)
      ) {
        seen.add(imgUrl);
        pages.push({
          index: idx++,
          url: proxyImageUrl(imgUrl) || imgUrl,
        });
      }
    }

    return pages;
  }

  credit(chapter: ChapterInfo): string {
    return `Scanlation: ${chapter.scanlationGroup || 'MangaPill'} · Hosted on MangaPill`;
  }
}

export const mangaPillSource = new MangaPillSource();

================
File: apps/client/src/sources/opds.ts
================
import { net } from '../lib/net';
import type {
  CatalogEntry,
  CatalogFormat,
  CatalogPage,
  CatalogSection,
  CatalogSource,
} from './types';

interface Opds2Link {
  rel?: string;
  href: string;
  type?: string;
}

interface Opds2Author {
  name?: string;
}

interface Opds2Publication {
  id?: string;
  metadata?: {
    identifier?: string;
    title?: string;
    author?: string | Opds2Author | Array<string | Opds2Author>;
    description?: string;
  };
  links?: Opds2Link[];
}

interface Opds2Feed {
  publications?: Opds2Publication[];
  links?: Opds2Link[];
}

export class OpdsSource implements CatalogSource {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  icon = 'BookOpen';
  kind = 'books' as const;
  baseUrl: string;
  authHeader?: string;

  constructor(
    id: string,
    name: string,
    baseUrl: string,
    credentials?: { username?: string; password?: string; apiKey?: string },
  ) {
    this.id = id;
    this.name = name;
    this.baseUrl = baseUrl;
    if (credentials?.username || credentials?.password) {
      const u = credentials.username || '';
      const p = credentials.password || '';
      this.authHeader = `Basic ${btoa(`${u}:${p}`)}`;
    } else if (credentials?.apiKey) {
      this.authHeader = `Bearer ${credentials.apiKey}`;
    }
  }

  private async fetchFeed(url: string): Promise<Response> {
    const headers: Record<string, string> = {};
    if (this.authHeader) {
      headers['Authorization'] = this.authHeader;
    }
    return net.fetch(url, { headers });
  }

  async sections(): Promise<CatalogSection[]> {
    return [
      { id: 'root', title: 'Catalog' },
      { id: 'new', title: 'New Releases' },
    ];
  }

  async browse(_sectionId: string, cursor?: string): Promise<CatalogPage> {
    const url = cursor || this.baseUrl;
    const res = await this.fetchFeed(url);
    if (!res.ok) {
      throw new Error(`Failed to load OPDS feed: ${res.statusText}`);
    }

    const contentType = res.headers.get('content-type') || '';
    if (contentType.includes('json')) {
      const data = await res.json();
      return this.parseOpds2(data);
    } else {
      const text = await res.text();
      return this.parseOpds1(text, url);
    }
  }

  async search(query: string, cursor?: string): Promise<CatalogPage> {
    let url = cursor;
    if (!url) {
      // Basic fallback search if OpenSearch URL is not already resolved
      const sep = this.baseUrl.includes('?') ? '&' : '?';
      url = `${this.baseUrl}${sep}q=${encodeURIComponent(query)}`;
    }
    const res = await this.fetchFeed(url);
    if (!res.ok) {
      throw new Error(`Failed to search OPDS: ${res.statusText}`);
    }
    const contentType = res.headers.get('content-type') || '';
    if (contentType.includes('json')) {
      const data = await res.json();
      return this.parseOpds2(data);
    } else {
      const text = await res.text();
      return this.parseOpds1(text, url);
    }
  }

  async details(id: string): Promise<CatalogEntry> {
    return {
      id,
      title: 'OPDS Publication',
      authors: [],
      kind: 'book',
    };
  }

  async download(
    entry: CatalogEntry,
    _format: string,
    onProgress?: (p: number) => void,
  ): Promise<File> {
    const format = entry.formats?.find(
      (f) => f.mime === 'application/epub+zip',
    ) || entry.formats?.[0];
    if (!format) {
      throw new Error('No acquisition download format available for this book.');
    }

    onProgress?.(0.1);
    const res = await this.fetchFeed(format.url);
    if (!res.ok) {
      throw new Error(`Failed to download book: ${res.statusText}`);
    }

    onProgress?.(0.5);
    const blob = await res.blob();
    onProgress?.(1.0);

    const safeTitle = entry.title.replace(/[<>:"/\\|?*]/g, '_').slice(0, 80);
    return new File([blob], `${safeTitle}.epub`, {
      type: 'application/epub+zip',
    });
  }

  private parseOpds1(xmlText: string, currentUrl: string): CatalogPage {
    const parser = new DOMParser();
    const doc = parser.parseFromString(xmlText, 'text/xml');
    const entries = Array.from(doc.querySelectorAll('entry'));
    const items: CatalogEntry[] = [];

    const resolveUrl = (href: string | null) => {
      if (!href) return undefined;
      try {
        return new URL(href, currentUrl).href;
      } catch {
        return href;
      }
    };

    for (const entry of entries) {
      const id = entry.querySelector('id')?.textContent?.trim() || uidFallback();
      const title = entry.querySelector('title')?.textContent?.trim() || 'Untitled';
      const authors = Array.from(entry.querySelectorAll('author name'))
        .map((n) => n.textContent?.trim() || '')
        .filter(Boolean);
      const summary =
        entry.querySelector('summary')?.textContent?.trim() ||
        entry.querySelector('content')?.textContent?.trim();

      const formats: CatalogFormat[] = [];
      let cover: string | undefined;

      // Extract thumbnail from media:thumbnail if present
      const mediaThumb = entry.getElementsByTagNameNS('*', 'thumbnail')[0] || entry.querySelector('thumbnail');
      if (mediaThumb) {
        cover = resolveUrl(mediaThumb.getAttribute('url') || mediaThumb.getAttribute('href'));
      }

      const links = Array.from(entry.querySelectorAll('link'));
      for (const link of links) {
        const rel = link.getAttribute('rel') || '';
        const href = resolveUrl(link.getAttribute('href'));
        const type = link.getAttribute('type') || '';

        if (
          !cover &&
          (rel.includes('image') || rel.includes('thumbnail') || type.startsWith('image/')) &&
          href
        ) {
          cover = href;
        }

        if (
          href &&
          (rel.includes('acquisition') ||
            rel === 'enclosure' ||
            type.includes('epub') ||
            href.includes('.epub'))
        ) {
          if (type.includes('epub') || href.includes('.epub') || rel.includes('acquisition') || rel === 'enclosure') {
            formats.push({
              mime: type || 'application/epub+zip',
              url: href,
            });
          }
        }
      }

      items.push({
        id: `${this.id}:${id}`,
        title,
        authors: authors.length > 0 ? authors : ['Unknown author'],
        cover,
        summary,
        formats,
        kind: 'book',
      });
    }

    // Look for next page link
    const nextLink = doc.querySelector('link[rel="next"]');
    const next = resolveUrl(nextLink?.getAttribute('href') || null);

    return { items, next };
  }

  private parseOpds2(data: Opds2Feed): CatalogPage {
    const publications = data?.publications || [];
    const items: CatalogEntry[] = [];

    for (const pub of publications) {
      const meta = pub.metadata || {};
      const formats: CatalogFormat[] = [];
      let cover: string | undefined;

      for (const link of pub.links || []) {
        if (link.rel === 'cover' || link.rel === 'thumbnail') {
          cover = link.href;
        }
        if (link.rel === 'acquisition' || link.type?.includes('epub')) {
          formats.push({
            mime: link.type || 'application/epub+zip',
            url: link.href,
          });
        }
      }

      let authors: string[] = ['Unknown author'];
      if (Array.isArray(meta.author)) {
        authors = meta.author.map((a) => (typeof a === 'string' ? a : a.name || 'Unknown'));
      } else if (meta.author) {
        authors = [typeof meta.author === 'string' ? meta.author : meta.author.name || 'Unknown'];
      }

      items.push({
        id: `${this.id}:${meta.identifier || pub.id || uidFallback()}`,
        title: meta.title || 'Untitled',
        authors,
        cover,
        summary: meta.description,
        formats,
        kind: 'book',
      });
    }

    const nextLink = (data?.links || []).find((l) => l.rel === 'next');
    return { items, next: nextLink?.href };
  }
}

function uidFallback(): string {
  return Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 10);
}

// Built-in Standard Ebooks Source (Public New Releases Atom Feed)
export const standardEbooksSource = new OpdsSource(
  'standardebooks',
  'Standard Ebooks',
  'https://standardebooks.org/feeds/atom/new-releases',
);

================
File: apps/client/src/sources/openlibrary.ts

import { net } from '../lib/net';
import { cachedJson } from '../lib/apiCache';
import type {
  CatalogEntry,
  CatalogFormat,
  CatalogPage,
  CatalogSection,
  CatalogSource,
} from './types';

interface OpenLibraryDoc {
  key: string;
  title: string;
  author_name?: string[];
  cover_i?: number;
  first_publish_year?: number;
  subject?: string[];
  language?: string[];
  ia?: string[];
  id_project_gutenberg?: string[];
  ebook_access?: string;
  has_fulltext?: boolean;
  edition_count?: number;
}

interface OpenLibrarySearchResponse {
  numFound: number;
  start: number;
  docs: OpenLibraryDoc[];
}

interface OpenLibrarySubjectWork {
  key: string;
  title: string;
  authors?: Array<{ key: string; name: string }>;
  cover_id?: number;
  first_publish_year?: number;
  subject?: string[];
  ia?: string;
}

interface OpenLibrarySubjectResponse {
  name: string;
  work_count: number;
  works: OpenLibrarySubjectWork[];
}

interface OpenLibraryTrendingResponse {
  query?: string;
  works: OpenLibraryDoc[];
}

interface OpenLibraryWorkDetail {
  key: string;
  title: string;
  description?: string | { type: string; value: string };
  covers?: number[];
  subjects?: string[];
}

function parseOpenLibraryDoc(doc: OpenLibraryDoc): CatalogEntry {
  const rawId = doc.key.replace(/^\/works\//, '');
  const coverUrl = doc.cover_i
    ? `https://covers.openlibrary.org/b/id/${doc.cover_i}-M.jpg`
    : undefined;

  const formats: CatalogFormat[] = [];

  // 1. Direct Project Gutenberg formats if mapped
  if (doc.id_project_gutenberg && doc.id_project_gutenberg.length > 0) {
    const gid = doc.id_project_gutenberg[0];
    formats.push({
      mime: 'application/epub+zip',
      url: `https://www.gutenberg.org/ebooks/${gid}.epub3.images`,
      label: 'Free EPUB (Project Gutenberg)',
    });
    formats.push({
      mime: 'application/epub+zip',
      url: `https://www.gutenberg.org/ebooks/${gid}.epub.noimages`,
      label: 'Free EPUB (Text only)',
    });
  } else if (doc.ebook_access !== 'borrowable' && doc.ebook_access !== 'printdisabled' && doc.ia && doc.ia.length > 0) {
    // 2. Unrestricted public domain Internet Archive item
    const primaryIa = doc.ia[0];
    formats.push({
      mime: 'application/epub+zip',
      url: `https://archive.org/download/${primaryIa}/${primaryIa}.epub`,
      label: 'Free EPUB (Internet Archive)',
    });
  }

  return {
    id: `openlibrary:${rawId}`,
    title: doc.title,
    authors: doc.author_name || ['Unknown Author'],
    cover: coverUrl,
    languages: doc.language,
    tags: doc.subject?.slice(0, 5),
    formats,
    kind: 'book',
    year: doc.first_publish_year,
  };
}

function parseSubjectWork(work: OpenLibrarySubjectWork): CatalogEntry {
  const rawId = work.key.replace(/^\/works\//, '');
  const coverUrl = work.cover_id
    ? `https://covers.openlibrary.org/b/id/${work.cover_id}-M.jpg`
    : undefined;

  const formats: CatalogFormat[] = [];
  if (work.ia) {
    formats.push({
      mime: 'application/epub+zip',
      url: `https://archive.org/download/${work.ia}/${work.ia}.epub`,
      label: 'Free EPUB (Internet Archive)',
    });
  }

  return {
    id: `openlibrary:${rawId}`,
    title: work.title,
    authors: work.authors?.map((a) => a.name) || ['Unknown Author'],
    cover: coverUrl,
    tags: work.subject?.slice(0, 5),
    formats,
    kind: 'book',
    year: work.first_publish_year,
  };
}

export class OpenLibrarySource implements CatalogSource {
  id = 'openlibrary';
  name = 'Open Library';
  icon = 'BookOpen';
  kind = 'books' as const;

  async sections(): Promise<CatalogSection[]> {
    return [
      { id: 'trending', title: 'Trending Today' },
      { id: 'classics', title: 'Classic Literature' },
      { id: 'fiction', title: 'Fiction' },
      { id: 'fantasy', title: 'Fantasy' },
      { id: 'science_fiction', title: 'Sci-Fi' },
      { id: 'romance', title: 'Romance' },
      { id: 'history', title: 'History' },
    ];
  }

  async browse(sectionId: string, cursor?: string): Promise<CatalogPage> {
    const limit = 24;
    const offset = cursor ? parseInt(cursor, 10) : 0;

    if (sectionId === 'trending') {
      const url = 'https://openlibrary.org/trending/daily.json';
      const data = await cachedJson<OpenLibraryTrendingResponse>(
        url,
        async () => {
          const res = await net.fetch(url);
          if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`Open Library trending error: ${res.statusText}`);
          return (await res.json()) as OpenLibraryTrendingResponse;
        },
        { freshMs: 60 * 60_000 },
      );

      const all = data.works || [];
      const sliced = all.slice(offset, offset + limit);
      const next = offset + limit < all.length ? String(offset + limit) : undefined;
      return {
        items: sliced.map(parseOpenLibraryDoc),
        next,
      };
    }

    const subjectMap: Record<string, string> = {
      classics: 'classic_literature',
      fiction: 'fiction',
      fantasy: 'fantasy',
      science_fiction: 'science_fiction',
      romance: 'romance',
      history: 'history',
    };

    const subject = subjectMap[sectionId] || sectionId;
    const url = `https://openlibrary.org/subjects/${subject}.json?limit=${limit}&offset=${offset}`;

    const data = await cachedJson<OpenLibrarySubjectResponse>(
      url,
      async () => {
        const res = await net.fetch(url);
        if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`Open Library subject error: ${res.statusText}`);
        return (await res.json()) as OpenLibrarySubjectResponse;
      },
      { freshMs: 60 * 60_000 },
    );

    const items = (data.works || []).map(parseSubjectWork);
    const hasMore = offset + limit < (data.work_count || 0);
    const next = hasMore ? String(offset + limit) : undefined;

    return { items, next };
  }

  async search(query: string, cursor?: string): Promise<CatalogPage> {
    const limit = 24;
    const page = cursor ? parseInt(cursor, 10) : 1;
    const url = `https://openlibrary.org/search.json?q=${encodeURIComponent(query.trim())}&page=${page}&limit=${limit}&fields=key,title,author_name,cover_i,ebook_access,has_fulltext,id_project_gutenberg,ia,first_publish_year,subject,language`;

    const data = await cachedJson<OpenLibrarySearchResponse>(
      url,
      async () => {
        const res = await net.fetch(url);
        if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`Open Library search error: ${res.statusText}`);
        return (await res.json()) as OpenLibrarySearchResponse;
      },
      { freshMs: 30 * 60_000 },
    );

    const items = (data.docs || []).map(parseOpenLibraryDoc);
    const hasMore = page * limit < (data.numFound || 0);
    const next = hasMore ? String(page + 1) : undefined;

    return { items, next };
  }

  async details(id: string): Promise<CatalogEntry> {
    const rawId = id.replace(/^openlibrary:/, '');
    const workUrl = `https://openlibrary.org/works/${rawId}.json`;
    const searchUrl = `https://openlibrary.org/search.json?q=%2Fworks%2F${rawId}&fields=key,title,author_name,cover_i,ebook_access,has_fulltext,id_project_gutenberg,ia,first_publish_year,subject,language`;

    const [workData, searchData] = await Promise.all([
      cachedJson<OpenLibraryWorkDetail>(
        workUrl,
        async () => {
          const res = await net.fetch(workUrl);
          if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`Open Library details error: ${res.statusText}`);
          return (await res.json()) as OpenLibraryWorkDetail;
        },
        { freshMs: 6 * 60 * 60_000 },
      ).catch(() => null),
      cachedJson<OpenLibrarySearchResponse>(
        searchUrl,
        async () => {
          const res = await net.fetch(searchUrl);
          if (!res.ok) return { numFound: 0, start: 0, docs: [] };
          return (await res.json()) as OpenLibrarySearchResponse;
        },
        { freshMs: 6 * 60 * 60_000 },
      ).catch(() => null),
    ]);

    const doc = searchData?.docs?.[0];
    const title = workData?.title || doc?.title || 'Unknown Title';

    let summary = '';
    if (typeof workData?.description === 'string') {
      summary = workData.description;
    } else if (workData?.description && typeof workData.description === 'object' && 'value' in workData.description) {
      summary = workData.description.value;
    }

    const coverId = (workData?.covers && workData.covers.length > 0) ? workData.covers[0] : doc?.cover_i;
    const coverUrl = coverId ? `https://covers.openlibrary.org/b/id/${coverId}-L.jpg` : undefined;

    const formats: CatalogFormat[] = [];
    if (doc?.id_project_gutenberg && doc.id_project_gutenberg.length > 0) {
      const gid = doc.id_project_gutenberg[0];
      formats.push({
        mime: 'application/epub+zip',
        url: `https://www.gutenberg.org/ebooks/${gid}.epub3.images`,
        label: 'Free EPUB (Project Gutenberg)',
      });
      formats.push({
        mime: 'application/epub+zip',
        url: `https://www.gutenberg.org/ebooks/${gid}.epub.noimages`,
        label: 'Free EPUB (Text only)',
      });
    } else if (doc?.ebook_access !== 'borrowable' && doc?.ebook_access !== 'printdisabled' && doc?.ia && doc.ia.length > 0) {
      const primaryIa = doc.ia[0];
      formats.push({
        mime: 'application/epub+zip',
        url: `https://archive.org/download/${primaryIa}/${primaryIa}.epub`,
        label: 'Free EPUB (Internet Archive)',
      });
    }

    return {
      id: `openlibrary:${rawId}`,
      title,
      authors: doc?.author_name || ['Unknown Author'],
      cover: coverUrl,
      summary,
      tags: workData?.subjects?.slice(0, 8) || doc?.subject?.slice(0, 8),
      formats,
      kind: 'book',
      year: doc?.first_publish_year,
    };
  }

  async download(
    entry: CatalogEntry,
    formatUrl: string,
    onProgress?: (p: number) => void,
  ): Promise<File> {
    const epubs = (entry.formats ?? []).filter((f) => f.mime === 'application/epub+zip');
    let chosenUrl = formatUrl || epubs[0]?.url;

    // 1. Direct Gutenberg download
    if (chosenUrl && chosenUrl.includes('gutenberg.org')) {
      onProgress?.(0.05);
      const blob = await net.download(chosenUrl, {
        onProgress: (p) => onProgress?.(0.05 + p * 0.9),
      });
      onProgress?.(1.0);
      const safeTitle = entry.title.replace(/[<>:"/\\|?*]/g, '_').slice(0, 80);
      return new File([blob], `${safeTitle}.epub`, {
        type: 'application/epub+zip',
      });
    }

    // 2. Internet Archive item with file discovery
    if (chosenUrl && chosenUrl.includes('archive.org/download/')) {
      const match = /archive\.org\/download\/([^/]+)/.exec(chosenUrl);
      const iaId = match ? match[1] : undefined;

      if (iaId) {
        try {
          // Resolve exact clean EPUB from metadata
          const metaRes = await net.fetch(`https://archive.org/metadata/${iaId}/files`);
          if (metaRes.ok) {
            const metaData = (await metaRes.json()) as { result?: Array<{ name: string; format?: string }> };
            const cleanEpub = (metaData.result || []).find(
              (f) => f.name.endsWith('.epub') && !f.name.endsWith('_lcp.epub') && !f.name.endsWith('_daisy.zip'),
            );
            if (cleanEpub) {
              chosenUrl = `https://archive.org/download/${iaId}/${cleanEpub.name}`;
            }
          }
        } catch {
          // Proceed with chosenUrl
        }
      }

      try {
        onProgress?.(0.05);
        const blob = await net.download(chosenUrl, {
          onProgress: (p) => onProgress?.(0.05 + p * 0.9),
        });
        onProgress?.(1.0);
        const safeTitle = entry.title.replace(/[<>:"/\\|?*]/g, '_').slice(0, 80);
        return new File([blob], `${safeTitle}.epub`, {
          type: 'application/epub+zip',
        });
      } catch (iaErr) {
        console.warn('Archive.org download failed, attempting Gutenberg fallback:', iaErr);
      }
    }

    // 3. Fallback: Search Project Gutenberg (Gutendex) for public domain title
    try {
      const gutendexUrl = `https://gutendex.com/books/?search=${encodeURIComponent(entry.title)}`;
      const gRes = await net.fetch(gutendexUrl);
      if (gRes.ok) {
        const gData = (await gRes.json()) as { results?: Array<{ formats?: Record<string, string> }> };
        const gMatch = gData.results?.[0];
        const gEpub = gMatch?.formats?.['application/epub+zip'];
        if (gEpub) {
          onProgress?.(0.1);
          const blob = await net.download(gEpub, {
            onProgress: (p) => onProgress?.(0.1 + p * 0.85),
          });
          onProgress?.(1.0);
          const safeTitle = entry.title.replace(/[<>:"/\\|?*]/g, '_').slice(0, 80);
          return new File([blob], `${safeTitle}.epub`, {
            type: 'application/epub+zip',
          });
        }
      }
    } catch {
      // ignore fallback error
    }

    throw new Error(
      `"${entry.title}" is in copyright or restricted for digital library borrowing on openlibrary.org. No public domain DRM-free EPUB is available.`,
    );
  }
}

export const openLibrarySource = new OpenLibrarySource();

================
File: apps/client/src/components/LookupCard.tsx

import { Fragment, useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useState, type ReactNode } from 'react';
import { ArrowLeft, Copy, ExternalLink, Volume2, X, Maximize2 } from 'lucide-react';
import { LANGUAGES, baseLang, cleanWord, define, langName, pronunciation, translate, wikipedia } from '../lib/lookup';
import { IconButton } from '../design/components/IconButton';
import { SegmentedControl } from '../design/components/SegmentedControl';

export type LookupTab = 'define' | 'translate' | 'wiki';

function useAsync<T>(fn: () => Promise<T>, deps: unknown[]) {
  // Results are tagged with the deps they belong to, so changing the term shows a
  // loading state instead of the previous word's definition until the new one lands.
  const key = JSON.stringify(deps);
  const [state, setState] = useState<{ key: string; data?: T; error?: string } | null>(null);
  useEffect(() => {
    let alive = true;
    fn().then(
      (data) => { if (alive) setState({ key, data }); },
      (e) => { if (alive) setState({ key, error: e instanceof Error ? e.message : String(e) }); },
    );
    return () => { alive = false; };
    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
  }, [key]);
  return state?.key === key ? { loading: false, data: state.data, error: state.error } : { loading: true };
}

function toNodes(node: Node, onLookup: (t: string) => void, key: string): ReactNode {
  if (node.nodeType === Node.TEXT_NODE) return node.textContent;
  if (node.nodeType !== Node.ELEMENT_NODE) return null;
  const el = node as Element;
  const kids = [...el.childNodes].map((c, i) => toNodes(c, onLookup, `${key}.${i}`));
  const href = el.tagName === 'A' ? el.getAttribute('href') ?? '' : '';
  if (href.startsWith('/wiki/') && !href.slice(6).includes(':')) {
    const term = decodeURIComponent(href.slice(6).split('#')[0]).replace(/_/g, ' ');
    return (
      <button
        key={key}
        type="button"
        className="text-[var(--accent)] underline hover:text-[var(--accent-hover)] cursor-pointer"
        onClick={() => onLookup(term)}
      >
        {kids}
      </button>
    );
  }
  if (el.tagName === 'I' || el.tagName === 'EM') return <em key={key}>{kids}</em>;
  if (el.tagName === 'B' || el.tagName === 'STRONG') return <strong key={key}>{kids}</strong>;
  return <Fragment key={key}>{kids}</Fragment>;
}

function RichText({ html, onLookup }: { html: string; onLookup: (t: string) => void }) {
  const nodes = useMemo(() => {
    const doc = new DOMParser().parseFromString(`<div>${html}</div>`, 'text/html');
    const root = doc.body.firstElementChild;
    return root ? [...root.childNodes].map((n, i) => toNodes(n, onLookup, String(i))) : null;
  }, [html, onLookup]);
  return <>{nodes}</>;
}

function DefinePane({ term, lang, onLookup }: { term: string; lang: string; onLookup: (t: string) => void }) {
  const s = useAsync(() => define(term, lang), [term, lang]);
  // Pronunciation comes from a slower, less reliable service; it fills in when ready.
  const p = useAsync(() => pronunciation(term, lang), [term, lang]);
  if (s.loading) return <p className="text-xs text-[var(--text-3)] p-3">Looking up “{cleanWord(term)}”…</p>;
  if (!s.data) return <p className="text-xs text-[var(--text-3)] p-3">No definition found for “{cleanWord(term)}”.</p>;
  const d = { ...s.data, phonetic: s.data.phonetic ?? p.data?.phonetic, audio: s.data.audio ?? p.data?.audio };
  return (
    <div className="space-y-3 p-3 max-h-[220px] overflow-y-auto text-xs">
      <div className="flex items-baseline gap-2">
        <h4 className="text-base font-bold text-[var(--text-1)] capitalize">{d.word}</h4>
        {d.phonetic && <span className="text-xs text-[var(--text-3)]">{d.phonetic}</span>}
        {d.audio && (
          <IconButton
            label="Pronounce"
            size="sm"
            icon={<Volume2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />}
            onClick={() => void new Audio(d.audio).play()}
          />
        )}
      </div>
      {d.groups.map((m, i) => (
        <div key={i} className="space-y-1">
          <span className="font-semibold text-[11px] text-[var(--accent)] uppercase tracking-wider">{m.partOfSpeech}</span>
          <ol className="list-decimal list-inside space-y-1 pl-1 text-[var(--text-1)]">
            {m.senses.slice(0, 3).map((def, j) => (
              <li key={j} className="leading-relaxed">
                <RichText html={def.html} onLookup={onLookup} />
                {def.examples[0] && <p className="text-[var(--text-3)] italic mt-0.5">“{def.examples[0]}”</p>}
              </li>
            ))}
          </ol>
        </div>
      ))}
    </div>
  );
}

function TranslatePane({
  text,
  from,
  to,
  onSpeak,
}: {
  text: string;
  from: string;
  to: string;
  onSpeak: (t: string) => void;
}) {
  const s = useAsync(() => translate(text, from, to), [text, from, to]);
  if (s.loading) return <p className="text-xs text-[var(--text-3)] p-3">Translating…</p>;
  if (!s.data) return <p className="text-xs text-[var(--danger)] p-3">Translation failed: {s.error}</p>;
  const t = s.data;
  return (
    <div className="space-y-3 p-3 text-xs">
      <div className="p-2.5 rounded-[var(--r-md)] bg-[var(--layer-2)] border border-[var(--stroke)] text-sm text-[var(--text-1)] leading-relaxed">
        {t}
      </div>
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-[11px] text-[var(--text-3)]">
        <span>From {langName(from)} to {langName(to)}</span>
        <div className="flex items-center gap-1">
          <IconButton label="Speak" size="sm" icon={<Volume2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />} onClick={() => onSpeak(t)} />
          <IconButton label="Copy" size="sm" icon={<Copy className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />} onClick={() => void navigator.clipboard.writeText(t)} />
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

function WikiPane({ term, lang }: { term: string; lang: string }) {
  const s = useAsync(() => wikipedia(term, lang), [term, lang]);
  if (s.loading) return <p className="text-xs text-[var(--text-3)] p-3">Searching Wikipedia…</p>;
  if (!s.data) return <p className="text-xs text-[var(--text-3)] p-3">No Wikipedia article found for “{term}”.</p>;
  const w = s.data;
  return (
    <div className="space-y-2.5 p-3 max-h-[220px] overflow-y-auto text-xs">
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
        <h4 className="text-sm font-bold text-[var(--text-1)]">{w.title}</h4>
        {w.url && (
          <a
            href={w.url}
            target="_blank"
            rel="noreferrer"
            className="text-[var(--accent)] hover:underline inline-flex items-center gap-1"
          >
            Read more <ExternalLink className="w-3 h-3" />
          </a>
        )}
      </div>
      <div className="text-[var(--text-2)] leading-relaxed">
        <p>{w.extract}</p>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

export function LookupCard({
  text,
  tab,
  onTab,
  lang,
  translateTo,
  onTranslateTo,
  onSpeak,
  onClose,
  onExpand,
}: {
  text: string;
  tab: LookupTab;
  onTab: (tab: LookupTab | null) => void;
  lang: string;
  translateTo: string;
  onTranslateTo: (lang: string) => void;
  onSpeak: (text: string) => void;
  onClose: () => void;
  onExpand?: () => void;
}) {
  const [currentTab, setCurrentTab] = useState<LookupTab>(tab);
  // Words followed from links inside a definition; the last one is shown.
  const [trail, setTrail] = useState<string[]>([]);
  const term = trail.at(-1) ?? text;

  const onFollowLink = useCallback((t: string) => setTrail((prev) => [...prev, t]), []);

  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col border-b border-[var(--stroke)] bg-[var(--layer-solid)] text-[var(--text-1)] select-none">
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between px-3 py-2 border-b border-[var(--stroke)]">
        <SegmentedControl
          size="sm"
          options={[
            { value: 'define', label: 'Define' },
            { value: 'translate', label: 'Translate' },
            { value: 'wiki', label: 'Wikipedia' },
          ]}
          value={currentTab}
          onChange={(val) => {
            setCurrentTab(val);
            onTab(val);
          }}
        />

        <div className="flex items-center gap-1">
          {currentTab === 'translate' && (
            <select
              value={translateTo}
              onChange={(e) => onTranslateTo(e.target.value)}
              className="h-6 text-[11px] rounded-[var(--r-sm)] bg-[var(--layer-2)] border border-[var(--stroke)] text-[var(--text-1)] px-1 outline-none"
            >
              {LANGUAGES.map(([code, name]) => (
                <option key={code} value={code}>
                  {name}
                </option>
              ))}
            </select>
          )}

          {onExpand && (
            <IconButton label="Expand to side panel" icon={<Maximize2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />} size="sm" onClick={onExpand} />
          )}

          <IconButton label="Close lookup" icon={<X className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />} size="sm" onClick={onClose} />
        </div>
      </div>

      <div>
        {currentTab === 'define' && (
          <>
            {trail.length > 0 && (
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setTrail((t) => t.slice(0, -1))}
                className="flex items-center gap-1 px-3 pt-2 text-[11px] text-[var(--accent)] hover:underline cursor-pointer"
              >
                <ArrowLeft className="w-3 h-3" /> Back to “{cleanWord(trail.at(-2) ?? text)}”
              </button>
            )}
            <DefinePane term={term} lang={lang} onLookup={onFollowLink} />
          </>
        )}
        {currentTab === 'translate' && (
          <TranslatePane text={text} from={baseLang(lang)} to={translateTo} onSpeak={onSpeak} />
        )}
        {currentTab === 'wiki' && <WikiPane term={text} lang={baseLang(lang)} />}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

import { useState, useRef, useEffect } from 'react';
import { Copy, Download, X, Check } from 'lucide-react';
import { Button } from '../design/components/Button';
import { IconButton } from '../design/components/IconButton';

export interface QuoteCardShareProps {
  quote: string;
  title: string;
  author: string;
  onClose: () => void;
}

export type CardThemeId = 'paper' | 'obsidian' | 'sepia' | 'sage';

const CARD_THEMES: Record<
  CardThemeId,
  { name: string; bg: string; text: string; subtext: string; quoteMark: string; border: string }
> = {
  paper: {
    name: 'Paper',
    bg: '#fcfbfa',
    text: '#1a1a1a',
    subtext: '#666666',
    quoteMark: '#d0c8b8',
    border: '#e8e4dc',
  },
  obsidian: {
    name: 'Obsidian',
    bg: '#141518',
    text: '#f2f2f2',
    subtext: '#8a8d93',
    quoteMark: '#2b2d33',
    border: '#2a2c33',
  },
  sepia: {
    name: 'Sepia',
    bg: '#f4ece1',
    text: '#4a3728',
    subtext: '#7d6856',
    quoteMark: '#dfd2c0',
    border: '#ded4c3',
  },
  sage: {
    name: 'Sage',
    bg: '#edf4ee',
    text: '#1f3322',
    subtext: '#526955',
    quoteMark: '#cde0cf',
    border: '#cde0cf',
  },
};

/**
 * Greedy word wrap for canvas text. Words wider than the line are broken by
 * character; with `maxLines`, the last line ends in an ellipsis if text remains.
 */
function wrapText(ctx: CanvasRenderingContext2D, text: string, maxWidth: number, maxLines = Infinity): string[] {
  const fits = (s: string) => ctx.measureText(s).width <= maxWidth;
  const lines: string[] = [];
  let line = '';
  for (const word of text.split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean)) {
    const candidate = line ? `${line} ${word}` : word;
    if (fits(candidate)) {
      line = candidate;
      continue;
    }
    if (line) lines.push(line);
    line = word;
    while (!fits(line)) {
      let cut = line.length - 1;
      while (cut > 1 && !fits(line.slice(0, cut))) cut--;
      lines.push(line.slice(0, cut));
      line = line.slice(cut);
    }
  }
  if (line) lines.push(line);
  if (lines.length <= maxLines) return lines;
  const kept = lines.slice(0, maxLines);
  let last = kept[maxLines - 1];
  while (last && !fits(`${last}…`)) last = last.slice(0, -1);
  kept[maxLines - 1] = `${last.trimEnd()}…`;
  return kept;
}

export function QuoteCardShare({ quote, title, author, onClose }: QuoteCardShareProps) {
  const [theme, setTheme] = useState<CardThemeId>('obsidian');
  const [copied, setCopied] = useState(false);
  const canvasRef = useRef<HTMLCanvasElement>(null);

  const cleanQuote = quote.replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim();

  // Draw card on canvas whenever theme or quote changes
  useEffect(() => {
    const canvas = canvasRef.current;
    if (!canvas) return;
    const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');
    if (!ctx) return;

    const t = CARD_THEMES[theme];
    const width = 1080;
    const height = 1350; // 4:5 aspect ratio optimal for mobile & sharing

    canvas.width = width;
    canvas.height = height;

    // Background
    ctx.fillStyle = t.bg;
    ctx.fillRect(0, 0, width, height);

    // Decorative border
    ctx.strokeStyle = t.border;
    ctx.lineWidth = 4;
    ctx.strokeRect(40, 40, width - 80, height - 80);

    // Giant quotation mark
    ctx.fillStyle = t.quoteMark;
    ctx.font = 'bold 180px Georgia, serif';
    ctx.fillText('“', 80, 200);

    const x = 100;
    const maxWidth = width - 200;

    // Footer block (divider + title + author) is measured first so the quote can
    // take whatever room is left. Long quotes shrink, then get an ellipsis, instead
    // of running off the bottom of the card; long titles wrap to two lines.
    ctx.font = '600 34px "Inter Variable", system-ui, sans-serif';
    const titleLines = wrapText(ctx, title, maxWidth, 2);
    const footerHeight = 80 + 70 + (titleLines.length - 1) * 44 + 46 + 40;
    const quoteTop = 300;
    const quoteBottom = height - 110 - footerHeight;

    let fontSize = 52;
    let quoteLines: string[] = [];
    let lineHeight = 0;
    for (; fontSize >= 24; fontSize -= 2) {
      ctx.font = `${fontSize}px Literata, Georgia, serif`;
      lineHeight = Math.round(fontSize * 1.5);
      quoteLines = wrapText(ctx, cleanQuote, maxWidth);
      if (quoteTop + (quoteLines.length - 1) * lineHeight <= quoteBottom) break;
    }
    const maxLines = Math.max(1, Math.floor((quoteBottom - quoteTop) / lineHeight) + 1);
    if (quoteLines.length > maxLines) quoteLines = wrapText(ctx, cleanQuote, maxWidth, maxLines);

    ctx.fillStyle = t.text;
    let y = quoteTop;
    quoteLines.forEach((l, i) => {
      if (i > 0) y += lineHeight;
      ctx.fillText(l, x, y);
    });

    // Divider line
    y += 80;
    ctx.strokeStyle = t.quoteMark;
    ctx.lineWidth = 2;
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.moveTo(x, y);
    ctx.lineTo(x + 120, y);
    ctx.stroke();

    // Book Title & Author
    y += 70;
    ctx.fillStyle = t.text;
    ctx.font = '600 34px "Inter Variable", system-ui, sans-serif';
    titleLines.forEach((l, i) => {
      if (i > 0) y += 44;
      ctx.fillText(l, x, y);
    });

    y += 46;
    ctx.fillStyle = t.subtext;
    ctx.font = '28px "Inter Variable", system-ui, sans-serif';
    ctx.fillText(wrapText(ctx, author, maxWidth, 1)[0] ?? '', x, y);

    // Verso watermark at bottom
    ctx.fillStyle = t.subtext;
    ctx.font = '600 22px "Inter Variable", system-ui, sans-serif';
    ctx.fillText('VERSO', width - 180, height - 90);
  }, [cleanQuote, title, author, theme]);

  const handleCopyImage = async () => {
    const canvas = canvasRef.current;
    if (!canvas) return;

    canvas.toBlob(async (blob) => {
      if (!blob) return;
      try {
        await navigator.clipboard.write([new ClipboardItem({ 'image/png': blob })]);
        setCopied(true);
        setTimeout(() => setCopied(false), 2000);
      } catch (err) {
        console.error('Clipboard write failed:', err);
      }
    }, 'image/png');
  };

  const handleDownload = () => {
    const canvas = canvasRef.current;
    if (!canvas) return;

    const url = canvas.toDataURL('image/png');
    const link = document.createElement('a');
    link.href = url;
    link.download = `quote-${Date.now()}.png`;
    document.body.appendChild(link);
    link.click();
    document.body.removeChild(link);
  };

  return (
    <div
      role="dialog"
      aria-modal="true"
      aria-label="Quote card share"
      className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center p-4 bg-black/80 backdrop-blur-md select-none animate-fade-in"
    >
      <div className="relative w-full max-w-lg max-h-[calc(100dvh-2rem)] overflow-y-auto rounded-[var(--r-xl)] bg-[var(--layer-solid)] border border-[var(--stroke)] shadow-2xl p-4 sm:p-6 flex flex-col gap-4 sm:gap-5">
        {/* Header */}
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between border-b border-[var(--stroke)] pb-3">
          <div>
            <h3 className="text-base font-bold text-[var(--text-1)]">Share Quote Card</h3>
            <p className="text-xs text-[var(--text-3)]">
              Export high-resolution typography card for social sharing
            </p>
          </div>
          <IconButton
            icon={<X className="w-5 h-5 text-[var(--text-2)]" />}
            label="Close"
            onClick={onClose}
          />
        </div>

        {/* Theme Picker */}
        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
          <span className="text-xs font-semibold text-[var(--text-2)] mr-1">Style:</span>
          {(Object.keys(CARD_THEMES) as CardThemeId[]).map((tid) => (
            <button
              key={tid}
              onClick={() => setTheme(tid)}
              className={`px-3 py-1.5 rounded-full text-xs font-medium transition-all ${
                theme === tid
                  ? 'bg-[var(--accent)] text-[var(--on-accent)] font-semibold shadow-xs'
                  : 'bg-[var(--layer-3)] text-[var(--text-2)] hover:text-[var(--text-1)] border border-[var(--stroke)]'
              }`}
            >
              {CARD_THEMES[tid].name}
            </button>
          ))}
        </div>

        {/* Canvas Preview Container */}
        <div className="w-full aspect-[4/5] max-h-[min(380px,50dvh)] flex items-center justify-center bg-[var(--layer-2)] rounded-[var(--r-lg)] border border-[var(--stroke)] p-3 overflow-hidden">
          <canvas
            ref={canvasRef}
            className="h-full w-auto max-w-full object-contain rounded-md shadow-md"
          />
        </div>

        {/* Footer Actions */}
        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-end gap-2 pt-2 border-t border-[var(--stroke)]">
          <Button
            variant="standard"
            icon={copied ? <Check className="w-4 h-4 text-[var(--success)]" /> : <Copy className="w-4 h-4" />}
            onClick={handleCopyImage}
          >
            {copied ? 'Copied Image' : 'Copy Image'}
          </Button>
          <Button
            variant="accent"
            icon={<Download className="w-4 h-4" />}
            onClick={handleDownload}
          >
            Download PNG
          </Button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

import { forwardRef, type ButtonHTMLAttributes, type ReactNode } from 'react';
import { clsx } from 'clsx';
import { twMerge } from 'tailwind-merge';

export type ButtonVariant = 'accent' | 'standard' | 'subtle' | 'danger';
export type ButtonSize = 'sm' | 'md' | 'lg';

export interface ButtonProps extends ButtonHTMLAttributes<HTMLButtonElement> {
  variant?: ButtonVariant;
  size?: ButtonSize;
  icon?: ReactNode;
  children?: ReactNode;
}

export const Button = forwardRef<HTMLButtonElement, ButtonProps>(
  ({ variant = 'standard', size = 'md', icon, children, className, disabled, ...props }, ref) => {
    const baseClasses =
      'inline-flex items-center justify-center whitespace-nowrap max-w-full font-medium transition-colors select-none cursor-pointer outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-[var(--accent)] disabled:pointer-events-none disabled:opacity-40';

    const sizeClasses: Record<ButtonSize, string> = {
      sm: 'h-[28px] px-2.5 text-xs rounded-[var(--r-sm)] gap-1.5',
      md: 'h-[32px] px-3.5 text-sm rounded-[var(--r-md)] gap-2',
      lg: 'h-[40px] px-4 text-base rounded-[var(--r-md)] gap-2.5',
    };

    const variantClasses: Record<ButtonVariant, string> = {
      accent:
        'bg-[var(--accent)] text-[var(--on-accent)] hover:bg-[var(--accent-hover)] active:bg-[var(--accent-pressed)] shadow-sm font-semibold',
      standard:
        'bg-[var(--layer-2)] hover:bg-[var(--layer-3)] active:bg-[var(--layer-1)] text-[var(--text-1)] border border-[var(--stroke)] shadow-xs',
      subtle:
        'bg-transparent hover:bg-[var(--layer-2)] active:bg-[var(--layer-3)] text-[var(--text-1)] border-transparent',
      danger:
        'bg-transparent hover:bg-[color-mix(in_oklab,var(--danger)_18%,transparent)] active:bg-[color-mix(in_oklab,var(--danger)_30%,transparent)] text-[var(--danger)] border border-[color-mix(in_oklab,var(--danger)_35%,transparent)]',
    };

    return (
      <button
        ref={ref}
        disabled={disabled}
        className={twMerge(clsx(baseClasses, sizeClasses[size], variantClasses[variant], className))}
        {...props}
      >
        {icon && <span className="inline-flex shrink-0">{icon}</span>}
        {/* inline-flex: callers often put an icon inside children, and preflight makes
            svg display:block, which stacked the icon above the label. */}
        {children && <span className="inline-flex items-center min-w-0 gap-[inherit]">{children}</span>}
      </button>
    );
  },
);

Button.displayName = 'Button';

  onOpenChange,
  onClose,
  title,
  description,
  children,
  footer,
  className,

        </RadixDialog.Content>
      </RadixDialog.Portal>
    </RadixDialog.Root>
  );
};

File: apps/client/src/design/components/SegmentedControl.tsx

import React, { useRef, useEffect, useState, useMemo } from 'react';
import { clsx } from 'clsx';
import { twMerge } from 'tailwind-merge';
import { useSwipeTabs } from '../../lib/useSwipeTabs';

export interface SegmentOption<T extends string = string> {
  value: T;
  label: React.ReactNode;
  icon?: React.ReactNode;
}

export interface SegmentedControlProps<T extends string = string> {
  options: SegmentOption<T>[];
  value: T;
  onChange: (value: T) => void;
  className?: string;
  size?: 'sm' | 'md';
}

export function SegmentedControl<T extends string = string>({
  options,
  value,
  onChange,
  className,
  size = 'md',
}: SegmentedControlProps<T>) {
  const containerRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const [indicatorStyle, setIndicatorStyle] = useState<{ left: number; width: number }>({
    left: 0,
    width: 0,
  });

  const tabValues = useMemo(() => options.map((o) => o.value), [options]);
  const { containerProps } = useSwipeTabs({
    tabs: tabValues,
    value,
    onChange,
  });

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!containerRef.current) return;
    const activeEl = containerRef.current.querySelector<HTMLButtonElement>(`[data-active="true"]`);
    if (activeEl) {
      setIndicatorStyle({
        left: activeEl.offsetLeft,
        width: activeEl.offsetWidth,
      });
    }
  }, [value, options]);

  return (
    <div
      ref={containerRef}
      role="tablist"
      {...containerProps}
      className={twMerge(
        clsx(
          'relative inline-flex items-center max-w-full overflow-x-auto no-scrollbar p-1 bg-[var(--layer-2)] border border-[var(--stroke)] rounded-[var(--r-pill)] select-none',
          size === 'sm' ? 'h-[28px]' : 'h-[36px]',
          className,
        ),
      )}
    >
      {/* Sliding thumb */}
      {indicatorStyle.width > 0 && (
        <div
          className="absolute top-1 bottom-1 bg-[var(--layer-solid)] rounded-[var(--r-pill)] shadow-sm transition-all duration-[var(--d-normal)] ease-[var(--ease-out)] pointer-events-none border border-[var(--stroke)]"
          style={{
            left: `${indicatorStyle.left}px`,
            width: `${indicatorStyle.width}px`,
          }}
        />
      )}

      {options.map((opt) => {
        const isActive = opt.value === value;
        return (
          <button
            key={opt.value}
            type="button"
            role="tab"
            aria-selected={isActive}
            data-active={isActive}
            onClick={() => onChange(opt.value)}
            className={clsx(
              'relative z-10 inline-flex shrink-0 items-center justify-center whitespace-nowrap gap-1.5 px-3 h-full rounded-[var(--r-pill)] font-medium transition-colors cursor-pointer outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-[var(--accent)]',
              size === 'sm' ? 'text-xs' : 'text-sm',
              isActive
                ? 'text-[var(--text-1)] font-semibold'
                : 'text-[var(--text-2)] hover:text-[var(--text-1)]',
            )}
          >
            {opt.icon && <span className="shrink-0">{opt.icon}</span>}
            <span>{opt.label}</span>
          </button>
        );
      })}
    </div>
  );
}

File: apps/client/src/lib/androidIntegration.ts

import { isTauri } from './tauri';
import { handleOpenedFile } from './tauriIntegration';
import { initAndroidBackHandler } from './backStack';

/**
 * Initializes Android-specific system integrations:
 * - Back button stack handler (layers dismiss in order, falls back to route back)
 * - ACTION_VIEW and ACTION_SEND file import
 */
export function initAndroidIntegration(
  navigate: (path: string) => void,
  goBack: () => void,
): () => void {
  if (!isTauri()) return () => {};

  // Setup Back Button Stack
  const cleanupBack = initAndroidBackHandler(() => {
    goBack();
  });

  // Setup Intent & File Open listener
  let cleanupFileOpen = () => {};
  void (async () => {
    try {
      const { listen } = await import('@tauri-apps/api/event');
      cleanupFileOpen = await listen<string>('verso://file-open', (event) => {
        if (event.payload) {
          void handleOpenedFile(event.payload, (bookId) => {
            navigate(`/read/${bookId}`);
          });
        }
      });
    } catch (err) {
      console.warn('Failed to register Android intent listener:', err);
    }
  })();

  return () => {
    cleanupBack();
    cleanupFileOpen();
  };
}

File: apps/client/src/lib/importBook.ts

import { db, dirty, type Book } from '../db';
import { makeBook, type FoliateBook } from './foliate';
import { sha256 } from './hash';

export const ACCEPT = '.epub,.mobi,.azw3,.azw,.fb2,.fbz,.cbz,.pdf';

const FORMATS: Record<string, string> = {
  epub: 'EPUB',
  mobi: 'MOBI',
  azw3: 'AZW3',
  azw: 'AZW',
  fb2: 'FB2',
  fbz: 'FB2',
  cbz: 'CBZ',
  pdf: 'PDF',
};

function text(x: unknown): string {
  if (typeof x === 'string') return x.trim();
  if (Array.isArray(x)) return text(x[0]);
  if (x && typeof x === 'object') {
    const o = x as Record<string, unknown>;
    if ('name' in o) return text(o.name);
    return text(Object.values(o)[0]);
  }
  return '';
}

function people(x: unknown): string {
  const list = Array.isArray(x) ? x : x ? [x] : [];
  return list.map(text).filter(Boolean).join(', ');
}

async function shrinkCover(blob: Blob): Promise<Blob> {
  try {
    const bmp = await createImageBitmap(blob);
    const scale = Math.min(1, 480 / bmp.width);
    const canvas = document.createElement('canvas');
    canvas.width = Math.round(bmp.width * scale);
    canvas.height = Math.round(bmp.height * scale);
    const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');
    if (!ctx) return blob;
    ctx.drawImage(bmp, 0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);
    bmp.close();
    return await new Promise((resolve) =>
      canvas.toBlob((b) => resolve(b ?? blob), 'image/jpeg', 0.85),
    );
  } catch {
    return blob;
  }
}

export interface ImportResult {
  added: number;
  skipped: number;
  failed: { name: string; error: string }[];
}

export interface ImportOptions {
  onProgress?: (
    index: number,
    total: number,
    file: File,
    status: 'processing' | 'done' | 'skipped' | 'error',
    error?: string,
  ) => void;
  onDuplicate?: (
    existingBook: Book,
    newFile: File,
  ) => Promise<'replace' | 'keep-both' | 'skip'>;
  signal?: AbortSignal;
}

export async function importFiles(
  files: File[],
  options?: ImportOptions,
): Promise<ImportResult> {
  const result: ImportResult = { added: 0, skipped: 0, failed: [] };
  const total = files.length;

  for (let i = 0; i < total; i++) {
    if (options?.signal?.aborted) {
      break;
    }
    const file = files[i];
    options?.onProgress?.(i, total, file, 'processing');

    try {
      const outcome = await importOne(file, options);
      if (outcome) {
        result.added++;
        options?.onProgress?.(i, total, file, 'done');
      } else {
        result.skipped++;
        options?.onProgress?.(i, total, file, 'skipped');
      }
    } catch (e) {
      const msg = e instanceof Error ? e.message : String(e);
      result.failed.push({ name: file.name, error: msg });
      options?.onProgress?.(i, total, file, 'error', msg);
    }
  }

  return result;
}

export async function importOne(
  file: File,
  options?: ImportOptions,
): Promise<boolean> {
  const ext = file.name.split('.').pop()?.toLowerCase() ?? '';
  const hash = await sha256(await file.arrayBuffer());
  const existing = await db.books.get(hash);
  if (existing && !existing.deleted_at) {
    const hasFile = await db.files.get(hash);
    if (!hasFile) {
      await db.files.put({ hash, blob: file });
      try {
        const book = await makeBook(file);
        const rawCover = await book.getCover?.();
        if (rawCover) {
          const coverBlob = await shrinkCover(rawCover);
          await db.covers.put({ id: hash, blob: coverBlob });
        }
      } catch {
        // cover extraction is optional
      }
      return true;
    }
    return false;
  }

  const book = await makeBook(file);
  const md = book.metadata ?? {};

  const title = text(md.title) || file.name.replace(/\.[^.]+$/, '');
  const author = people(md.author) || 'Unknown author';
  const normTitle = title.trim().toLowerCase();
  const normAuthor = author.trim().toLowerCase();

  // Duplicate detection by title and author with differing hash
  const potentialDup = await db.books
    .filter(
      (b) =>
        !b.deleted_at &&
        b.title.trim().toLowerCase() === normTitle &&
        b.author.trim().toLowerCase() === normAuthor &&
        b.file_hash !== hash,
    )
    .first();

  let coverBlob: Blob | null = null;
  let coverHash: string | null = null;
  try {
    const rawCover = await book.getCover?.();
    if (rawCover) {
      coverBlob = await shrinkCover(rawCover);
      coverHash = await sha256(await coverBlob.arrayBuffer());
    }
  } catch {
    // Ignore broken cover images; fallback to generated cover
  }

  if (potentialDup) {
    const decision = options?.onDuplicate
      ? await options.onDuplicate(potentialDup, file)
      : 'keep-both';

    if (decision === 'skip') {
      return false;
    }

    if (decision === 'replace') {
      await db.files.put({ hash, blob: file });
      if (coverBlob) {
        await db.covers.put({ id: potentialDup.id, blob: coverBlob });
      }
      await db.books.update(potentialDup.id, {
        file_hash: hash,
        file_size: file.size,
        format: FORMATS[ext] ?? ext.toUpperCase(),
        cover_hash: coverHash ?? potentialDup.cover_hash,
        ...dirty(),
      });
      enqueueWordCount(potentialDup.id, book, FORMATS[ext] ?? ext.toUpperCase());
      return true;
    }
    // If 'keep-both', proceed with normal import
  }

  if (coverBlob) {
    await db.covers.put({ id: hash, blob: coverBlob });
  }

  await db.files.put({ hash, blob: file });

  const t = Date.now();
  const row: Book = {
    id: hash,
    title,
    author,
    format: FORMATS[ext] ?? ext.toUpperCase(),
    file_hash: hash,
    file_size: file.size,
    cover_hash: coverHash,
    meta: {
      file_name: file.name,
      mime: file.type,
      language: text(md.language),
      publisher: people(md.publisher),
      description: text(md.description)
        .replace(/<[^>]+>/g, ' ')
        .replace(/\s+/g, ' ')
        .trim(),
      series: text(
        (md.belongsTo as Record<string, unknown> | undefined)?.series,
      ),
    },
    added_at: existing?.added_at ?? t,
    last_opened_at: existing?.last_opened_at ?? null,
    deleted_at: null,
    ...dirty(),
  };

  await db.books.put(row);
  enqueueWordCount(hash, book, row.format);
  return true;
}

// Background Word Count Processing Queue
interface WordCountJob {
  bookId: string;
  book: FoliateBook;
  format: string;
}

const wordCountQueue: WordCountJob[] = [];
let isProcessingQueue = false;

function enqueueWordCount(bookId: string, book: FoliateBook, format: string) {
  if (format === 'PDF' || format === 'CBZ' || book.rendition?.layout === 'pre-paginated') {
    return;
  }
  wordCountQueue.push({ bookId, book, format });
  if (!isProcessingQueue) {
    void processWordCountQueue();
  }
}

async function processWordCountQueue() {
  if (wordCountQueue.length === 0) {
    isProcessingQueue = false;
    return;
  }

  isProcessingQueue = true;
  const job = wordCountQueue.shift();
  if (job) {
    try {
      await countText(job.bookId, job.book);
    } catch {
      // Ignore background counting errors
    }
  }

  // Yield to browser frame before next item in queue
  if (typeof requestIdleCallback !== 'undefined') {
    requestIdleCallback(() => void processWordCountQueue());
  } else {
    setTimeout(() => void processWordCountQueue(), 50);
  }
}

async function countText(id: string, book: FoliateBook) {
  let words = 0;
  let paragraphs = 0;
  const sections = book.sections ?? [];

  for (const section of sections) {
    if (section.linear === 'no' || !section.createDocument) continue;
    try {
      const doc = await section.createDocument();
      words +=
        (doc.body?.textContent ?? '').match(
          /[\p{L}\p{N}]+(?:['’-][\p{L}\p{N}]+)*/gu,
        )?.length ?? 0;
      paragraphs += doc.querySelectorAll(
        'p, li, h1, h2, h3, h4, h5, h6, blockquote, dd',
      ).length;
    } catch {
      // Skip unreadable sections
    }
    // Non-blocking tick between sections
    await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 0));
  }

  const current = await db.books.get(id);
  if (current) {
    await db.books.update(id, {
      meta: { ...current.meta, word_count: words, paragraph_count: paragraphs },
      ...dirty(),
    });
  }
}

File: apps/client/src/pages/Updates.tsx
