import { useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import { useLiveQuery } from 'dexie-react-hooks';
import { BookOpen, Check, ExternalLink, RotateCw, Sparkles } from 'lucide-react';
import { db, type Series } from '../db';
import { mangaDexSource } from '../sources/mangadex';
import { getSourceById } from '../sources/registry';
import type { ChapterInfo, MangaSource } from '../sources/types';
import { openInAppWindow } from '../lib/tauri';
import { isFollowedSeries } from '../lib/series';
import { CommandBar } from '../design/components/CommandBar';
import { Button } from '../design/components/Button';
import { IconButton } from '../design/components/IconButton';
import { EmptyState } from '../design/components/EmptyState';
import { ProgressRing } from '../design/components/Progress';
import { PullToRefresh } from '../components/PullToRefresh';

type Update = ChapterInfo & { seriesId: string };

const WINDOW_DAYS = 30;
const DAY_MS = 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000;

// Kept for the session so switching tabs doesn't refetch; the button refreshes.
let cache: { key: string; at: number; items: Update[] } | null = null;
const CACHE_MS = 15 * 60 * 1000;

function dayLabel(iso: string | undefined): string {
  if (!iso) return 'Unknown date';
  const d = new Date(iso);
  const today = new Date();
  const startOf = (x: Date) => new Date(x.getFullYear(), x.getMonth(), x.getDate()).getTime();
  const diff = Math.round((startOf(today) - startOf(d)) / DAY_MS);
  if (diff === 0) return 'Today';
  if (diff === 1) return 'Yesterday';
  return d.toLocaleDateString(undefined, { weekday: 'long', month: 'short', day: 'numeric' });
}

export default function Updates() {
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const followed = useLiveQuery(
    () => db.series.filter((s) => isFollowedSeries(s)).toArray(),
    [],
  );
  const readIds = useLiveQuery(
    async () =>
      new Set(
        (await db.chapter_progress.filter((p) => !p.deleted_at && p.read).toArray()).map(
          (p) => p.chapter_id,
        ),
      ),
    [],
    new Set<string>(),
  );

  const [items, setItems] = useState<Update[] | null>(null);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const seq = useRef(0);

  const seriesKey = useMemo(
    () => (followed ?? []).map((s) => s.id).sort().join(','),
    [followed],
  );
  const seriesById = useMemo(
    () => new Map<string, Series>((followed ?? []).map((s) => [s.id, s])),
    [followed],
  );

  const load = useCallback(
    async (force: boolean) => {
      if (!seriesKey) return;
      if (!force && cache && cache.key === seriesKey && Date.now() - cache.at < CACHE_MS) {
        setItems(cache.items);
        return;
      }
      const ticket = ++seq.current;
      setLoading(true);
      setError(null);
      try {
        const mdSeriesIds = (followed ?? [])
          .filter((s) => s.source === 'mangadex' || s.id.startsWith('mangadex:'))
          .map((s) => s.id);

        const otherSeries = (followed ?? []).filter(
          (s) => s.source !== 'mangadex' && !s.id.startsWith('mangadex:'),
        );

        let result: Update[] = [];
        if (mdSeriesIds.length > 0) {
          result = await mangaDexSource.recentChapters(
            mdSeriesIds,
            Date.now() - WINDOW_DAYS * DAY_MS,
          );
        }

        // Fetch latest chapters for other followed series
        for (const ser of otherSeries) {
          try {
            const src = (await getSourceById(ser.id)) || (await getSourceById(ser.source));
            if (src && 'chapters' in src) {
              const chs = await (src as MangaSource).chapters(ser.id);
              const cutoff = Date.now() - WINDOW_DAYS * DAY_MS;
              for (const ch of chs.slice(0, 5)) {
                const pubTime = ch.publishedAt ? new Date(ch.publishedAt).getTime() : Date.now();
                if (pubTime >= cutoff) {
                  result.push({ ...ch, seriesId: ser.id });
                }
              }
            }
          } catch {
            // Ignore non-blocking failure for individual series
          }
        }

        result.sort((a, b) => (b.publishedAt ?? '').localeCompare(a.publishedAt ?? ''));

        if (ticket !== seq.current) return;
        cache = { key: seriesKey, at: Date.now(), items: result };
        setItems(result);
      } catch (err) {
        if (ticket !== seq.current) return;
        setError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err));
      } finally {
        if (ticket === seq.current) setLoading(false);
      }
    },
    [seriesKey, followed],
  );

  useEffect(() => {
    void load(false);
  }, [load]);

  const groups = useMemo(() => {
    const out: { label: string; items: Update[] }[] = [];
    for (const u of items ?? []) {
      const label = dayLabel(u.publishedAt);
      const last = out.at(-1);
      if (last?.label === label) last.items.push(u);
      else out.push({ label, items: [u] });
    }
    return out;
  }, [items]);

  const unread = (items ?? []).filter((u) => !readIds.has(u.id)).length;

  const open = (u: Update) => {
    if (u.externalUrl) void openInAppWindow(u.externalUrl, u.title || 'Chapter');
    else navigate(`/read/manga/${encodeURIComponent(u.seriesId)}/${encodeURIComponent(u.id)}`);
  };

  let body: React.ReactNode;
  if (followed === undefined) {
    body = null;
  } else if (followed.length === 0) {
    body = (
      <EmptyState
        icon={<Sparkles className="w-7 h-7" />}
        title="No followed manga yet"
        description="Follow a series in the Store and its new chapters will show up here."
        action={
          <Button variant="accent" onClick={() => navigate('/store')}>
            Browse manga
          </Button>
        }
      />
    );
  } else if (error && !items) {
    body = (
      <EmptyState
        title="Couldn't check for new chapters"
        description={error}
        action={
          <Button variant="standard" icon={<RotateCw className="w-4 h-4" />} onClick={() => void load(true)}>
            Try again
          </Button>
        }
      />
    );
  } else if (!items) {
    body = (
      <div className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center py-20 gap-3">
        <ProgressRing size={32} />
        <p className="text-sm text-[var(--text-3)]">Checking {followed.length} followed series…</p>
      </div>
    );
  } else if (items.length === 0) {
    body = (
      <EmptyState
        icon={<Check className="w-7 h-7" />}
        title="You're all caught up"
        description={`No new chapters from the series you follow in the last ${WINDOW_DAYS} days.`}
      />
    );
  } else {
    body = (
      <div className="flex flex-col gap-6 max-w-3xl mx-auto w-full">
        {groups.map((g) => (
          <section key={g.label} className="flex flex-col gap-2">
            <h2 className="text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider text-[var(--text-3)]">{g.label}</h2>
            {g.items.map((u) => {
              const s = seriesById.get(u.seriesId);
              const isRead = readIds.has(u.id);
              return (
                <button
                  key={u.id}
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => open(u)}
                  className={`flex items-center gap-3 p-2.5 rounded-[var(--r-md)] border border-[var(--stroke)] text-left cursor-pointer transition-colors hover:bg-[var(--layer-3)] focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-[var(--accent)] outline-none ${
                    isRead ? 'bg-[var(--layer-1)] opacity-60' : 'bg-[var(--layer-2)]'
                  }`}
                >
                  <div className="w-10 shrink-0 aspect-[2/3] rounded-[var(--r-sm)] overflow-hidden bg-[var(--layer-3)]">
                    {s?.cover_url && (
                      <img src={s.cover_url} alt="" loading="lazy" decoding="async" className="w-full h-full object-cover" />
                    )}
                  </div>
                  <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
                    <p className="text-sm font-semibold text-[var(--text-1)] truncate">{s?.title ?? 'Unknown series'}</p>
                    <p className="text-xs text-[var(--text-2)] truncate">{u.title}</p>
                    <p className="text-[11px] text-[var(--text-3)] truncate">
                      {u.externalUrl ? 'Official release · opens on the publisher’s site' : u.scanlationGroup}
                    </p>
                  </div>
                  {u.externalUrl ? (
                    <ExternalLink className="w-4 h-4 text-[var(--text-3)] shrink-0" />
                  ) : isRead ? (
                    <Check className="w-4 h-4 text-[var(--success)] shrink-0" />
                  ) : (
                    <BookOpen className="w-4 h-4 text-[var(--accent)] shrink-0" />
                  )}
                </button>
              );
            })}
          </section>
        ))}
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="flex-1 flex flex-col h-full overflow-hidden">
      <CommandBar
        title="Updates"
        subtitle={items && items.length > 0 ? `${unread} unread · last ${WINDOW_DAYS} days` : undefined}
        rightActions={
          followed && followed.length > 0 ? (
            <IconButton
              label="Check for new chapters"
              icon={<RotateCw className={`w-4 h-4 ${loading ? 'animate-spin' : ''}`} />}
              onClick={() => void load(true)}
              disabled={loading}
            />
          ) : undefined
        }
      />
      <PullToRefresh onRefresh={() => load(true)} className="flex-1 px-4 sm:px-6 py-4">
        {body}
      </PullToRefresh>
    </div>
  );
}

File: apps/client/src/sources/gutenberg.ts

import { net } from '../lib/net';
import { db } from '../db';
import type {
  CatalogEntry,
  CatalogFormat,
  CatalogPage,
  CatalogSection,
  CatalogSource,
} from './types';

interface GutendexPerson {
  name: string;
  birth_year?: number;
  death_year?: number;
}

interface GutendexBook {
  id: number;
  title: string;
  authors: GutendexPerson[];
  translators?: GutendexPerson[];
  subjects: string[];
  bookshelves: string[];
  languages: string[];
  copyright: boolean | null;
  media_type: string;
  formats: Record<string, string>;
  download_count: number;
}

interface GutendexResponse {
  count: number;
  next: string | null;
  previous: string | null;
  results: GutendexBook[];
}

function parseGutendexBook(b: GutendexBook): CatalogEntry {
  const formats: CatalogFormat[] = [];

  // Look for preferred EPUB versions first: epub3.images -> epub.images -> general epub
  let epubUrl: string | undefined;
  for (const [, url] of Object.entries(b.formats)) {
    if (url.includes('.epub3.images')) {
      epubUrl = url;
      break;
    }
  }
  if (!epubUrl) {
    for (const [mime, url] of Object.entries(b.formats)) {
      if (url.includes('.epub.images') || (mime === 'application/epub+zip' && !url.includes('.noimages'))) {
        epubUrl = url;
        break;
      }
    }
  }
  if (!epubUrl && b.formats['application/epub+zip']) {
    epubUrl = b.formats['application/epub+zip'];
  }

  if (epubUrl) {
    // Text-only edition first: it's often a tiny fraction of the illustrated one
    // (Pride and Prejudice: 0.5 MB vs 24.8 MB). download() picks between them.
    formats.push({ mime: 'application/epub+zip', url: `https://www.gutenberg.org/ebooks/${b.id}.epub.noimages`, label: 'Text only' });
    formats.push({ mime: 'application/epub+zip', url: epubUrl, label: 'With illustrations' });
  }

  // Cover image
  const coverUrl = b.formats['image/jpeg'];

  return {
    id: `gutenberg:${b.id}`,
    title: b.title,
    authors: b.authors.map((a) => {
      if (a.name.includes(',')) {
        return a.name.split(',').reverse().map((s) => s.trim()).join(' ');
      }
      return a.name.trim();
    }),
    cover: coverUrl,
    languages: b.languages,
    tags: [...b.subjects.slice(0, 5), ...b.bookshelves.slice(0, 3)],
    formats,
    kind: 'book',
  };
}

const MAX_AUTO_ILLUSTRATED_BYTES = 6 * 1024 * 1024;

// Gutendex can take a minute to answer. Catalog pages are cached on the device:
// fresh copies (< 6h) are returned instantly, and a stale copy is shown if the
// live request hasn't answered within a few seconds (it still refreshes the cache).
const GUTENDEX_FRESH_MS = 6 * 60 * 60 * 1000;
const GUTENDEX_STALE_WAIT_MS = 6000;

async function gutendexJson<T>(url: string, errorPrefix: string): Promise<T> {
  const key = `gutendex:${url}`;
  const cached = (await db.kv.get(key).catch(() => undefined))?.value as { at: number; data: T } | undefined;
  if (cached && Date.now() - cached.at < GUTENDEX_FRESH_MS) return cached.data;

  const live = (async () => {
    const res = await net.fetch(url);
    if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`${errorPrefix}: ${res.statusText}`);
    const data = (await res.json()) as T;
    void db.kv.put({ key, value: { at: Date.now(), data } }).catch(() => {});
    return data;
  })();
  if (!cached) return live;
  return Promise.race([
    live,
    new Promise<T>((resolve) => setTimeout(() => resolve(cached.data), GUTENDEX_STALE_WAIT_MS)),
  ]).catch(() => cached.data);
}

export class GutenbergSource implements CatalogSource {
  id = 'gutenberg';
  name = 'Project Gutenberg';
  icon = 'BookOpen';
  kind = 'books' as const;

  async sections(): Promise<CatalogSection[]> {
    return [
      { id: 'popular', title: 'Popular' },
      { id: 'latest', title: 'Latest' },
      { id: 'topic:fiction', title: 'Fiction' },
      { id: 'topic:science fiction', title: 'Sci-Fi' },
      { id: 'topic:mystery', title: 'Mystery' },
      { id: 'topic:philosophy', title: 'Philosophy' },
      { id: 'topic:history', title: 'History' },
    ];
  }

  filters = [
    {
      id: 'languages',
      label: 'Language',
      options: [
        { value: '', label: 'Any language' },
        { value: 'en', label: 'English' },
        { value: 'fr', label: 'French' },
        { value: 'de', label: 'German' },
        { value: 'es', label: 'Spanish' },
        { value: 'it', label: 'Italian' },
        { value: 'pt', label: 'Portuguese' },
        { value: 'nl', label: 'Dutch' },
        { value: 'fi', label: 'Finnish' },
        { value: 'zh', label: 'Chinese' },
        { value: 'la', label: 'Latin' },
      ],
    },
  ];

  async browse(sectionId: string, cursor?: string, filters?: Record<string, string>): Promise<CatalogPage> {
    let url = cursor;
    if (!url) {
      const params = new URLSearchParams();
      if (sectionId === 'popular') params.set('sort', 'popular');
      else if (sectionId === 'latest') params.set('sort', 'descending');
      else if (sectionId.startsWith('topic:')) params.set('topic', sectionId.slice(6));
      if (filters?.languages) params.set('languages', filters.languages);
      url = `https://gutendex.com/books?${params.toString()}`;
    }

    const data = await gutendexJson<GutendexResponse>(url, 'Failed to browse Gutenberg');

    return {
      items: data.results.map(parseGutendexBook),
      next: data.next || undefined,
    };
  }

  async search(
    query: string,
    cursor?: string,
    filters?: Record<string, string>,
  ): Promise<CatalogPage> {
    let url = cursor;
    if (!url) {
      const params = new URLSearchParams();
      if (query.trim()) params.set('search', query.trim());
      if (filters?.languages) params.set('languages', filters.languages);
      if (filters?.topic) params.set('topic', filters.topic);
      url = `https://gutendex.com/books?${params.toString()}`;
    }

    const data = await gutendexJson<GutendexResponse>(url, 'Failed to search Gutenberg');

    return {
      items: data.results.map(parseGutendexBook),
      next: data.next || undefined,
    };
  }

  async details(id: string): Promise<CatalogEntry> {
    const rawId = id.replace(/^gutenberg:/, '');
    const url = `https://gutendex.com/books/${rawId}`;
    const res = await net.fetch(url);
    if (!res.ok) {
      throw new Error(`Failed to fetch Gutenberg book ${id}: ${res.statusText}`);
    }
    const data = (await res.json()) as GutendexBook;
    return parseGutendexBook(data);
  }

  async download(
    entry: CatalogEntry,
    _format: string,
    onProgress?: (p: number) => void,
  ): Promise<File> {
    const epubs = (entry.formats ?? []).filter((f) => f.mime === 'application/epub+zip');
    if (!epubs.length) {
      throw new Error('No downloadable EPUB format available for this book.');
    }
    // An explicit URL picks that edition; otherwise take the illustrated edition
    // only when it's small enough to arrive in reasonable time.
    let chosen = epubs.find((f) => f.url === _format);
    if (!chosen) {
      const illustrated = epubs.find((f) => f.label === 'With illustrations');
      const textOnly = epubs.find((f) => f.label === 'Text only');
      const size = illustrated ? await net.size(illustrated.url) : undefined;
      chosen =
        illustrated && (!textOnly || (size !== undefined && size <= MAX_AUTO_ILLUSTRATED_BYTES))
          ? illustrated
          : (textOnly ?? epubs[0]);
    }

    onProgress?.(0.05);
    const blob = await net.download(chosen.url, {
      onProgress: (f) => onProgress?.(0.05 + f * 0.9),
    });
    onProgress?.(1.0);

    const safeTitle = entry.title.replace(/[<>:"/\\|?*]/g, '_').slice(0, 80);
    return new File([blob], `${safeTitle}.epub`, {
      type: 'application/epub+zip',
    });
  }
}

export const gutenbergSource = new GutenbergSource();

================
File: apps/client/src/sources/mangadex.ts

import { net } from '../lib/net';
import { getContentRatings } from '../lib/mangaPrefs';
import { cachedJson } from '../lib/apiCache';
import type {
  CatalogFilter,
  CatalogEntry,
  CatalogPage,
  CatalogSection,
  ChapterInfo,
  MangaSource,
  PageRef,
} from './types';

interface MangaDexRelationship {
  id: string;
  type: string;
  attributes?: {
    fileName?: string;
    name?: string;
  };
}

interface MangaDexMangaAttributes {
  title: Record<string, string>;
  description: Record<string, string>;
  status: string;
  contentRating: string;
  year?: number | null;
  publicationDemographic?: string | null;
  tags: Array<{
    id: string;
    attributes: {
      name: Record<string, string>;
    };
  }>;
}

interface MangaDexMangaItem {
  id: string;
  type: string;
  attributes: MangaDexMangaAttributes;
  relationships: MangaDexRelationship[];
}

interface MangaDexListResponse {
  result: string;
  response: string;
  data: MangaDexMangaItem[];
  limit: number;
  offset: number;
  total: number;
}

interface MangaDexChapterItem {
  id: string;
  type: string;
  attributes: {
    volume: string | null;
    chapter: string | null;
    title: string | null;
    publishAt: string;
    externalUrl: string | null;
    pages: number;
  };
  relationships: MangaDexRelationship[];
}

interface MangaDexFeedResponse {
  result: string;
  response: string;
  data: MangaDexChapterItem[];
  limit: number;
  offset: number;
  total: number;
}

interface MangaDexAtHomeResponse {
  result: string;
  baseUrl: string;
  chapter: {
    hash: string;
    data: string[];
    dataSaver: string[];
  };
}

function parseMangaDexItem(item: MangaDexMangaItem): CatalogEntry {
  const title =
    item.attributes.title.en ||
    Object.values(item.attributes.title)[0] ||
    'Untitled Manga';

  const authorRel = item.relationships.find((r) => r.type === 'author' || r.type === 'artist');
  const authors = authorRel?.attributes?.name ? [authorRel.attributes.name] : ['MangaDex Creator'];

  const coverRel = item.relationships.find((r) => r.type === 'cover_art');
  const coverFileName = coverRel?.attributes?.fileName;
  const coverUrl = coverFileName
    ? `https://uploads.mangadex.org/covers/${item.id}/${coverFileName}.256.jpg`
    : undefined;

  const summary =
    item.attributes.description.en ||
    Object.values(item.attributes.description)[0] ||
    '';

  const tags = item.attributes.tags
    .map((t) => t.attributes.name.en || Object.values(t.attributes.name)[0])
    .filter(Boolean);

  return {
    id: `mangadex:${item.id}`,
    title,
    authors,
    cover: coverUrl,
    summary,
    tags,
    kind: 'manga',
    status: item.attributes.status || undefined,
    contentRating: item.attributes.contentRating || undefined,
    year: item.attributes.year ?? undefined,
    demographic: item.attributes.publicationDemographic ?? undefined,
  };
}

function applyMangaDexFilters(params: URLSearchParams, filters?: Record<string, string>): void {
  if (filters?.rating === 'adult') {
    const adult = getContentRatings().filter((r) => r === 'erotica' || r === 'pornographic');
    // Only reachable when opted in; never widen beyond what Settings allows.
    if (adult.length) {
      params.delete('contentRating[]');
      for (const r of adult) params.append('contentRating[]', r);
    }
  }
  if (filters?.genre) params.append('includedTags[]', filters.genre);
  if (filters?.origin) params.append('originalLanguage[]', filters.origin);
  if (filters?.status) params.append('status[]', filters.status);
  if (filters?.demographic) params.append('publicationDemographic[]', filters.demographic);
}

export class MangaDexSource implements MangaSource {
  id = 'mangadex';
  name = 'MangaDex';
  icon = 'BookOpen';
  kind = 'manga' as const;

  async sections(): Promise<CatalogSection[]> {
    return [
      { id: 'popular', title: 'Popular' },
      { id: 'rating', title: 'Top Rated' },
      { id: 'latest', title: 'Latest Updates' },
      { id: 'recent', title: 'Recently Added' },
    ];
  }

  // Shown in the Store. The 18+ filter only exists once adult content has been
  // enabled in Settings (opt-in with age confirmation); otherwise it's not offered.
  get filters(): CatalogFilter[] {
    const adultOn = getContentRatings().some((r) => r === 'erotica' || r === 'pornographic');
    return adultOn
      ? [
          ...this.baseFilters,
          {
            id: 'rating',
            label: '18+',
            options: [
              { value: '', label: 'All ratings' },
              { value: 'adult', label: '18+ only', hint: 'Only erotica and pornographic titles.' },
            ],
          },
        ]
      : this.baseFilters;
  }

  private baseFilters: CatalogFilter[] = [
    {
      // Tag IDs from GET /manga/tag (stable UUIDs).
      id: 'genre',
      label: 'Genre',
      options: [
        { value: '', label: 'Any genre' },
        { value: '391b0423-d847-456f-aff0-8b0cfc03066b', label: 'Action' },
        { value: '87cc87cd-a395-47af-b27a-93258283bbc6', label: 'Adventure' },
        { value: '4d32cc48-9f00-4cca-9b5a-a839f0764984', label: 'Comedy' },
        { value: 'b9af3a63-f058-46de-a9a0-e0c13906197a', label: 'Drama' },

        { value: '33771934-028e-4cb3-8744-691e866a923e', label: 'Historical' },

        { value: '799c202e-7daa-44eb-9cf7-8a3c0441531e', label: 'Martial Arts' },

        { value: 'ee968100-4191-4968-93d3-f82d72be7e46', label: 'Mystery' },
        { value: '3b60b75c-a2d7-4860-ab56-05f391bb889c', label: 'Psychological' },
        { value: '423e2eae-a7a2-4a8b-ac03-a8351462d71d', label: 'Romance' },
        { value: 'caaa44eb-cd40-4177-b930-79d3ef2afe87', label: 'School Life' },
        { value: '256c8bd9-4904-4360-bf4f-508a76d67183', label: 'Sci-Fi' },

        { value: '69964a64-2f90-4d33-beeb-f3ed2875eb4c', label: 'Sports' },

    {

      ],
    },
    {

      ],
    },

      label: 'Audience',
      options: [

        { value: 'shoujo', label: 'Shoujo · teen girls', hint: 'Shoujo: aimed at teenage girls. Romance, relationships, drama (e.g. Fruits Basket).' },
        { value: 'seinen', label: 'Seinen · adult men', hint: 'Seinen: aimed at adult men. Often darker or more mature themes (e.g. Berserk, Vagabond).' },

      ],

  async browse(sectionId: string, cursor?: string, filters?: Record<string, string>): Promise<CatalogPage> {
    const limit = 24;
    const offset = cursor ? parseInt(cursor, 10) : 0;
    const params = new URLSearchParams();

    params.set('limit', String(limit));
    params.set('offset', String(offset));
    params.append('includes[]', 'cover_art');
    params.append('includes[]', 'author');
    params.append('includes[]', 'artist');
    for (const rating of getContentRatings()) params.append('contentRating[]', rating);
    params.append('availableTranslatedLanguage[]', 'en');
    applyMangaDexFilters(params, filters);

    if (sectionId === 'popular') {
      params.set('order[followedCount]', 'desc');
    } else if (sectionId === 'rating') {
      params.set('order[rating]', 'desc');
    } else if (sectionId === 'recent') {
      params.set('order[createdAt]', 'desc');
    } else {
      params.set('order[latestUploadedChapter]', 'desc');
    }

    const url = `https://api.mangadex.org/manga?${params.toString()}`;
    const json = await cachedJson<MangaDexListResponse>(
      url,
      async () => {
        const res = await net.fetch(url);
        if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`Failed to browse MangaDex: ${res.statusText}`);
        return (await res.json()) as MangaDexListResponse;
      },
      { freshMs: 30 * 60_000 },
    );
    const items = json.data.map(parseMangaDexItem);
    const hasMore = offset + limit < json.total;
    const next = hasMore ? String(offset + limit) : undefined;

    return { items, next };
  }

  async search(
    query: string,
    cursor?: string,
    filters?: Record<string, string>,
  ): Promise<CatalogPage> {
    const limit = 24;
    const offset = cursor ? parseInt(cursor, 10) : 0;
    const params = new URLSearchParams();

    params.set('limit', String(limit));
    params.set('offset', String(offset));
    params.append('includes[]', 'cover_art');
    params.append('includes[]', 'author');
    for (const rating of getContentRatings()) params.append('contentRating[]', rating);
    params.append('availableTranslatedLanguage[]', 'en');

    applyMangaDexFilters(params, filters);
    if (query.trim()) {
      params.set('title', query.trim());
    }

    const url = `https://api.mangadex.org/manga?${params.toString()}`;
    const json = await cachedJson<MangaDexListResponse>(
      url,
      async () => {
        const res = await net.fetch(url);
        if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`Failed to search MangaDex: ${res.statusText}`);
        return (await res.json()) as MangaDexListResponse;
      },
      { freshMs: 30 * 60_000 },
    );
    const items = json.data.map(parseMangaDexItem);
    const hasMore = offset + limit < json.total;
    const next = hasMore ? String(offset + limit) : undefined;

    return { items, next };
  }

  async details(id: string): Promise<CatalogEntry> {
    const rawId = id.replace(/^mangadex:/, '');
    const url = `https://api.mangadex.org/manga/${rawId}?includes[]=cover_art&includes[]=author&includes[]=artist`;
    const json = await cachedJson<{ data: MangaDexMangaItem }>(
      url,
      async () => {
        const res = await net.fetch(url);
        if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`Failed to fetch manga details: ${res.statusText}`);
        return (await res.json()) as { data: MangaDexMangaItem };
      },
      { freshMs: 6 * 60 * 60_000 },
    );
    return parseMangaDexItem(json.data);
  }

  async chapters(seriesId: string): Promise<ChapterInfo[]> {
    const rawId = seriesId.replace(/^mangadex:/, '');
    const items = await cachedJson<MangaDexChapterItem[]>(
      `mangadex:chapters:${rawId}`,
      () => this.fetchFeed(rawId, seriesId),
      { freshMs: 30 * 60_000 },
    );
    return this.buildChapterList(items);
  }

  private async fetchFeed(rawId: string, seriesId: string): Promise<MangaDexChapterItem[]> {
    const PAGE = 500;
    const items: MangaDexChapterItem[] = [];
    // The feed is paginated; long series (Berserk, One Piece) have far more than one page.
    for (let offset = 0; ; offset += PAGE) {
      const params = new URLSearchParams();
      params.set('limit', String(PAGE));
      params.set('offset', String(offset));
      params.append('translatedLanguage[]', 'en');
      params.append('includes[]', 'scanlation_group');
      params.set('order[chapter]', 'asc');
      params.set('includeFuturePublishAt', '0');
      const res = await net.fetch(`https://api.mangadex.org/manga/${rawId}/feed?${params.toString()}`);
      if (!res.ok) {
        throw new Error(`Failed to fetch chapters for ${seriesId}: ${res.statusText}`);
      }
      const json = (await res.json()) as MangaDexFeedResponse;
      items.push(...json.data);
      if (json.data.length < PAGE || offset + PAGE >= json.total) break;
    }
    return items;
  }

  private buildChapterList(items: MangaDexChapterItem[]): ChapterInfo[] {
    const rawChapters: ChapterInfo[] = [];
    for (const ch of items) {
      const externalUrl = ch.attributes.externalUrl || undefined;
      // Hosted chapters with no pages can't be read anywhere; skip them.
      if (!externalUrl && !ch.attributes.pages) continue;
      const groupRel = ch.relationships.find((r) => r.type === 'scanlation_group');
      const scanlationGroup = groupRel?.attributes?.name || 'No Group';
      const num = ch.attributes.chapter ? parseFloat(ch.attributes.chapter) : undefined;
      const chapterLabel = ch.attributes.chapter ? `Ch. ${ch.attributes.chapter}` : 'Oneshot';
      const title = ch.attributes.title
        ? `${chapterLabel} — ${ch.attributes.title}`
        : chapterLabel;

      rawChapters.push({
        id: ch.id,
        number: num,
        title,
        scanlationGroup,
        publishedAt: ch.attributes.publishAt,
        externalUrl,
      });
    }

    // De-duplicate by chapter number. A version readable in the app always beats an
    // official external link (previously the newest won, which was often the
    // external one, so tapping the chapter left the app); then prefer the newest.
    const better = (a: ChapterInfo, b: ChapterInfo) => {
      if (!a.externalUrl !== !b.externalUrl) return !a.externalUrl;
      const ta = a.publishedAt ? new Date(a.publishedAt).getTime() : 0;
      const tb = b.publishedAt ? new Date(b.publishedAt).getTime() : 0;
      return ta > tb;
    };
    const byNumber = new Map<number, ChapterInfo>();
    const withoutNumber: ChapterInfo[] = [];

    for (const c of rawChapters) {
      if (c.number !== undefined && !isNaN(c.number)) {
        const existing = byNumber.get(c.number);
        if (!existing || better(c, existing)) byNumber.set(c.number, c);
      } else {
        withoutNumber.push(c);
      }
    }

    const sortedDeduped = Array.from(byNumber.values()).sort(
      (a, b) => (a.number ?? 0) - (b.number ?? 0),
    );

    return [...sortedDeduped, ...withoutNumber];
  }

  /**
   * Chapters published since `sinceMs` across followed series, newest first.
   * GET /chapter only accepts a single `manga` id, so each series' own feed is
   * queried, three at a time (the shared rate limiter keeps us under 4 req/s).
   */
  async recentChapters(
    seriesIds: string[],
    sinceMs: number,
  ): Promise<(ChapterInfo & { seriesId: string })[]> {
    // MangaDex wants "YYYY-MM-DDTHH:MM:SS" (no milliseconds or zone).
    const since = new Date(sinceMs).toISOString().slice(0, 19);
    const out: (ChapterInfo & { seriesId: string })[] = [];
    let failures = 0;
    const queue = [...seriesIds];
    const worker = async () => {
      for (let id = queue.shift(); id; id = queue.shift()) {
        const params = new URLSearchParams();
        params.append('translatedLanguage[]', 'en');
        params.append('includes[]', 'scanlation_group');
        params.set('publishAtSince', since);
        params.set('order[publishAt]', 'desc');
        params.set('includeFuturePublishAt', '0');
        params.set('limit', '50');
        const rawId = id.replace(/^mangadex:/, '');
        try {
          const res = await net.fetch(`https://api.mangadex.org/manga/${rawId}/feed?${params.toString()}`);
          if (!res.ok) throw new Error(res.statusText);
          const json = (await res.json()) as MangaDexFeedResponse;
          for (const ch of json.data) {
            const externalUrl = ch.attributes.externalUrl || undefined;
            if (!externalUrl && !ch.attributes.pages) continue;
            const groupRel = ch.relationships.find((r) => r.type === 'scanlation_group');
            const label = ch.attributes.chapter ? `Ch. ${ch.attributes.chapter}` : 'Oneshot';
            out.push({
              id: ch.id,
              seriesId: `mangadex:${rawId}`,
              number: ch.attributes.chapter ? parseFloat(ch.attributes.chapter) : undefined,
              title: ch.attributes.title ? `${label} — ${ch.attributes.title}` : label,
              scanlationGroup: groupRel?.attributes?.name || 'No Group',
              publishedAt: ch.attributes.publishAt,
              externalUrl,
            });
          }
        } catch {
          failures++;
        }
      }
    };
    await Promise.all([worker(), worker(), worker()]);
    if (failures && failures === seriesIds.length) {
      throw new Error('MangaDex did not answer. Check your connection and try again.');
    }
    return out.sort((a, b) => (b.publishedAt ?? '').localeCompare(a.publishedAt ?? ''));
  }

  async pages(chapterId: string, dataSaver = false): Promise<PageRef[]> {
    const url = `https://api.mangadex.org/at-home/server/${chapterId}`;
    const res = await net.fetch(url);
    if (!res.ok) {
      throw new Error(`Failed to fetch pages for chapter ${chapterId}: ${res.statusText}`);
    }

    const json = (await res.json()) as MangaDexAtHomeResponse;
    const baseUrl = json.baseUrl;
    const hash = json.chapter.hash;
    const files = dataSaver && json.chapter.dataSaver.length > 0
      ? json.chapter.dataSaver
      : json.chapter.data;
    const subpath = dataSaver && json.chapter.dataSaver.length > 0 ? 'data-saver' : 'data';

    return files.map((file, idx) => ({
      index: idx + 1,
      url: `${baseUrl}/${subpath}/${hash}/${file}`,
    }));
  }

  credit(chapter: ChapterInfo): string {
    const group = chapter.scanlationGroup || 'No Group';
    return `Scanlation: ${group} · Hosted on MangaDex`;
  }
}

export const mangaDexSource = new MangaDexSource();

export interface CatalogFormat {
  mime: string;
  url: string;
  size?: number;
  /** Human label when a source offers several editions of one format. */
  label?: string;
}

export interface CatalogEntry {
  id: string;
  title: string;
  authors: string[];
  cover?: string;
  summary?: string;
  languages?: string[];
  tags?: string[];
  formats?: CatalogFormat[];
  kind: 'book' | 'manga' | 'comic';
  /** Publication status, e.g. "ongoing", "completed". */
  status?: string;
  /** safe | suggestive | erotica | pornographic (MangaDex ratings). */
  contentRating?: string;
  year?: number;
  /** shounen, seinen, shoujo, josei. */
  demographic?: string;
}

/** Adult content ratings get an 18+ badge everywhere a title is shown. */
export const isAdultRating = (r?: string) => r === 'erotica' || r === 'pornographic';

export interface CatalogPage {
  items: CatalogEntry[];
  next?: string;
}

export interface CatalogSection {
  id: string;
  title: string;
}

/** A single-choice filter a source supports (empty value = any). */
export interface CatalogFilter {
  id: string;
  label: string;
  options: { value: string; label: string; /** Shown on hover. */ hint?: string }[];
}

export interface CatalogSource {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  icon: string;
  kind: 'books' | 'manga';
  /** Filters shown in the Store for this source; values are passed to browse/search. */
  filters?: CatalogFilter[];
  sections(): Promise<CatalogSection[]>;
  browse(sectionId: string, cursor?: string, filters?: Record<string, string>): Promise<CatalogPage>;
  search(
    query: string,
    cursor?: string,
    filters?: Record<string, string>,
  ): Promise<CatalogPage>;
  details(id: string): Promise<CatalogEntry>;
  download?(
    entry: CatalogEntry,
    format: string,
    onProgress?: (p: number) => void,
  ): Promise<File>;
}

export interface ChapterInfo {
  id: string;
  number?: number;
  title?: string;
  scanlationGroup?: string;
  publishedAt?: string;
  externalUrl?: string;
}

export interface PageRef {
  index: number;
  url: string;
}

export interface MangaSource extends CatalogSource {
  chapters(seriesId: string): Promise<ChapterInfo[]>;
  /** `dataSaver` asks for smaller images where the source offers them (thumbnails, mobile data). */
  pages(chapterId: string, dataSaver?: boolean): Promise<PageRef[]>;
  credit?(chapter: ChapterInfo): string;
}

import React, { useEffect } from 'react';
import {
  BookA,
  Bookmark,
  Copy,
  Globe,
  Highlighter,
  Languages,
  Search,
  StickyNote,
  Strikethrough,
  Trash2,
  Underline,
  Volume2,
  Waves,
  Quote,
} from 'lucide-react';
import type { HighlightStyle } from '../db';
import { HIGHLIGHT_COLORS } from '../lib/readerSettings';
import { LookupCard, type LookupTab } from './LookupCard';
import { define } from '../lib/lookup';
import { IconButton } from '../design/components/IconButton';

export interface MenuAnchor {
  x: number;
  top: number;
  bottom: number;
}

const STYLES: { id: HighlightStyle; label: string; icon: React.FC<{ className?: string }> }[] = [
  { id: 'highlight', label: 'Highlight', icon: Highlighter },
  { id: 'underline', label: 'Underline', icon: Underline },
  { id: 'strikethrough', label: 'Strikethrough', icon: Strikethrough },
  { id: 'squiggly', label: 'Squiggly', icon: Waves },
];

export function SelectionPopover(props: {
  anchor: MenuAnchor;
  text: string;
  color: string;
  style: HighlightStyle;
  existing: boolean;
  hasNote: boolean;
  lookup: LookupTab | null;
  lang: string;
  translateTo: string;
  onLookup: (tab: LookupTab | null) => void;
  onTranslateTo: (lang: string) => void;
  onSpeakText: (text: string) => void;
  onColor: (color: string) => void;
  onStyle: (style: HighlightStyle) => void;
  onNote: () => void;
  onBookmark: () => void;
  onReadAloud: () => void;
  onSearch: () => void;
  onCopy: () => void;
  onDelete?: () => void;
  onExpandLookup?: () => void;
  onShareQuote?: () => void;
}) {
  const { anchor, lookup } = props;

  // Warm the dictionary as soon as a single word is selected, so by the time the
  // reader taps "Define" the answer is usually already cached.
  const { text, lang } = props;
  useEffect(() => {
    const word = text.trim();
    if (!word || word.length > 40 || /\s/.test(word)) return;
    const timer = setTimeout(() => void define(word, lang).catch(() => {}), 250);
    return () => clearTimeout(timer);
  }, [text, lang]);
  const width = Math.min(390, window.innerWidth - 16);
  const left = Math.min(Math.max(anchor.x - width / 2, 8), window.innerWidth - width - 8);
  const needed = lookup ? 420 : 130;
  const above = anchor.top > needed + 56;
  const pos = above ? { bottom: window.innerHeight - anchor.top + 10 } : { top: anchor.bottom + 10 };

  const card = lookup && (
    <LookupCard
      key={props.text}
      text={props.text}
      tab={lookup}
      onTab={props.onLookup}
      lang={props.lang}
      translateTo={props.translateTo}
      onTranslateTo={props.onTranslateTo}
      onSpeak={props.onSpeakText}
      onClose={() => props.onLookup(null)}
      onExpand={props.onExpandLookup}
    />
  );

  const toggle = (t: LookupTab) => props.onLookup(lookup === t ? null : t);

  return (
    <div
      className="fixed z-50 rounded-[var(--r-lg)] bg-[var(--layer-solid)] text-[var(--text-1)] border border-[var(--stroke)] shadow-[var(--shadow-flyout)] overflow-hidden select-none animate-in fade-in-0 duration-150"
      style={{ left, width, ...pos }}
    >
      {above && card}

      {/* Row 1: 6 Color dots + 4 style selectors */}
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between px-3 py-2 border-b border-[var(--stroke)] bg-[var(--layer-1)]">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
          {HIGHLIGHT_COLORS.map((c) => (
            <button
              key={c.id}
              type="button"
              title={c.label}
              aria-label={`${c.label} highlight`}
              onClick={() => props.onColor(c.id)}
              className={`w-5 h-5 rounded-full cursor-pointer transition-transform hover:scale-115 flex items-center justify-center ${
                props.existing && props.color === c.id
                  ? 'ring-2 ring-[var(--text-1)] ring-offset-2 ring-offset-[var(--layer-solid)] scale-110'
                  : ''
              }`}
              style={{ backgroundColor: c.hex }}
            />
          ))}
        </div>

        <div className="h-4 w-[1px] bg-[var(--stroke)]" />

        <div className="flex items-center gap-1">
          {STYLES.map((s) => (
            <button
              key={s.id}
              type="button"
              title={s.label}
              aria-label={s.label}
              onClick={() => props.onStyle(s.id)}
              className={`p-1 rounded-[var(--r-sm)] text-[var(--text-2)] hover:text-[var(--text-1)] hover:bg-[var(--layer-2)] cursor-pointer ${
                props.style === s.id ? 'bg-[var(--accent-soft)] text-[var(--accent)] font-semibold' : ''
              }`}
            >
              <s.icon className="w-4 h-4" />
            </button>
          ))}
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Row 2: 9 Action tool icons */}
      <div className="flex items-center justify-around px-2 py-1.5 bg-[var(--layer-solid)]">
        <IconButton
          label={props.hasNote ? 'Edit note' : 'Add note'}
          icon={<StickyNote className={`w-4 h-4 ${props.hasNote ? 'text-[var(--accent)]' : ''}`} />}
          size="sm"
          onClick={props.onNote}
        />
        <IconButton
          label="Bookmark here"
          icon={<Bookmark className="w-4 h-4" />}
          size="sm"
          onClick={props.onBookmark}
        />
        <IconButton
          label="Dictionary"
          icon={<BookA className={`w-4 h-4 ${lookup === 'define' ? 'text-[var(--accent)]' : ''}`} />}
          size="sm"
          onClick={() => toggle('define')}
        />
        <IconButton
          label="Translate"
          icon={<Languages className={`w-4 h-4 ${lookup === 'translate' ? 'text-[var(--accent)]' : ''}`} />}
          size="sm"
          onClick={() => toggle('translate')}
        />
        <IconButton
          label="Wikipedia"
          icon={<Globe className={`w-4 h-4 ${lookup === 'wiki' ? 'text-[var(--accent)]' : ''}`} />}
          size="sm"
          onClick={() => toggle('wiki')}
        />
        <IconButton
          label="Read aloud"
          icon={<Volume2 className="w-4 h-4" />}
          size="sm"
          onClick={props.onReadAloud}
        />
        <IconButton
          label="Search in book"
          icon={<Search className="w-4 h-4" />}
          size="sm"
          onClick={props.onSearch}
        />
        <IconButton
          label="Copy text"
          icon={<Copy className="w-4 h-4" />}
          size="sm"
          onClick={props.onCopy}
        />
        {props.onShareQuote && (
          <IconButton
            label="Share quote card"
            icon={<Quote className="w-4 h-4" />}
            size="sm"
            onClick={props.onShareQuote}
          />
        )}
        {props.onDelete && (
          <IconButton
            label="Delete highlight"
            icon={<Trash2 className="w-4 h-4" />}
            size="sm"
            variant="danger"
            onClick={props.onDelete}
          />
        )}
      </div>

      {!above && card}
    </div>
  );
}

File: apps/client/src/components/TTSBar.tsx

import { Pause, Play, SkipBack, SkipForward, X, Volume2 } from 'lucide-react';
import { baseLang } from '../lib/lookup';
import type { TTSState } from '../lib/tts';
import type { TtsVoice } from '../lib/ttsEngine';
import { IconButton } from '../design/components/IconButton';

const RATES = [0.75, 1, 1.25, 1.5, 1.75, 2, 2.5];

export function TTSBar({
  state,
  rate,
  voiceURI,
  voices,
  lang,
  onPlayPause,
  onPrev,
  onNext,
  onStop,
  onRate,
  onVoice,
  sleepTimer,
  onSleepTimer,
}: {
  state: TTSState;
  rate: number;
  voiceURI: string;
  voices: (SpeechSynthesisVoice | TtsVoice)[];
  lang: string;
  onPlayPause: () => void;
  onPrev: () => void;
  onNext: () => void;
  onStop: () => void;
  onRate: (r: number) => void;
  onVoice: (uri: string) => void;
  sleepTimer?: number;
  onSleepTimer?: (mins: number) => void;
}) {
  const base = baseLang(lang);
  const matches = (v: SpeechSynthesisVoice | TtsVoice) => v.lang.toLowerCase().startsWith(base);
  const sorted = [...voices].sort(
    (a, b) => Number(matches(b)) - Number(matches(a)) || a.name.localeCompare(b.name),
  );

  return (
    <div
      role="toolbar"
      aria-label="Read aloud controls"
      className="fixed bottom-8 left-1/2 -translate-x-1/2 z-50 flex items-center gap-2 px-3.5 py-2 rounded-[var(--r-pill)] bg-[var(--layer-solid)] text-[var(--text-1)] border border-[var(--stroke)] shadow-[var(--shadow-flyout)] backdrop-blur-[var(--blur)] select-none animate-in fade-in-0 slide-in-from-bottom-2 duration-150"
    >
      <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-[var(--accent)] pl-1 pr-2 border-r border-[var(--stroke)]">
        <Volume2 className="w-4 h-4 animate-pulse" />
        <span className="text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider hidden sm:inline">TTS</span>
      </div>

      <IconButton
        label="Previous paragraph"
        icon={<SkipBack className="w-4 h-4" />}
        size="sm"
        onClick={onPrev}
      />

      <button
        type="button"
        aria-label={state === 'playing' ? 'Pause read aloud' : 'Play read aloud'}
        onClick={onPlayPause}
        className="w-8 h-8 rounded-full bg-[var(--accent)] hover:bg-[var(--accent-hover)] text-[var(--on-accent)] flex items-center justify-center transition-transform hover:scale-105 active:scale-95 shadow-xs cursor-pointer outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-[var(--accent)]"
      >
        {state === 'playing' ? (
          <Pause className="w-4 h-4 fill-current" />
        ) : (
          <Play className="w-4 h-4 fill-current ml-0.5" />
        )}
      </button>

      <IconButton
        label="Next paragraph"
        icon={<SkipForward className="w-4 h-4" />}
        size="sm"
        onClick={onNext}
      />

      <div className="h-4 w-[1px] bg-[var(--stroke)] mx-1" />

      {/* Speed Rate Select */}
      <select
        value={rate}
        onChange={(e) => onRate(Number(e.target.value))}
        aria-label="Reading Speed"
        className="h-7 px-2 text-xs font-medium rounded-[var(--r-sm)] bg-[var(--layer-2)] border border-[var(--stroke)] text-[var(--text-1)] outline-none cursor-pointer hover:bg-[var(--layer-3)]"
      >
        {RATES.map((r) => (
          <option key={r} value={r}>
            {r}×
          </option>
        ))}
      </select>

      {/* Voice Select */}
      <select
        value={voiceURI}
        onChange={(e) => onVoice(e.target.value)}
        aria-label="Speech Voice"
        className="h-7 px-2 text-xs font-medium rounded-[var(--r-sm)] bg-[var(--layer-2)] border border-[var(--stroke)] text-[var(--text-1)] outline-none cursor-pointer max-w-[130px] sm:max-w-[180px] truncate hover:bg-[var(--layer-3)]"
      >
        <option value="">Default voice</option>
        {sorted.map((v) => (
          <option key={v.voiceURI} value={v.voiceURI}>
            {v.name} ({v.lang})
          </option>
        ))}
      </select>

      {/* Sleep Timer Select */}
      {onSleepTimer && (
        <select
          value={sleepTimer ?? 0}
          onChange={(e) => onSleepTimer(Number(e.target.value))}
          aria-label="Sleep timer"
          className="h-7 px-2 text-xs font-medium rounded-[var(--r-sm)] bg-[var(--layer-2)] border border-[var(--stroke)] text-[var(--text-1)] outline-none cursor-pointer hover:bg-[var(--layer-3)]"
        >
          <option value={0}>Timer off</option>
          <option value={15}>15 min sleep</option>
          <option value={30}>30 min sleep</option>
          <option value={60}>60 min sleep</option>
          <option value={-1}>End of chapter</option>
        </select>
      )}

      <div className="h-4 w-[1px] bg-[var(--stroke)] mx-1" />

      <IconButton
        label="Stop read aloud"
        icon={<X className="w-4 h-4" />}
        size="sm"
        variant="subtle"
        onClick={onStop}
      />
    </div>
  );
}

================
File: apps/client/src/design/components/BookCover.tsx

import React, { useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { Heart, Check } from 'lucide-react';
import { clsx } from 'clsx';
import { twMerge } from 'tailwind-merge';
import { db } from '../../db';

export interface BookCoverProps {
  id: string;
  title: string;
  author: string;
  coverHash?: string | null;
  coverUrl?: string | null;
  progressPercent?: number; // 0 to 100
  badge?: React.ReactNode;
  isFavourite?: boolean;
  onToggleFavourite?: () => void;
  onClick?: () => void;
  className?: string;
  glow?: boolean;
  isSelected?: boolean;
  isSelectable?: boolean;
  onToggleSelect?: () => void;
}

// Shared, ref-counted object URLs for cover blobs. Previously every mounted cover
// read IndexedDB and minted its own object URL, so switching pages re-read and
// re-decoded every cover. URLs are revoked a little after their last user goes
// away, so quick back-and-forth navigation reuses them.
interface CoverEntry {
  url: Promise<string | null>;
  refs: number;
  revokeTimer?: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout>;
}
const coverUrls = new Map<string, CoverEntry>();
const COVER_URL_GRACE_MS = 30_000;

function acquireCoverUrl(id: string, coverHash: string): Promise<string | null> {
  const key = `${id}:${coverHash}`;
  let entry = coverUrls.get(key);
  if (!entry) {
    entry = {
      refs: 0,
      url: db.covers
        .get(id)
        .then((cover) => (cover ? URL.createObjectURL(cover.blob) : null))
        .catch(() => null),
    };
    coverUrls.set(key, entry);
  }
  if (entry.revokeTimer) {
    clearTimeout(entry.revokeTimer);
    entry.revokeTimer = undefined;
  }
  entry.refs++;
  return entry.url;
}

function releaseCoverUrl(id: string, coverHash: string): void {
  const key = `${id}:${coverHash}`;
  const entry = coverUrls.get(key);
  if (!entry) return;
  entry.refs--;
  if (entry.refs > 0) return;
  entry.revokeTimer = setTimeout(() => {
    coverUrls.delete(key);
    void entry.url.then((u) => u && URL.revokeObjectURL(u));
  }, COVER_URL_GRACE_MS);
}

/** Object URL for a stored cover. Pass `enabled: false` to defer loading (e.g. off-screen). */
export function useCoverBlobUrl(id: string, coverHash?: string | null, enabled = true) {
  const [loaded, setLoaded] = useState<{ key: string; url: string | null } | null>(null);
  const key = coverHash ? `${id}:${coverHash}` : null;

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!coverHash || !enabled) return;
    let alive = true;
    void acquireCoverUrl(id, coverHash).then((url) => {
      if (alive) setLoaded({ key: `${id}:${coverHash}`, url });
    });
    return () => {
      alive = false;
      releaseCoverUrl(id, coverHash);
    };
  }, [id, coverHash, enabled]);

  return key && loaded?.key === key ? loaded.url : null;
}

/** True once the element has come within `margin` of the viewport (stays true). */
function useNearViewport<T extends Element>(ref: React.RefObject<T | null>, margin = '600px') {
  const [seen, setSeen] = useState(() => typeof IntersectionObserver === 'undefined');
  useEffect(() => {
    if (seen || !ref.current) return;
    const io = new IntersectionObserver(
      (entries) => {
        if (entries.some((e) => e.isIntersecting)) {
          setSeen(true);
          io.disconnect();
        }
      },
      { rootMargin: margin },
    );
    io.observe(ref.current);
    return () => io.disconnect();
  }, [ref, margin, seen]);
  return seen;
}

export const BookCover: React.FC<BookCoverProps> = ({
  id,
  title,
  author,
  coverHash,
  coverUrl: coverUrlProp,
  progressPercent,
  badge,
  isFavourite,
  onToggleFavourite,
  onClick,
  className,
  glow = true,
  isSelected = false,
  isSelectable = false,
  onToggleSelect,
}) => {
  const rootRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const nearViewport = useNearViewport(rootRef);
  const blobCoverUrl = useCoverBlobUrl(id, coverHash, nearViewport && !coverUrlProp);
  const coverUrl = coverUrlProp || blobCoverUrl;
  const hue = parseInt(id.slice(0, 6), 16) % 360;
  const fallbackBg = `hsl(${hue} 35% 32%)`;

  const handleClick = () => {
    if (isSelectable) {
      onToggleSelect?.();
    } else {
      onClick?.();
    }
  };

  return (
    <div
      ref={rootRef}
      role={onClick || isSelectable ? 'button' : undefined}
      tabIndex={onClick || isSelectable ? 0 : undefined}
      onClick={handleClick}
      onKeyDown={(e) => (e.key === 'Enter' || e.key === ' ') && handleClick()}
      className={twMerge(
        clsx(
          'group relative aspect-[2/3] w-full rounded-[var(--r-cover)] overflow-hidden select-none cursor-pointer',
          'shadow-[var(--shadow-cover)] transition-all duration-[var(--d-normal)] ease-[var(--ease-out)]',
          'hover:-translate-y-1 focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-[var(--accent)] outline-none',
          glow && 'hover:shadow-[0_18px_40px_rgba(0,0,0,0.55)]',
          isSelected && 'ring-2 ring-[var(--accent)] ring-offset-2 ring-offset-[var(--bg-base)]',
          className,
        ),
      )}
    >
      {/* 2px inner spine highlight */}
      <div className="absolute inset-y-0 left-0 w-2 z-20 pointer-events-none bg-gradient-to-r from-white/15 to-transparent" />

      {/* Cover Image or Fallback */}
      {coverUrl ? (
        <img
          src={coverUrl}
          alt={title}
          loading="lazy"
          decoding="async"
          draggable={false}
          className="w-full h-full object-cover transition-transform duration-[var(--d-slow)] group-hover:scale-102"
        />
      ) : (
        // Sized off the cover's own width (container query units) so the same
        // component reads well as a 44px thumbnail and as a 300px hero.
        <div
          className="@container w-full h-full text-white transition-colors"
          style={{ background: fallbackBg }}
        >
          <div className="w-full h-full hidden @min-[72px]:flex flex-col justify-end p-[8cqw] min-w-0">
            <span className="font-display font-semibold text-[clamp(10px,9.5cqw,22px)] leading-tight line-clamp-3 mb-[0.25em] drop-shadow-sm [overflow-wrap:anywhere]">
              {title}
            </span>
            <span className="text-[clamp(9px,7.5cqw,15px)] text-white/80 line-clamp-1 [overflow-wrap:anywhere]">
              {author}
            </span>
          </div>
          <div className="w-full h-full flex @min-[72px]:hidden items-center justify-center font-display font-semibold text-[40cqw] leading-none drop-shadow-sm">
            {title.trim().charAt(0).toUpperCase()}
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Selection checkbox */}
      {isSelectable && (
        <button
          type="button"
          aria-label={isSelected ? 'Deselect book' : 'Select book'}
          onClick={(e) => {
            e.stopPropagation();
            onToggleSelect?.();
          }}
          className={clsx(
            'absolute top-2 left-2 z-30 w-6 h-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center transition-all cursor-pointer outline-none border shadow-xs',
            isSelected
              ? 'bg-[var(--accent)] border-[var(--accent)] text-[var(--on-accent)]'
              : 'bg-black/60 border-white/50 text-transparent hover:border-white',
          )}
        >
          <Check className="w-3.5 h-3.5 stroke-[2.5]" />
        </button>
      )}

      {/* Badge slot */}
      {badge && (
        <div
          className={clsx(
            'absolute z-20 pointer-events-none',
            isSelectable ? 'top-2 left-10' : 'top-2 left-2',
          )}
        >
          {badge}
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Favourite Heart button on hover */}
      {onToggleFavourite && (
        <button
          type="button"
          aria-label={isFavourite ? 'Remove from favourites' : 'Add to favourites'}
          onClick={(e) => {
            e.stopPropagation();
            onToggleFavourite();
          }}
          className={clsx(
            'absolute top-2 right-2 z-20 w-7 h-7 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center transition-all cursor-pointer outline-none',
            isFavourite
              ? 'bg-black/60 text-[var(--accent)] opacity-100 shadow-xs'
              : 'bg-black/50 text-white/80 opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100 hover:text-white',
          )}
        >
          <Heart className={clsx('w-3.5 h-3.5', isFavourite && 'fill-current')} />
        </button>
      )}

      {/* Progress Bar overlay on bottom scrim */}
      {progressPercent !== undefined && progressPercent > 0 && (
        <div className="absolute inset-x-0 bottom-0 pt-6 pb-2 px-2.5 z-20 bg-gradient-to-t from-black/80 via-black/40 to-transparent pointer-events-none">
          <div className="w-full h-[3px] bg-white/20 rounded-full overflow-hidden">
            <div
              className="h-full bg-[var(--accent)] rounded-full transition-all duration-[var(--d-fast)]"
              style={{ width: `${Math.min(100, progressPercent)}%` }}
            />
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
};

          <div className="flex items-center gap-4">
            <Button onClick={() => setDialogOpen(true)}>Open Dialog</Button>
            <Popover

              </div>
            </Popover>

            <Dropdown
              trigger={<Button variant="standard" icon={<Filter className="w-4 h-4" />}>Filter</Button>}
              sections={[
                {
                  title: 'Format',
                  items: [
                    { key: 'all', label: 'All formats', checked: true },
                    { key: 'epub', label: 'EPUB only' },
                    { key: 'cbz', label: 'CBZ / Comics' },

                  ],
                },
              ]}
            />

            <ContextMenu
              sections={[
                {
                  items: [
                    { key: 'open', label: 'Open reader' },
                    { key: 'details', label: 'Book details' },
                    { key: 'fav', label: 'Add to favourites' },
                  ],
                },
              ]}
            >
              <div className="p-3 bg-[var(--layer-2)] border border-[var(--stroke)] rounded-[var(--r-md)] text-xs text-[var(--text-2)] select-none">
                Right-click here for ContextMenu
              </div>
            </ContextMenu>
          </div>

          <Dialog
            open={dialogOpen}
            onOpenChange={setDialogOpen}
            title="Sample Dialog"
            description="Fluent centered modal dialog with smooth fade and scale."
            footer={
              <>
                <Button variant="subtle" onClick={() => setDialogOpen(false)}>Cancel</Button>
                <Button variant="accent" onClick={() => setDialogOpen(false)}>Confirm</Button>
              </>
            }
          >
            <p className="text-sm text-[var(--text-2)]">
              This dialog adheres to the 560px max width and token-based layer styles.
            </p>
          </Dialog>

          <div className="pt-2">
            <Button variant="standard" onClick={() => setTrapOpen(!trapOpen)}>
              {trapOpen ? 'Close Focus Trap' : 'Open Focus Trap Demo'}
            </Button>
            {trapOpen && (
              <div className="mt-3 p-4 bg-[var(--layer-2)] border border-[var(--stroke)] rounded-[var(--r-md)] max-w-md">
                <FocusTrap onClose={() => setTrapOpen(false)} className="space-y-3">
                  <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                    <h4 className="text-sm font-semibold text-[var(--text-1)]">Focus Trap Active</h4>
                    <span className="text-xs text-[var(--text-2)]">Press Esc to close</span>
                  </div>
                  <p className="text-xs text-[var(--text-2)]">
                    Tab and Shift+Tab cycle only through the buttons below. Closing restores focus to the trigger button above.
                  </p>
                  <div className="flex gap-2">
                    <Button size="sm" variant="accent">Action 1</Button>
                    <Button size="sm" variant="standard">Action 2</Button>
                    <Button size="sm" variant="subtle" onClick={() => setTrapOpen(false)}>Done</Button>
                  </div>
                </FocusTrap>
              </div>
            )}
          </div>
        </section>

        {/* Feedback & Indicators */}
        <section className="space-y-4">
          <h2 className="text-lg font-semibold text-[var(--text-1)]">Feedback & Progress</h2>
          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-4">
            <InfoBar severity="info" title="Information" message="Verso synced 12 new items across your devices." />
            <InfoBar severity="success" title="Success" message="Book imported and ready to read." />
            <InfoBar severity="warning" title="Warning" message="Server connection slow. Offline cache active." />
            <InfoBar severity="error" title="Error" message="Could not authenticate with server." />
          </div>
          <div className="flex items-center gap-6 pt-3">
            <ProgressRing size={28} />
            <div className="w-64">
              <ProgressBar value={72} />
            </div>
            <Skeleton className="w-32 h-8" />
          </div>
        </section>

        {/* Covers, Cards & Shelf */}
        <section className="space-y-4">
          <h2 className="text-lg font-semibold text-[var(--text-1)]">Book Covers, Cards & Shelves</h2>
          <Shelf title="Featured Books" action={<a href="#more">See all ›</a>}>
            {[1, 2, 3, 4, 5].map((i) => (
              <div key={i} className="w-[140px] shrink-0">
                <BookCard
                  id={`book-sample-${i}`}
                  title={`Sample Masterpiece Volume ${i}`}
                  author="Legendary Author"
                  progressPercent={i * 20}
                  isFavourite={i % 2 === 0}
                />
              </div>
            ))}
          </Shelf>
        </section>

        {/* Empty State */}
        <section className="space-y-4 border-t border-[var(--stroke)] pt-6">
          <h2 className="text-lg font-semibold text-[var(--text-1)]">Empty State</h2>
          <EmptyState
            icon={<BookOpen className="w-6 h-6" />}
            title="No books yet"
            description="Your library is currently empty. Drop an EPUB, MOBI or CBZ file here to start reading."
            action={<Button variant="accent">Import Book</Button>}
          />
        </section>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

     Content scrolls underneath, so it must stay legible without backdrop blur. */
  --chrome: color-mix(in oklab, var(--bg-base) 88%, transparent);
}

     built against) used a subtle grey. Bare `border` utilities should be hairlines. */

    border-color: var(--stroke);

     draws wide grey bars with arrow buttons). */
  * {
    scrollbar-width: thin;
    scrollbar-color: var(--stroke-strong) transparent;
