================
File: apps/client/src/features/auth/AccountSettings.tsx
================
import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import {
  authLogin,
  authRegister,
  authLogout,
  getAccount,
  getSessions,
  deleteSession,
  patchPassword,
  deleteAccount,
  type AccountInfo,
  type SessionInfo,
} from '../sync/api';
import {
  getAuthUser,
  getServerUrl,
  setServerUrl,
  normalizeServerUrl,
  resetServerUrl,
  clearAuth,
  DEFAULT_SERVER_URL,
  type StoredUser,
} from './storage';
import { db, type Device } from '../../db';
import { syncEngine, useSyncStore, markAllForUpload, wipeLocalAccountData } from '../sync/engine';
import { Button } from '../../design/components/Button';
import { Dialog } from '../../design/components/Dialog';
import { fmtBytes, fmtDate } from '../../lib/format';
import {
  User,
  HardDrive,
  Laptop,
  LogOut,
  Trash2,
  Server,
  ShieldAlert,
  Loader2,
} from 'lucide-react';

export interface AccountSettingsProps {
  onAuthChange?: () => void;
}

export const AccountSettings: React.FC<AccountSettingsProps> = ({ onAuthChange }) => {
  const [currentUser, setCurrentUser] = useState<StoredUser | null>(null);
  const [serverUrlVal, setServerUrlVal] = useState(DEFAULT_SERVER_URL);
  const [isEditingServer, setIsEditingServer] = useState(false);
  const [serverError, setServerError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [serverCheck, setServerCheck] = useState<'checking' | 'ok' | 'unreachable' | null>(null);

  // Auth form states
  const [authMode, setAuthMode] = useState<'login' | 'register'>('login');
  const [email, setEmail] = useState('');
  const [password, setPassword] = useState('');
  const [authLoading, setAuthLoading] = useState(false);
  const [authError, setAuthError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  // Account details states
  const [accountInfo, setAccountInfo] = useState<AccountInfo | null>(null);
  const [sessions, setSessions] = useState<SessionInfo[]>([]);
  const [devices, setDevices] = useState<Device[]>([]);

  // Password change states
  const [currPass, setCurrPass] = useState('');
  const [newPass, setNewPass] = useState('');
  const [passMsg, setPassMsg] = useState<{ text: string; error?: boolean } | null>(null);
  const [passLoading, setPassLoading] = useState(false);

  // Dialogs
  const [signOutOpen, setSignOutOpen] = useState(false);
  const [deleteAccountOpen, setDeleteAccountOpen] = useState(false);
  const [mergePromptUser, setMergePromptUser] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const runSync = useSyncStore((s) => s.runSync);

  const loadUserState = async () => {
    const u = await getAuthUser();
    setCurrentUser(u);
    const sUrl = await getServerUrl();
    setServerUrlVal(sUrl);

    if (u) {
      try {
        const [acc, sess, devs] = await Promise.all([
          getAccount().catch(() => null),
          getSessions().catch(() => []),
          db.devices.toArray().catch(() => []),
        ]);
        setAccountInfo(acc);
        setSessions(sess);
        setDevices(devs);
      } catch {
        // Non-fatal
      }
    }
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    void loadUserState();
  }, []);

  const handleSaveServerUrl = async () => {
    const checked = normalizeServerUrl(serverUrlVal);
    if ('error' in checked) {
      setServerError(checked.error);
      return;
    }
    setServerError(null);
    await setServerUrl(checked.url);
    setServerUrlVal(checked.url);
    setIsEditingServer(false);
    // Tell the user straight away whether the server answers.
    setServerCheck('checking');
    try {
      const res = await fetch(`${checked.url}/health`, { signal: AbortSignal.timeout(8000) });
      setServerCheck(res.ok ? 'ok' : 'unreachable');
    } catch {
      setServerCheck('unreachable');
    }
  };

  const handleResetServerUrl = async () => {
    await resetServerUrl();
    setServerUrlVal(DEFAULT_SERVER_URL);
    setServerError(null);
    setServerCheck(null);
    setIsEditingServer(false);
  };

  const handleAuthSubmit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!email || !password) return;
    setAuthLoading(true);
    setAuthError(null);

    try {
      const prevUserRow = await db.kv.get('last_logged_in_email');
      const prevEmail = typeof prevUserRow?.value === 'string' ? prevUserRow.value : null;

      let authResult;
      if (authMode === 'register') {
        authResult = await authRegister(email.trim(), password);
      } else {
        authResult = await authLogin(email.trim(), password);
      }

      const newEmail = authResult.user.email;
      await db.kv.put({ key: 'last_logged_in_email', value: newEmail });

      // Check if signing in into a different account than last time
      if (prevEmail && prevEmail !== newEmail) {
        setMergePromptUser(newEmail);
      } else {
        // Mark everything dirty on first sync or existing data
        await dirtyAllSyncedRows();
        void runSync();
        void syncEngine.connectSSE();
      }

      await loadUserState();
      onAuthChange?.();
    } catch (err: unknown) {
      setAuthError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Authentication failed');
    } finally {
      setAuthLoading(false);
    }
  };

  // Queue local rows for upload without touching updated_at, so newer edits from
  // other devices still win (bumping the timestamp let a device that had been
  // offline for weeks overwrite newer progress the moment it signed in).
  const dirtyAllSyncedRows = markAllForUpload;
  const wipeLocalSyncedData = wipeLocalAccountData;

  const handleMergeChoice = async (choice: 'merge' | 'fresh') => {
    if (choice === 'merge') {
      await dirtyAllSyncedRows();
    } else {
      await wipeLocalSyncedData();
    }
    setMergePromptUser(null);
    void runSync();
    void syncEngine.connectSSE();
  };

  const handleRevokeSession = async (tokenHash: string) => {
    try {
      await deleteSession(tokenHash);
      setSessions((prev) => prev.filter((s) => s.token_hash !== tokenHash));
    } catch {
      // Non-fatal
    }
  };

  const handleChangePassword = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!currPass || !newPass) return;
    setPassLoading(true);
    setPassMsg(null);
    try {
      await patchPassword(currPass, newPass);
      setPassMsg({ text: 'Password updated successfully!' });
      setCurrPass('');
      setNewPass('');
    } catch (err: unknown) {
      setPassMsg({
        text: err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Failed to update password',
        error: true,
      });
    } finally {
      setPassLoading(false);
    }
  };

  const handleSignOut = async (removeLocal: boolean) => {
    await authLogout();
    syncEngine.disconnectSSE();
    if (removeLocal) {
      await wipeLocalSyncedData();
    }
    await clearAuth();
    setSignOutOpen(false);
    await loadUserState();
    onAuthChange?.();
  };

  const handleDeleteAccount = async () => {
    try {
      await deleteAccount();
      syncEngine.disconnectSSE();
      await wipeLocalSyncedData();
      await clearAuth();
      setDeleteAccountOpen(false);
      await loadUserState();
      onAuthChange?.();
    } catch (err: unknown) {
      alert(err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Failed to delete account');
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col gap-6 py-1">
      {/* Server URL setting */}
      <section className="flex flex-col gap-2 p-3 rounded-[var(--r-md)] bg-[var(--layer-2)] border border-[var(--stroke)]">
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <Server className="w-4 h-4 text-[var(--accent)]" />
            <span className="text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider text-[var(--text-2)]">
              Sync Server
            </span>
          </div>
          {!isEditingServer && (
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setIsEditingServer(true)}
              className="text-xs font-medium text-[var(--text-3)] hover:text-[var(--accent)] hover:underline cursor-pointer"
              title="Only needed if you run your own Verso server"
            >
              Advanced…
            </button>
          )}
        </div>

        {isEditingServer ? (
          <div className="flex flex-col gap-2 mt-1">
            <p className="text-[11px] text-[var(--text-3)]">
              The address of a Verso API server, not the database. Leave the default unless you host your own.
            </p>
            <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
              <input
                type="url"
                aria-label="Verso server address"
                value={serverUrlVal}
                onChange={(e) => {
                  setServerUrlVal(e.target.value);
                  setServerError(null);
                }}
                className="flex-1 min-w-[180px] px-3 py-1.5 text-xs rounded-[var(--r-sm)] bg-[var(--bg-base)] border border-[var(--stroke)] text-[var(--text-1)] outline-none focus:border-[var(--accent)]"
                placeholder={DEFAULT_SERVER_URL}
              />
              <Button size="sm" variant="accent" onClick={handleSaveServerUrl}>
                Save
              </Button>
              <Button size="sm" variant="subtle" onClick={handleResetServerUrl}>
                Use default
              </Button>
              <Button size="sm" variant="subtle" onClick={() => setIsEditingServer(false)}>
                Cancel
              </Button>
            </div>
            {serverError && <p className="text-xs text-[var(--danger)]">{serverError}</p>}
          </div>
        ) : (
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2 min-w-0">
            <span className="text-xs font-mono text-[var(--text-2)] truncate">{serverUrlVal}</span>
            {serverCheck === 'checking' && (
              <span className="text-[11px] text-[var(--text-3)] shrink-0">checking…</span>
            )}
            {serverCheck === 'ok' && <span className="text-[11px] text-[var(--success)] shrink-0">reachable</span>}
            {serverCheck === 'unreachable' && (
              <span className="text-[11px] text-[var(--danger)] shrink-0">not reachable</span>
            )}
          </div>
        )}
      </section>

      {/* When NOT signed in: Login / Register Form */}
      {!currentUser ? (
        <section className="flex flex-col gap-4">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
            <h3 className="text-sm font-semibold text-[var(--text-1)]">
              {authMode === 'login' ? 'Sign in to Verso' : 'Create an Account'}
            </h3>
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => {
                setAuthMode(authMode === 'login' ? 'register' : 'login');
                setAuthError(null);
              }}
              className="text-xs text-[var(--accent)] hover:underline cursor-pointer"
            >
              {authMode === 'login' ? 'Need an account? Register' : 'Already have an account? Sign in'}
            </button>
          </div>

          <form onSubmit={handleAuthSubmit} className="flex flex-col gap-3">
            <div className="flex flex-col gap-1">
              <label htmlFor="auth-email-input" className="text-xs font-medium text-[var(--text-2)]">Email address</label>
              <input
                id="auth-email-input"
                type="email"
                required
                value={email}
                onChange={(e) => setEmail(e.target.value)}
                placeholder="reader@example.com"
                className="px-3 py-2 text-sm rounded-[var(--r-md)] bg-[var(--layer-1)] border border-[var(--stroke)] text-[var(--text-1)] outline-none focus:border-[var(--accent)]"
              />
            </div>

            <div className="flex flex-col gap-1">
              <label htmlFor="auth-password-input" className="text-xs font-medium text-[var(--text-2)]">Password</label>
              <input
                id="auth-password-input"
                type="password"
                required
                minLength={8}
                value={password}
                onChange={(e) => setPassword(e.target.value)}
                placeholder="••••••••"
                className="px-3 py-2 text-sm rounded-[var(--r-md)] bg-[var(--layer-1)] border border-[var(--stroke)] text-[var(--text-1)] outline-none focus:border-[var(--accent)]"
              />
            </div>

            {authError && (
              <div className="p-2.5 rounded-[var(--r-sm)] bg-[color-mix(in_oklab,var(--danger)_15%,transparent)] text-xs text-[var(--danger)]">
                {authError}
              </div>
            )}

            <Button
              type="submit"
              variant="accent"
              disabled={authLoading}
              className="mt-2 w-full justify-center"
            >
              {authLoading ? (
                <>
                  <Loader2 className="w-4 h-4 animate-spin mr-1.5" />
                  {authMode === 'login' ? 'Signing in…' : 'Creating account…'}
                </>
              ) : (
                authMode === 'login' ? 'Sign in' : 'Create account'
              )}
            </Button>
          </form>
        </section>
      ) : (
        /* When SIGNED IN: Account Management */
        <section className="flex flex-col gap-5">
          {/* User overview */}
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between p-3 rounded-[var(--r-md)] bg-[var(--layer-2)] border border-[var(--stroke)]">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
              <div className="w-9 h-9 rounded-full bg-[var(--accent-soft)] text-[var(--accent)] flex items-center justify-center font-bold">
                <User className="w-5 h-5" />
              </div>
              <div>
                <div className="text-sm font-semibold text-[var(--text-1)]">{currentUser.email}</div>
                <div className="text-xs text-[var(--text-2)] flex items-center gap-1.5 mt-0.5">
                  <HardDrive className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                  <span>
                    Storage used: {accountInfo ? fmtBytes(accountInfo.storageUsed) : 'Calculating…'}
                  </span>
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>

            <Button
              size="sm"
              variant="subtle"
              onClick={() => setSignOutOpen(true)}
              icon={<LogOut className="w-4 h-4" />}
            >
              Sign out
            </Button>
          </div>

          {/* Connected Devices */}
          <div className="flex flex-col gap-2">
            <div className="text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider text-[var(--text-3)]">
              Registered Devices ({devices.length})
            </div>
            <div className="flex flex-col gap-1.5 max-h-36 overflow-y-auto pr-1">
              {devices.map((d) => (
                <div
                  key={d.id}
                  className="flex items-center justify-between p-2 rounded-[var(--r-sm)] bg-[var(--layer-1)] border border-[var(--stroke)] text-xs"
                >
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-2 truncate">
                    <Laptop className="w-4 h-4 text-[var(--text-2)] shrink-0" />
                    <span className="font-medium text-[var(--text-1)] truncate">{d.name}</span>
                    <span className="text-[10px] px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded-full bg-[var(--layer-3)] text-[var(--text-3)] uppercase">
                      {d.platform}
                    </span>
                  </div>
                  {d.last_seen_at && (
                    <span className="text-[11px] text-[var(--text-3)] shrink-0">
                      {fmtDate(d.last_seen_at)}
                    </span>
                  )}
                </div>
              ))}
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Active Sessions */}
          <div className="flex flex-col gap-2">
            <div className="text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider text-[var(--text-3)]">
              Active Sessions ({sessions.length})
            </div>
            <div className="flex flex-col gap-1.5 max-h-36 overflow-y-auto pr-1">
              {sessions.map((s) => (
                <div
                  key={s.token_hash}
                  className="flex items-center justify-between p-2 rounded-[var(--r-sm)] bg-[var(--layer-1)] border border-[var(--stroke)] text-xs"
                >
                  <div>
                    <span className="font-medium text-[var(--text-1)]">
                      {s.device_name || 'Browser Session'}
                    </span>
                    <span className="text-[11px] text-[var(--text-3)] ml-2">
                      Created {new Date(s.created_at).toLocaleDateString()}
                    </span>
                  </div>
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => handleRevokeSession(s.token_hash)}
                    className="text-[11px] text-[var(--danger)] hover:underline cursor-pointer"
                  >
                    Revoke
                  </button>
                </div>
              ))}
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Change password */}
          <div className="flex flex-col gap-2 pt-2 border-t border-[var(--stroke)]">
            <div className="text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider text-[var(--text-3)]">
              Change Password
            </div>
            <form onSubmit={handleChangePassword} className="flex flex-col gap-2">
              <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2">
                <input
                  type="password"
                  required
                  placeholder="Current password"
                  value={currPass}
                  onChange={(e) => setCurrPass(e.target.value)}
                  className="px-3 py-1.5 text-xs rounded-[var(--r-sm)] bg-[var(--layer-1)] border border-[var(--stroke)] text-[var(--text-1)] outline-none focus:border-[var(--accent)]"
                />
                <input
                  type="password"
                  required
                  minLength={8}
                  placeholder="New password"
                  value={newPass}
                  onChange={(e) => setNewPass(e.target.value)}
                  className="px-3 py-1.5 text-xs rounded-[var(--r-sm)] bg-[var(--layer-1)] border border-[var(--stroke)] text-[var(--text-1)] outline-none focus:border-[var(--accent)]"
                />
              </div>

              {passMsg && (
                <div
                  className={`p-2 rounded-[var(--r-sm)] text-xs ${
                    passMsg.error
                      ? 'bg-[color-mix(in_oklab,var(--danger)_15%,transparent)] text-[var(--danger)]'
                      : 'bg-[color-mix(in_oklab,var(--success)_15%,transparent)] text-[var(--success)]'
                  }`}
                >
                  {passMsg.text}
                </div>
              )}

              <Button
                type="submit"
                size="sm"
                variant="standard"
                disabled={passLoading || !currPass || !newPass}
                className="self-start"
              >
                {passLoading ? 'Updating…' : 'Update password'}
              </Button>
            </form>
          </div>

          {/* Danger zone: Delete account */}
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between pt-4 border-t border-[var(--stroke)]">
            <div>
              <div className="text-xs font-semibold text-[var(--danger)]">Delete Account</div>
              <div className="text-[11px] text-[var(--text-3)]">Permanently erase your account and server storage</div>
            </div>
            <Button
              size="sm"
              variant="subtle"
              className="text-[var(--danger)] hover:bg-[color-mix(in_oklab,var(--danger)_15%,transparent)]"
              onClick={() => setDeleteAccountOpen(true)}
              icon={<Trash2 className="w-4 h-4" />}
            >
              Delete account
            </Button>
          </div>
        </section>
      )}

      {/* Sign Out Confirmation Dialog */}
      <Dialog
        open={signOutOpen}
        onOpenChange={setSignOutOpen}
        title="Sign Out"
        description="Choose how you'd like to sign out from this device."
      >
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-4 py-3">
          <p className="text-xs text-[var(--text-2)] leading-relaxed">
            Would you like to keep downloaded books and your local reading history on this device, or completely clear everything?
          </p>
          <div className="flex flex-col gap-2">
            <Button
              variant="standard"
              onClick={() => handleSignOut(false)}
              className="w-full justify-center"
            >
              Keep books on this device
            </Button>
            <Button
              variant="accent"
              onClick={() => handleSignOut(true)}
              className="w-full justify-center bg-[var(--danger)] hover:opacity-90"
            >
              Remove everything
            </Button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </Dialog>

      {/* Delete Account Confirmation Dialog */}
      <Dialog
        open={deleteAccountOpen}
        onOpenChange={setDeleteAccountOpen}
        title="Delete Account Permanently"
        description="This action cannot be undone."
      >
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-4 py-3">
          <div className="p-3 rounded-[var(--r-md)] bg-[color-mix(in_oklab,var(--danger)_15%,transparent)] border border-[color-mix(in_oklab,var(--danger)_30%,transparent)] flex items-start gap-2.5">
            <ShieldAlert className="w-5 h-5 text-[var(--danger)] shrink-0 mt-0.5" />
            <p className="text-xs text-[var(--text-1)] leading-relaxed">
              Are you sure? This will permanently delete your account, your synced library, reading progress, and all uploaded files from the server.
            </p>
          </div>

          <div className="flex justify-end gap-2 mt-2">
            <Button variant="subtle" onClick={() => setDeleteAccountOpen(false)}>
              Cancel
            </Button>
            <Button
              variant="accent"
              onClick={handleDeleteAccount}
              className="bg-[var(--danger)] hover:opacity-90"
            >
              Yes, delete account
            </Button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </Dialog>

      {/* Merge Prompt Dialog */}
      <Dialog
        open={!!mergePromptUser}
        onOpenChange={(open) => !open && setMergePromptUser(null)}
        title="Merge Account Data?"
        description={`Signing into ${mergePromptUser}`}
      >
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-4 py-3">
          <p className="text-xs text-[var(--text-2)] leading-relaxed">
            This device has existing library data from a different account. Would you like to merge this device's library into <strong>{mergePromptUser}</strong>, or start fresh?
          </p>
          <div className="flex flex-col gap-2">
            <Button
              variant="accent"
              onClick={() => handleMergeChoice('merge')}
              className="w-full justify-center"
            >
              Merge into {mergePromptUser}
            </Button>
            <Button
              variant="standard"
              onClick={() => handleMergeChoice('fresh')}
              className="w-full justify-center"
            >
              Start fresh (wipe local data)
            </Button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </Dialog>
    </div>
  );
};

import { db } from '../../db';

let memoryAccessToken: string | null = null;

export const DEFAULT_SERVER_URL =
  import.meta.env.PROD
    ? (import.meta.env.VITE_SERVER_URL || 'https://verso-6epp.onrender.com')
    : (import.meta.env.VITE_SERVER_URL || 'http://localhost:8080');

export interface StoredUser {
  id: string;
  email: string;
}

export function getAccessToken(): string | null {
  return memoryAccessToken;
}

export function setAccessToken(token: string | null): void {
  memoryAccessToken = token;
}

export async function getRefreshToken(): Promise<string | null> {
  const row = await db.kv.get('refresh_token');
  return typeof row?.value === 'string' ? row.value : null;
}

export async function setRefreshToken(token: string | null): Promise<void> {
  if (token) {
    await db.kv.put({ key: 'refresh_token', value: token });
  } else {
    await db.kv.delete('refresh_token');
  }
}

/**
 * Validates and normalises a Verso server address. Returns an error message for
 * values that can't work, notably a database address: the app talks to the Verso
 * API, and only the API talks to Postgres/Supabase.
 */
export function normalizeServerUrl(input: string): { url: string } | { error: string } {
  let raw = input.trim().replace(/\/+$/, '');
  if (!raw) return { error: 'Enter the address of your Verso server.' };
  if (/^postgres(ql)?:/i.test(raw)) {
    return {
      error:
        'That is a database connection string. Enter the Verso server address instead (for example https://verso.example.com).',
    };
  }
  if (!/^https?:\/\//i.test(raw)) {
    const local = /^(localhost|127\.|10\.|192\.168\.)/.test(raw);
    raw = `${local ? 'http' : 'https'}://${raw}`;
  }
  let u: URL;
  try {
    u = new URL(raw);
  } catch {
    return { error: 'That does not look like a web address.' };
  }
  if (/supabase\.(co|com)$/i.test(u.hostname) || u.port === '5432' || u.port === '6543') {
    return {
      error:
        'That is the Supabase database address. The app connects to the Verso API server, which then talks to Supabase (for example http://localhost:8080 or your deployed server).',
    };
  }
  return { url: `${u.origin}${u.pathname.replace(/\/+$/, '')}` };
}

export async function getServerUrl(): Promise<string> {
  const row = await db.kv.get('server_url');
  if (typeof row?.value !== 'string') return DEFAULT_SERVER_URL;
  if (row.value.includes('duckdns.org')) {
    await db.kv.delete('server_url');
    return DEFAULT_SERVER_URL;
  }
  // Ignore a previously saved unusable value (e.g. a database address).
  const checked = normalizeServerUrl(row.value);
  return 'url' in checked ? checked.url : DEFAULT_SERVER_URL;
}

export async function setServerUrl(url: string): Promise<void> {
  const checked = normalizeServerUrl(url);
  if ('error' in checked) throw new Error(checked.error);
  await db.kv.put({ key: 'server_url', value: checked.url });
}

/** Forget a custom server and use the address built into the app. */
export async function resetServerUrl(): Promise<void> {
  await db.kv.delete('server_url');
}

export async function getAuthUser(): Promise<StoredUser | null> {
  const row = await db.kv.get('auth_user');
  if (row?.value && typeof row.value === 'object') {
    const u = row.value as Record<string, unknown>;
    if (typeof u.id === 'string' && typeof u.email === 'string') {
      return { id: u.id, email: u.email };
    }
  }
  return null;
}

export async function setAuthUser(user: StoredUser | null): Promise<void> {
  if (user) {
    await db.kv.put({ key: 'auth_user', value: user });
  } else {
    await db.kv.delete('auth_user');
  }
}

export async function clearAuth(): Promise<void> {
  memoryAccessToken = null;
  await Promise.all([
    db.kv.delete('refresh_token'),
    db.kv.delete('auth_user'),
  ]);
}

import {
  getAccessToken,
  setAccessToken,
  getRefreshToken,
  setRefreshToken,
  getServerUrl,
  setAuthUser,
  getAuthUser,
  clearAuth,
} from '../auth/storage';
import { ACCOUNT_DELETED, STORAGE_OFFLINE } from '@verso/shared';
import { setClockOffset } from '../../db';
import type {
  AuthResponse,
  SyncPushResponse,
  SyncPullResponse,
  EventsTokenResponse,
  SyncTableName,
} from '@verso/shared';

export class ApiError extends Error {
  status: number;
  data: unknown;

  constructor(
    status: number,
    data: unknown,
    message?: string,
  ) {
    super(message || `API error ${status}`);
    this.name = 'ApiError';
    this.status = status;
    this.data = data;
  }
}

/** The server reports this account no longer exists (deleted on another device). */
export class AccountDeletedError extends ApiError {
  constructor(data: unknown) {
    super(401, data, 'This account was deleted.');
    this.name = 'AccountDeletedError';
  }
}

/** The server is up but can't reach the book file storage (s3rver PC / tunnel down). */
export class StorageOfflineError extends ApiError {
  constructor(data: unknown) {
    super(
      503,
      data,
      'Your book storage is offline. Start your storage (s3rver + ngrok) on your PC, then try again.',
    );
    this.name = 'StorageOfflineError';
  }
}

const isStorageOffline = (body: unknown) =>
  !!body && typeof body === 'object' && (body as { code?: unknown }).code === STORAGE_OFFLINE;

const isAccountDeleted = (body: unknown) =>
  !!body && typeof body === 'object' && (body as { code?: unknown }).code === ACCOUNT_DELETED;

let refreshPromise: Promise<string> | null = null;

export async function executeTokenRefresh(): Promise<string> {
  if (refreshPromise) {
    return refreshPromise;
  }

  refreshPromise = (async () => {
    try {
      const refreshToken = await getRefreshToken();
      if (!refreshToken) {
        throw new ApiError(401, null, 'No refresh token available');
      }

      const serverUrl = await getServerUrl();
      // The user id lets the server say "account deleted" even though deleting
      // the account also removed this refresh token.
      const user = await getAuthUser();
      const res = await fetch(`${serverUrl}/auth/refresh`, {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({ refreshToken, userId: user?.id }),
      });

      if (!res.ok) {
        const body = await res.json().catch(() => null);
        if (isAccountDeleted(body)) throw new AccountDeletedError(body);
        // Only a real rejection ends the session. A 5xx (e.g. the server waking
        // from sleep) or a proxy error used to sign the user out silently.
        if (res.status === 401 || res.status === 403) {
          await clearAuth();
          throw new ApiError(res.status, body, 'Session expired');
        }
        throw new ApiError(res.status, body, `Server unavailable (${res.status}). Try again shortly.`);
      }

      const data: AuthResponse = await res.json();
      setAccessToken(data.accessToken);
      await setRefreshToken(data.refreshToken);
      await setAuthUser(data.user);
      return data.accessToken;
    } finally {
      refreshPromise = null;
    }
  })();

  return refreshPromise;
}

interface RequestOptions {
  method?: string;
  headers?: Record<string, string>;
  body?: BodyInit | null;
  auth?: boolean;
}

export async function request<T>(path: string, options: RequestOptions = {}): Promise<T> {
  const serverUrl = await getServerUrl();
  const requiresAuth = options.auth !== false;
  const headers: Record<string, string> = { ...options.headers };

  if (requiresAuth) {
    let token = getAccessToken();
    if (!token) {
      try {
        token = await executeTokenRefresh();
      } catch (err) {
        if (err instanceof AccountDeletedError) throw err;
        throw new ApiError(401, null, 'Authentication required');
      }
    }
    headers['Authorization'] = `Bearer ${token}`;
  }

  let res = await fetch(`${serverUrl}${path}`, {
    method: options.method || 'GET',
    headers,
    body: options.body,
  });

  // Handle 401 with single-flight token refresh and retry
  if (res.status === 401 && requiresAuth) {
    const body = await res.clone().json().catch(() => null);
    if (isAccountDeleted(body)) throw new AccountDeletedError(body);
    // executeTokenRefresh clears auth itself when the server rejects the session
    // (and only then; a temporarily unavailable server no longer signs you out).
    const newToken = await executeTokenRefresh();
    headers['Authorization'] = `Bearer ${newToken}`;
    res = await fetch(`${serverUrl}${path}`, {
      method: options.method || 'GET',
      headers,
      body: options.body,
    });
  }

  if (!res.ok) {
    const errorBody = await res.json().catch(() => null);
    if (res.status === 401 && isAccountDeleted(errorBody)) throw new AccountDeletedError(errorBody);
    if (res.status === 503 && isStorageOffline(errorBody)) throw new StorageOfflineError(errorBody);
    throw new ApiError(res.status, errorBody);
  }

  const contentType = res.headers.get('content-type') || '';
  if (contentType.includes('application/json')) {
    const data = await res.json();
    // Update client clock offset if serverTime is present in response
    if (data && typeof data.serverTime === 'number') {
      setClockOffset(data.serverTime - Date.now());
    }
    return data as T;
  }

  return (res as unknown) as T;
}

// Auth API
export async function authRegister(
  email: string,
  password: string,
  deviceName: string = 'Browser',
): Promise<AuthResponse> {
  const data = await request<AuthResponse>('/auth/register', {
    method: 'POST',
    headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
    body: JSON.stringify({ email, password, deviceName }),
    auth: false,
  });
  setAccessToken(data.accessToken);
  await setRefreshToken(data.refreshToken);
  await setAuthUser(data.user);
  return data;
}

export async function authLogin(
  email: string,
  password: string,
  deviceName: string = 'Browser',
): Promise<AuthResponse> {
  const data = await request<AuthResponse>('/auth/login', {
    method: 'POST',
    headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
    body: JSON.stringify({ email, password, deviceName }),
    auth: false,
  });
  setAccessToken(data.accessToken);
  await setRefreshToken(data.refreshToken);
  await setAuthUser(data.user);
  return data;
}

export async function authLogout(): Promise<void> {
  const refreshToken = await getRefreshToken();
  if (refreshToken) {
    try {
      await request('/auth/logout', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({ refreshToken }),
        auth: false,
      });
    } catch {
      // Ignore network errors on logout
    }
  }
  await clearAuth();
}

// Sync API
export async function syncPush(
  changes: Partial<Record<SyncTableName, Record<string, unknown>[]>>,
): Promise<SyncPushResponse> {
  return request<SyncPushResponse>('/sync/push', {
    method: 'POST',
    headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
    body: JSON.stringify({ changes }),
  });
}

export async function syncPull(since: number = 0): Promise<SyncPullResponse> {
  return request<SyncPullResponse>(`/sync/pull?since=${since}`);
}

export async function syncEventsToken(): Promise<EventsTokenResponse> {
  return request<EventsTokenResponse>('/sync/events-token', {
    method: 'POST',
  });
}

// Files API
export async function headFile(hash: string): Promise<boolean> {
  const serverUrl = await getServerUrl();
  const token = getAccessToken() || (await executeTokenRefresh().catch(() => null));
  if (!token) return false;

  const res = await fetch(`${serverUrl}/files/${hash}`, {
    method: 'HEAD',
    headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` },
  });
  return res.status === 200;
}

export async function putFile(hash: string, blob: Blob): Promise<{ stored: boolean; size?: number }> {
  return request<{ stored: boolean; size?: number }>(`/files/${hash}`, {
    method: 'PUT',
    headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/octet-stream' },
    body: blob,
  });
}

async function sha256Hex(blob: Blob): Promise<string> {
  const digest = await crypto.subtle.digest('SHA-256', await blob.arrayBuffer());
  return Array.from(new Uint8Array(digest), (b) => b.toString(16).padStart(2, '0')).join('');
}

/**
 * Downloads a stored file and verifies it against its hash before returning it.
 * An unverified download (cut-off transfer, proxy error page) used to be saved
 * as the book and could then never open, and wiping the server never fixed it.
 */
export async function getFile(hash: string): Promise<Blob> {
  const serverUrl = await getServerUrl();
  // A sleeping free-tier server can take about a minute to answer.
  const fetchWithTimeout = async (token: string) => {
    const ctrl = new AbortController();
    const timer = setTimeout(() => ctrl.abort(), 120_000);
    try {
      return await fetch(`${serverUrl}/files/${hash}`, {
        headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` },
        signal: ctrl.signal,
      });
    } finally {
      clearTimeout(timer);
    }
  };

  let res = await fetchWithTimeout(getAccessToken() || (await executeTokenRefresh()));
  if (res.status === 401) {
    const body = await res.clone().json().catch(() => null);
    if (isAccountDeleted(body)) throw new AccountDeletedError(body);
    res = await fetchWithTimeout(await executeTokenRefresh());
  }

  if (!res.ok) {
    if (res.status === 503) {
      const body = await res.json().catch(() => null);
      if (isStorageOffline(body)) throw new StorageOfflineError(body);
    }
    throw new ApiError(
      res.status,
      null,
      res.status === 404 ? 'This book file is not on your server.' : `Failed to download file (${res.status})`,
    );
  }
  const blob = await res.blob();
  if ((await sha256Hex(blob)) !== hash) {
    throw new ApiError(422, null, 'The downloaded file was incomplete or corrupted. Try again.');
  }
  return blob;
}

export async function listFiles(): Promise<Array<{ hash: string; size: number }>> {
  return request<Array<{ hash: string; size: number }>>('/files');
}

// Account API
export interface AccountInfo {
  id: string;
  email: string;
  storageUsed: number;
  createdAt: string;
}

export async function getAccount(): Promise<AccountInfo> {
  return request<AccountInfo>('/account');
}

export async function patchPassword(currentPassword: string, newPassword: string): Promise<void> {
  await request('/account/password', {
    method: 'PATCH',
    headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
    body: JSON.stringify({ currentPassword, newPassword }),
  });
}

export async function deleteAccount(): Promise<void> {
  try {
    await request('/account', { method: 'DELETE' });
  } catch (err) {
    // Already deleted (e.g. from another device): that's the outcome we wanted.
    if (!(err instanceof AccountDeletedError)) throw err;
  }
  await clearAuth();
}

export interface SessionInfo {
  token_hash: string;
  device_name: string | null;
  created_at: string;
  expires_at: string;
}

export async function getSessions(): Promise<SessionInfo[]> {
  return request<SessionInfo[]>('/account/sessions');
}

export async function deleteSession(tokenHash: string): Promise<void> {
  await request(`/account/sessions/${tokenHash}`, { method: 'DELETE' });
}

================
File: apps/client/src/features/sync/engine.ts
================
import { create } from 'zustand';
import { db, SYNC_TABLES, now, dirty, deviceId } from '../../db';
import {
  syncPush,
  syncPull,
  putFile,
  getFile,
  listFiles,
  syncEventsToken,
  AccountDeletedError,
  StorageOfflineError,
} from './api';
import { getAuthUser, getRefreshToken, getServerUrl, clearAuth } from '../auth/storage';
import type { SyncTableName } from '@verso/shared';

/**
 * Sync bookkeeping, scoped to one server database + account. The cursor and the
 * set of files known to be on the server are only meaningful for the database
 * they came from: after the server's database or bucket is wiped, or the app is
 * pointed at another server or account, they must be reset, otherwise the
 * client never re-pulls (cursor ahead of the new sequence) or re-uploads (it
 * "knows" the files are there).
 */
interface SyncBookkeeping {
  scope: string;
  cursor: number;
  uploaded: string[];
  lastFileCheck: number;
}
const SYNC_STATE_KEY = 'sync_state';
// How often to compare local files with the server's file list (self-heals a
// wiped or partially lost bucket) even when nothing else changed.
const FILE_RECONCILE_MS = 10 * 60_000;
// The device row is refreshed this rarely; writing it on every sync made every
// sync schedule the next one, so sync ran in a loop every ~2s.
const DEVICE_SEEN_MS = 12 * 60 * 60_000;

async function readSyncState(): Promise<SyncBookkeeping | null> {
  const row = await db.kv.get(SYNC_STATE_KEY);
  const v = row?.value as SyncBookkeeping | undefined;
  return v && typeof v.scope === 'string' ? v : null;
}

async function writeSyncState(state: SyncBookkeeping): Promise<void> {
  await db.kv.put({ key: SYNC_STATE_KEY, value: state });
}

/**
 * Marks every synced row for upload WITHOUT changing updated_at, so the server's
 * last-write-wins still keeps whichever copy really is newer. (Bumping
 * updated_at here let a long-offline device overwrite newer edits on sign-in.)
 */
export async function markAllForUpload(): Promise<void> {
  for (const table of SYNC_TABLES) {
    await db.table(table).toCollection().modify({ _dirty: 1 });
  }
}

/** Removes everything this device holds for the account (used on deletion/sign-out). */
export async function wipeLocalAccountData(): Promise<void> {
  for (const table of SYNC_TABLES) {
    await db.table(table).clear();
  }
  await db.files.clear();
  await db.covers.clear();
  await db.kv.bulkDelete([SYNC_STATE_KEY, 'sync_cursor', 'uploaded_files']);
}

// 'signedOut': no account, so nothing is synced (previously shown as 'idle' = "Synced").
export type SyncStatus = 'idle' | 'syncing' | 'offline' | 'error' | 'signedOut';

export interface SyncState {
  status: SyncStatus;
  lastSyncAt: number | null;
  lastError: string | null;
  pendingChanges: number;
  filesUploadingCount: number;
  filesUploadingTotal: number;
  isOnline: boolean;
  /** Set when the server reported this account deleted (local copy was wiped). */
  accountDeleted: boolean;
  /** Book file storage unreachable; data still syncs, files wait until it's back. */
  storageOffline: boolean;
  runSync: () => Promise<void>;
  updatePendingCount: () => Promise<number>;
}

export const useSyncStore = create<SyncState>((set) => ({
  status: typeof navigator !== 'undefined' && !navigator.onLine ? 'offline' : 'signedOut',
  lastSyncAt: null,
  lastError: null,
  pendingChanges: 0,
  filesUploadingCount: 0,
  filesUploadingTotal: 0,
  isOnline: typeof navigator !== 'undefined' ? navigator.onLine : true,
  accountDeleted: false,
  storageOffline: false,

  updatePendingCount: async () => {
    let count = 0;
    try {
      for (const table of SYNC_TABLES) {
        const dirtyInTable = await db[table].where('_dirty').equals(1).count();
        count += dirtyInTable;
      }
      set({ pendingChanges: count });
    } catch {
      // Ignore count errors if DB is closing/migrating
    }
    return count;
  },

  runSync: async () => {
    await syncEngine.runSync();
  },
}));

export class SyncEngine {
  private isRunning = false;
  private backoffAttempt = 0;
  private backoffTimer: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | null = null;
  private debounceTimer: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | null = null;
  private eventSource: EventSource | null = null;
  private sseReconnectTimer: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | null = null;
  private intervalTimer: ReturnType<typeof setInterval> | null = null;
  private initialized = false;

  async isUserSignedIn(): Promise<boolean> {
    const token = await getRefreshToken();
    return !!token;
  }

  // Device registration helper
  async registerDevice(): Promise<void> {
    try {
      const id = deviceId();
      let name = localStorage.getItem('folio_device_name');
      if (!name) {
        const isWindows = typeof navigator !== 'undefined' && navigator.userAgent.includes('Windows');
        const isAndroid = typeof navigator !== 'undefined' && navigator.userAgent.includes('Android');
        name = isWindows ? 'Windows PC' : isAndroid ? 'Android Device' : 'Web Browser';
        localStorage.setItem('folio_device_name', name);
      }
      const platform = typeof window !== 'undefined' && '__TAURI__' in window
        ? (navigator.userAgent.includes('Android') ? 'android' : 'windows')
        : 'web';

      const existing = await db.devices.get(id);
      const fresh =
        existing &&
        !existing.deleted_at &&
        existing.name === name &&
        existing.platform === platform &&
        now() - (existing.last_seen_at ?? 0) < DEVICE_SEEN_MS;
      if (fresh) return;
      await db.devices.put({
        id,
        name,
        platform,
        last_seen_at: now(),
        deleted_at: null,
        ...dirty(),
      });
    } catch {
      // Device registration is non-fatal
    }
  }

  // Trigger sync with 2s debounce on local dirty writes
  scheduleDebouncedSync(): void {
    if (this.debounceTimer) clearTimeout(this.debounceTimer);
    this.debounceTimer = setTimeout(() => {
      void this.runSync();
    }, 2000);
    void useSyncStore.getState().updatePendingCount();
  }

  // Core 5-stage sync algorithm per §6.4
  async runSync(): Promise<void> {
    const isOnline = typeof navigator !== 'undefined' ? navigator.onLine : true;
    useSyncStore.setState({ isOnline });

    if (!isOnline) {
      useSyncStore.setState({ status: 'offline' });
      return;
    }

    const signedIn = await this.isUserSignedIn();
    if (!signedIn) {
      useSyncStore.setState({ status: 'signedOut' });
      return;
    }

    if (this.isRunning) {
      return;
    }

    // Single-tab coordination via Web Locks API
    if (typeof navigator !== 'undefined' && navigator.locks) {
      await navigator.locks.request('folio-sync', { ifAvailable: true }, async (lock) => {
        if (!lock) {
          // Another tab is actively holding the sync lock
          return;
        }
        await this.executeSyncStages();
      });
    } else {
      await this.executeSyncStages();
    }
  }

  private async executeSyncStages(): Promise<void> {
    this.isRunning = true;
    useSyncStore.setState({ status: 'syncing', lastError: null });

    try {
      // Stage 0: which server database + account is this? Reset if it changed.
      const state = await this.resolveScope();

      // Register current device first
      await this.registerDevice();

      // Stage 1: PUSH
      await this.stage1Push();

      // Stage 2: FILES UP (book storage may be offline: then only files wait)
      const filesOk = await this.withFileStorage(() => this.stage2FilesUp(state));

      // Stage 3: PULL
      await this.stage3Pull(state);

      // Stage 4: FILES DOWN
      if (filesOk) await this.withFileStorage(() => this.stage4FilesDown());

      // Stage 5: Success
      useSyncStore.setState({
        status: 'idle',
        lastSyncAt: Date.now(),
        lastError: null,
      });
      this.backoffAttempt = 0;
      await useSyncStore.getState().updatePendingCount();
    } catch (err: unknown) {
      if (err instanceof AccountDeletedError) {
        await this.handleAccountDeleted();
        return;
      }
      const errorMessage = err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err);
      useSyncStore.setState({
        status: 'error',
        lastError: errorMessage,
      });

      // Exponential backoff 5s → 5m
      const delay = Math.min(300_000, 5000 * Math.pow(2, this.backoffAttempt));
      this.backoffAttempt++;
      if (this.backoffTimer) clearTimeout(this.backoffTimer);
      this.backoffTimer = setTimeout(() => {
        void this.runSync();
      }, delay);
    } finally {
      this.isRunning = false;
      await useSyncStore.getState().updatePendingCount();
    }
  }

  /**
   * Identifies the server database and account this device syncs with. When it
   * differs from what the stored cursor belongs to, starts over: cursor 0, no
   * files assumed uploaded, every local row queued for upload (timestamps kept).
   */
  private async resolveScope(): Promise<SyncBookkeeping> {
    const [serverUrl, user, probe] = await Promise.all([
      getServerUrl(),
      getAuthUser(),
      // since=MAX returns no rows, just the server's instance id.
      syncPull(Number.MAX_SAFE_INTEGER),
    ]);
    const scope = `${serverUrl}|${user?.id ?? ''}|${probe.instanceId ?? 'legacy'}`;
    const saved = await readSyncState();
    if (saved?.scope === scope) return saved;

    const fresh: SyncBookkeeping = { scope, cursor: 0, uploaded: [], lastFileCheck: 0 };
    await markAllForUpload();
    await db.kv.bulkDelete(['sync_cursor', 'uploaded_files']);
    await writeSyncState(fresh);
    return fresh;
  }

  /**
   * Runs a file stage. If the book storage is offline (the PC running s3rver is
   * off or its tunnel is closed), library/progress/highlights still sync and the
   * files are retried on the next sync, instead of the whole sync failing.
   */
  private async withFileStorage(stage: () => Promise<void>): Promise<boolean> {
    try {
      await stage();
      useSyncStore.setState({ storageOffline: false });
      return true;
    } catch (err) {
      if (err instanceof StorageOfflineError) {
        useSyncStore.setState({ storageOffline: true, filesUploadingCount: 0, filesUploadingTotal: 0 });
        return false;
      }
      throw err;
    }
  }

  /** The server says the account no longer exists: wipe this device's copy. */
  private async handleAccountDeleted(): Promise<void> {
    this.disconnectSSE();
    await wipeLocalAccountData();
    await clearAuth();
    useSyncStore.setState({
      status: 'signedOut',
      lastError: 'This account was deleted. Its data has been removed from this device.',
      accountDeleted: true,
    });
  }

  // 1) PUSH
  private async stage1Push(): Promise<void> {
    for (const table of SYNC_TABLES) {
      const targetTable = db.table(table);
      while (true) {
        // Query dirty rows in batches of 500
        const dirtyRows = await targetTable.where('_dirty').equals(1).limit(500).toArray();
        if (dirtyRows.length === 0) break;

        const cleanRows = dirtyRows.map((r: { _dirty?: number }) => {
          const { _dirty, ...rest } = r;
          return rest as Record<string, unknown>;
        });

        const res = await syncPush({ [table]: cleanRows });
        const winners = new Map<string, Record<string, unknown>>(
          (res.serverRows?.[table] ?? []).map((r) => [String(r.id), r]),
        );

        // Compare-and-set: only rows unchanged since we read them are settled.
        // A rejected (stale) row takes the server's winning copy, so this device
        // converges instead of keeping its losing edit forever.
        await db.transaction('rw', targetTable, async () => {
          for (const row of dirtyRows) {
            const current = await targetTable.get(row.id);
            if (!current || current.updated_at !== row.updated_at) continue;
            const winner = winners.get(String(row.id));
            if (winner) await targetTable.put({ ...winner, _dirty: 0 });
            else await targetTable.update(row.id, { _dirty: 0 });
          }
        });

        if (dirtyRows.length < 500) break;
      }
    }
  }

  // 2) FILES UP
  private async stage2FilesUp(state: SyncBookkeeping): Promise<void> {
    const uploaded = new Set<string>(state.uploaded);

    // Periodically (and right after a reset) trust the server's actual file list
    // over the local "already uploaded" memory, so a wiped or partly lost bucket
    // is refilled from whichever devices still have the files.
    if (Date.now() - state.lastFileCheck > FILE_RECONCILE_MS) {
      const onServer = new Set((await listFiles()).map((f) => f.hash));
      uploaded.clear();
      for (const h of onServer) uploaded.add(h);
      state.lastFileCheck = Date.now();
    }

    const pending: Array<{ hash: string; getBlob: () => Promise<Blob | null> }> = [];
    let storageDown: StorageOfflineError | null = null;
    const books = await db.books.filter((b) => !b.deleted_at).toArray();
    for (const book of books) {
      if (book.file_hash && !uploaded.has(book.file_hash)) {
        pending.push({ hash: book.file_hash, getBlob: async () => (await db.files.get(book.file_hash))?.blob ?? null });
      }
      // Covers are stored under the book id; cover_hash is the hash of that image.
      // (Looking them up by cover_hash found nothing, so covers never uploaded.)
      if (book.cover_hash && !uploaded.has(book.cover_hash)) {
        pending.push({ hash: book.cover_hash, getBlob: async () => (await db.covers.get(book.id))?.blob ?? null });
      }
    }

    if (pending.length > 0) {
      useSyncStore.setState({ filesUploadingCount: 0, filesUploadingTotal: pending.length });
      let index = 0;
      let completed = 0;
      const worker = async () => {
        while (index < pending.length) {
          const item = pending[index++];
          if (!item) break;
          try {
            const blob = await item.getBlob();
            if (blob) {
              await putFile(item.hash, blob); // server answers "exists" if already stored
              uploaded.add(item.hash);
            }
          } catch (err) {
            if (err instanceof AccountDeletedError) throw err;
            if (err instanceof StorageOfflineError) {
              storageDown = err;
              index = pending.length; // stop the other worker too
              continue;
            }
            // Other per-file errors don't fail the sync; the file is retried next time.
          } finally {
            completed++;
            useSyncStore.setState({ filesUploadingCount: completed });
          }
        }
      };
      await Promise.all([worker(), worker()]);
      useSyncStore.setState({ filesUploadingCount: 0, filesUploadingTotal: 0 });
    }

    state.uploaded = Array.from(uploaded);
    await writeSyncState(state);
    if (storageDown) throw storageDown;
  }

  // 3) PULL
  private async stage3Pull(state: SyncBookkeeping): Promise<void> {
    let cursor = state.cursor;

    while (true) {
      const page = await syncPull(cursor);
      const tableNames = Object.keys(page.changes) as SyncTableName[];

      if (tableNames.length > 0) {
        const tablesToLock = tableNames.map((t) => db.table(t));
        await db.transaction('rw', [...tablesToLock, db.kv], async () => {
          for (const table of tableNames) {
            const rows = page.changes[table];
            if (!rows || rows.length === 0) continue;
            const targetTable = db.table(table);

            for (const r of rows) {
              const row = r as unknown as { id: string; updated_at: number; [key: string]: unknown };
              const local = (await targetTable.get(row.id)) as { updated_at: number; _dirty: number } | undefined;
              if (!local) {
                await targetTable.put({ ...row, _dirty: 0 });
              } else if (local._dirty === 0) {
                await targetTable.put({ ...row, _dirty: 0 });
              } else if (row.updated_at > local.updated_at) {
                await targetTable.put({ ...row, _dirty: 0 });
              } else {
                // local._dirty === 1 and local.updated_at >= row.updated_at: keep local
              }
            }
          }
          state.cursor = page.cursor;
          await db.kv.put({ key: SYNC_STATE_KEY, value: state });
        });
      } else {
        state.cursor = page.cursor;
        await writeSyncState(state);
      }

      cursor = page.cursor;
      if (!page.hasMore) break;
    }
  }

  // 4) FILES DOWN
  private async stage4FilesDown(): Promise<void> {
    // Missing cover hashes: download immediately
    const books = await db.books.filter((b) => !!b.cover_hash && !b.deleted_at).toArray();
    for (const book of books) {
      const coverHash = book.cover_hash!;
      // The UI reads covers by book id. Older versions saved downloads under the
      // cover hash, where they were never shown: move those over.
      if (await db.covers.get(book.id)) continue;
      const misfiled = await db.covers.get(coverHash);
      if (misfiled) {
        await db.covers.put({ id: book.id, blob: misfiled.blob });
        continue;
      }
      try {
        const blob = await getFile(coverHash);
        await db.covers.put({ id: book.id, blob });
      } catch (err) {
        if (err instanceof AccountDeletedError || err instanceof StorageOfflineError) throw err;
        // Skip on cover download error; retried next sync.
      }
    }

    // Check if "Download all books" is enabled in settings
    const settingsRow = await db.settings.get('sync');
    const syncSettings = settingsRow?.data as { download_all_books?: boolean } | undefined;
    if (syncSettings?.download_all_books) {
      const allBooks = await db.books.filter((b) => !b.deleted_at).toArray();
      for (const book of allBooks) {
        const existing = await db.files.get(book.file_hash);
        if (!existing) {
          try {
            const blob = await getFile(book.file_hash);
            await db.files.put({ hash: book.file_hash, blob });
          } catch {
            // Skip individual file failures
          }
        }
      }
    }
  }

  // SSE event stream listener
  async connectSSE(): Promise<void> {
    if (this.eventSource) {
      this.eventSource.close();
      this.eventSource = null;
    }

    const signedIn = await this.isUserSignedIn();
    if (!signedIn || (typeof navigator !== 'undefined' && !navigator.onLine)) {
      return;
    }

    try {
      const { token } = await syncEventsToken();
      const serverUrl = await getServerUrl();
      const es = new EventSource(`${serverUrl}/sync/events?token=${token}`);

      es.addEventListener('changed', () => {
        void this.runSync();
      });

      es.onerror = () => {
        es.close();
        this.eventSource = null;
        if (this.sseReconnectTimer) clearTimeout(this.sseReconnectTimer);
        this.sseReconnectTimer = setTimeout(() => {
          void this.connectSSE();
        }, 5000);
      };

      this.eventSource = es;
    } catch (err) {
      if (err instanceof AccountDeletedError) {
        await this.handleAccountDeleted();
        return;
      }
      if (this.sseReconnectTimer) clearTimeout(this.sseReconnectTimer);
      this.sseReconnectTimer = setTimeout(() => {
        void this.connectSSE();
      }, 10000);
    }
  }

  disconnectSSE(): void {
    if (this.eventSource) {
      this.eventSource.close();
      this.eventSource = null;
    }
    if (this.sseReconnectTimer) {
      clearTimeout(this.sseReconnectTimer);
      this.sseReconnectTimer = null;
    }
  }

  // Set up all triggers per Task 4.4
  init(): void {
    if (this.initialized || typeof window === 'undefined') return;
    this.initialized = true;

    // Window focus / visibility
    window.addEventListener('focus', () => {
      void this.runSync();
    });

    document.addEventListener('visibilitychange', () => {
      if (!document.hidden) {
        void this.runSync();
      }
    });

    // Online / Offline
    window.addEventListener('online', () => {
      useSyncStore.setState({ isOnline: true });
      void this.runSync();
      void this.connectSSE();
    });

    window.addEventListener('offline', () => {
      useSyncStore.setState({ isOnline: false, status: 'offline' });
      this.disconnectSSE();
    });

    // 30s interval while visible
    this.intervalTimer = setInterval(() => {
      if (!document.hidden && navigator.onLine) {
        void this.runSync();
      }
    }, 30000);

    // Dexie table write hooks on synced tables (debounced 2s)
    for (const table of SYNC_TABLES) {
      db[table].hook('creating', (_primKey, obj) => {
        if (obj && (obj as { _dirty?: number })._dirty === 1) {
          this.scheduleDebouncedSync();
        }
      });
      db[table].hook('updating', (modifications) => {
        if (modifications && (modifications as { _dirty?: number })._dirty === 1) {
          this.scheduleDebouncedSync();
        }
      });
      db[table].hook('deleting', () => {
        this.scheduleDebouncedSync();
      });
    }

    // Startup sync & SSE connection
    void this.runSync();
    void this.connectSSE();
  }

  destroy(): void {
    this.disconnectSSE();
    if (this.intervalTimer) clearInterval(this.intervalTimer);
    if (this.backoffTimer) clearTimeout(this.backoffTimer);
    if (this.debounceTimer) clearTimeout(this.debounceTimer);
    this.initialized = false;
  }
}

export const syncEngine = new SyncEngine();

File: apps/client/src/lib/tauri.ts

/**
 * Tauri desktop integration helpers.
 * Gracefully degrades when running in browser / web environments.
 */

export function isTauri(): boolean {
  return (
    typeof window !== 'undefined' &&
    ('__TAURI__' in window || '__TAURI_INTERNALS__' in window)
  );
}

/**
 * Minimizes the current desktop window.
 */
export async function minimizeWindow(): Promise<void> {
  if (!isTauri()) return;
  try {
    const { getCurrentWindow } = await import('@tauri-apps/api/window');
    await getCurrentWindow().minimize();
  } catch (err) {
    console.warn('Failed to minimize window:', err);
  }
}

/**
 * Toggles maximize / restore on the current desktop window.
 */
export async function toggleMaximizeWindow(): Promise<void> {
  if (!isTauri()) return;
  try {
    const { getCurrentWindow } = await import('@tauri-apps/api/window');
    await getCurrentWindow().toggleMaximize();
  } catch (err) {
    console.warn('Failed to toggle maximize window:', err);
  }
}

/**
 * Checks if the window is currently maximized.
 */
export async function isWindowMaximized(): Promise<boolean> {
  if (!isTauri()) return false;
  try {
    const { getCurrentWindow } = await import('@tauri-apps/api/window');
    return await getCurrentWindow().isMaximized();
  } catch {
    return false;
  }
}

/**
 * Closes the current desktop application window.
 */
/**
 * Calls `cb` whenever the window is resized (maximise/restore/snap included).
 * Event-driven replacement for polling `isMaximized()`. Returns an unsubscribe.
 */
export async function onWindowResized(cb: () => void): Promise<() => void> {
  if (!isTauri()) return () => {};
  try {
    const { getCurrentWindow } = await import('@tauri-apps/api/window');
    return await getCurrentWindow().onResized(cb);
  } catch (err) {
    console.error('Failed to listen for window resize:', err);
    return () => {};
  }
}

export async function closeWindow(): Promise<void> {
  if (!isTauri()) return;
  try {
    const { getCurrentWindow } = await import('@tauri-apps/api/window');
    await getCurrentWindow().close();
  } catch (err) {
    console.warn('Failed to close window:', err);
  }
}

/**
 * Toggles fullscreen on the desktop window.
 */
export async function toggleTauriFullscreen(): Promise<void> {
  if (!isTauri()) {
    if (document.fullscreenElement) {
      void document.exitFullscreen();
    } else {
      void document.documentElement.requestFullscreen?.();
    }
    return;
  }
  try {
    const { getCurrentWindow } = await import('@tauri-apps/api/window');
    const win = getCurrentWindow();
    const isFs = await win.isFullscreen();
    await win.setFullscreen(!isFs);
  } catch (err) {
    console.warn('Failed to toggle Tauri fullscreen:', err);
  }
}

/**
 * Listens for file-open events emitted when a file is opened via Windows Explorer
 * or dropped onto the application executable.
 */
export async function listenFileOpen(
  callback: (filePath: string) => void,
): Promise<() => void> {
  if (!isTauri()) return () => {};
  try {
    const { listen } = await import('@tauri-apps/api/event');
    const unlisten = await listen<string>('verso://file-open', (event) => {
      if (event.payload) callback(event.payload);
    });
    return unlisten;
  } catch (err) {
    console.warn('Failed to register file-open listener:', err);
    return () => {};
  }
}

/**
 * Listens for deep-link protocol events (verso://).
 */
export async function listenDeepLink(
  callback: (url: string) => void,
): Promise<() => void> {
  if (!isTauri()) return () => {};
  try {
    const { onOpenUrl } = await import('@tauri-apps/plugin-deep-link');
    const unlisten = await onOpenUrl((urls) => {
      for (const url of urls) {
        callback(url);
      }
    });
    return unlisten;
  } catch (err) {
    console.warn('Deep-link plugin not available:', err);
    return () => {};
  }
}

/**
 * Opens an external URL in the default desktop system browser.
 */
export async function openExternalUrl(url: string): Promise<void> {
  if (isTauri()) {
    try {
      const { openUrl } = await import('@tauri-apps/plugin-opener');
      await openUrl(url);
      return;
    } catch {
      // Fall through to window.open
    }
  }
  window.open(url, '_blank', 'noopener,noreferrer');
}

/** Running on an Android device (the Tauri mobile build or a mobile browser). */
export function isAndroidDevice(): boolean {
  return typeof navigator !== 'undefined' && /Android/i.test(navigator.userAgent);
}

/**
 * Opens an external page (e.g. an official publisher's chapter) inside Verso: in a
 * separate app window on desktop, falling back to the system browser elsewhere.
 * The window loads remote content only; it gets no access to Verso's APIs because
 * capabilities are granted to the "main" window alone.
 */
export async function openInAppWindow(url: string, title = 'Verso'): Promise<void> {
  if (isTauri() && !isAndroidDevice()) {
    try {
      const { WebviewWindow } = await import('@tauri-apps/api/webviewWindow');
      const win = new WebviewWindow(`external-${Date.now()}`, {
        url,
        title,
        width: 1100,
        height: 900,
        center: true,
        focus: true,
      });
      await new Promise<void>((resolve, reject) => {
        void win.once('tauri://created', () => resolve());
        void win.once('tauri://error', (e) => reject(new Error(String(e.payload))));
      });
      return;
    } catch (err) {
      console.warn('In-app window failed, using the browser instead:', err);
    }
  }
  await openExternalUrl(url);
}

/**
 * Asks for confirmation and waits for the answer. In Tauri, window.confirm does
 * not block (it returns before the user answers), so actions ran immediately and
 * the dialog appeared afterwards. Uses the native dialog there instead.
 */
export async function confirmAction(message: string, title = 'Verso'): Promise<boolean> {
  if (isTauri()) {
    try {
      const { ask } = await import('@tauri-apps/plugin-dialog');
      return await ask(message, { title, kind: 'warning' });
    } catch {
      // Fall through to the browser dialog.
    }
  }
  return window.confirm(message);
}

File: apps/client/src/lib/tts.ts

import { useEffect, useState } from 'react'
import { Overlayer, type FoliateTTS, type FoliateView } from './foliate'
import { getTtsEngine, type TtsVoice } from './ttsEngine'

export type TTSState = 'idle' | 'playing' | 'paused'
interface Mark { name: string; offset: number }

export function isSkippableTTSElement(el: Element): boolean {
  const epubType = el.getAttribute('epub:type') || el.getAttributeNS('http://www.idpf.org/2007/ops', 'type') || ''
  const role = el.getAttribute('role') || ''
  const cls = el.getAttribute('class') || ''
  if (epubType.includes('noteref') || epubType.includes('pagebreak')) return true
  if (role === 'doc-noteref' || role === 'doc-pagebreak') return true
  if (/(\bnoteref\b|\bfootnote-ref\b|\bpage-break\b|\bpage-number\b)/i.test(cls)) return true
  if (el.localName === 'sup' && /^\s*\[?\d+\]?\s*$/.test(el.textContent || '')) return true
  return false
}

export function parseSSML(ssml: string): { text: string; marks: Mark[]; lang?: string } {
  const doc = new DOMParser().parseFromString(ssml, 'application/xml')
  let text = ''
  const marks: Mark[] = []
  const walk = (n: Node) => {
    if (n.nodeType === Node.TEXT_NODE) { text += n.nodeValue ?? ''; return }
    if (n.nodeType !== Node.ELEMENT_NODE) return
    const el = n as Element
    if (isSkippableTTSElement(el)) return
    if (el.localName === 'mark') { marks.push({ name: el.getAttribute('name') ?? '', offset: text.length }); return }
    if (el.localName === 'break') { text += ' '; return }
    n.childNodes.forEach(walk)
  }
  walk(doc.documentElement)
  return { text, marks, lang: doc.documentElement.getAttribute('xml:lang') ?? undefined }
}

export class ReadAloud {
  state: TTSState = 'idle'
  rate = 1
  voiceURI = ''
  onState: ((s: TTSState) => void) | null = null
  private view: FoliateView
  private token = 0
  private section = 0

  constructor(view: FoliateView) {
    this.view = view
  }

  static supported() {
    return getTtsEngine().isSupported()
  }

  setSection(index: number) {
    this.section = index
  }

  private set(s: TTSState) {
    this.state = s
    this.onState?.(s)
  }

  private highlight = (range: Range) => {
    const r = this.view.renderer
    void r?.scrollToAnchor?.(range, true)
    const c = r?.getContents?.()[0]
    c?.overlayer?.remove('tts')
    c?.overlayer?.add('tts', range, Overlayer.highlight, { color: 'var(--accent)' })
  }

  private clearHighlight() {
    for (const c of this.view.renderer?.getContents?.() ?? []) c.overlayer?.remove('tts')
  }

  private halt(): number {
    this.token++
    void getTtsEngine().stop()
    return this.token
  }

  private async prepare(): Promise<FoliateTTS> {
    if (!this.view.initTTS) throw new Error('Read aloud needs a newer foliate-js. Run "git pull" inside src/foliate-js.')
    await this.view.initTTS('word', this.highlight)
    if (!this.view.tts) throw new Error('Could not start read aloud.')
    return this.view.tts
  }

  async start(from?: Range | null) {
    if (!ReadAloud.supported()) throw new Error('No speech engine is available on this device.')
    const token = this.halt()
    const tts = await this.prepare()
    if (token !== this.token) return
    this.set('playing')
    let ssml: string | undefined
    try {
      ssml = from ? tts.from(from) : tts.start()
    } catch {
      ssml = tts.start()
    }
    void this.speak(ssml, token)
  }

  pause() {
    if (this.state !== 'playing') return
    this.halt()
    this.set('paused')
    void getTtsEngine().pause()
  }

  resume() {
    if (this.state === 'paused') {
      void getTtsEngine().resume()
      this.continueWith((t) => t.resume())
    }
  }

  toggle(from?: Range | null) {
    if (this.state === 'playing') {
      this.pause()
    } else if (this.state === 'paused') {
      this.resume()
    } else {
      void this.start(from)
    }
  }

  next() {
    if (this.state !== 'idle') this.continueWith((t) => t.next())
  }

  prev() {
    if (this.state !== 'idle') this.continueWith((t) => t.prev() ?? t.start())
  }

  restartBlock() {
    if (this.state === 'playing') this.continueWith((t) => t.resume())
  }

  stop() {
    this.halt()
    this.clearHighlight()
    this.set('idle')
  }

  private continueWith(get: (tts: FoliateTTS) => string | undefined) {
    const tts = this.view.tts
    if (!tts) return
    const token = this.halt()
    this.set('playing')
    void this.speak(get(tts), token)
  }

  private async speak(ssml: string | undefined, token: number): Promise<void> {
    if (token !== this.token) return

    if (!ssml) {
      const next = this.section + 1
      if (next >= (this.view.book.sections?.length ?? 0)) { this.stop(); return }
      try {
        await this.view.goTo(next)
        if (token !== this.token) return
        const tts = await this.prepare()
        if (token !== this.token) return
        return this.speak(tts.start(), token)
      } catch {
        this.stop()
        return
      }
    }

    const tts = this.view.tts
    if (!tts) return
    const { text, marks, lang } = parseSSML(ssml)
    if (!text.trim()) return this.speak(tts.next(), token)

    if (marks[0]) tts.setMark(marks[0].name)

    const engine = getTtsEngine()
    await engine.speak(text, `utt-${token}-${Date.now()}`, {
      rate: this.rate,
      voiceURI: this.voiceURI,
      lang,
      onRangeStart: (charIndex) => {
        if (token !== this.token) return
        let mark: Mark | undefined
        for (const m of marks) {
          if (m.offset <= charIndex) mark = m
          else break
        }
        if (mark) tts.setMark(mark.name)
      },
      onDone: () => {
        if (token === this.token) void this.speak(tts.next(), token)
      },
      onError: (e) => {
        if (token !== this.token) return
        const err = e as { error?: string }
        if (err?.error === 'interrupted' || err?.error === 'canceled') return
        this.stop()
      },
    })
  }
}

export function speakOnce(text: string, lang: string, voiceURI: string, rate: number) {
  const engine = getTtsEngine()
  if (!engine.isSupported()) return
  void engine.stop()
  void engine.speak(text, `once-${Date.now()}`, { lang, voiceURI, rate })
}

export function useVoices(): TtsVoice[] {
  const [voices, setVoices] = useState<TtsVoice[]>([])
  useEffect(() => {
    const engine = getTtsEngine()
    if (!engine.isSupported()) return
    const load = () => {
      void engine.getVoices().then(setVoices)
    }
    load()
    if (typeof window !== 'undefined' && 'speechSynthesis' in window) {
      window.speechSynthesis.addEventListener('voiceschanged', load)
      return () => window.speechSynthesis.removeEventListener('voiceschanged', load)
    }
  }, [])
  return voices
}
