import { db, dirty, uid } from '../db';
import type { CatalogSource, MangaSource } from './types';
import { gutenbergSource } from './gutenberg';
import { standardEbooksSource, OpdsSource } from './opds';
import { mangaDexSource } from './mangadex';
import { openLibrarySource } from './openlibrary';
import { archiveOrgSource } from './archiveorg';
import { mangaPillSource } from './mangapill';
import { SuwayomiSource, defaultSuwayomiSource } from './suwayomi';
import { KomgaSource } from './komga';

export {
  gutenbergSource,
  standardEbooksSource,
  openLibrarySource,
  archiveOrgSource,
  mangaDexSource,
  mangaPillSource,
  SuwayomiSource,
  defaultSuwayomiSource,
  KomgaSource,
  OpdsSource,
};

export async function getAllBookSources(): Promise<CatalogSource[]> {
  const customSources = await db.sources
    .filter((s) => !s.deleted_at)
    .toArray();

  const customInstances: CatalogSource[] = [];
  for (const cs of customSources) {
    const cred = await db.source_credentials.get(cs.id);
    if (cs.kind === 'suwayomi') {
      customInstances.push(new SuwayomiSource(cs.id, cs.name, cs.url, cred?.apiKey));
    } else if (cs.kind === 'komga') {
      customInstances.push(new KomgaSource(cs.id, cs.name, cs.url, cred));
    } else {
      customInstances.push(
        new OpdsSource(
          cs.id,
          cs.name,
          cs.url,
          cred
            ? {
                username: cred.username,
                password: cred.password,
                apiKey: cred.apiKey,
              }
            : undefined,
        ),
      );
    }
  }

  return [
    gutenbergSource,
    standardEbooksSource,
    openLibrarySource,
    archiveOrgSource,
    mangaDexSource,
    mangaPillSource,
    defaultSuwayomiSource,
    ...customInstances,
  ];
}

export async function getAllMangaSources(): Promise<MangaSource[]> {
  const customSources = await db.sources
    .filter((s) => !s.deleted_at && (s.kind === 'suwayomi' || s.kind === 'komga'))
    .toArray();

  const customInstances: MangaSource[] = [];
  let hasDefaultSuwayomi = false;
  for (const cs of customSources) {
    const cred = await db.source_credentials.get(cs.id);
    if (cs.kind === 'suwayomi') {
      if (cs.url === defaultSuwayomiSource.baseUrl) hasDefaultSuwayomi = true;
      customInstances.push(new SuwayomiSource(cs.id, cs.name, cs.url, cred?.apiKey));
    } else if (cs.kind === 'komga') {
      customInstances.push(new KomgaSource(cs.id, cs.name, cs.url, cred));
    }
  }

  return [
    mangaDexSource,
    mangaPillSource,
    ...(hasDefaultSuwayomi ? [] : [defaultSuwayomiSource]),
    ...customInstances,
  ];
}

export async function getSourceById(id: string): Promise<CatalogSource | undefined> {
  if (id === 'gutenberg') return gutenbergSource;
  if (id === 'standardebooks') return standardEbooksSource;
  if (id === 'openlibrary') return openLibrarySource;
  if (id === 'archiveorg') return archiveOrgSource;
  if (id === 'mangadex') return mangaDexSource;
  if (id === 'mangapill') return mangaPillSource;
  if (id === 'suwayomi') return defaultSuwayomiSource;

  // Direct prefix matching for series/item IDs
  if (id.startsWith('openlibrary:')) return openLibrarySource;
  if (id.startsWith('archiveorg:')) return archiveOrgSource;
  if (id.startsWith('mangadex:')) return mangaDexSource;
  if (id.startsWith('mangapill:')) return mangaPillSource;
  if (id.startsWith('gutenberg:')) return gutenbergSource;
  if (id.startsWith('standardebooks:')) return standardEbooksSource;

  // Prefix matching for custom Suwayomi & Komga sources
  if (id.startsWith('suwayomi:')) {
    const parts = id.split(':');
    const instanceId = parts[1];
    if (instanceId && instanceId !== 'all') {
      const cs = await db.sources.get(instanceId);
      if (cs && !cs.deleted_at) {
        const cred = await db.source_credentials.get(instanceId);
        return new SuwayomiSource(cs.id, cs.name, cs.url, cred?.apiKey);
      }
    }
    // Fallback default local Suwayomi
    return new SuwayomiSource();
  }

  if (id.startsWith('komga:')) {
    const parts = id.split(':');
    const instanceId = parts[1];
    if (instanceId) {
      const cs = await db.sources.get(instanceId);
      if (cs && !cs.deleted_at) {
        const cred = await db.source_credentials.get(instanceId);
        return new KomgaSource(cs.id, cs.name, cs.url, cred);
      }
    }
    return new KomgaSource();
  }

  const cs = await db.sources.get(id);
  if (!cs || cs.deleted_at) return undefined;

  const cred = await db.source_credentials.get(id);
  if (cs.kind === 'suwayomi') {
    return new SuwayomiSource(cs.id, cs.name, cs.url, cred?.apiKey);
  }
  if (cs.kind === 'komga') {
    return new KomgaSource(cs.id, cs.name, cs.url, cred);
  }

  return new OpdsSource(
    cs.id,
    cs.name,
    cs.url,
    cred
      ? {
          username: cred.username,
          password: cred.password,
          apiKey: cred.apiKey,
        }
      : undefined,
  );
}

export async function addCustomOpdsSource(data: {
  name: string;
  url: string;
  username?: string;
  password?: string;
  apiKey?: string;
}): Promise<CatalogSource> {
  const id = uid();
  const sourceRecord = {
    id,
    kind: 'opds',
    name: data.name.trim(),
    url: data.url.trim(),
    meta: {},
    sort_order: Date.now(),
    deleted_at: null,
    ...dirty(),
  };

  await db.sources.put(sourceRecord);

  if (data.username || data.password || data.apiKey) {
    await db.source_credentials.put({
      id,
      username: data.username,
      password: data.password,
      apiKey: data.apiKey,
    });
  }

  return new OpdsSource(id, sourceRecord.name, sourceRecord.url, {
    username: data.username,
    password: data.password,
    apiKey: data.apiKey,
  });
}

export async function addCustomSuwayomiSource(data: {
  name: string;
  url: string;
  apiKey?: string;
}): Promise<CatalogSource> {
  const id = uid();
  const sourceRecord = {
    id,
    kind: 'suwayomi',
    name: data.name.trim(),
    url: data.url.trim(),
    meta: {},
    sort_order: Date.now(),
    deleted_at: null,
    ...dirty(),
  };

  await db.sources.put(sourceRecord);

  if (data.apiKey) {
    await db.source_credentials.put({
      id,
      apiKey: data.apiKey,
    });
  }

  return new SuwayomiSource(id, sourceRecord.name, sourceRecord.url, data.apiKey);
}

export async function addCustomKomgaSource(data: {
  name: string;
  url: string;
  username?: string;
  password?: string;
  apiKey?: string;
}): Promise<CatalogSource> {
  const id = uid();
  const sourceRecord = {
    id,
    kind: 'komga',
    name: data.name.trim(),
    url: data.url.trim(),
    meta: {},
    sort_order: Date.now(),
    deleted_at: null,
    ...dirty(),

  if (data.username || data.password || data.apiKey) {
    await db.source_credentials.put({
      id,
      username: data.username,
      password: data.password,
      apiKey: data.apiKey,
    });
  }

  return new KomgaSource(id, sourceRecord.name, sourceRecord.url, {
    username: data.username,
    password: data.password,
    apiKey: data.apiKey,
  });
}

export async function removeCustomSource(id: string): Promise<void> {
  const existing = await db.sources.get(id);
  if (existing) {
    await db.sources.update(id, {
      deleted_at: Date.now(),
      ...dirty(),
    });
  }
  await db.source_credentials.delete(id);
}

import { useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { Download, StickyNote, Search, Upload, Trash2, Moon, Sun } from 'lucide-react';
import type { Annotation } from '../db';
import { compareCFI, type FoliateView, type SearchHit, type TocItem } from '../lib/foliate';
import { FONTS, THEMES, colorHex, type ReaderSettings, type ThemeId, type PageAnimation, type TapZones } from '../lib/readerSettings';
import { useCustomFonts } from '../lib/customFonts';
import { Dialog } from '../design/components/Dialog';
import { Button } from '../design/components/Button';
import { SegmentedControl } from '../design/components/SegmentedControl';
import { Switch } from '../design/components/Switch';
import { Slider } from '../design/components/Slider';
import { ProgressBar } from '../design/components/Progress';

/* ---------- Table of contents ---------- */

export function TocPanel({ toc, current, onGo }: { toc: TocItem[]; current?: string; onGo: (href: string) => void }) {
  if (!toc.length) return <p className="text-xs text-[var(--text-3)] p-4">This book has no table of contents.</p>;
  return (
    <nav className="py-2 text-sm">
      <TocList items={toc} current={current} onGo={onGo} depth={0} />
    </nav>
  );
}

function TocList({
  items,
  current,
  onGo,
  depth,
}: {
  items: TocItem[];
  current?: string;
  onGo: (href: string) => void;
  depth: number;
}) {
  return (
    <ul className="space-y-0.5">
      {items.map((it, i) => (
        <li key={`${it.href}-${i}`}>
          <button
            type="button"
            className={`w-full text-left py-2 pr-3 rounded-[var(--r-sm)] transition-colors cursor-pointer outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-[var(--accent)] text-xs truncate ${
              it.href === current
                ? 'bg-[var(--layer-3)] text-[var(--accent)] font-semibold'
                : 'text-[var(--text-2)] hover:text-[var(--text-1)] hover:bg-[var(--layer-2)]'
            }`}
            style={{ paddingLeft: `${12 + depth * 16}px` }}
            onClick={() => onGo(it.href)}
          >
            {it.label?.trim() || 'Untitled'}
          </button>
          {it.subitems?.length ? (
            <TocList items={it.subitems} current={current} onGo={onGo} depth={depth + 1} />
          ) : null}
        </li>
      ))}
    </ul>
  );
}

/* ---------- Full-text search ---------- */

export function SearchPanel({ view, initial, onGo }: { view: FoliateView; initial: string; onGo: (cfi: string) => void }) {
  const [query, setQuery] = useState(initial);
  const [groups, setGroups] = useState<{ label: string; hits: SearchHit[] }[]>([]);
  const [progress, setProgress] = useState<number | null>(null);
  const [searched, setSearched] = useState(false);
  const run = useRef(0);

  async function search(q: string) {
    const id = ++run.current;
    view.clearSearch();
    setGroups([]);
    setSearched(false);
    if (!q.trim()) {
      setProgress(null);
      return;
    }
    setProgress(0);
    for await (const ev of view.search({ query: q.trim() })) {
      if (id !== run.current) return;
      if (ev === 'done') break;
      if ('progress' in ev) setProgress(ev.progress);
      else if (ev.subitems?.length) {
        const hits = ev.subitems;
        setGroups((g) => [...g, { label: ev.label ?? '', hits }]);
      }
    }
    if (id === run.current) {
      setProgress(null);
      setSearched(true);
    }
  }

  useEffect(() => {
    if (initial) void search(initial);
    return () => {
      run.current++;
      view.clearSearch();
    };
    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
  }, []);

  const total = groups.reduce((n, g) => n + g.hits.length, 0);

  return (
    <div className="space-y-4 p-2">
      <form
        onSubmit={(e) => {
          e.preventDefault();
          void search(query);
        }}
      >
        <div className="flex items-center h-9 px-2.5 rounded-[var(--r-md)] bg-[var(--layer-2)] border border-[var(--stroke)] text-sm text-[var(--text-1)] gap-2">
          <Search className="w-4 h-4 text-[var(--text-3)] shrink-0" />
          <input
            className="w-full bg-transparent outline-none placeholder:text-[var(--text-3)] text-xs"
            placeholder="Search in book…"
            value={query}
            onChange={(e) => setQuery(e.target.value)}
          />
        </div>
      </form>

      {progress !== null && <ProgressBar value={Math.round(progress * 100)} height={2} />}

      {searched && (
        <p className="text-xs text-[var(--text-3)]">
          {total ? `${total} result${total === 1 ? '' : 's'}` : 'No results found'}
        </p>
      )}

      <div className="space-y-4 overflow-y-auto max-h-[calc(100vh-200px)]">
        {groups.map((g, i) => (
          <div key={i} className="space-y-1.5">
            {g.label && <div className="text-[11px] font-semibold text-[var(--accent)] uppercase tracking-wider">{g.label}</div>}
            {g.hits.map((h) => (
              <button
                key={h.cfi}
                type="button"
                className="w-full text-left p-2 rounded-[var(--r-sm)] bg-[var(--layer-1)] hover:bg-[var(--layer-2)] border border-[var(--stroke)] text-xs text-[var(--text-2)] cursor-pointer leading-relaxed transition-colors"
                onClick={() => onGo(h.cfi)}
              >
                …{h.excerpt.pre}
                <mark className="bg-[var(--accent-soft)] text-[var(--accent)] rounded-xs px-0.5 font-semibold">
                  {h.excerpt.match}
                </mark>
                {h.excerpt.post}…
              </button>
            ))}
          </div>
        ))}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

/* ---------- Highlights, notes, bookmarks ---------- */

type Tab = 'all' | 'highlight' | 'note' | 'bookmark';
const TAB_LABEL: Record<Tab, string> = {
  all: 'All',
  highlight: 'Highlights',
  note: 'Notes',
  bookmark: 'Bookmarks',
};

function exportMarkdown(title: string, author: string, list: Annotation[]) {
  const lines = [`# ${title}`, `*${author}*`, ''];
  let chapter = '';
  for (const a of list) {
    if (a.chapter && a.chapter !== chapter) {
      chapter = a.chapter;
      lines.push(`\n## ${chapter}\n`);
    }
    if (a.type === 'bookmark') {
      lines.push(`- 🔖 **Bookmark**: *${a.text || 'Page'}* (${a.cfi})`);
    } else {
      lines.push(`> ${a.text}`);
      if (a.note) lines.push(`\n> **Note**: ${a.note}`);
      lines.push('');
    }
  }
  const blob = new Blob([lines.join('\n')], { type: 'text/markdown' });
  const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
  const link = document.createElement('a');
  link.href = url;
  link.download = `${title} — Highlights.md`;
  link.click();
  URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
}

export function AnnotationsPanel({
  initialTab = 'all',
  annotations,
  title,
  author,
  onGo,
  onEditNote,
  onDelete,
}: {
  initialTab?: Tab;
  annotations: Annotation[];
  title: string;
  author: string;
  onGo: (cfi: string) => void;
  onEditNote: (a: Annotation) => void;
  onDelete: (a: Annotation) => void;
}) {
  const [tab, setTab] = useState<Tab>(initialTab);
  const active = useMemo(
    () =>
      annotations
        .filter((a) => !a.deleted_at && (tab === 'all' || a.type === tab))
        .sort((x, y) => compareCFI(x.cfi, y.cfi) || x.created_at - y.created_at),
    [annotations, tab],
  );

  return (
    <div className="space-y-3 p-2">
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
        <SegmentedControl
          size="sm"
          options={[
            { value: 'all', label: 'All' },
            { value: 'highlight', label: 'Highlights' },
            { value: 'note', label: 'Notes' },
            { value: 'bookmark', label: 'Bookmarks' },
          ]}
          value={tab}
          onChange={setTab}
        />
        {active.length > 0 && (
          <Button
            size="sm"
            variant="subtle"
            icon={<Download className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />}
            onClick={() => exportMarkdown(title, author, active)}
          >
            Export
          </Button>
        )}
      </div>

      <div className="space-y-2 overflow-y-auto max-h-[calc(100vh-200px)]">
        {active.length === 0 ? (
          <p className="text-xs text-[var(--text-3)] p-4 text-center">
            No {TAB_LABEL[tab].toLowerCase()} yet.
          </p>
        ) : (
          active.map((a) => {
            const hex = colorHex(a.color);
            return (
              <div
                key={a.id}
                role="button"
                tabIndex={0}
                className="relative flex flex-col gap-1.5 p-3 rounded-[var(--r-md)] bg-[var(--layer-1)] hover:bg-[var(--layer-2)] border border-[var(--stroke)] text-left cursor-pointer transition-colors"
                onClick={() => onGo(a.cfi)}
                onKeyDown={(e) => (e.key === 'Enter' || e.key === ' ') && onGo(a.cfi)}
              >
                {a.type !== 'bookmark' && (
                  <div
                    className="absolute left-1 top-3 bottom-3 w-1 rounded-full"
                    style={{ backgroundColor: hex }}
                  />
                )}
                <div className="pl-2 space-y-1">
                  <div className="text-xs text-[var(--text-1)] line-clamp-3 leading-relaxed">
                    {a.text || (a.type === 'bookmark' ? 'Bookmark' : '')}
                  </div>
                  {a.note && (
                    <div className="flex items-start gap-1.5 text-[11px] text-[var(--text-2)] bg-[var(--layer-2)] p-1.5 rounded-[var(--r-sm)]">
                      <StickyNote className="w-3 h-3 text-[var(--accent)] shrink-0 mt-0.5" />
                      <span>{a.note}</span>
                    </div>
                  )}
                  <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-[10px] text-[var(--text-3)] pt-1">
                    <span>{a.chapter || 'Chapter'}</span>
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                      {a.type !== 'bookmark' && (
                        <button
                          type="button"
                          className="hover:text-[var(--accent)] cursor-pointer"
                          onClick={(e) => {
                            e.stopPropagation();
                            onEditNote(a);
                          }}
                        >
                          {a.note ? 'Edit note' : 'Add note'}
                        </button>
                      )}
                      <button
                        type="button"
                        className="hover:text-[var(--danger)] cursor-pointer"
                        onClick={(e) => {
                          e.stopPropagation();
                          onDelete(a);
                        }}
                      >
                        Delete
                      </button>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>
            );
          })
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

/* ---------- Reader settings ---------- */

export function SettingsPanel({
  s,
  onChange,
}: {
  s: ReaderSettings;
  onChange: (p: Partial<ReaderSettings>) => void;
}) {
  const { fonts: customFonts, importFont, removeFont } = useCustomFonts();
  const fileInputRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);
  const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = useState<'theme' | 'typography' | 'layout'>('theme');

  const handleFontUpload = async (e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {
    const file = e.target.files?.[0];
    if (!file) return;
    try {
      const record = await importFont(file);
      onChange({ font: record.name });
    } catch (err) {
      alert(`Failed to import font: ${err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err)}`);
    } finally {
      if (fileInputRef.current) fileInputRef.current.value = '';
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="space-y-4 p-3 text-xs text-[var(--text-1)]">
      {/* Sub-navigation tabs */}
      <SegmentedControl
        size="sm"
        options={[
          { value: 'theme', label: 'Theme' },
          { value: 'typography', label: 'Fonts & Text' },
          { value: 'layout', label: 'Layout & Motion' },
        ]}
        value={activeTab}
        onChange={(val) => setActiveTab(val as 'theme' | 'typography' | 'layout')}
      />

      {/* TAB 1: Theme & Lighting */}
      {activeTab === 'theme' && (
        <div className="space-y-4 animate-fade-in">
          {/* Preset Themes Grid */}
          <div className="space-y-2">
            <span className="font-semibold text-[11px] text-[var(--text-3)] uppercase tracking-wider">
              Reading Theme
            </span>
            <div className="grid grid-cols-3 gap-2">
              <button
                type="button"
                title="Paper in light mode, OLED black in dark mode"
                className={`h-9 rounded-[var(--r-md)] border text-xs font-medium cursor-pointer transition-transform hover:scale-102 flex items-center justify-center ${
                  s.theme === 'auto'
                    ? 'border-[var(--accent)] ring-2 ring-[var(--accent)]'
                    : 'border-[var(--stroke)]'
                }`}
                style={{
                  background: `linear-gradient(135deg, ${THEMES.light.bg} 50%, ${THEMES.black.bg} 50%)`,
                  color: THEMES.light.fg,
                }}
                onClick={() => onChange({ theme: 'auto' })}
              >
                <span className="px-1.5 rounded-[var(--r-sm)] bg-[var(--layer-solid)] text-[var(--text-1)]">Match app</span>
              </button>
              {(Object.keys(THEMES) as Exclude<ThemeId, 'custom' | 'auto'>[]).map((id) => (
                <button
                  key={id}
                  type="button"
                  className={`h-9 rounded-[var(--r-md)] border text-xs font-medium cursor-pointer transition-transform hover:scale-102 flex items-center justify-center ${
                    s.theme === id
                      ? 'border-[var(--accent)] ring-2 ring-[var(--accent)]'
                      : 'border-[var(--stroke)]'
                  }`}
                  style={{ background: THEMES[id].bg, color: THEMES[id].fg }}
                  onClick={() => onChange({ theme: id })}
                >
                  {THEMES[id].name}
                </button>
              ))}
              <button
                type="button"
                className={`h-9 rounded-[var(--r-md)] border text-xs font-medium cursor-pointer transition-transform hover:scale-102 flex items-center justify-center ${
                  s.theme === 'custom'
                    ? 'border-[var(--accent)] ring-2 ring-[var(--accent)]'
                    : 'border-[var(--stroke)]'
                }`}
                style={{
                  background: s.customTheme?.bg || '#fbfbf8',
                  color: s.customTheme?.fg || '#1f1f1f',
                }}
                onClick={() => onChange({ theme: 'custom' })}
              >
                Custom
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Custom Theme Editor */}
          {s.theme === 'custom' && (
            <div className="p-3 rounded-[var(--r-lg)] bg-[var(--layer-2)] border border-[var(--stroke)] space-y-2.5">
              <span className="font-semibold text-[11px] text-[var(--accent)] uppercase tracking-wider block">
                Custom Palette
              </span>
              <div className="grid grid-cols-3 gap-2">
                <div>
                  <label htmlFor="custom-palette-bg" className="text-[10px] text-[var(--text-3)] block mb-1">Background</label>
                  <input
                    id="custom-palette-bg"
                    type="color"
                    value={s.customTheme?.bg || '#fbfbf8'}
                    onChange={(e) =>
                      onChange({
                        customTheme: {
                          ...(s.customTheme || { name: 'Custom', link: '#2563eb', dark: false }),
                          bg: e.target.value,
                          name: 'Custom',
                          fg: s.customTheme?.fg || '#1f1f1f',
                        },
                      })
                    }
                    className="w-full h-8 rounded-[var(--r-sm)] border border-[var(--stroke)] bg-transparent cursor-pointer"
                  />
                </div>
                <div>
                  <label htmlFor="custom-palette-fg" className="text-[10px] text-[var(--text-3)] block mb-1">Text</label>
                  <input
                    id="custom-palette-fg"
                    type="color"
                    value={s.customTheme?.fg || '#1f1f1f'}
                    onChange={(e) =>
                      onChange({
                        customTheme: {
                          ...(s.customTheme || { name: 'Custom', link: '#2563eb', dark: false }),
                          fg: e.target.value,
                          name: 'Custom',
                          bg: s.customTheme?.bg || '#fbfbf8',
                        },
                      })
                    }
                    className="w-full h-8 rounded-[var(--r-sm)] border border-[var(--stroke)] bg-transparent cursor-pointer"
                  />
                </div>
                <div>
                  <label htmlFor="custom-palette-link" className="text-[10px] text-[var(--text-3)] block mb-1">Links</label>
                  <input
                    id="custom-palette-link"
                    type="color"
                    value={s.customTheme?.link || '#2563eb'}
                    onChange={(e) =>
                      onChange({
                        customTheme: {
                          ...(s.customTheme || { name: 'Custom', bg: '#fbfbf8', fg: '#1f1f1f', dark: false }),
                          link: e.target.value,
                          name: 'Custom',
                        },
                      })
                    }
                    className="w-full h-8 rounded-[var(--r-sm)] border border-[var(--stroke)] bg-transparent cursor-pointer"
                  />
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>
          )}

          {/* Match App Theme Toggle */}
          <Switch
            label="Match app dark / light theme"
            checked={s.matchAppTheme}
            onCheckedChange={(val) => onChange({ matchAppTheme: val })}
          />

          {/* Night Light Amber Overlay */}
          <div className="space-y-1.5 pt-2 border-t border-[var(--stroke)]">
            <div className="flex justify-between text-xs">
              <span className="font-medium text-[var(--text-2)] flex items-center gap-1.5">
                <Sun className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-[var(--warning)]" /> Night Light (Warm Tint)
              </span>
              <span className="text-[var(--text-3)]">{s.nightLight}%</span>
            </div>
            <Slider
              value={[s.nightLight]}
              min={0}
              max={40}
              step={2}
              onValueChange={([val]) => onChange({ nightLight: val })}
            />
          </div>

          {/* Dim Overlay for Night Reading */}
          <div className="space-y-1.5">
            <div className="flex justify-between text-xs">
              <span className="font-medium text-[var(--text-2)] flex items-center gap-1.5">
                <Moon className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-[var(--accent)]" /> Reduce Brightness
              </span>
              <span className="text-[var(--text-3)]">{s.dimOverlay}%</span>
            </div>
            <Slider
              value={[s.dimOverlay]}
              min={0}
              max={60}
              step={2}
              onValueChange={([val]) => onChange({ dimOverlay: val })}
            />
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* TAB 2: Typography */}
      {activeTab === 'typography' && (
        <div className="space-y-4 animate-fade-in">
          {/* Font Family Selection */}
          <div className="space-y-1.5">
            <span className="font-semibold text-[11px] text-[var(--text-3)] uppercase tracking-wider">
              Typeface
            </span>
            <select
              value={s.font}
              onChange={(e) => onChange({ font: e.target.value })}
              className="w-full h-8 px-2 text-xs rounded-[var(--r-md)] bg-[var(--layer-2)] border border-[var(--stroke)] text-[var(--text-1)] outline-none cursor-pointer"
            >
              <optgroup label="Bundled Fonts">
                {FONTS.map((f) => (
                  <option key={f.id} value={f.id}>
                    {f.label}
                  </option>
                ))}
              </optgroup>
              {customFonts.length > 0 && (
                <optgroup label="Custom Imported Fonts">
                  {customFonts.map((cf) => (
                    <option key={cf.id} value={cf.name}>
                      {cf.name}
                    </option>
                  ))}
                </optgroup>
              )}
            </select>
          </div>

          {/* Import Custom Font Button */}
          <div>
            <input
              type="file"
              ref={fileInputRef}
              accept=".ttf,.otf,.woff2,.woff"
              onChange={handleFontUpload}
              className="hidden"
            />
            <Button
              variant="standard"
              size="sm"
              icon={<Upload className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />}
              onClick={() => fileInputRef.current?.click()}
              className="w-full justify-center text-xs"
            >
              Import Custom Font (.ttf, .otf, .woff2)
            </Button>
          </div>

          {/* List of Custom Fonts with Delete */}
          {customFonts.length > 0 && (
            <div className="space-y-1">
              <span className="text-[11px] text-[var(--text-3)]">Installed custom fonts:</span>
              <div className="space-y-1">
                {customFonts.map((cf) => (
                  <div
                    key={cf.id}
                    className="flex items-center justify-between px-2 py-1 rounded-[var(--r-sm)] bg-[var(--layer-2)] text-[11px]"
                  >
                    <span className="truncate">{cf.name}</span>
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => void removeFont(cf.id)}
                      className="text-[var(--danger)] hover:opacity-80 p-0.5 cursor-pointer"
                      title="Remove font"
                    >
                      <Trash2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                    </button>
                  </div>
                ))}
              </div>
            </div>
          )}

          {/* Font Size */}
          <div className="space-y-1.5 pt-2 border-t border-[var(--stroke)]">
            <div className="flex justify-between text-xs">
              <span className="font-medium text-[var(--text-2)]">Font size</span>
              <span className="text-[var(--text-3)]">{s.fontSize}px</span>
            </div>
            <Slider
              value={[s.fontSize]}
              min={12}
              max={36}
              step={1}
              onValueChange={([val]) => onChange({ fontSize: val })}
            />
          </div>

          {/* Line Height */}
          <div className="space-y-1.5">
            <div className="flex justify-between text-xs">
              <span className="font-medium text-[var(--text-2)]">Line height</span>
              <span className="text-[var(--text-3)]">{s.lineHeight.toFixed(2)}</span>
            </div>
            <Slider
              value={[s.lineHeight]}
              min={1}
              max={2.4}
              step={0.05}
              onValueChange={([val]) => onChange({ lineHeight: val })}
            />
          </div>

          {/* Paragraph Spacing */}
          <div className="space-y-1.5">
            <div className="flex justify-between text-xs">
              <span className="font-medium text-[var(--text-2)]">Paragraph spacing</span>
              <span className="text-[var(--text-3)]">{s.paragraphSpacing}px</span>
            </div>
            <Slider
              value={[s.paragraphSpacing]}
              min={0}
              max={24}
              step={2}
              onValueChange={([val]) => onChange({ paragraphSpacing: val })}
            />
          </div>

          {/* First Line Indent */}
          <div className="space-y-1.5">
            <div className="flex justify-between text-xs">
              <span className="font-medium text-[var(--text-2)]">First line indent</span>
              <span className="text-[var(--text-3)]">{s.firstLineIndent}px</span>
            </div>
            <Slider
              value={[s.firstLineIndent]}
              min={0}
              max={32}
              step={4}
              onValueChange={([val]) => onChange({ firstLineIndent: val })}
            />
          </div>

          {/* Word Spacing */}
          <div className="space-y-1.5">
            <div className="flex justify-between text-xs">
              <span className="font-medium text-[var(--text-2)]">Word spacing</span>
              <span className="text-[var(--text-3)]">{s.wordSpacing}px</span>
            </div>
            <Slider
              value={[s.wordSpacing]}
              min={-2}
              max={8}
              step={1}
              onValueChange={([val]) => onChange({ wordSpacing: val })}
            />
          </div>

          {/* Letter Spacing */}
          <div className="space-y-1.5">
            <div className="flex justify-between text-xs">
              <span className="font-medium text-[var(--text-2)]">Letter spacing</span>
              <span className="text-[var(--text-3)]">{s.letterSpacing}px</span>
            </div>
            <Slider
              value={[s.letterSpacing]}
              min={-1}
              max={4}
              step={0.5}
              onValueChange={([val]) => onChange({ letterSpacing: val })}
            />
          </div>

          {/* Bold text & Override Publisher switches */}
          <div className="space-y-3 pt-2 border-t border-[var(--stroke)]">
            <Switch
              label="Bold text"
              checked={s.boldText}
              onCheckedChange={(val) => onChange({ boldText: val })}
            />
            <Switch
              label="Override publisher fonts & colours"
              checked={s.overridePublisher}
              onCheckedChange={(val) => onChange({ overridePublisher: val })}
            />
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* TAB 3: Layout & Motion */}
      {activeTab === 'layout' && (
        <div className="space-y-4 animate-fade-in">
          {/* Columns */}
          <div className="space-y-2">
            <span className="font-semibold text-[11px] text-[var(--text-3)] uppercase tracking-wider">
              Layout Columns
            </span>
            <SegmentedControl
              size="sm"
              options={[
                { value: '1', label: 'Single page' },
                { value: '2', label: 'Two pages' },
              ]}
              value={String(s.columns)}
              onChange={(val) => onChange({ columns: Number(val) as 1 | 2 })}
            />
          </div>

          {/* Reading Flow */}
          <div className="space-y-2">
            <span className="font-semibold text-[11px] text-[var(--text-3)] uppercase tracking-wider">
              Flow Mode
            </span>
            <SegmentedControl
              size="sm"
              options={[
                { value: 'paginated', label: 'Paginated' },
                { value: 'scrolled', label: 'Continuous scroll' },
              ]}
              value={s.flow}
              onChange={(val) => onChange({ flow: val as 'paginated' | 'scrolled' })}
            />
          </div>

          {/* Page Turn Animation */}
          <div className="space-y-2">
            <span className="font-semibold text-[11px] text-[var(--text-3)] uppercase tracking-wider">
              Page Turn Animation
            </span>
            <SegmentedControl
              size="sm"
              options={[
                { value: 'none', label: 'None' },
                { value: 'slide', label: 'Slide' },
                { value: 'fade', label: 'Fade' },
              ]}
              value={s.pageAnimation}
              onChange={(val) => onChange({ pageAnimation: val as PageAnimation })}
            />
          </div>

          {/* Auto-Paging Mode */}
          <div className="space-y-2 pt-2 border-t border-[var(--stroke)]">
            <Switch
              label="Auto page turn"
              checked={s.autoPaging}
              onCheckedChange={(val) => onChange({ autoPaging: val })}
            />
            {s.autoPaging && (
              <div className="space-y-1.5 pl-2">
                <div className="flex justify-between text-xs">
                  <span className="text-[var(--text-2)]">Paging pace</span>
                  <span className="text-[var(--text-3)]">{s.autoPagingInterval}s per page</span>
                </div>
                <Slider
                  value={[s.autoPagingInterval]}
                  min={5}
                  max={60}
                  step={5}
                  onValueChange={([val]) => onChange({ autoPagingInterval: val })}
                />
              </div>
            )}
          </div>

          {/* Toggles */}
          <div className="space-y-3 pt-2 border-t border-[var(--stroke)]">
            <Switch
              label="Justify text"
              checked={s.justify}
              onCheckedChange={(val) => onChange({ justify: val })}
            />
            <Switch
              label="Hyphenation"
              checked={s.hyphenate}
              onCheckedChange={(val) => onChange({ hyphenate: val })}
            />
            <Switch
              label="Auto dictionary lookup"
              checked={s.autoDefine}
              onCheckedChange={(val) => onChange({ autoDefine: val })}
            />
            <Switch
              label="Mouse wheel turns pages"
              checked={s.wheelPaging}
              onCheckedChange={(val) => onChange({ wheelPaging: val })}
            />
            {s.wheelPaging && (
              <Switch
                label="Invert wheel direction"
                checked={s.wheelInvert}
                onCheckedChange={(val) => onChange({ wheelInvert: val })}
              />
            )}
          </div>

          {/* Tap Zones */}
          <div className="space-y-2 pt-2 border-t border-[var(--stroke)]">
            <span className="font-semibold text-[11px] text-[var(--text-3)] uppercase tracking-wider">
              Tap Navigation
            </span>
            <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-1.5" role="radiogroup" aria-label="Tap navigation">
              {(
                [
                  { value: 'split', label: 'Left back · Right next' },
                  { value: 'reversed', label: 'Left next · Right back' },
                  { value: 'allNext', label: 'Both sides next' },
                  { value: 'off', label: 'Off (taps show menu)' },
                ] as { value: TapZones; label: string }[]
              ).map((o) => (
                <button
                  key={o.value}
                  type="button"
                  role="radio"
                  aria-checked={s.tapZones === o.value}
                  onClick={() => onChange({ tapZones: o.value })}
                  className={`px-2 py-1.5 rounded-[var(--r-md)] border text-[11px] leading-tight text-left cursor-pointer transition-colors ${
                    s.tapZones === o.value
                      ? 'border-[var(--accent)] bg-[var(--accent-soft)] text-[var(--accent)] font-semibold'
                      : 'border-[var(--stroke)] text-[var(--text-2)] hover:text-[var(--text-1)]'
                  }`}
                >
                  {o.label}
                </button>
              ))}
            </div>
            <p className="text-[11px] text-[var(--text-3)]">The outer quarter of each side turns the page; the middle shows the menu.</p>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

/* ---------- Note editor ---------- */

export function NoteEditor({
  annotation,
  onSave,
  onClose,
}: {
  annotation: Annotation;
  onSave: (note: string) => void;
  onClose: () => void;
}) {
  const [note, setNote] = useState(annotation.note);

  return (
    <Dialog
      open={true}
      onOpenChange={(open) => !open && onClose()}
      title={annotation.note ? 'Edit Note' : 'Add Note'}
      footer={
        <div className="flex items-center justify-end gap-2">
          <Button variant="subtle" onClick={onClose}>
            Cancel
          </Button>
          <Button variant="accent" onClick={() => onSave(note)}>
            Save Note
          </Button>
        </div>
      }
    >
      <div className="space-y-4 py-2">
        <blockquote className="p-3 rounded-[var(--r-md)] bg-[var(--layer-2)] border-l-2 border-[var(--accent)] text-xs text-[var(--text-2)] italic">
          “{annotation.text}”
        </blockquote>
        <textarea
          value={note}
          placeholder="Write your notes here…"
          onChange={(e) => setNote(e.target.value)}
          onKeyDown={(e) => {
            if (e.key === 'Enter' && (e.ctrlKey || e.metaKey)) onSave(note);
          }}
          className="w-full h-32 p-3 text-sm rounded-[var(--r-md)] bg-[var(--layer-1)] border border-[var(--stroke)] text-[var(--text-1)] outline-none focus:border-[var(--accent)] focus:ring-2 focus:ring-[var(--accent)] resize-none"
          autoFocus
        />
      </div>
    </Dialog>
  );
}

================
File: apps/client/src/components/Shell.tsx
================
import { useRef, useState, type DragEvent } from 'react';
import { Outlet, useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import { NavRail } from '../design/components/NavRail';
import { BottomNav } from '../design/components/BottomNav';
import { AmbientBackdrop } from '../design/components/AmbientBackdrop';
import { SettingsDialog } from '../design/components/SettingsDialog';
import { ShortcutsDialog } from '../design/components/ShortcutsDialog';
import { InfoBar } from '../design/components/InfoBar';
import { useTheme } from '../design/theme';
import { OnboardingModal } from './OnboardingModal';
import { ACCEPT, importFiles } from '../lib/importBook';
import { useGlobalHotkeys } from '../lib/useHotkeys';

export interface ShellContext {
  openImport: () => void;
  importing: boolean;
  openSettings: (tab?: 'appearance' | 'account') => void;
}

export default function Shell() {
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const inputRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);
  const [importing, setImporting] = useState(false);
  const [dragging, setDragging] = useState(false);
  const [settingsOpen, setSettingsOpen] = useState(false);
  const [settingsTab, setSettingsTab] = useState<'appearance' | 'account'>('appearance');
  const [shortcutsOpen, setShortcutsOpen] = useState(false);
  const [onboardingOpen, setOnboardingOpen] = useState(() => {
    return typeof window !== 'undefined' && !localStorage.getItem('folio_onboarded');
  });
  const [toast, setToast] = useState<{ id: number; message: string; severity?: 'info' | 'success' | 'warning' | 'error' } | null>(null);

  const { activeCoverUrl, settings } = useTheme();

  const openImport = () => inputRef.current?.click();
  const openSettings = (tab: 'appearance' | 'account' = 'appearance') => {
    navigate('/settings');
    setSettingsTab(tab);
  };

  useGlobalHotkeys({
    onSearch: () => {
      const input = document.querySelector<HTMLInputElement>('input[type="text"][placeholder*="Search"], input[type="text"][placeholder*="search"]');
      if (input) {
        input.focus();
        input.select();
      } else {
        navigate('/library');
      }
    },
    onImport: openImport,
    onSettings: () => openSettings('appearance'),
    onNavigate: (path) => navigate(path),
    onHelp: () => setShortcutsOpen((o) => !o),
    onEscape: () => {
      if (shortcutsOpen) setShortcutsOpen(false);
      else if (settingsOpen) setSettingsOpen(false);
    },
  });

  async function handleFiles(files: File[]) {
    if (!files.length) return;
    setImporting(true);
    try {
      const r = await importFiles(files);
      const parts: string[] = [];
      if (r.added) parts.push(`${r.added} added`);
      if (r.skipped) parts.push(`${r.skipped} already in library`);
      if (r.failed.length) parts.push(`${r.failed.length} failed to open`);
      setToast({
        id: Date.now(),
        message: parts.join(' · ') || 'Nothing imported',
        severity: r.failed.length ? 'warning' : 'success',
      });
    } finally {
      setImporting(false);
    }
  }

  const onDragOver = (e: DragEvent) => {
    if (!e.dataTransfer.types.includes('Files')) return;
    e.preventDefault();
    setDragging(true);
  };

  const onDrop = (e: DragEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setDragging(false);
    void handleFiles([...e.dataTransfer.files]);
  };

  return (
    <div
      className="relative flex h-full w-full overflow-hidden bg-[var(--bg-base)] text-[var(--text-1)] select-none"
      onDragOver={onDragOver}
      onDragLeave={(e) => {
        if (!e.currentTarget.contains(e.relatedTarget as Node)) setDragging(false);
      }}
      onDrop={onDrop}
    >
      {/* Ambient backdrop layer */}
      <AmbientBackdrop coverUrl={activeCoverUrl} enabled={settings.ambientEnabled} />

      {/* Desktop/Tablet Navigation Rail */}
      <NavRail onOpenSettings={() => openSettings('appearance')} />

      {/* Main Content Area */}
      <main className="relative z-10 flex-1 flex flex-col h-full overflow-hidden pb-[56px] md:pb-0">
        <Outlet context={{ openImport, importing, openSettings } satisfies ShellContext} />
      </main>

      {/* Mobile Bottom Navigation Bar (< 600px) */}
      <BottomNav onOpenSettings={() => openSettings('appearance')} />

      {/* Global Hidden File Input */}
      <input
        ref={inputRef}
        type="file"
        multiple
        accept={ACCEPT}
        hidden
        onChange={(e) => {
          void handleFiles([...(e.target.files ?? [])]);
          e.target.value = '';
        }}
      />

      {/* Drag & Drop Overlay */}
      {dragging && (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center bg-black/60 backdrop-blur-sm pointer-events-none animate-in fade-in-0 duration-150">
          <div className="p-8 rounded-[var(--r-xl)] bg-[var(--layer-solid)] border-2 border-dashed border-[var(--accent)] text-center shadow-[var(--shadow-flyout)]">
            <h3 className="text-xl font-bold text-[var(--text-1)]">Drop books to import</h3>
            <p className="text-sm text-[var(--text-2)] mt-1">EPUB, MOBI, AZW3, FB2, CBZ, PDF</p>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Toast notification */}
      {toast && (
        <div className="fixed bottom-16 md:bottom-6 right-6 z-50 max-w-sm">
          <InfoBar
            severity={toast.severity || 'info'}
            message={toast.message}
            onClose={() => setToast(null)}
          />
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Global Settings Modal */}
      <SettingsDialog
        open={settingsOpen}
        onOpenChange={setSettingsOpen}
        initialTab={settingsTab}
      />

      {/* Keyboard Shortcuts Help Modal */}
      <ShortcutsDialog open={shortcutsOpen} onOpenChange={setShortcutsOpen} />

      {/* First-launch Onboarding Modal */}
      <OnboardingModal
        open={onboardingOpen}
        onComplete={() => {
          localStorage.setItem('folio_onboarded', 'true');
          setOnboardingOpen(false);
        }}
        onNavigateStore={() => navigate('/store')}
        onOpenSignIn={() => navigate('/settings')}
      />
    </div>
  );
}

================
File: apps/client/src/components/TitleBar.tsx
================
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { Minus, Square, Copy, X, BookOpen } from 'lucide-react';
import {
  isTauri,
  minimizeWindow,
  toggleMaximizeWindow,
  isWindowMaximized,
  closeWindow,
  onWindowResized,
  isAndroidDevice,
} from '../lib/tauri';
import { toggleAppFullscreen, useAppFullscreen } from '../lib/useAppFullscreen';

export function TitleBar() {
  const [maximized, setMaximized] = useState(false);
  // Desktop only: on Android the system draws the status bar; this custom
  // window bar (minimise/maximise/close) was showing on phones too.
  const runningInTauri = isTauri() && !isAndroidDevice();

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!runningInTauri) return;

    let active = true;
    const checkState = async () => {
      const isMax = await isWindowMaximized();
      if (active) setMaximized(isMax);
    };

    void checkState();
    // Event-driven: polling isMaximized() every 500ms meant a constant stream of
    // IPC round-trips and re-renders for the lifetime of the app.
    let unlistenResize: (() => void) | null = null;
    void onWindowResized(() => void checkState()).then((un) => {
      if (active) unlistenResize = un;
      else un();
    });

    const onKey = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
      if (e.key === 'F11') {
        e.preventDefault();
        void toggleAppFullscreen();
      }
    };
    window.addEventListener('keydown', onKey);

    return () => {
      active = false;
      unlistenResize?.();
      window.removeEventListener('keydown', onKey);
    };
  }, [runningInTauri]);

  // Hidden in fullscreen, like a native title bar.
  const fullscreen = useAppFullscreen();

  // Full-window (position: fixed) screens like the reader offset themselves by this
  // so their top bar isn't hidden underneath the custom title bar.
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!runningInTauri) return;
    document.documentElement.style.setProperty('--titlebar-h', fullscreen ? '0px' : '32px');
    return () => {
      document.documentElement.style.removeProperty('--titlebar-h');
    };
  }, [runningInTauri, fullscreen]);

  if (!runningInTauri || fullscreen) return null;

  return (
    <div
      data-tauri-drag-region
      className="h-8 w-full flex items-center justify-between select-none bg-[var(--layer-solid)] border-b border-[var(--stroke)] text-[var(--text-1)] text-xs z-50 shrink-0"
      style={{ WebkitAppRegion: 'drag' } as React.CSSProperties}
    >
      {/* Left: App Logo & Title */}
      <div className="flex items-center gap-2 px-3 pointer-events-none">
        <BookOpen className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-[var(--accent)]" />
        <span className="font-semibold text-xs tracking-tight">Verso</span>
      </div>

      {/* Middle: Drag area spacer */}
      <div data-tauri-drag-region className="flex-1 h-full" />

      {/* Right: Window Control Buttons */}
      <div
        className="flex items-center h-full"
        style={{ WebkitAppRegion: 'no-drag' } as React.CSSProperties}
      >
        <button
          type="button"
          aria-label="Minimize"
          onClick={() => void minimizeWindow()}
          className="h-full w-11 flex items-center justify-center hover:bg-[var(--layer-2)] active:bg-[var(--layer-3)] text-[var(--text-2)] hover:text-[var(--text-1)] transition-colors cursor-pointer border-none p-0 outline-none"
        >
          <Minus className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
        </button>

        <button
          type="button"
          aria-label={maximized ? 'Restore' : 'Maximize'}
          onClick={() => {
            void toggleMaximizeWindow();
            setMaximized(!maximized);
          }}
          className="h-full w-11 flex items-center justify-center hover:bg-[var(--layer-2)] active:bg-[var(--layer-3)] text-[var(--text-2)] hover:text-[var(--text-1)] transition-colors cursor-pointer border-none p-0 outline-none"
        >
          {maximized ? (
            <Copy className="w-3 h-3 rotate-180" />
          ) : (
            <Square className="w-3 h-3" />
          )}
        </button>

        <button
          type="button"
          aria-label="Close"
          onClick={() => void closeWindow()}
          className="h-full w-11 flex items-center justify-center hover:bg-[var(--danger)] hover:text-[var(--on-accent)] active:opacity-90 text-[var(--text-2)] transition-colors cursor-pointer border-none p-0 outline-none"
        >
          <X className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
        </button>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

import React, { useState, useEffect, type ReactNode } from 'react';
import { NavLink } from 'react-router-dom';
import {
  Menu,
  Home,
  BookOpen,
  BookMarked,
  ShoppingBag,
  Sparkles,
  BarChart2,
  Settings,
  RefreshCw,
  Check,
  CloudOff,
  AlertCircle,
} from 'lucide-react';
import { clsx } from 'clsx';
import { twMerge } from 'tailwind-merge';
import { Tooltip } from './Tooltip';
import { Popover } from './Popover';
import { Button } from './Button';
import { useSyncStore } from '../../features/sync/engine';
import { fmtRelativeTime } from '../../lib/format';

export interface NavRailItem {
  to: string;
  label: string;
  icon: ReactNode;
  badge?: number | string;
}

export const MAIN_NAV_ITEMS: NavRailItem[] = [
  { to: '/', label: 'Home', icon: <Home className="w-5 h-5" /> },
  { to: '/library', label: 'Library', icon: <BookOpen className="w-5 h-5" /> },
  { to: '/notebook', label: 'Notebook', icon: <BookMarked className="w-5 h-5" /> },
  { to: '/store', label: 'Store', icon: <ShoppingBag className="w-5 h-5" /> },
  { to: '/updates', label: 'Updates', icon: <Sparkles className="w-5 h-5" /> },
  { to: '/stats', label: 'Statistics', icon: <BarChart2 className="w-5 h-5" /> },
];

export interface NavRailProps {
  syncStatus?: 'idle' | 'syncing' | 'error' | 'offline' | 'signedOut';
  className?: string;
  onOpenSettings?: () => void;
}

export const NavRail: React.FC<NavRailProps> = ({
  syncStatus: syncStatusProp,
  className,
  onOpenSettings,
}) => {
  const [expanded, setExpanded] = useState(() => {
    return localStorage.getItem('folio_nav_expanded') === 'true';
  });

  const {
    status: storeSyncStatus,
    lastSyncAt,
    lastError,
    pendingChanges,
    filesUploadingCount,
    filesUploadingTotal,
    runSync,
    storageOffline,
  } = useSyncStore();

  const effectiveStatus = syncStatusProp ?? storeSyncStatus;

  useEffect(() => {
    localStorage.setItem('folio_nav_expanded', String(expanded));
  }, [expanded]);

  const syncLabels = {
    idle: lastSyncAt ? (storageOffline ? 'Synced · book storage offline' : 'Synced') : 'Not synced yet',
    signedOut: 'Sync off · not signed in',
    syncing: 'Syncing…',
    error: 'Sync error',
    offline: 'Offline',
  };

  const syncIcons = {
    idle: <Check className="w-4 h-4 text-[var(--success)] shrink-0" />,
    signedOut: <CloudOff className="w-4 h-4 text-[var(--text-3)] shrink-0" />,
    syncing: <RefreshCw className="w-4 h-4 animate-spin text-[var(--accent)] shrink-0" />,
    offline: <CloudOff className="w-4 h-4 text-[var(--text-3)] shrink-0" />,
    error: <AlertCircle className="w-4 h-4 text-[var(--danger)] shrink-0" />,
  };

  return (
    <aside
      className={twMerge(
        clsx(
          'hidden md:flex flex-col h-full bg-[var(--layer-1)] border-r border-[var(--stroke)] select-none shrink-0 transition-all duration-[var(--d-slow)] ease-[var(--ease-out)] z-40',
          expanded ? 'w-[260px]' : 'w-[56px]',
          className,
        ),
      )}
    >
      {/* Top hamburger toggle */}
      <div className="h-[48px] flex items-center px-2 shrink-0">
        <button
          type="button"
          aria-label={expanded ? 'Collapse navigation' : 'Expand navigation'}
          onClick={() => setExpanded(!expanded)}
          className="w-10 h-10 rounded-[var(--r-md)] flex items-center justify-center text-[var(--text-2)] hover:text-[var(--text-1)] hover:bg-[var(--layer-2)] active:bg-[var(--layer-3)] transition-colors cursor-pointer outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-[var(--accent)]"
        >
          <Menu className="w-5 h-5" />
        </button>
        {expanded && (
          <span className="font-display font-bold text-lg text-[var(--text-1)] ml-2.5 tracking-tight animate-in fade-in-0 duration-150">
            Verso
          </span>
        )}
      </div>

      {/* Main navigation list */}
      <nav className="flex-1 flex flex-col gap-1 px-2 py-3 overflow-y-auto no-scrollbar">
        {MAIN_NAV_ITEMS.map((item) => {
          return (
            <NavLink
              key={item.to}
              to={item.to}
              end={item.to === '/'}
              className={({ isActive }) =>
                clsx(
                  'group relative flex items-center h-[40px] rounded-[var(--r-md)] transition-colors cursor-pointer outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-[var(--accent)]',
                  isActive
                    ? 'bg-[var(--layer-2)] text-[var(--text-1)] font-semibold'
                    : 'text-[var(--text-2)] hover:text-[var(--text-1)] hover:bg-[var(--layer-1)] active:bg-[var(--layer-2)]',
                  expanded ? 'px-3 gap-3.5' : 'justify-center px-0',
                )
              }
            >
              {({ isActive }) => {
                const navContent = (
                  <>
                    {/* Animated 3px accent pill for active item */}
                    {isActive && (
                      <span className="absolute left-0 top-[12px] bottom-[12px] w-[3px] bg-[var(--accent)] rounded-r-full" />
                    )}

                    <span className="shrink-0">{item.icon}</span>

                    {expanded && (
                      <span className="truncate text-sm grow">{item.label}</span>
                    )}

                    {item.badge && expanded && (
                      <span className="px-1.5 py-0.5 text-[11px] font-bold rounded-full bg-[var(--accent-soft)] text-[var(--accent)] shrink-0">
                        {item.badge}
                      </span>
                    )}
                  </>
                );

                if (!expanded) {
                  return (
                    <Tooltip content={item.label} side="right">
                      <div className="flex items-center justify-center w-full h-full">
                        {navContent}
                      </div>
                    </Tooltip>
                  );
                }

                return navContent;
              }}
            </NavLink>
          );
        })}
      </nav>

      {/* Footer controls: Settings & Sync */}
      <div className="p-2 border-t border-[var(--stroke)] flex flex-col gap-1 shrink-0">
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={onOpenSettings}
          className={clsx(
            'flex items-center h-[40px] rounded-[var(--r-md)] text-[var(--text-2)] hover:text-[var(--text-1)] hover:bg-[var(--layer-2)] active:bg-[var(--layer-3)] transition-colors cursor-pointer outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-[var(--accent)]',
            expanded ? 'px-3 gap-3.5' : 'justify-center',
          )}
        >
          {expanded ? (
            <>
              <Settings className="w-5 h-5 shrink-0" />
              <span className="text-sm truncate">Settings</span>
            </>
          ) : (
            <Tooltip content="Settings" side="right">
              <div className="flex items-center justify-center w-full h-full">
                <Settings className="w-5 h-5" />
              </div>
            </Tooltip>
          )}
        </button>

        {/* Sync status indicator & Popover flyout */}
        <Popover
          side="right"
          align="end"
          trigger={
            <button
              type="button"
              className={clsx(
                'flex items-center h-[36px] w-full rounded-[var(--r-md)] text-xs text-[var(--text-2)] hover:text-[var(--text-1)] hover:bg-[var(--layer-2)] transition-colors cursor-pointer outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-[var(--accent)] select-none',
                expanded ? 'px-3 gap-3' : 'justify-center px-0',
              )}
            >
              {syncIcons[effectiveStatus]}
              {expanded && <span className="truncate">{syncLabels[effectiveStatus]}</span>}
            </button>
          }
        >
          <div className="w-64 flex flex-col gap-3 p-1">
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between border-b border-[var(--stroke)] pb-2">
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                {syncIcons[effectiveStatus]}
                <span className="font-semibold text-sm capitalize">{syncLabels[effectiveStatus]}</span>
              </div>
              {pendingChanges > 0 && (
                <span className="text-[11px] font-bold px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded-full bg-[var(--accent-soft)] text-[var(--accent)]">
                  {pendingChanges} pending
                </span>
              )}
            </div>

            <div className="flex flex-col gap-1 text-xs text-[var(--text-2)]">
              <div>Last synced: {fmtRelativeTime(lastSyncAt)}</div>
              {filesUploadingTotal > 0 && (
                <div>Uploading files: {filesUploadingCount} / {filesUploadingTotal}</div>
              )}
              {lastError && (
                <div className="text-[var(--danger)] mt-1 font-mono text-[11px] break-words">
                  {lastError}
                </div>
              )}
            </div>

            <Button
              size="sm"
              variant="accent"
              disabled={effectiveStatus === 'syncing' || effectiveStatus === 'offline'}
              onClick={() => void runSync()}
              className="w-full mt-1"
            >
              {effectiveStatus === 'syncing' ? 'Syncing…' : 'Sync now'}
            </Button>
          </div>
        </Popover>
      </div>
    </aside>
  );
};

import { useCallback, useMemo } from 'react'
import { useLiveQuery } from 'dexie-react-hooks'
import { db, dirty, type HighlightStyle } from '../db'
import type { FoliateView } from './foliate'

export type ThemeId = 'auto' | 'light' | 'sepia' | 'green' | 'dusk' | 'dark' | 'black' | 'custom'
export type PageAnimation = 'none' | 'slide' | 'fade'
export type TapZones = 'split' | 'reversed' | 'allNext' | 'off'

export interface CustomTheme {
  name: string
  bg: string
  fg: string
  link: string
  dark: boolean
  highlightOpacity?: number
}

export const THEMES: Record<Exclude<ThemeId, 'custom' | 'auto'>, { name: string; bg: string; fg: string; link: string; dark: boolean }> = {
  light: { name: 'Paper', bg: '#fbfbf8', fg: '#1f1f1f', link: '#2563eb', dark: false },
  sepia: { name: 'Sepia', bg: '#f4ecd8', fg: '#5b4636', link: '#8b5a2b', dark: false },
  green: { name: 'Sage', bg: '#dfeedd', fg: '#22331f', link: '#2f6b3a', dark: false },
  dusk: { name: 'Dusk', bg: '#2b2a33', fg: '#d8d4cf', link: '#9ab8ff', dark: true },
  dark: { name: 'Night', bg: '#1c1c1e', fg: '#d6d6d6', link: '#8ab4f8', dark: true },
  black: { name: 'OLED', bg: '#000000', fg: '#b8b8b8', link: '#8ab4f8', dark: true },
}

export const BUNDLED_FONTS: { id: string; label: string; css: string }[] = [
  { id: 'book', label: 'Book default', css: '' },
  { id: 'literata', label: 'Literata', css: '"Literata", Georgia, serif' },
  { id: 'source-serif', label: 'Source Serif 4', css: '"Source Serif 4", Georgia, serif' },
  { id: 'atkinson', label: 'Atkinson Hyperlegible', css: '"Atkinson Hyperlegible", sans-serif' },
  { id: 'inter', label: 'Inter', css: '"Inter Variable", "Inter", system-ui, sans-serif' },
  { id: 'serif', label: 'Classic serif', css: 'Georgia, "Iowan Old Style", "Palatino Linotype", Palatino, serif' },
  { id: 'literary', label: 'Literary', css: 'Charter, "Bitstream Charter", "Sitka Text", Cambria, serif' },
  { id: 'mono', label: 'Monospace', css: '"JetBrains Mono Variable", "JetBrains Mono", ui-monospace, monospace' },
]

export const FONTS = BUNDLED_FONTS

export const HIGHLIGHT_COLORS = [
  { id: 'yellow', label: 'Yellow', hex: '#facc15' },
  { id: 'green', label: 'Green', hex: '#4ade80' },
  { id: 'blue', label: 'Blue', hex: '#60a5fa' },
  { id: 'pink', label: 'Pink', hex: '#f472b6' },
  { id: 'purple', label: 'Purple', hex: '#a78bfa' },
  { id: 'orange', label: 'Orange', hex: '#fb923c' },
]

export const colorHex = (id: string) => HIGHLIGHT_COLORS.find((c) => c.id === id)?.hex ?? (id || '#facc15')

export interface ReaderSettings {
  theme: ThemeId
  customTheme?: CustomTheme
  matchAppTheme: boolean
  nightLight: number // 0 to 40%
  dimOverlay: number // 0 to 60%
  font: string
  fontSize: number
  lineHeight: number
  paragraphSpacing: number // 0 to 24px
  firstLineIndent: number // 0 to 32px
  wordSpacing: number // -2 to 8px
  letterSpacing: number // -1 to 4px
  boldText: boolean
  overridePublisher: boolean
  pageAnimation: PageAnimation
  autoPaging: boolean
  autoPagingInterval: number // seconds per page
  gap: number
  maxWidth: number
  columns: 1 | 2
  flow: 'paginated' | 'scrolled'
  justify: boolean
  hyphenate: boolean
  animated: boolean
  lastColor: string
  lastStyle: HighlightStyle
  ttsRate: number
  ttsVoice: string
  ttsSleepTimer: number // minutes, 0 = off
  ttsVoiceMap: Record<string, string> // lang -> voice URI
  translateTo: string
  autoDefine: boolean
  wheelPaging: boolean
  wheelInvert: boolean
  /**
   * What tapping/clicking the page edges does. split: left = back, right = next;
   * reversed: left = next, right = back; allNext: both edges go forward;
   * off: edges don't turn pages (only toggle the toolbars).
   */
  tapZones: TapZones
  volumeKeysTurn?: boolean
  haptics?: boolean
  keepScreenOn?: boolean
}

export const DEFAULT_SETTINGS: ReaderSettings = {
  // Follows the app: a dark app opens books on a true-black (OLED) page.
  theme: 'auto',
  customTheme: {
    name: 'Custom',
    bg: '#fbfbf8',
    fg: '#1f1f1f',
    link: '#2563eb',
    dark: false,
    highlightOpacity: 0.35,
  },
  matchAppTheme: false,
  nightLight: 0,
  dimOverlay: 0,
  font: 'book',
  fontSize: 18,
  lineHeight: 1.6,
  paragraphSpacing: 0,
  firstLineIndent: 0,
  wordSpacing: 0,
  letterSpacing: 0,
  boldText: false,
  overridePublisher: true,
  pageAnimation: 'slide',
  autoPaging: false,
  autoPagingInterval: 15,
  gap: 6,
  maxWidth: 720,
  columns: 2,
  flow: 'paginated',
  justify: true,
  hyphenate: true,
  animated: true,
  lastColor: 'yellow',
  lastStyle: 'highlight',
  ttsRate: 1,
  ttsVoice: '',
  ttsSleepTimer: 0,
  ttsVoiceMap: {},
  translateTo: 'hi',
  autoDefine: true,
  wheelPaging: true,
  wheelInvert: false,
  tapZones: 'split',
  volumeKeysTurn: false,
  haptics: true,
  keepScreenOn: true,
}

/** The app's current light/dark mode (set on <html> by the ThemeProvider). */
const appIsDark = () =>
  typeof document !== 'undefined' && document.documentElement.getAttribute('data-theme') !== 'light'

export function getEffectiveTheme(s: ReaderSettings): { name: string; bg: string; fg: string; link: string; dark: boolean } {
  if (s.theme === 'custom' && s.customTheme) {
    return s.customTheme
  }
  if (s.theme === 'auto') return appIsDark() ? THEMES.black : THEMES.light
  return THEMES[s.theme as keyof typeof THEMES] ?? THEMES.light
}

export function bookCSS(s: ReaderSettings, extraFontFaceCSS: string = ''): string {
  const t = getEffectiveTheme(s)
  const bundled = BUNDLED_FONTS.find((f) => f.id === s.font)?.css
  const font = bundled || (s.font !== 'book' ? `"${s.font}", sans-serif` : '')
  const hyph = s.hyphenate ? 'auto' : 'manual'

  return `
@namespace epub "http://www.idpf.org/2007/ops";
${extraFontFaceCSS}
html { color-scheme: ${t.dark ? 'dark' : 'light'}; font-size: ${s.fontSize}px !important; }
html, body { background: transparent !important; color: ${t.fg} !important; }
${s.overridePublisher ? 'body *:not(a) { color: inherit !important; }' : ''}
${t.dark ? 'body * { background-color: transparent !important; }' : ''}
a:any-link, a:any-link * { color: ${t.link} !important; }
${font ? `body, body *:not(code):not(pre):not(kbd) { font-family: ${font} !important; }` : ''}
p, li, blockquote, dd, dt, div {
  line-height: ${s.lineHeight} !important;
  ${s.wordSpacing !== 0 ? `word-spacing: ${s.wordSpacing}px !important;` : ''}
  ${s.letterSpacing !== 0 ? `letter-spacing: ${s.letterSpacing}px !important;` : ''}
}
${s.paragraphSpacing > 0 ? `p { margin-bottom: ${s.paragraphSpacing}px !important; margin-top: 0 !important; }` : ''}
${s.firstLineIndent > 0 ? `p { text-indent: ${s.firstLineIndent}px !important; }` : ''}
${s.boldText ? 'body, p, li, blockquote, dd, dt, div, span { font-weight: 600 !important; }' : ''}
p, li, blockquote, dd {
  text-align: ${s.justify ? 'justify' : 'start'} !important;
  -webkit-hyphens: ${hyph}; hyphens: ${hyph};
  widows: 2; orphans: 2;
}
pre { white-space: pre-wrap !important; }
aside[epub|type~="endnote"], aside[epub|type~="footnote"],
aside[epub|type~="note"], aside[epub|type~="rearnote"] { display: none; }
`
}

export function applyLayout(renderer: FoliateView['renderer'], s: ReaderSettings, extraFontFaceCSS: string = '') {
  if (!renderer) return
  renderer.setAttribute('flow', s.flow)
  renderer.setAttribute('gap', `${s.gap}%`)
  renderer.setAttribute('margin', '20px')
  renderer.setAttribute('max-inline-size', `${s.maxWidth}px`)
  renderer.setAttribute('max-column-count', String(s.columns))
  if (s.animated || s.pageAnimation !== 'none') renderer.setAttribute('animated', '')
  else renderer.removeAttribute('animated')
  renderer.setStyles?.(bookCSS(s, extraFontFaceCSS))
}

export function useReaderSettings(): [ReaderSettings, (patch: Partial<ReaderSettings>) => void] {
  const row = useLiveQuery(() => db.settings.get('reader'), [])
  const settings = useMemo(() => {
    const stored = (row?.data ?? {}) as Partial<ReaderSettings> & { themeV?: number }
    const merged = { ...DEFAULT_SETTINGS, ...stored }
    // Before 'auto' existed, saving any setting also persisted the then-default
    // 'light' theme, so a stored 'light' from that era wasn't a real choice.
    if (!stored.themeV && stored.theme === 'light') merged.theme = 'auto'
    return merged
  }, [row])
  const update = useCallback((patch: Partial<ReaderSettings>) => {
    void db.transaction('rw', db.settings, async () => {
      const cur = await db.settings.get('reader')
      await db.settings.put({
        id: 'reader',
        // themeV marks settings written after 'auto' was introduced (see above).
        data: { ...DEFAULT_SETTINGS, ...(cur?.data ?? {}), ...patch, themeV: 2 },
        deleted_at: null,
        ...dirty(),
      })
    })
  }, [])
  return [settings, update]
}
