import { useMemo, useState } from 'react';
import { useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import { useLiveQuery } from 'dexie-react-hooks';
import {
  NotebookPen,
  StickyNote,
  Download,
  ChevronDown,
  ChevronRight,
  ExternalLink,
  Copy,
  Trash2,
  BookOpen,
} from 'lucide-react';
import { db, dirty, type Annotation, type Book } from '../db';
import { compareCFI } from '../lib/foliate';
import { HIGHLIGHT_COLORS, colorHex } from '../lib/readerSettings';
import { fmtDate } from '../lib/format';
import { CommandBar } from '../design/components/CommandBar';
import { SearchBox } from '../design/components/SearchBox';
import { Button } from '../design/components/Button';
import { Dropdown } from '../design/components/Dropdown';
import { ContextMenu } from '../design/components/ContextMenu';
import { BookCard } from '../design/components/BookCard';
import { EmptyState } from '../design/components/EmptyState';

function Marked({ text, q }: { text: string; q: string }) {
  if (!q) return <>{text}</>;
  const parts = text.split(new RegExp(`(${q.replace(/[.*+?^${}()|[\]\\]/g, '\\$&')})`, 'gi'));
  return (
    <>
      {parts.map((p, i) =>
        i % 2 ? (
          <mark key={i} className="bg-[var(--accent-soft)] text-[var(--accent)] rounded-xs px-0.5">
            {p}
          </mark>
        ) : (
          p
        ),
      )}
    </>
  );
}

export default function Notebook() {
  const navigate = useNavigate();

  const annotations = useLiveQuery(
    () => db.annotations.filter((a) => !a.deleted_at && a.type !== 'bookmark').toArray(),
    [],
    [] as Annotation[],
  );
  const books = useLiveQuery(() => db.books.filter((b) => !b.deleted_at).toArray(), [], [] as Book[]);

  const [query, setQuery] = useState('');
  const [colorFilter, setColorFilter] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [notesOnly, setNotesOnly] = useState(false);
  const [collapsedBooks, setCollapsedBooks] = useState<Record<string, boolean>>({});

  const toggleCollapse = (bookId: string) => {
    setCollapsedBooks((prev) => ({ ...prev, [bookId]: !prev[bookId] }));
  };

  const groups = useMemo(() => {
    const bookMap = new Map(books.map((b) => [b.id, b]));
    const needle = query.trim().toLowerCase();
    const byBook = new Map<string, Annotation[]>();

    for (const a of annotations) {
      if (!bookMap.has(a.book_id)) continue;
      if (colorFilter && a.color !== colorFilter) continue;
      if (notesOnly && !a.note) continue;
      if (needle && !`${a.text} ${a.note} ${a.chapter}`.toLowerCase().includes(needle)) continue;
      byBook.set(a.book_id, [...(byBook.get(a.book_id) ?? []), a]);
    }

    return [...byBook.entries()]
      .map(([id, list]) => ({
        book: bookMap.get(id)!,
        items: list.sort((x, y) => compareCFI(x.cfi, y.cfi) || x.created_at - y.created_at),
        latest: Math.max(...list.map((a) => a.updated_at)),
      }))
      .sort((a, b) => b.latest - a.latest);
  }, [annotations, books, query, colorFilter, notesOnly]);

  const total = groups.reduce((n, g) => n + g.items.length, 0);

  // Export functions
  const exportMarkdown = () => {
    let md = '# Verso Notebook Export\n\n';
    for (const g of groups) {
      md += `## ${g.book.title} — ${g.book.author}\n\n`;
      for (const item of g.items) {
        md += `> ${item.text}\n\n`;
        if (item.note) md += `**Note**: ${item.note}\n\n`;
        md += `*${item.chapter || 'Chapter'} · ${fmtDate(item.created_at)}*\n\n---\n\n`;
      }
    }
    const blob = new Blob([md], { type: 'text/markdown' });
    const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
    const a = document.createElement('a');
    a.href = url;
    a.download = `folio-annotations-${Date.now()}.md`;
    a.click();
    URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
  };

  const exportJSON = () => {
    const data = groups.map((g) => ({
      book: { id: g.book.id, title: g.book.title, author: g.book.author },
      annotations: g.items,
    }));
    const blob = new Blob([JSON.stringify(data, null, 2)], { type: 'application/json' });
    const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
    const a = document.createElement('a');
    a.href = url;
    a.download = `folio-annotations-${Date.now()}.json`;
    a.click();
    URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
  };

  const exportDropdownSections = [
    {
      title: 'Export Annotations',
      items: [
        {
          key: 'markdown',
          label: 'Export as Markdown (.md)',
          onSelect: exportMarkdown,
        },
        {
          key: 'json',
          label: 'Export as JSON (.json)',
          onSelect: exportJSON,
        },
      ],
    },
  ];

  return (
    <div className="flex-1 flex flex-col h-full overflow-hidden bg-[var(--bg-base)] text-[var(--text-1)]">
      {/* 48px CommandBar */}
      <CommandBar
        title="Notebook"
        subtitle={`${total} ${total === 1 ? 'highlight' : 'highlights'}`}
        rightActions={
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <div className="w-36 sm:w-52 md:w-64">
              <SearchBox value={query} onChange={setQuery} placeholder="Search notes…" />
            </div>

            <Dropdown
              trigger={
                <Button
                  variant="standard"
                  size="sm"
                  aria-label="Export"
                  icon={<Download className="w-4 h-4" />}
                >
                  <span className="hidden sm:inline">Export</span>
                </Button>
              }
              sections={exportDropdownSections}
            />
          </div>
        }
      />

      {/* Filter Toolbar */}
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-3 px-4 sm:px-6 py-2.5 border-b border-[var(--stroke)] bg-[var(--layer-1)] overflow-x-auto no-scrollbar">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-4 shrink-0">
          {/* Color Dots */}
          <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setColorFilter(null)}
              className={`text-xs px-2 py-0.5 rounded-[var(--r-sm)] border cursor-pointer ${
                colorFilter === null
                  ? 'border-[var(--accent)] bg-[var(--accent-soft)] text-[var(--accent)] font-semibold'
                  : 'border-[var(--stroke)] text-[var(--text-3)] hover:text-[var(--text-1)]'
              }`}
            >
              All
            </button>
            {HIGHLIGHT_COLORS.map((c) => (
              <button
                key={c.id}
                type="button"
                aria-label={c.label}
                onClick={() => setColorFilter(colorFilter === c.id ? null : c.id)}
                className={`w-5 h-5 rounded-full cursor-pointer transition-transform hover:scale-110 flex items-center justify-center ${
                  colorFilter === c.id ? 'ring-2 ring-[var(--text-1)] ring-offset-2 ring-offset-[var(--layer-solid)]' : ''
                }`}
                style={{ backgroundColor: c.hex }}
              />
            ))}
          </div>

          {/* Notes Only Toggle */}
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => setNotesOnly(!notesOnly)}
            className={`flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs px-2.5 py-1 rounded-[var(--r-md)] border cursor-pointer transition-colors ${
              notesOnly
                ? 'border-[var(--accent)] bg-[var(--accent-soft)] text-[var(--accent)] font-semibold'
                : 'border-[var(--stroke)] text-[var(--text-2)] hover:bg-[var(--layer-2)]'
            }`}
          >
            <StickyNote className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
            <span>Notes only</span>
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Main Highlights List */}
      <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto py-6 space-y-6 w-full px-[max(1.5rem,calc((100%-64rem)/2))]">
        {annotations.length === 0 ? (
          <EmptyState
            icon={<NotebookPen className="w-7 h-7" />}
            title="No highlights yet"
            description="Select text in any book while reading to highlight passages, choose colors, or attach personal notes."
          />
        ) : groups.length === 0 ? (
          <EmptyState
            title="No matching annotations"
            description="Try choosing a different color filter or clearing your search term."
            action={
              <Button
                variant="standard"
                onClick={() => {
                  setQuery('');
                  setColorFilter(null);
                  setNotesOnly(false);
                }}
              >
                Clear Filters
              </Button>
            }
          />
        ) : (
          groups.map(({ book, items }) => {
            const isCollapsed = !!collapsedBooks[book.id];
            return (
              <div
                key={book.id}
                className="rounded-[var(--r-lg)] bg-[var(--layer-2)] border border-[var(--stroke)] overflow-hidden shadow-xs"
              >
                {/* Book Header Accordion Toggle */}
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => toggleCollapse(book.id)}
                  className="w-full flex items-center justify-between p-4 bg-[var(--layer-1)] hover:bg-[var(--layer-3)] transition-colors cursor-pointer text-left outline-none"
                >
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-3.5 min-w-0">
                    <div className="w-10 shrink-0">
                      <BookCard
                        id={book.id}
                        title={book.title}
                        author={book.author}
                        coverHash={book.cover_hash}
                        showMetadata={false}
                      />
                    </div>
                    <div className="min-w-0">
                      <h3 className="font-semibold text-sm text-[var(--text-1)] truncate">
                        {book.title}
                      </h3>
                      <p className="text-xs text-[var(--text-3)] truncate mt-0.5">
                        {book.author} · {items.length} {items.length === 1 ? 'item' : 'items'}
                      </p>
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-[var(--text-3)]">
                    {isCollapsed ? <ChevronRight className="w-4 h-4" /> : <ChevronDown className="w-4 h-4" />}
                  </div>
                </button>

                {/* Items in Book Group */}
                {!isCollapsed && (
                  <div className="divide-y divide-[var(--stroke)] p-2 space-y-2">
                    {items.map((a) => {
                      const hex = colorHex(a.color);
                      const contextSections = [
                        {
                          items: [
                            {
                              key: 'goto',
                              label: 'Go to in book',
                              icon: <BookOpen className="w-4 h-4" />,
                              onSelect: () =>
                                navigate(`/read/${a.book_id}?cfi=${encodeURIComponent(a.cfi)}`),
                            },
                            {
                              key: 'copy',
                              label: 'Copy quote',
                              icon: <Copy className="w-4 h-4" />,
                              onSelect: () => void navigator.clipboard.writeText(a.text),
                            },
                            {
                              key: 'delete',
                              label: 'Delete highlight',
                              icon: <Trash2 className="w-4 h-4" />,
                              danger: true,
                              onSelect: () =>
                                void db.annotations.update(a.id, {
                                  deleted_at: Date.now(),
                                  ...dirty(),
                                }),
                            },
                          ],
                        },
                      ];

                      return (
                        <ContextMenu key={a.id} sections={contextSections}>
                          <div
                            role="button"
                            tabIndex={0}
                            onClick={() =>
                              navigate(`/read/${a.book_id}?cfi=${encodeURIComponent(a.cfi)}`)
                            }
                            onKeyDown={(e) =>
                              (e.key === 'Enter' || e.key === ' ') &&
                              navigate(`/read/${a.book_id}?cfi=${encodeURIComponent(a.cfi)}`)
                            }
                            className="group relative flex flex-col gap-2 p-3.5 rounded-[var(--r-md)] hover:bg-[var(--layer-3)] transition-colors cursor-pointer select-none text-left"
                          >
                            {/* Color bar indicator on left */}
                            <div
                              className="absolute left-1 top-3.5 bottom-3.5 w-1 rounded-full"
                              style={{ backgroundColor: hex }}
                            />

                            <div className="pl-3.5 space-y-2">
                              <blockquote className="text-sm text-[var(--text-1)] leading-relaxed italic border-l-2 border-transparent pl-0">
                                “<Marked text={a.text} q={query} />”
                              </blockquote>

                              {a.note && (
                                <div className="flex items-start gap-2 p-2 rounded-[var(--r-sm)] bg-[var(--layer-1)] text-xs text-[var(--text-2)] border border-[var(--stroke)]">
                                  <StickyNote className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-[var(--accent)] shrink-0 mt-0.5" />
                                  <div>
                                    <Marked text={a.note} q={query} />
                                  </div>
                                </div>
                              )}

                              <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-[11px] text-[var(--text-3)] pt-1">
                                <span>
                                  {a.chapter && <span className="font-medium">{a.chapter} · </span>}
                                  {fmtDate(a.created_at)}
                                </span>
                                <span className="opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100 transition-opacity text-[var(--accent)] font-medium inline-flex items-center gap-1">
                                  Go to page <ExternalLink className="w-3 h-3" />
                                </span>
                              </div>
                            </div>
                          </div>
                        </ContextMenu>
                      );
                    })}
                  </div>
                )}
              </div>
            );
          })
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

import React, { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import {
  Palette,
  BookOpen,
  Layers,
  Library as LibraryIcon,
  User,
  HardDrive,
  Archive,
  Info,
  Check,
  Download,
  Upload,
  Trash2,
  ChevronLeft,
  ChevronRight,
} from 'lucide-react';
import { useTheme, ACCENT_PRESETS } from '../design/theme';
import { registerBackHandler } from '../lib/backStack';
import { useReaderSettings } from '../lib/readerSettings';
import { db } from '../db';
import { Button } from '../design/components/Button';
import { Dialog } from '../design/components/Dialog';
import { exportBackup, importBackup, downloadBackupBlob } from '../lib/backup';
import { t, setAppLanguage } from '../lib/i18n';
import { isCrashReportingEnabled, setCrashReportingEnabled } from '../lib/crashReporter';
import { useLiveQuery } from 'dexie-react-hooks';
import { AccountSettings } from '../features/auth/AccountSettings';
import { getContentRatings, setContentRating, type ContentRating } from '../lib/mangaPrefs';
import { isAndroidDevice } from '../lib/tauri';
import { clearMangaHistory } from '../lib/series';
import { clearApiCache } from '../lib/apiCache';

type SettingsTab =
  | 'appearance'
  | 'reading'
  | 'manga'
  | 'library'
  | 'account'
  | 'storage'
  | 'backup'
  | 'about';

export default function Settings() {
  const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = useState<SettingsTab>('appearance');
  // Phones: a category list first; picking one opens it full screen with Back
  // (the desktop sidebar squeezed into a scrolling strip didn't fit).
  const [mobileDetail, setMobileDetail] = useState(false);
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!mobileDetail) return;
    // Android back button returns to the category list first.
    return registerBackHandler(() => {
      setMobileDetail(false);
      return true;
    }, 30);
  }, [mobileDetail]);
  const { settings, updateSettings } = useTheme();
  const [readerSettings, updateReaderSettings] = useReaderSettings();

  // Storage metrics
  const books = useLiveQuery(() => db.books.toArray(), []) || [];
  const mangaPagesCount = useLiveQuery(() => db.manga_pages.count(), []) || 0;
  const coversCount = useLiveQuery(() => db.covers.count(), []) || 0;

  const totalBookBytes = books.reduce((acc, b) => acc + (b.file_size || 0), 0);
  const totalBookMB = (totalBookBytes / (1024 * 1024)).toFixed(1);

  // Backup state
  const [exporting, setExporting] = useState(false);
  const [importing, setImporting] = useState(false);

  // Adult rating confirmation
  const [adultConfirmOpen, setAdultConfirmOpen] = useState(false);
  const [pendingAdultKey, setPendingAdultKey] = useState<'erotica' | 'pornographic' | null>(null);
  // Previously the opt-in only showed a toast; nothing was stored or sent to MangaDex.
  const [ratings, setRatings] = useState<ContentRating[]>(() => getContentRatings());
  const toggleRating = (rating: ContentRating, enabled: boolean) => setRatings(setContentRating(rating, enabled));

  // Crash reporting opt-in
  const [crashReporting, setCrashReporting] = useState(isCrashReportingEnabled());

  // Cache cleared toast
  const [toastMessage, setToastMessage] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const showToast = (msg: string) => {
    setToastMessage(msg);
    setTimeout(() => setToastMessage(null), 3000);
  };

  const handleExportBackup = async () => {
    setExporting(true);
    try {
      const blob = await exportBackup();
      downloadBackupBlob(blob);
      showToast(t('backup.exportSuccess'));
    } catch (err) {
      console.error('Export failed:', err);
    } finally {
      setExporting(false);
    }
  };

  const handleImportBackup = async (e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {
    const file = e.target.files?.[0];
    if (!file) return;
    setImporting(true);
    try {
      const res = await importBackup(file);
      showToast(
        `${t('backup.importSuccess')} (${res.importedBooks} books, ${res.importedAnnotations} annotations)`,
      );
    } catch (err) {
      console.error('Import failed:', err);
      showToast(t('backup.importError'));
    } finally {
      setImporting(false);
    }
  };

  const clearMangaCache = async () => {
    await db.manga_pages.clear();
    showToast('Manga offline page cache cleared');
  };

  const navItems: { id: SettingsTab; label: string; icon: React.ReactNode }[] = [
    { id: 'appearance', label: t('settings.appearance'), icon: <Palette className="w-4 h-4" /> },
    { id: 'reading', label: t('settings.reading'), icon: <BookOpen className="w-4 h-4" /> },
    { id: 'manga', label: t('settings.manga'), icon: <Layers className="w-4 h-4" /> },
    { id: 'library', label: t('settings.library'), icon: <LibraryIcon className="w-4 h-4" /> },
    { id: 'account', label: t('settings.account'), icon: <User className="w-4 h-4" /> },
    { id: 'storage', label: t('settings.storage'), icon: <HardDrive className="w-4 h-4" /> },
    { id: 'backup', label: t('settings.backup'), icon: <Archive className="w-4 h-4" /> },
    { id: 'about', label: t('settings.about'), icon: <Info className="w-4 h-4" /> },
  ];

  return (
    <div className="w-full h-full flex flex-col md:flex-row overflow-hidden bg-[var(--surface-base)] text-[var(--text-primary)]">
      {/* Toast Alert */}
      {toastMessage && (
        <div className="fixed top-4 right-4 z-50 px-4 py-2.5 rounded-[var(--r-md)] bg-[var(--surface-overlay)] border border-[var(--border-subtle)] text-[var(--accent)] font-semibold shadow-lg animate-in fade-in-0 duration-200">
          {toastMessage}
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Side Navigation Rail */}
      <div
        className={`${mobileDetail ? 'hidden md:flex' : 'flex'} w-full md:w-60 flex-1 md:flex-none md:border-r border-[var(--border-subtle)] p-3 md:p-4 flex-col gap-1 overflow-y-auto shrink-0 bg-[var(--surface-subtle)]`}
      >
        <h1 className="text-xl md:text-lg font-bold px-3 py-2 text-[var(--text-primary)]">
          {t('settings.title')}
        </h1>

        {navItems.map((item) => (
          <button
            key={item.id}
            type="button"
            onClick={() => {
              setActiveTab(item.id);
              setMobileDetail(true);
            }}
            className={`flex items-center gap-3 md:gap-2.5 px-3 py-3 md:py-2 rounded-[var(--r-md)] text-base md:text-sm font-medium transition-all shrink-0 cursor-pointer outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-[var(--accent)] ${
              activeTab === item.id
                ? 'md:bg-[var(--accent-subtle)] md:text-[var(--accent)] md:font-semibold md:shadow-sm text-[var(--text-primary)]'
                : 'text-[var(--text-secondary)] hover:text-[var(--text-primary)] hover:bg-[var(--surface-overlay)]'
            }`}
          >
            {item.icon}
            <span className="flex-1 text-left">{item.label}</span>
            <ChevronRight className="w-4 h-4 text-[var(--text-muted)] md:hidden" />
          </button>
        ))}
      </div>

      {/* Content Area */}
      <div className={`${mobileDetail ? 'block' : 'hidden md:block'} flex-1 overflow-y-auto p-4 sm:p-6 md:p-10 max-w-3xl`}>
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={() => setMobileDetail(false)}
          className="md:hidden flex items-center gap-1 -ml-1 mb-4 text-sm font-semibold text-[var(--accent)] cursor-pointer"
        >
          <ChevronLeft className="w-4 h-4" /> {t('settings.title')}
        </button>
        {/* Appearance Tab */}
        {activeTab === 'appearance' && (
          <div className="flex flex-col gap-8 animate-in fade-in-50 duration-150">
            <div>
              <h2 className="text-xl font-bold">{t('settings.appearance')}</h2>
              <p className="text-sm text-[var(--text-muted)]">
                Customize colors, backdrop, and visual density.
              </p>
            </div>

            {/* Theme */}
            <div className="flex flex-col gap-3">
              <label className="text-sm font-semibold">{t('settings.theme')}</label>
              <div className="grid grid-cols-3 gap-3">
                {(['system', 'light', 'dark'] as const).map((tMode) => (
                  <button
                    key={tMode}
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => updateSettings({ theme: tMode })}
                    className={`p-3 rounded-[var(--r-md)] border text-sm font-medium capitalize text-center transition-all cursor-pointer ${
                      settings.theme === tMode
                        ? 'border-[var(--accent)] bg-[var(--accent-subtle)] text-[var(--accent)] font-semibold'
                        : 'border-[var(--border-subtle)] hover:bg-[var(--surface-subtle)] text-[var(--text-secondary)]'
                    }`}
                  >
                    {tMode}
                  </button>
                ))}
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* Accent Palette */}
            <div className="flex flex-col gap-3">
              <label className="text-sm font-semibold">{t('settings.accentColor')}</label>
              <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-3">
                <button
                  type="button"
                  title="Dynamic"
                  onClick={() => updateSettings({ accent: 'dynamic' })}
                  className={`px-3 py-1.5 rounded-[var(--r-md)] border text-xs font-semibold cursor-pointer transition-all ${
                    settings.accent === 'dynamic'
                      ? 'border-[var(--accent)] bg-[var(--accent-subtle)] text-[var(--accent)]'
                      : 'border-[var(--border-subtle)] text-[var(--text-secondary)] hover:bg-[var(--surface-subtle)]'
                  }`}
                >
                  Dynamic
                </button>
                {Object.entries(ACCENT_PRESETS).map(([id, color]) => (
                  <button
                    key={id}
                    type="button"
                    title={id}
                    onClick={() => updateSettings({ accent: id })}
                    className={`w-10 h-10 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center transition-transform hover:scale-110 cursor-pointer shadow-sm ${
                      settings.accent === id ? 'ring-2 ring-offset-2 ring-[var(--accent)] scale-105' : ''
                    }`}
                    style={{ backgroundColor: color }}
                  >
                    {settings.accent === id && <Check className="w-5 h-5 text-white" />}
                  </button>
                ))}
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* Ambient Cover Backdrop */}
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between p-4 rounded-[var(--r-md)] bg-[var(--surface-subtle)] border border-[var(--border-subtle)]">
              <div>
                <h4 className="text-sm font-semibold">{t('settings.ambientBackdrop')}</h4>
                <p className="text-xs text-[var(--text-muted)]">
                  Glow subtle book cover colors in backgrounds and navigation.
                </p>
              </div>
              <input
                type="checkbox"
                checked={settings.ambientEnabled}
                onChange={(e) => updateSettings({ ambientEnabled: e.target.checked })}
                className="w-5 h-5 rounded cursor-pointer accent-[var(--accent)]"
              />
            </div>

            {/* App Language */}
            <div className="flex flex-col gap-3">
              <label className="text-sm font-semibold">{t('settings.language')}</label>
              <select
                onChange={(e) => void setAppLanguage(e.target.value)}
                defaultValue="en"
                className="w-full max-w-xs p-2.5 rounded-[var(--r-md)] bg-[var(--surface-subtle)] border border-[var(--border-subtle)] text-sm"
              >
                <option value="en">English (LTR)</option>
                <option value="ar">العربية (Arabic - RTL)</option>
              </select>
            </div>
          </div>
        )}

        {/* Reading Tab */}
        {activeTab === 'reading' && (
          <div className="flex flex-col gap-8 animate-in fade-in-50 duration-150">
            <div>
              <h2 className="text-xl font-bold">{t('settings.reading')}</h2>
              <p className="text-sm text-[var(--text-muted)]">
                Default layout, typography, controls, and accessibility options.
              </p>
            </div>

            {/* Keep Screen Awake */}
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between p-4 rounded-[var(--r-md)] bg-[var(--surface-subtle)] border border-[var(--border-subtle)]">
              <div>
                <h4 className="text-sm font-semibold">{t('settings.keepScreenOn')}</h4>
                <p className="text-xs text-[var(--text-muted)]">
                  Prevents the device display from sleeping during reading sessions.
                </p>
              </div>
              <input
                type="checkbox"
                checked={readerSettings.keepScreenOn ?? true}
                onChange={(e) => updateReaderSettings({ keepScreenOn: e.target.checked })}
                className="w-5 h-5 rounded cursor-pointer accent-[var(--accent)]"
              />
            </div>

            {/* Volume Key Navigation (phones only: desktops have no volume rocker) */}
            {isAndroidDevice() && (
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between p-4 rounded-[var(--r-md)] bg-[var(--surface-subtle)] border border-[var(--border-subtle)]">
              <div>
                <h4 className="text-sm font-semibold">{t('settings.volumeKeys')}</h4>
                <p className="text-xs text-[var(--text-muted)]">
                  Use hardware Volume Up / Down buttons to turn pages.
                </p>
              </div>
              <input
                type="checkbox"
                checked={readerSettings.volumeKeysTurn ?? false}
                onChange={(e) => updateReaderSettings({ volumeKeysTurn: e.target.checked })}
                className="w-5 h-5 rounded cursor-pointer accent-[var(--accent)]"
              />
            </div>
            )}

            {/* Haptic Feedback */}
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between p-4 rounded-[var(--r-md)] bg-[var(--surface-subtle)] border border-[var(--border-subtle)]">
              <div>
                <h4 className="text-sm font-semibold">{t('settings.haptics')}</h4>
                <p className="text-xs text-[var(--text-muted)]">
                  Subtle vibration feedback on page turns and tap zones.
                </p>
              </div>
              <input
                type="checkbox"
                checked={readerSettings.haptics ?? true}
                onChange={(e) => updateReaderSettings({ haptics: e.target.checked })}
                className="w-5 h-5 rounded cursor-pointer accent-[var(--accent)]"
              />
            </div>
          </div>
        )}

        {/* Library Tab */}
        {activeTab === 'library' && <LibraryPrefs />}

        {/* Account & Sync Tab (the tab existed but nothing was rendered for it) */}
        {activeTab === 'account' && (
          <div className="flex flex-col gap-6 animate-in fade-in-50 duration-150">
            <div>
              <h2 className="text-xl font-bold">{t('settings.account')}</h2>
              <p className="text-sm text-[var(--text-muted)]">
                Sign in to sync your library, progress and highlights across devices.
              </p>
            </div>
            <AccountSettings />
          </div>
        )}

        {/* Manga Tab */}
        {activeTab === 'manga' && (
          <div className="flex flex-col gap-8 animate-in fade-in-50 duration-150">
            <div>
              <h2 className="text-xl font-bold">{t('settings.manga')}</h2>
              <p className="text-sm text-[var(--text-muted)]">
                MangaDex settings, content filtering, and reading modes.
              </p>
            </div>

            {/* Content Ratings */}
            <div className="flex flex-col gap-3">
              <label className="text-sm font-semibold">{t('settings.contentRating')}</label>
              <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-3">
                <div className="flex items-center justify-between p-3 rounded-[var(--r-md)] bg-[var(--surface-subtle)] border border-[var(--border-subtle)]">
                  <span className="text-sm">{t('settings.safe')}</span>
                  <input type="checkbox" defaultChecked disabled className="w-4 h-4 accent-[var(--accent)]" />
                </div>
                <div className="flex items-center justify-between p-3 rounded-[var(--r-md)] bg-[var(--surface-subtle)] border border-[var(--border-subtle)]">
                  <span className="text-sm">{t('settings.suggestive')}</span>
                  <input
                    type="checkbox"
                    aria-label={t('settings.suggestive')}
                    checked={ratings.includes('suggestive')}
                    onChange={(e) => toggleRating('suggestive', e.target.checked)}
                    className="w-4 h-4 accent-[var(--accent)] cursor-pointer"
                  />
                </div>
                <div className="flex items-center justify-between p-3 rounded-[var(--r-md)] bg-[var(--surface-subtle)] border border-[var(--border-subtle)]">
                  <span className="text-sm text-[var(--warning)] font-medium">{t('settings.erotica')}</span>
                  {ratings.includes('erotica') ? (
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => toggleRating('erotica', false)}
                      className="shrink-0 text-xs font-semibold px-2.5 py-1 rounded bg-[var(--accent-soft)] text-[var(--accent)] border border-[var(--accent)] cursor-pointer"
                    >
                      On · Turn off
                    </button>
                  ) : (
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => {
                        setPendingAdultKey('erotica');
                        setAdultConfirmOpen(true);
                      }}
                      className="shrink-0 text-xs font-semibold px-2.5 py-1 rounded bg-[var(--surface-overlay)] border border-[var(--border-subtle)] hover:bg-[var(--surface-base)] cursor-pointer"
                    >
                      Opt-in (18+)
                    </button>
                  )}
                </div>
                <div className="flex items-center justify-between p-3 rounded-[var(--r-md)] bg-[var(--surface-subtle)] border border-[var(--border-subtle)]">
                  <span className="text-sm text-[var(--danger)] font-medium">{t('settings.pornographic')}</span>
                  {ratings.includes('pornographic') ? (
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => toggleRating('pornographic', false)}
                      className="shrink-0 text-xs font-semibold px-2.5 py-1 rounded bg-[var(--accent-soft)] text-[var(--accent)] border border-[var(--accent)] cursor-pointer"
                    >
                      On · Turn off
                    </button>
                  ) : (
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => {
                        setPendingAdultKey('pornographic');
                        setAdultConfirmOpen(true);
                      }}
                      className="shrink-0 text-xs font-semibold px-2.5 py-1 rounded bg-[var(--surface-overlay)] border border-[var(--border-subtle)] hover:bg-[var(--surface-base)] cursor-pointer"
                    >
                      Opt-in (18+)
                    </button>
                  )}
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* Clear manga reading history */}
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-4 p-4 rounded-[var(--r-md)] bg-[var(--surface-subtle)] border border-[var(--border-subtle)]">
              <div className="min-w-0">
                <h4 className="text-sm font-semibold">Manga reading history</h4>
                <p className="text-xs text-[var(--text-muted)]">
                  Removes series you only read from history. Series in your library stay.
                </p>
              </div>
              <Button
                variant="standard"
                size="sm"
                className="shrink-0"
                icon={<Trash2 className="w-4 h-4 text-[var(--danger)]" />}
                onClick={async () => {
                  const n = await clearMangaHistory();
                  showToast(n ? `Cleared ${n} series from history` : 'History was already empty');
                }}
              >
                Clear
              </Button>
            </div>

            {/* Clear Manga Cache */}
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-4 p-4 rounded-[var(--r-md)] bg-[var(--surface-subtle)] border border-[var(--border-subtle)]">
              <div className="min-w-0">
                <h4 className="text-sm font-semibold">{t('settings.clearMangaCache')}</h4>
                <p className="text-xs text-[var(--text-muted)]">
                  {mangaPagesCount} pages cached offline.
                </p>
              </div>
              <Button
                variant="standard"
                size="sm"
                className="shrink-0"
                icon={<Trash2 className="w-4 h-4 text-[var(--danger)]" />}
                onClick={clearMangaCache}
              >
                Clear
              </Button>
            </div>
          </div>
        )}

        {/* Storage Tab */}
        {activeTab === 'storage' && (
          <div className="flex flex-col gap-8 animate-in fade-in-50 duration-150">
            <div>
              <h2 className="text-xl font-bold">{t('settings.storage')}</h2>
              <p className="text-sm text-[var(--text-muted)]">
                Local disk usage and cache management.
              </p>
            </div>

            <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-3 gap-4">
              <div className="p-4 rounded-[var(--r-lg)] bg-[var(--surface-subtle)] border border-[var(--border-subtle)]">
                <span className="text-xs font-semibold uppercase text-[var(--text-muted)]">
                  {t('settings.booksStorage')}
                </span>
                <h3 className="text-2xl font-bold mt-1 text-[var(--accent)]">{totalBookMB} MB</h3>
                <p className="text-xs text-[var(--text-muted)] mt-1">{books.length} items</p>
              </div>
              <div className="p-4 rounded-[var(--r-lg)] bg-[var(--surface-subtle)] border border-[var(--border-subtle)]">
                <span className="text-xs font-semibold uppercase text-[var(--text-muted)]">
                  {t('settings.coversStorage')}
                </span>
                <h3 className="text-2xl font-bold mt-1">{coversCount} covers</h3>
                <p className="text-xs text-[var(--text-muted)] mt-1">IndexedDB / FS</p>
              </div>
              <div className="p-4 rounded-[var(--r-lg)] bg-[var(--surface-subtle)] border border-[var(--border-subtle)]">
                <span className="text-xs font-semibold uppercase text-[var(--text-muted)]">
                  {t('settings.mangaStorage')}
                </span>
                <h3 className="text-2xl font-bold mt-1">{mangaPagesCount} pages</h3>
                <p className="text-xs text-[var(--text-muted)] mt-1">Offline cache</p>
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* App cache: catalog lists, series details, chapter lists, image cache.
                Your books, downloads, progress and settings are not touched. */}
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-4 p-4 rounded-[var(--r-md)] bg-[var(--surface-subtle)] border border-[var(--border-subtle)]">
              <div className="min-w-0">
                <h4 className="text-sm font-semibold">Clear app cache</h4>
                <p className="text-xs text-[var(--text-muted)]">
                  Cached store listings, manga details and chapter lists. Books, downloads, reading progress and settings are kept.
                </p>
              </div>
              <Button
                variant="standard"
                size="sm"
                className="shrink-0"
                icon={<Trash2 className="w-4 h-4 text-[var(--danger)]" />}
                onClick={async () => {
                  const n = await clearApiCache();
                  try {
                    for (const key of await caches.keys()) await caches.delete(key);
                  } catch {
                    // Cache Storage unavailable (some WebViews): nothing else to clear.
                  }
                  showToast(`Cleared ${n} cached responses`);
                }}
              >
                Clear cache
              </Button>
            </div>
          </div>
        )}

        {/* Backup Tab */}
        {activeTab === 'backup' && (
          <div className="flex flex-col gap-8 animate-in fade-in-50 duration-150">
            <div>
              <h2 className="text-xl font-bold">{t('settings.backup')}</h2>
              <p className="text-sm text-[var(--text-muted)]">
                Create full offline archives of your books, progress, highlights, and settings.
              </p>
            </div>

            <div className="flex flex-col gap-4 p-5 rounded-[var(--r-lg)] bg-[var(--surface-subtle)] border border-[var(--border-subtle)]">
              <div>
                <h3 className="text-base font-semibold">{t('settings.exportBackup')}</h3>
                <p className="text-xs text-[var(--text-muted)] mt-0.5">
                  Produces a standalone `.foliobackup` archive containing all tables and book files.
                </p>
              </div>
              <div>
                <Button variant="accent" onClick={handleExportBackup} disabled={exporting}>
                  <Download className="w-4 h-4 mr-1.5" />
                  {exporting ? 'Exporting…' : 'Export Full Backup'}
                </Button>
              </div>
            </div>

            <div className="flex flex-col gap-4 p-5 rounded-[var(--r-lg)] bg-[var(--surface-subtle)] border border-[var(--border-subtle)]">
              <div>
                <h3 className="text-base font-semibold">{t('settings.restoreBackup')}</h3>
                <p className="text-xs text-[var(--text-muted)] mt-0.5">
                  Merge a `.versobackup` or `.foliobackup` archive into Verso using Last-Write-Wins rules.
                </p>
              </div>
              <div>
                <label className="cursor-pointer">
                  <input
                    type="file"
                    accept=".versobackup,.foliobackup,.zip"
                    onChange={handleImportBackup}
                    disabled={importing}
                    className="hidden"
                  />
                  <span className="inline-flex items-center text-sm font-semibold px-4 py-2 rounded-[var(--r-md)] bg-[var(--surface-overlay)] hover:bg-[var(--surface-base)] border border-[var(--border-subtle)] text-[var(--accent)] transition-colors">
                    <Upload className="w-4 h-4 mr-1.5" />
                    {importing ? 'Restoring…' : 'Select Backup File'}
                  </span>
                </label>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        )}

        {/* About Tab */}
        {activeTab === 'about' && (
          <div className="flex flex-col gap-8 animate-in fade-in-50 duration-150">
            <div>
              <h2 className="text-xl font-bold">Verso</h2>
              <p className="text-sm text-[var(--text-muted)]">
                Version 0.1.0 · Designed for distraction-free reading.
              </p>
            </div>

            <div className="flex flex-col gap-3 p-4 rounded-[var(--r-lg)] bg-[var(--surface-subtle)] border border-[var(--border-subtle)] text-xs text-[var(--text-secondary)] leading-relaxed">
              <h4 className="font-semibold text-sm text-[var(--text-primary)]">
                {t('settings.licenses')}
              </h4>
              <p>• Foliate-js — MIT License (John Factotum)</p>
              <p>• Atkinson Hyperlegible, Literata, Inter, Source Serif 4 — Open Font Licenses</p>
              <p>• MangaDex API — Powered by MangaDex.org. Follows official Acceptable Use Policy.</p>
            </div>

            {/* Privacy & Diagnostics */}
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between p-4 rounded-[var(--r-md)] bg-[var(--surface-subtle)] border border-[var(--border-subtle)]">
              <div>
                <h4 className="text-sm font-semibold">Anonymous Error Reporting</h4>
                <p className="text-xs text-[var(--text-muted)]">
                  Help improve Verso by sending anonymous crash logs. Off by default; no personal data or book text is ever sent.
                </p>
              </div>
              <input
                type="checkbox"
                checked={crashReporting}
                onChange={(e) => {
                  setCrashReportingEnabled(e.target.checked);
                  setCrashReporting(e.target.checked);
                  showToast(e.target.checked ? 'Crash reporting enabled' : 'Crash reporting disabled');
                }}
                className="w-5 h-5 rounded cursor-pointer accent-[var(--accent)]"
              />
            </div>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>

      {/* 18+ Confirmation Dialog */}
      <Dialog
        open={adultConfirmOpen}
        onOpenChange={setAdultConfirmOpen}
        title="Confirm Age (18+)"
      >
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-4 text-sm">
          <p className="text-[var(--text-secondary)]">
            You are requesting access to adult-rated content ({pendingAdultKey}). Please confirm that
            you are at least 18 years old.
          </p>
          <div className="flex justify-end gap-2 pt-2">
            <Button variant="standard" onClick={() => setAdultConfirmOpen(false)}>
              Cancel
            </Button>
            <Button
              variant="accent"
              onClick={() => {
                setAdultConfirmOpen(false);
                if (pendingAdultKey) toggleRating(pendingAdultKey, true);
                showToast(`Opted into ${pendingAdultKey} ratings`);
              }}
            >
              Confirm 18+
            </Button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </Dialog>
    </div>
  );
}

// Defaults the Library page reads on open (it also remembers the last choice).
const LAYOUTS = [
  { value: 'grid', label: 'Grid' },
  { value: 'list', label: 'List' },
] as const;
const SORTS = [
  { value: 'recent', label: 'Recently read' },
  { value: 'added', label: 'Recently added' },
  { value: 'title', label: 'Title' },
  { value: 'author', label: 'Author' },
  { value: 'progress', label: 'Progress' },
  { value: 'series', label: 'Series' },
] as const;

function readPref(key: string, fallback: string): string {
  try {
    return localStorage.getItem(key) || fallback;
  } catch {
    return fallback;
  }
}

function LibraryPrefs() {
  const [layout, setLayout] = useState(() => readPref('folio_lib_layout', 'grid'));
  const [sort, setSort] = useState(() => readPref('folio_lib_sort', 'recent'));
  const save = (key: string, value: string, set: (v: string) => void) => {
    set(value);
    try {
      localStorage.setItem(key, value);
    } catch {
      // Storage unavailable: the choice still applies for this session.
    }
  };
  const chip = (active: boolean) =>
    `px-3 py-2 rounded-[var(--r-md)] border text-sm cursor-pointer transition-colors ${
      active
        ? 'border-[var(--accent)] bg-[var(--accent-soft)] text-[var(--accent)] font-semibold'
        : 'border-[var(--stroke)] bg-[var(--layer-2)] text-[var(--text-2)] hover:text-[var(--text-1)]'
    }`;

  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col gap-8 animate-in fade-in-50 duration-150">
      <div>
        <h2 className="text-xl font-bold">{t('settings.library')}</h2>
        <p className="text-sm text-[var(--text-muted)]">How your library is shown when you open it.</p>
      </div>
      <div className="flex flex-col gap-3">
        <span className="text-sm font-semibold">Layout</span>
        <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2" role="radiogroup" aria-label="Library layout">
          {LAYOUTS.map((o) => (
            <button
              key={o.value}
              type="button"
              role="radio"
              aria-checked={layout === o.value}
              className={chip(layout === o.value)}
              onClick={() => save('folio_lib_layout', o.value, setLayout)}
            >
              {o.label}
            </button>
          ))}
        </div>
      </div>
      <div className="flex flex-col gap-3">
        <span className="text-sm font-semibold">Sort by</span>
        <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2" role="radiogroup" aria-label="Library sort order">
          {SORTS.map((o) => (
            <button
              key={o.value}
              type="button"
              role="radio"
              aria-checked={sort === o.value}
              className={chip(sort === o.value)}
              onClick={() => save('folio_lib_sort', o.value, setSort)}
            >
              {o.label}
            </button>
          ))}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

================
File: apps/client/src/sources/suwayomi.ts
================
import { net } from '../lib/net';
import { cachedJson } from '../lib/apiCache';
import { db } from '../db';
import type {
  CatalogEntry,
  CatalogFilter,
  CatalogPage,
  CatalogSection,
  ChapterInfo,
  MangaSource,
  PageRef,
} from './types';

export interface SuwayomiExtension {
  name: string;
  pkgName: string;
  versionName: string;
  versionCode?: number;
  lang: string;
  isNsfw: boolean;
  installed: boolean;
  hasUpdate: boolean;
  iconUrl?: string;
}

interface SuwayomiSourceInfo {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  lang: string;
  iconUrl?: string;
  supportsLatest?: boolean;
}

interface SuwayomiMangaItem {
  id: number;
  sourceId: string;
  title: string;
  thumbnailUrl?: string;
  artist?: string;
  author?: string;
  description?: string;
  genre?: string[];
  status?: string;
  inLibrary?: boolean;
}

interface SuwayomiMangaListResponse {
  mangaList: SuwayomiMangaItem[];
  hasNextPage: boolean;
}

interface SuwayomiChapterItem {
  id: number;
  name: string;
  chapterNumber?: number;
  uploadDate?: number;
  scanlator?: string;
  pageCount?: number;
}

export class SuwayomiSource implements MangaSource {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  icon = 'BookOpen';
  kind = 'manga' as const;
  baseUrl: string;
  apiKey?: string;

  filters: CatalogFilter[] = [
    {
      id: 'feed',
      label: 'Feed',
      options: [
        { value: '', label: '🔥 Popular', hint: 'Browse most popular titles' },
        { value: 'latest', label: '⏱️ Latest Updates', hint: 'Browse recently updated chapters' },
      ],
    },
    {
      id: 'lang',
      label: 'Language',
      options: [
        { value: '', label: '🌐 All (EN & Multi)', hint: 'All English & multi-language sources' },
        { value: 'en', label: '🇬🇧 English (EN)', hint: 'Show only English sources' },
        { value: 'all', label: '🌍 Multi (ALL)', hint: 'Show only multi-language sources' },
      ],
    },
  ];

  constructor(
    id = 'suwayomi',
    name = 'Suwayomi (Tachiyomi)',
    baseUrl = 'http://localhost:4567',
    apiKey?: string,
  ) {
    this.id = id;
    this.name = name;
    // Strip trailing slash
    this.baseUrl = baseUrl.replace(/\/+$/, '');
    this.apiKey = apiKey;
  }

  private getHeaders(): Record<string, string> {
    const headers: Record<string, string> = {};
    if (this.apiKey) {
      headers['Authorization'] = `Bearer ${this.apiKey}`;
    }
    return headers;
  }

  private resolveUrl(path: string): string {
    if (!path) return '';
    if (path.startsWith('http://') || path.startsWith('https://')) return path;
    const cleanPath = path.startsWith('/') ? path : `/${path}`;
    return `${this.baseUrl}${cleanPath}`;
  }

  private getFetch(): (input: string | URL, init?: RequestInit) => Promise<Response> {
    return typeof window !== 'undefined' && typeof window.fetch === 'function'
      ? (input, init) => window.fetch(input, init)
      : (input, init) => net.fetch(input, init);
  }

  async getInstalledExtensions(forceRefresh = false): Promise<SuwayomiSourceInfo[]> {
    const cacheKey = `suwayomi:${this.id}:extensions`;
    if (forceRefresh) {
      await db.kv.delete(`cache:${cacheKey}`).catch(() => {});
    }
    const url = `${this.baseUrl}/api/v1/source/list`;
    return cachedJson<SuwayomiSourceInfo[]>(
      cacheKey,
      async () => {
        try {
          const fetchFn = this.getFetch();
          const res = await fetchFn(url, { headers: this.getHeaders() });
          if (!res.ok) return [];
          const list = (await res.json()) as SuwayomiSourceInfo[];
          return Array.isArray(list) ? list : [];
        } catch {
          return [];
        }
      },
      { freshMs: forceRefresh ? 0 : 10 * 60_000 },
    );
  }

  /** Fetch all extensions in the Suwayomi extension store (Keiyoushi repo & installed) */
  async getExtensionList(): Promise<SuwayomiExtension[]> {
    const url = `${this.baseUrl}/api/graphql`;
    try {
      const fetchImpl = typeof window !== 'undefined' && typeof window.fetch === 'function' ? window.fetch.bind(window) : net.fetch;
      const res = await fetchImpl(url, {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: {
          'Content-Type': 'application/json',
          ...this.getHeaders(),
        },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          query: `
            query GetExtensions {
              extensions {
                nodes {
                  name
                  pkgName
                  lang
                  isInstalled
                  hasUpdate
                  iconUrl
                  apkName
                }
              }
            }
          `,
        }),
      });

      if (res.ok) {
        const data = (await res.json()) as {
          data?: {
            extensions?: {
              nodes?: Array<{
                name: string;
                pkgName: string;
                lang: string;
                isInstalled: boolean;
                hasUpdate: boolean;
                iconUrl?: string;
                apkName?: string;
              }>;
            };
          };
        };

        const rawNodes = data.data?.extensions?.nodes;
        if (Array.isArray(rawNodes) && rawNodes.length > 0) {
          const nodes = rawNodes.filter(
            (node) =>
              !node.pkgName.endsWith('.keiyoushi') &&
              !node.pkgName.endsWith('.mihon') &&
              !node.name.toLowerCase().includes('outdated app') &&
              !node.name.toLowerCase().includes('update to mihon') &&
              (node.isInstalled || node.lang === 'en' || node.lang === 'all'),
          );
          return nodes.map((node) => {
            const verMatch = node.apkName?.match(/-v?([0-9.]+)\.apk$/i);
            const versionName = verMatch ? verMatch[1] : '1.0';
            return {
              name: node.name,
              pkgName: node.pkgName,
              versionName,
              lang: node.lang || 'all',
              isNsfw: false,
              installed: Boolean(node.isInstalled),
              hasUpdate: Boolean(node.hasUpdate),
              iconUrl: node.iconUrl ? this.resolveUrl(node.iconUrl) : undefined,
            };
          });
        }
      }
    } catch (err) {
      console.warn('[Suwayomi] GraphQL GetExtensions failed, trying fallback:', err);
    }

    // Fallback: If GraphQL returned no extensions or errored, list installed sources from Suwayomi REST API
    try {
      const installedSources = await this.getInstalledExtensions();
      if (installedSources.length > 0) {
        return installedSources
          .filter((s) => s.id !== '0')
          .map((s) => ({
            name: s.name,
            pkgName: `source:${s.id}`,
            versionName: '1.0',
            lang: s.lang || 'all',
            isNsfw: false,
            installed: true,
            hasUpdate: false,
            iconUrl: s.iconUrl ? this.resolveUrl(s.iconUrl) : undefined,
          }));
      }
    } catch {
      // Ignore
    }

    return [];
  }

  /** Install an extension from the Suwayomi extension store */
  async installExtension(pkgName: string): Promise<boolean> {
    const url = `${this.baseUrl}/api/graphql`;
    try {
      const fetchImpl = typeof window !== 'undefined' && typeof window.fetch === 'function' ? window.fetch.bind(window) : net.fetch;
      const res = await fetchImpl(url, {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: {
          'Content-Type': 'application/json',
          ...this.getHeaders(),
        },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          query: `
            mutation InstallExt($input: UpdateExtensionInput!) {
              updateExtension(input: $input) {
                clientMutationId
              }
            }
          `,
          variables: {
            input: {
              id: pkgName,
              patch: { install: true },
            },
          },
        }),
      });
      if (!res.ok) return false;
      const json = (await res.json()) as { errors?: unknown[] };
      return !json.errors || json.errors.length === 0;
    } catch {
      return false;
    }
  }

  /** Uninstall an extension */
  async uninstallExtension(pkgName: string): Promise<boolean> {
    const url = `${this.baseUrl}/api/graphql`;
    try {
      const fetchImpl = typeof window !== 'undefined' && typeof window.fetch === 'function' ? window.fetch.bind(window) : net.fetch;
      const res = await fetchImpl(url, {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: {
          'Content-Type': 'application/json',
          ...this.getHeaders(),
        },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          query: `
            mutation UninstallExt($input: UpdateExtensionInput!) {
              updateExtension(input: $input) {
                clientMutationId
              }
            }
          `,
          variables: {
            input: {
              id: pkgName,
              patch: { uninstall: true },
            },
          },
        }),
      });
      if (!res.ok) return false;
      const json = (await res.json()) as { errors?: unknown[] };
      return !json.errors || json.errors.length === 0;
    } catch {
      return false;
    }
  }

  private lastBrowsedSourceId = '';

  private sortSources(extensions: SuwayomiSourceInfo[]): SuwayomiSourceInfo[] {
    // Only keep English ('en'), multi-language ('all'), and local on-disk source ('0')
    const realSources = extensions.filter(
      (ext) => ext.id !== '0' && (ext.lang === 'en' || ext.lang === 'all'),
    );

    // Prioritize prominent sources like MangaDex to the top of English sources if present
    const dexSources = realSources.filter((ext) => /mangadex/i.test(ext.name));
    const otherFavSources = realSources.filter(
      (ext) => /kodansha|globalcomix/i.test(ext.name) && !/mangadex/i.test(ext.name),
    );
    const regularEn = realSources.filter(
      (ext) => !/mangadex|kodansha|globalcomix/i.test(ext.name),
    );

    const localSources = extensions.filter((ext) => ext.id === '0');
    return [...dexSources, ...otherFavSources, ...regularEn, ...localSources];
  }

  async sections(forceRefresh = false): Promise<CatalogSection[]> {
    let extensions = await this.getInstalledExtensions(forceRefresh);
    if (extensions.length === 0 && !forceRefresh) {
      extensions = await this.getInstalledExtensions(true);
    }
    if (extensions.length === 0) {
      return [
        { id: 'all', title: 'Tachiyomi Extensions' },
      ];
    }

    const sorted = this.sortSources(extensions);

    return sorted.map((ext) => ({
      id: ext.id,
      title:
        ext.id === '0'
          ? 'Local source (on-disk)'
          : `${ext.name} (${ext.lang.toUpperCase()})`,
    }));
  }

  async browse(sectionId: string, cursor?: string, filters?: Record<string, string>): Promise<CatalogPage> {
    let extensions = await this.getInstalledExtensions();
    if (extensions.length === 0) {
      extensions = await this.getInstalledExtensions(true);
    }
    const sorted = this.sortSources(extensions);
    const defaultSourceId = sorted.length > 0 ? sorted[0].id : extensions[0]?.id || '';
    const sourceId =
      sectionId && sectionId !== 'all'
        ? sectionId
        : defaultSourceId;

    if (!sourceId) {
      return {
        items: [],
        next: undefined,
      };
    }
    this.lastBrowsedSourceId = sourceId;

    const page = cursor ? parseInt(cursor, 10) : 1;
    const isLatest = filters?.feed === 'latest';
    const primaryEndpoint = isLatest ? 'latest' : 'popular';
    const fallbackEndpoint = isLatest ? 'popular' : 'latest';
    const url = `${this.baseUrl}/api/v1/source/${sourceId}/${primaryEndpoint}/${page}`;

    let res = await this.getFetch()(url, { headers: this.getHeaders() });
    const contentType = res.headers.get('content-type') || '';
    if (!res.ok || contentType.includes('text/html')) {
      // Fallback to alternative endpoint if primary endpoint fails
      const fallbackUrl = `${this.baseUrl}/api/v1/source/${sourceId}/${fallbackEndpoint}/${page}`;
      const fallbackRes = await this.getFetch()(fallbackUrl, { headers: this.getHeaders() });
      const fallbackContentType = fallbackRes.headers.get('content-type') || '';
      if (fallbackRes.ok && !fallbackContentType.includes('text/html')) {
        res = fallbackRes;
      } else {
        let detail = res.statusText;
        try {
          const errText = await res.text();
          if (errText) detail = errText.slice(0, 120);
        } catch {
          // Body unreadable: keep the status text as the detail.
        }
        throw new Error(`Failed to load Suwayomi source: ${detail || res.statusText || 'Request failed'}`);
      }
    }

    const data = (await res.json()) as SuwayomiMangaListResponse;
    const items: CatalogEntry[] = (data.mangaList || []).map((m) => {
      const coverUrl = m.thumbnailUrl ? this.resolveUrl(m.thumbnailUrl) : undefined;
      const authors = m.author ? [m.author] : m.artist ? [m.artist] : ['Tachiyomi Creator'];

      return {
        id: `suwayomi:${this.id}:${sourceId}:${m.id}`,
        title: m.title,
        authors,
        cover: coverUrl,
        summary: m.description,
        tags: m.genre?.slice(0, 5),
        kind: 'manga',
      };
    });

    return {
      items,
      next: data.hasNextPage ? String(page + 1) : undefined,
    };
  }

  async search(query: string, cursor?: string, filters?: Record<string, string>): Promise<CatalogPage> {
    let extensions = await this.getInstalledExtensions();
    if (extensions.length === 0) {
      extensions = await this.getInstalledExtensions(true);
    }
    const sorted = this.sortSources(extensions);
    const sourceId =
      filters?.source ||
      this.lastBrowsedSourceId ||
      (sorted.length > 0 ? sorted[0].id : extensions[0]?.id || '');

    if (!sourceId) {
      return { items: [] };
    }

    const page = cursor ? parseInt(cursor, 10) : 1;
    const url = `${this.baseUrl}/api/v1/source/${sourceId}/search?query=${encodeURIComponent(query.trim())}&page=${page}`;

    const res = await this.getFetch()(url, { headers: this.getHeaders() });
    if (!res.ok) {
      throw new Error(`Failed to search Suwayomi: ${res.statusText}`);
    }

    const data = (await res.json()) as SuwayomiMangaListResponse;
    const items: CatalogEntry[] = (data.mangaList || []).map((m) => {
      const coverUrl = m.thumbnailUrl ? this.resolveUrl(m.thumbnailUrl) : undefined;
      const authors = m.author ? [m.author] : m.artist ? [m.artist] : ['Tachiyomi Creator'];

      return {
        id: `suwayomi:${this.id}:${sourceId}:${m.id}`,
        title: m.title,
        authors,
        cover: coverUrl,
        summary: m.description,
        tags: m.genre?.slice(0, 5),
        kind: 'manga',
      };
    });

    return {
      items,
      next: data.hasNextPage ? String(page + 1) : undefined,
    };
  }

  async details(id: string): Promise<CatalogEntry> {
    // ID format: suwayomi:{instanceId}:{sourceId}:{mangaId} or suwayomi:{mangaId}
    const parts = id.split(':');
    const mangaId = parts[parts.length - 1];
    const url = `${this.baseUrl}/api/v1/manga/${mangaId}`;

    const res = await this.getFetch()(url, { headers: this.getHeaders() });
    if (!res.ok) {
      throw new Error(`Failed to fetch Suwayomi manga details: ${res.statusText}`);
    }

    const m = (await res.json()) as SuwayomiMangaItem;
    const coverUrl = m.thumbnailUrl ? this.resolveUrl(m.thumbnailUrl) : undefined;
    const authors = m.author ? [m.author] : m.artist ? [m.artist] : ['Tachiyomi Creator'];

    return {
      id,
      title: m.title,
      authors,
      cover: coverUrl,
      summary: m.description,
      tags: m.genre?.slice(0, 6),
      kind: 'manga',
      status: m.status?.toLowerCase(),
    };
  }

  async chapters(seriesId: string): Promise<ChapterInfo[]> {
    const parts = seriesId.split(':');
    const mangaId = parts[parts.length - 1];
    const url = `${this.baseUrl}/api/v1/manga/${mangaId}/chapters`;

    const res = await this.getFetch()(url, { headers: this.getHeaders() });
    const contentType = res.headers.get('content-type') || '';
    if (!res.ok || contentType.includes('text/html')) {
      throw new Error(`Failed to fetch Suwayomi chapters: ${res.statusText || 'Invalid response'}`);
    }

    const rawChapters = (await res.json()) as SuwayomiChapterItem[];
    if (!Array.isArray(rawChapters)) return [];

    return rawChapters.map((ch) => ({
      id: String(ch.id),
      title: ch.name || (ch.chapterNumber !== undefined ? `Chapter ${ch.chapterNumber}` : 'Chapter'),
      number: ch.chapterNumber,
      scanlationGroup: ch.scanlator || 'Suwayomi',
      publishedAt: ch.uploadDate ? new Date(ch.uploadDate).toISOString() : undefined,
    }));
  }

  async pages(chapterId: string): Promise<PageRef[]> {
    // 1. Primary: Query Suwayomi GraphQL mutation fetchChapterPages
    try {
      const numId = parseInt(chapterId, 10);
      if (!Number.isNaN(numId)) {
        const gqlRes = await this.getFetch()(`${this.baseUrl}/api/graphql`, {
          method: 'POST',
          headers: {
            'Content-Type': 'application/json',
            ...this.getHeaders(),
          },
          body: JSON.stringify({
            query: `
              mutation FetchPages($input: FetchChapterPagesInput!) {
                fetchChapterPages(input: $input) {
                  chapter {
                    id
                    pageCount
                  }
                  pages
                }
              }
            `,
            variables: { input: { chapterId: numId } },
          }),
        });

        const contentType = gqlRes.headers.get('content-type') || '';
        if (gqlRes.ok && !contentType.includes('text/html')) {
          const gqlData = (await gqlRes.json()) as {
            data?: {
              fetchChapterPages?: {
                pages?: string[];
                chapter?: { pageCount?: number };
              };
            };
          };
          const rawPages = gqlData.data?.fetchChapterPages?.pages;
          if (Array.isArray(rawPages) && rawPages.length > 0) {
            return rawPages.map((pagePath, idx) => ({
              index: idx + 1,
              url: this.resolveUrl(pagePath),
            }));
          }
        }
      }
    } catch {
      // Fallback below
    }

    // 2. Fallback: Query REST chapter endpoint (used by test mocks and older Suwayomi versions)
    const chUrl = `${this.baseUrl}/api/v1/chapter/${chapterId}`;
    try {
      const chRes = await this.getFetch()(chUrl, { headers: this.getHeaders() });
      const contentType = chRes.headers.get('content-type') || '';
      if (chRes.ok && !contentType.includes('text/html')) {
        const ch = (await chRes.json()) as SuwayomiChapterItem;
        const count = ch.pageCount && ch.pageCount > 0 ? ch.pageCount : 20;
        const pages: PageRef[] = [];
        for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
          pages.push({
            index: i + 1,
            url: `${this.baseUrl}/api/v1/chapter/${chapterId}/page/${i}`,
          });
        }
        return pages;
      }
    } catch {
      // Fall through to error
    }

    throw new Error(`Failed to load chapter ${chapterId}: Suwayomi server did not return chapter pages`);
  }

  credit(chapter: ChapterInfo): string {
    return `Scanlation: ${chapter.scanlationGroup || 'Tachiyomi Extension'} · Suwayomi Server`;
  }
}

export const defaultSuwayomiSource = new SuwayomiSource(
  'suwayomi',
  'Suwayomi (Tachiyomi)',
  'http://localhost:4567',
);
