import React, { useState, useEffect, useRef, useMemo, useCallback } from 'react';
import { useParams, useNavigate, useSearchParams } from 'react-router-dom';
import {
  ArrowLeft,
  ChevronLeft,
  ChevronRight,
  Maximize2,
  Minimize2,
  RotateCw,
  CheckCircle,
  AlertCircle,
  Bookmark,
  BookmarkCheck,
  Columns2,
} from 'lucide-react';
import { db, dirty, now, uid, type ChapterProgress } from '../../db';
import { getSourceById } from '../../sources/registry';
import type { MangaSource, ChapterInfo, PageRef } from '../../sources/types';
import { net } from '../../lib/net';
import { Button } from '../../design/components/Button';
import { IconButton } from '../../design/components/IconButton';
import { ProgressRing } from '../../design/components/Progress';
import { useReadingSession } from '../../lib/session';
import { recordSeriesRead } from '../../lib/series';
import { setKeepScreenOn, triggerHaptic, useVolumeKeys } from '../../lib/mobileUx';
import { registerBackHandler } from '../../lib/backStack';
import { toggleAppFullscreen, useAppFullscreen, exitAppFullscreen } from '../../lib/useAppFullscreen';
import { isMangaPageBookmarked, toggleMangaBookmark } from '../../lib/series';
import { useLiveQuery } from 'dexie-react-hooks';

export type ReadingMode = 'paged-rtl' | 'paged-ltr' | 'webtoon';
export type FitMode = 'width' | 'height' | 'screen' | 'original';

interface MangaPrefs {
  readingMode: ReadingMode;
  fitMode: FitMode;
  /** Show two pages side by side in paged modes. */
  spread: boolean;
  /** In spread mode, show the first page (usually the cover) on its own. */
  coverAlone: boolean;
  /** Go straight to the next chapter at the end instead of stopping. */
  autoNext: boolean;
}
const PREFS_KEY = 'folio_manga_reader';
const DEFAULT_PREFS: MangaPrefs = {
  readingMode: 'paged-rtl',
  fitMode: 'screen',
  spread: false,
  coverAlone: true,
  autoNext: true,
};
function loadPrefs(): MangaPrefs {
  try {
    return { ...DEFAULT_PREFS, ...(JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(PREFS_KEY) ?? '{}') as Partial<MangaPrefs>) };
  } catch {
    return DEFAULT_PREFS;
  }
}

/** First page of the spread containing `page` (1-based). */
function spreadStart(page: number, coverAlone: boolean): number {
  if (coverAlone) return page <= 1 ? 1 : page - ((page - 2) % 2);
  return page - ((page - 1) % 2);
}

export default function MangaReader() {
  const { seriesId: rawSeriesId, chapterId: rawChapterId } = useParams<{
    seriesId: string;
    chapterId: string;
  }>();
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const [searchParams] = useSearchParams();
  // ?page=N (from a bookmark) overrides the saved position for this open.
  const requestedPage = Number(searchParams.get('page')) || 0;

  const seriesId = decodeURIComponent(rawSeriesId || '');
  const chapterId = decodeURIComponent(rawChapterId || '');
  const sourceKind = seriesId.split(':')[0] || 'mangadex';

  // Chapter & Page data
  const [chapters, setChapters] = useState<ChapterInfo[]>([]);
  const [currentChapter, setCurrentChapter] = useState<ChapterInfo | null>(null);
  const [pages, setPages] = useState<PageRef[]>([]);
  const [pageObjectUrls, setPageObjectUrls] = useState<Map<number, string>>(new Map());
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  // Reader state
  const [currentPage, setCurrentPage] = useState(1);
  const [prefs, setPrefs] = useState<MangaPrefs>(loadPrefs);
  const updatePrefs = (patch: Partial<MangaPrefs>) =>
    setPrefs((p) => {
      const next = { ...p, ...patch };
      try {
        localStorage.setItem(PREFS_KEY, JSON.stringify(next));
      } catch {
        // Storage unavailable: applies for this session only.
      }
      return next;
    });
  const { readingMode, fitMode } = prefs;
  const spread = prefs.spread && readingMode !== 'webtoon';
  const [hudVisible, setHudVisible] = useState(true);
  const isFullscreen = useAppFullscreen();
  const [isTransitionScreen, setIsTransitionScreen] = useState(false);
  const [chapterToast, setChapterToast] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const containerRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const hudTimerRef = useRef<ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | null>(null);

  // Session tracking with idle detection
  const fractionRef = useRef<number>(0);
  const pagesReadRef = useRef<number>(0);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (pages.length > 0) {
      fractionRef.current = currentPage / pages.length;
      pagesReadRef.current = Math.max(pagesReadRef.current, currentPage);
    }
  }, [currentPage, pages.length]);

  const { recordActivity } = useReadingSession(
    seriesId,
    !loading && pages.length > 0 && !!seriesId,
    fractionRef,
    undefined,
    { kind: 'manga', pagesReadRef },
  );

  // Auto-hide HUD on idle
  const resetHudTimer = useCallback(() => {
    if (hudTimerRef.current) clearTimeout(hudTimerRef.current);
    setHudVisible(true);
    hudTimerRef.current = setTimeout(() => {
      setHudVisible(false);
    }, 4000);
  }, []);

  // Load Chapter and Pages
  // Only the latest chapter load may write state: flipping chapters quickly used to
  // let the previous chapter's pages land on top of the new one.
  const loadSeq = useRef(0);
  const loadChapterData = useCallback(async () => {
    if (!seriesId || !chapterId) return;
    const ticket = ++loadSeq.current;
    const isCurrent = () => ticket === loadSeq.current;
    setLoading(true);
    setError(null);
    setIsTransitionScreen(false);

    try {
      const source = ((await getSourceById(seriesId)) || (await getSourceById(sourceKind))) as MangaSource;
      if (!source) throw new Error(`Unsupported source: ${sourceKind}`);

      const chList = await source.chapters(seriesId);
      if (!isCurrent()) return;
      setChapters(chList);

      const ch = chList.find((c) => c.id === chapterId) || {
        id: chapterId,
        title: 'Chapter',
      };
      setCurrentChapter(ch);

      // Check if pages are cached in db.manga_pages offline table
      const cachedPages = await db.manga_pages
        .where('[series_id+chapter_id]')
        .equals([seriesId, chapterId])
        .toArray();
      if (!isCurrent()) return;

      const objectUrlMap = new Map<number, string>();
      if (cachedPages.length > 0) {
        cachedPages.sort((a, b) => a.page - b.page);
        const pageRefs: PageRef[] = [];
        for (const cp of cachedPages) {
          const url = URL.createObjectURL(cp.blob);
          objectUrlMap.set(cp.page, url);
          pageRefs.push({ index: cp.page, url });
        }
        setPages(pageRefs);
      } else {
        const remotePages = await source.pages(chapterId);
        if (!isCurrent()) return;
        setPages(remotePages);

        // Preload initial pages as local blob URLs (bypasses any Referer / 403 / CORS restrictions)
        void (async () => {
          for (const rp of remotePages.slice(0, 3)) {
            try {
              const blobUrl = await net.loadProtectedImage(rp.url);
              if (!isCurrent()) return;
              setPageObjectUrls((prev) => new Map(prev).set(rp.index, blobUrl));
            } catch {
              // fallback to remote/proxy URL
            }
          }
        })();
      }
      setPageObjectUrls(objectUrlMap);

      // Restore saved progress
      const savedProgress = await db.chapter_progress
        .filter((p) => p.series_id === seriesId && p.chapter_id === chapterId && !p.deleted_at)
        .first();
      if (!isCurrent()) return;

      if (requestedPage > 0) {
        setCurrentPage(requestedPage);
      } else if (savedProgress && savedProgress.page && savedProgress.page > 0 && !savedProgress.read) {
        setCurrentPage(savedProgress.page);
      } else {
        // New or already-finished chapter: start from the beginning.
        setCurrentPage(1);
      }
    } catch (err) {
      if (isCurrent()) setError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err));
    } finally {
      if (isCurrent()) {
        setLoading(false);
        resetHudTimer();
      }
    }
  }, [seriesId, chapterId, sourceKind, resetHudTimer, requestedPage]);

  useEffect(() => {
    void loadChapterData();

    return () => {
      // Revoke created blob URLs
      pageObjectUrls.forEach((url) => URL.revokeObjectURL(url));
    };
  }, [loadChapterData]);

  // Preload upcoming images
  useEffect(() => {
    if (pages.length === 0) return;
    const preloadAhead = 4;
    for (let i = currentPage; i <= Math.min(pages.length, currentPage + preloadAhead); i++) {
      const p = pages[i - 1];
      if (p) {
        const img = new Image();
        img.src = pageObjectUrls.get(p.index) || p.url;
      }
    }
  }, [currentPage, pages, pageObjectUrls]);

  // Save chapter progress (debounced)
  const saveProgress = useCallback(
    async (pageIndex: number, isFinished: boolean) => {
      if (!seriesId || !chapterId) return;

      const existing = await db.chapter_progress
        .filter((p) => p.series_id === seriesId && p.chapter_id === chapterId && !p.deleted_at)
        .first();

      if (existing) {
        await db.chapter_progress.update(existing.id, {
          page: pageIndex,
          total_pages: pages.length,
          read: isFinished || existing.read,
          read_at: isFinished ? now() : existing.read_at,
          ...dirty(),
        });
      } else {
        const newProg: ChapterProgress = {
          id: uid(),
          series_id: seriesId,
          source: sourceKind,
          chapter_id: chapterId,
          chapter_number: currentChapter?.number,
          title: currentChapter?.title,
          page: pageIndex,
          total_pages: pages.length,
          read: isFinished,
          read_at: isFinished ? now() : undefined,
          deleted_at: null,
          ...dirty(),
        };
        await db.chapter_progress.put(newProg);
      }

      // Reading history: previously only followed series got last_read_at, so
      // anything read without following left no trace.
      await recordSeriesRead(seriesId, sourceKind, async () => {
        const source = (await getSourceById(sourceKind)) as MangaSource | undefined;
        return source?.details ? source.details(seriesId) : null;
      });
    },
    [seriesId, chapterId, sourceKind, currentChapter, pages.length],
  );

  // Navigation logic
  const currentChapterIndex = useMemo(() => {
    return chapters.findIndex((c) => c.id === chapterId);
  }, [chapters, chapterId]);

  // Skip official external-only chapters: their pages aren't hosted, so they can't
  // be shown here and used to dead-end the reader.
  const prevChapter = useMemo(() => {
    if (currentChapterIndex <= 0) return null;
    return chapters.slice(0, currentChapterIndex).reverse().find((c) => !c.externalUrl) ?? null;
  }, [chapters, currentChapterIndex]);

  const nextChapter = useMemo(() => {
    if (currentChapterIndex === -1) return null;
    return chapters.slice(currentChapterIndex + 1).find((c) => !c.externalUrl) ?? null;
  }, [chapters, currentChapterIndex]);

  // Chapter-to-chapter moves replace the history entry: pushing each chapter (and
  // pushing the series page on "Back") made Back bounce between reader and series.
  const goToChapter = useCallback(
    (targetChapterId: string) => {
      navigate(
        `/read/manga/${encodeURIComponent(seriesId)}/${encodeURIComponent(targetChapterId)}`,
        { replace: true },
      );
    },
    [navigate, seriesId],
  );

  // Pages currently on screen: one, or two in spread mode.
  const visiblePages = useMemo(() => {
    if (!spread) return [currentPage];
    const start = spreadStart(currentPage, prefs.coverAlone);
    if (prefs.coverAlone && start === 1) return [1];
    return start + 1 <= pages.length ? [start, start + 1] : [start];
  }, [spread, currentPage, prefs.coverAlone, pages.length]);
  const lastVisible = visiblePages[visiblePages.length - 1];

  const goToNextPage = useCallback(() => {
    recordActivity();
    if (lastVisible < pages.length) {
      const next = lastVisible + 1;
      setCurrentPage(next);
      const nextLast = spread ? Math.min(pages.length, next + (prefs.coverAlone && next === 1 ? 0 : 1)) : next;
      void saveProgress(next, nextLast >= pages.length);
      return;
    }
    void saveProgress(pages.length, true);
    // Keep reading: jump straight into the next chapter instead of stopping on a
    // "Finished chapter" screen every time (the end screen remains when caught up).
    if (prefs.autoNext && nextChapter) {
      setChapterToast(nextChapter.title || `Chapter ${nextChapter.number ?? ''}`);
      goToChapter(nextChapter.id);
    } else {
      setIsTransitionScreen(true);
    }
  }, [lastVisible, pages.length, spread, prefs.coverAlone, prefs.autoNext, nextChapter, goToChapter, saveProgress, recordActivity]);

  const goToPrevPage = useCallback(() => {
    recordActivity();
    if (isTransitionScreen) {
      setIsTransitionScreen(false);
      return;
    }
    const first = visiblePages[0];
    if (first > 1) {
      const prev = spread ? spreadStart(first - 1, prefs.coverAlone) : first - 1;
      setCurrentPage(prev);
      void saveProgress(prev, false);
    }
  }, [isTransitionScreen, visiblePages, spread, prefs.coverAlone, saveProgress, recordActivity]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!chapterToast) return;
    const t = setTimeout(() => setChapterToast(null), 1800);
    return () => clearTimeout(t);
  }, [chapterToast]);

  // Keep screen on while reading manga (P11.1 & P11.3)
  useEffect(() => {
    void setKeepScreenOn(true);
    return () => {
      void setKeepScreenOn(false);
    };
  }, []);

  // Volume keys for page turn (P11.5)
  useVolumeKeys(
    () => {
      goToNextPage();
      triggerHaptic('light');
    },
    () => {
      goToPrevPage();
      triggerHaptic('light');
    },
    !loading,
  );

  // Android back button closes transition screen or hud before navigating
  useEffect(() => {
    return registerBackHandler(() => {
      if (isTransitionScreen) {
        setIsTransitionScreen(false);
        return true;
      }
      if (hudVisible) {
        setHudVisible(false);
        return true;
      }
      return false;
    }, 40);
  }, [isTransitionScreen, hudVisible]);


  const exitReader = () => {
    if (((window.history.state as { idx?: number } | null)?.idx ?? 0) > 0) navigate(-1);
    else navigate(`/series/${encodeURIComponent(seriesId)}`, { replace: true });
  };

  // Keyboard navigation
  useEffect(() => {
    const handleKeyDown = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
      resetHudTimer();
      if (e.key === 'Escape') {
        if (isFullscreen) {
          void exitAppFullscreen();
        } else {
          exitReader();
        }
      } else if (e.key === 'ArrowRight') {
        if (readingMode === 'paged-rtl') goToPrevPage();
        else goToNextPage();
      } else if (e.key === 'ArrowLeft') {
        if (readingMode === 'paged-rtl') goToNextPage();
        else goToPrevPage();
      } else if (e.key === ' ' || e.key === 'PageDown') {
        e.preventDefault();
        goToNextPage();
      } else if (e.key === 'PageUp') {
        e.preventDefault();
        goToPrevPage();
      }
    };

    window.addEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown);
    return () => window.removeEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown);
  }, [readingMode, goToNextPage, goToPrevPage, isFullscreen, navigate, seriesId, resetHudTimer]);

  // Wheel handling (§5.2: In RTL paged, wheel down still advances next page)
  const handleWheel = (e: React.WheelEvent) => {
    if (readingMode === 'webtoon') return; // native scroll in webtoon mode
    if (Math.abs(e.deltaY) > 20) {
      if (e.deltaY > 0) goToNextPage();
      else goToPrevPage();
    }
  };

  // Tap zones: Left third / Center / Right third
  const handleScreenClick = (e: React.MouseEvent<HTMLDivElement>) => {
    const rect = e.currentTarget.getBoundingClientRect();
    const x = e.clientX - rect.left;
    const width = rect.width;

    if (x < width * 0.33) {
      if (readingMode === 'paged-rtl') goToNextPage();
      else goToPrevPage();
    } else if (x > width * 0.67) {
      if (readingMode === 'paged-rtl') goToPrevPage();
      else goToNextPage();
    } else {
      setHudVisible((v) => !v);
    }
  };

  // Toggle fullscreen
  const toggleFullscreen = () => {
    void toggleAppFullscreen();
  };

  // Bookmarks live on the series row (synced), keyed by chapter + page.
  const seriesRow = useLiveQuery(() => db.series.get(seriesId), [seriesId]);
  const pageBookmarked = isMangaPageBookmarked(seriesRow, chapterId, currentPage);
  const toggleBookmark = () =>
    void toggleMangaBookmark(seriesId, sourceKind, {
      chapterId,
      chapterTitle: currentChapter?.title || 'Chapter',
      page: currentPage,
    });

  if (loading) {
    return (
      <div className="fixed inset-x-0 bottom-0 top-[var(--titlebar-h,0px)] z-50 bg-black flex flex-col items-center justify-center text-white">
        <ProgressRing size={40} />
        <p className="text-xs text-white/70 mt-3">Loading chapter pages…</p>
      </div>
    );
  }

  if (error || pages.length === 0) {
    return (
      <div className="fixed inset-x-0 bottom-0 top-[var(--titlebar-h,0px)] z-50 bg-black flex flex-col items-center justify-center p-6 text-center text-white">
        <AlertCircle className="w-12 h-12 text-[var(--danger)] mb-3" />
        <h2 className="text-lg font-bold">Failed to load chapter</h2>
        <p className="text-xs text-white/70 mt-1 max-w-sm">{error || 'No pages found'}</p>
        <div className="flex gap-3 mt-4">
          <Button variant="subtle" onClick={exitReader}>
            Back to Series
          </Button>
          <Button variant="standard" onClick={loadChapterData}>
            <RotateCw className="w-4 h-4 mr-2" /> Retry
          </Button>
        </div>
      </div>
    );
  }

  const activePageRef = pages[currentPage - 1];
  const activeImageUrl = activePageRef
    ? pageObjectUrls.get(activePageRef.index) || activePageRef.url
    : '';

  return (
    <div
      ref={containerRef}
      onMouseMove={resetHudTimer}
      className="fixed inset-x-0 bottom-0 top-[var(--titlebar-h,0px)] z-50 bg-black flex flex-col select-none overflow-hidden"
    >
      {/* Top HUD Header */}
      <header
        className={`absolute top-0 inset-x-0 z-40 bg-black/90 px-3 sm:px-4 py-2 flex items-center justify-between gap-2 text-white transition-all duration-200 border-b border-white/10 ${
          hudVisible ? 'translate-y-0 opacity-100' : '-translate-y-full opacity-0 pointer-events-none'
        }`}
      >
        <div className="flex items-center gap-3 min-w-0 flex-1">
          <IconButton
            icon={<ArrowLeft className="w-5 h-5 text-white" />}
            label="Back to series"
            variant="subtle"
            onClick={exitReader}
          />
          <div className="min-w-0">
            <h3 className="text-xs font-semibold text-white truncate max-w-xs md:max-w-md">
              {currentChapter?.title || 'Chapter'}
            </h3>
            <p className="text-[10px] text-white/60 truncate">
              Scanlation: {currentChapter?.scanlationGroup || 'Unknown'} · MangaDex
            </p>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Reader settings in header */}
        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-end gap-1.5 sm:gap-2">
          <IconButton
            icon={
              pageBookmarked ? (
                <BookmarkCheck className="w-4 h-4 text-[var(--accent)]" />
              ) : (
                <Bookmark className="w-4 h-4 text-white" />
              )
            }
            label={pageBookmarked ? 'Remove bookmark' : 'Bookmark this page'}
            variant="subtle"
            onClick={toggleBookmark}
          />
          {readingMode !== 'webtoon' && (
            <IconButton
              icon={<Columns2 className={`w-4 h-4 ${prefs.spread ? 'text-[var(--accent)]' : 'text-white'}`} />}
              label={prefs.spread ? 'Single page' : 'Two pages side by side'}
              variant="subtle"
              onClick={() => updatePrefs({ spread: !prefs.spread })}
            />
          )}
          <label className="hidden sm:flex items-center gap-1.5 text-[11px] text-white/80 cursor-pointer select-none">
            <input
              type="checkbox"
              checked={prefs.autoNext}
              onChange={(e) => updatePrefs({ autoNext: e.target.checked })}
              className="accent-[var(--accent)]"
            />
            Auto next chapter
          </label>
          {/* Reading Mode Selector */}
          <select
            aria-label="Reading mode"
            value={readingMode}
            onChange={(e) => updatePrefs({ readingMode: e.target.value as ReadingMode })}
            className="bg-white/10 text-white text-xs px-2 py-1 rounded-[var(--r-sm)] border border-white/20 outline-none cursor-pointer"
          >
            <option value="paged-rtl" className="bg-neutral-900 text-white">RTL (Manga)</option>
            <option value="paged-ltr" className="bg-neutral-900 text-white">LTR (Comic)</option>
            <option value="webtoon" className="bg-neutral-900 text-white">Webtoon (Strip)</option>
          </select>

          {/* Fit Mode Selector */}
          <select
            aria-label="Fit"
            value={fitMode}
            onChange={(e) => updatePrefs({ fitMode: e.target.value as FitMode })}
            className="bg-white/10 text-white text-xs px-2 py-1 rounded-[var(--r-sm)] border border-white/20 outline-none cursor-pointer"
          >
            <option value="width" className="bg-neutral-900 text-white">Fit Width</option>
            <option value="height" className="bg-neutral-900 text-white">Fit Height</option>
            <option value="screen" className="bg-neutral-900 text-white">Fit Screen</option>
            <option value="original" className="bg-neutral-900 text-white">Original</option>
          </select>

          <IconButton
            icon={isFullscreen ? <Minimize2 className="w-4 h-4 text-white" /> : <Maximize2 className="w-4 h-4 text-white" />}
            label="Toggle fullscreen"
            variant="subtle"
            onClick={toggleFullscreen}
          />
        </div>
      </header>

      {/* Main Reader View */}
      <div
        className="flex-1 w-full h-full flex items-center justify-center overflow-auto"
        onWheel={handleWheel}
        onClick={handleScreenClick}
      >
        {isTransitionScreen ? (
          /* Chapter Transition Screen */
          <div className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center p-6 text-center text-white max-w-md animate-in fade-in-0 duration-200">
            <CheckCircle className="w-16 h-16 text-[var(--success)] mb-4" />
            <h2 className="text-xl font-bold">Finished Chapter</h2>
            <p className="text-sm text-white/70 mt-1">
              {currentChapter?.title || 'Chapter completed'}
            </p>

            <div className="p-3 my-6 rounded-[var(--r-md)] bg-white/10 border border-white/20 text-xs text-white/80 w-full">
              Scanlation: {currentChapter?.scanlationGroup || 'No Group'}
              <br />
              Hosted on MangaDex
            </div>

            <div className="flex flex-wrap justify-center gap-3 max-w-full">
              <Button
                variant="subtle"
                onClick={exitReader}
              >
                Back to Series
              </Button>
              {nextChapter ? (
                <Button variant="accent" className="max-w-full" onClick={() => goToChapter(nextChapter.id)}>
                  Next: {nextChapter.title || `Chapter ${nextChapter.number}`}
                </Button>
              ) : (
                <p className="text-xs text-[var(--accent)] font-semibold mt-2">
                  You are all caught up!
                </p>
              )}
            </div>
          </div>
        ) : readingMode === 'webtoon' ? (
          /* Webtoon Continuous Vertical Strip */
          <div className="w-full flex flex-col items-center overflow-y-auto no-scrollbar py-8">
            {pages.map((p) => {
              const url = pageObjectUrls.get(p.index) || p.url;
              return (
                <div key={p.index} className="relative w-full max-w-3xl flex justify-center">
                  <img
                    src={url}
                    alt={`Page ${p.index}`}
                    loading="lazy"
                    onError={(e) => {
                      const target = e.currentTarget;
                      if (target.dataset.retried) return;
                      target.dataset.retried = '1';
                      void net.loadProtectedImage(p.url).then((blobUrl) => {
                        target.src = blobUrl;
                        setPageObjectUrls((prev) => new Map(prev).set(p.index, blobUrl));
                      }).catch(() => {});
                    }}
                    className="w-full object-contain select-none"
                  />
                  {p.index === 1 && (
                    <div className="absolute top-2 left-2 px-2 py-0.5 rounded bg-black/60 text-[10px] text-white/80 pointer-events-none">
                      Scanlation: {currentChapter?.scanlationGroup || 'Unknown'} · MangaPill
                    </div>
                  )}
                </div>
              );
            })}
          </div>
        ) : (
          /* Paged Mode (LTR or RTL) */
          <div
            className={`relative w-full h-full flex items-center justify-center p-2 gap-1 ${
              readingMode === 'paged-rtl' ? 'flex-row-reverse' : 'flex-row'
            }`}
          >
            {visiblePages.map((n) => {
              const ref = pages.find((p) => p.index === n);
              const url = ref ? pageObjectUrls.get(ref.index) || ref.url : activeImageUrl;
              const two = visiblePages.length === 2;
              return (
                <img
                  key={n}
                  src={url}
                  alt={`Page ${n}`}
                  draggable={false}
                  onError={(e) => {
                    const target = e.currentTarget;
                    if (target.dataset.retried) return;
                    target.dataset.retried = '1';
                    if (ref) {
                      void net.loadProtectedImage(ref.url).then((blobUrl) => {
                        target.src = blobUrl;
                        setPageObjectUrls((prev) => new Map(prev).set(ref.index, blobUrl));
                      }).catch(() => {});
                    }
                  }}
                  className={`select-none ${
                    two
                      ? 'max-w-[50%] max-h-full object-contain'
                      : fitMode === 'width'
                        ? 'w-full max-w-5xl object-contain'
                        : fitMode === 'height'
                          ? 'h-full object-contain'
                          : fitMode === 'screen'
                            ? 'max-w-full max-h-full object-contain'
                            : 'object-none'
                  }`}
                />
              );
            })}

            {/* Credit notice on first page */}
            {currentPage === 1 && (
              <div className="absolute top-14 left-4 px-2.5 py-1 rounded bg-black/70 border border-white/10 text-[11px] text-white/80 pointer-events-none backdrop-blur-sm">
                Scanlation: {currentChapter?.scanlationGroup || 'Unknown'} · Hosted on MangaDex
              </div>
            )}
          </div>
        )}
      </div>

      {chapterToast && (
        <div className="absolute top-16 left-1/2 -translate-x-1/2 z-50 px-4 py-2 rounded-full bg-black/85 border border-white/15 text-white text-xs font-medium pointer-events-none animate-in fade-in-0 duration-200 max-w-[90%] truncate">
          Now reading: {chapterToast}
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Bottom HUD Scrubber Bar */}
      <footer
        className={`absolute bottom-0 inset-x-0 z-40 bg-black/90 px-3 sm:px-6 py-3 flex flex-col gap-2 text-white transition-all duration-200 border-t border-white/10 ${
          hudVisible ? 'translate-y-0 opacity-100' : 'translate-y-full opacity-0 pointer-events-none'
        }`}
      >
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-xs font-medium text-white/80">
          <button
            type="button"
            disabled={!prevChapter}
            onClick={() => prevChapter && goToChapter(prevChapter.id)}
            className="flex items-center gap-1 hover:text-white disabled:opacity-30 disabled:hover:text-white/80 cursor-pointer"
          >
            <ChevronLeft className="w-4 h-4" /> Prev Chapter
          </button>

          <span>
            Page {visiblePages.length === 2 ? `${visiblePages[0]}–${visiblePages[1]}` : currentPage} of {pages.length}
          </span>

          <button
            type="button"
            disabled={!nextChapter}
            onClick={() => nextChapter && goToChapter(nextChapter.id)}
            className="flex items-center gap-1 hover:text-white disabled:opacity-30 disabled:hover:text-white/80 cursor-pointer"
          >
            Next Chapter <ChevronRight className="w-4 h-4" />
          </button>
        </div>

        {/* Page Scrubber Slider */}
        {readingMode !== 'webtoon' && (
          <input
            type="range"
            min={1}
            max={pages.length}
            value={currentPage}
            onChange={(e) => {
              const p = parseInt(e.target.value, 10);
              setCurrentPage(p);
              void saveProgress(p, p === pages.length);
            }}
            className="w-full h-1.5 bg-white/20 rounded-lg appearance-none cursor-pointer accent-[var(--accent)]"
          />
        )}
      </footer>
    </div>
  );
}

// Version 1: initial tables
db.version(1).stores({
  books: 'id, title, author, added_at, last_opened_at, _dirty',
  progress: 'id, _dirty',
  annotations: 'id, book_id, type, _dirty',
  collections: 'id, _dirty',
  collection_books: 'id, collection_id, book_id, _dirty',
  reading_sessions: 'id, book_id, started_at, _dirty',
  settings: 'id, _dirty',
  files: 'hash',
  covers: 'id',
  kv: 'key',
})

// Version 2: manga, series, sources, devices
db.version(2).stores({
  books: 'id, title, author, added_at, last_opened_at, _dirty',
  progress: 'id, _dirty',
  annotations: 'id, book_id, type, _dirty',
  collections: 'id, _dirty',
  collection_books: 'id, collection_id, book_id, _dirty',
  reading_sessions: 'id, book_id, started_at, _dirty',
  settings: 'id, _dirty',
  series: 'id, source, title, last_read_at, _dirty',
  chapter_progress: 'id, series_id, source, _dirty',
  sources: 'id, kind, _dirty',
  devices: 'id, _dirty',
  files: 'hash',
  covers: 'id',
  kv: 'key',
})

// Version 3: client-only tables (downloads, catalog_cache, source_credentials)
db.version(3).stores({
  downloads: 'id, source, status, created_at',
  catalog_cache: 'key, expires_at',
  source_credentials: 'id',
})

// Version 4: client-only manga offline pages
db.version(4).stores({
  manga_pages: 'id, series_id, chapter_id, [series_id+chapter_id]',
})

// Version 5: hot query compound indexes for annotations, sessions, and chapter progress
db.version(5).stores({
  annotations: 'id, book_id, type, created_at, [book_id+type], _dirty',
  reading_sessions: 'id, book_id, started_at, [book_id+started_at], _dirty',
  chapter_progress: 'id, series_id, source, [series_id+read], [series_id+chapter_number], _dirty',
})

export const SYNC_TABLES = Object.keys(SHARED_SYNC_TABLES) as SyncTableName[]

export const uid = () => crypto.randomUUID()

let clockOffset = 0
export function setClockOffset(offset: number) {
  clockOffset = offset
  db.kv.put({ key: 'clock_offset', value: offset }).catch(() => {})
}
export function getClockOffset(): number {
  return clockOffset
}

// Restore cached clock offset on initialization
db.kv.get('clock_offset').then((row) => {
  if (row && typeof row.value === 'number') {
    clockOffset = row.value
  }
}).catch(() => {})

export const now = () => Date.now() + clockOffset
export const dirty = () => ({ updated_at: now(), _dirty: 1 as const })

export function deviceId(): string {
  let id = localStorage.getItem('device_id')
  if (!id) {
    id = uid()
    localStorage.setItem('device_id', id)
  }
  return id
}

import { getServerUrl, getAccessToken, DEFAULT_SERVER_URL } from '../features/auth/storage';
import { executeTokenRefresh } from '../features/sync/api';

export interface RateLimiterConfig {
  capacity: number;
  refillPerSec: number;
}

export class TokenBucketLimiter {
  private buckets = new Map<string, { tokens: number; lastRefill: number }>();

  getConfig(hostname: string): RateLimiterConfig {
    const host = hostname.toLowerCase();
    // At-home manga servers have 40 requests per minute limit
    if (host.endsWith('.mangadex.network') || host === 'uploads.mangadex.org') {
      return { capacity: 40, refillPerSec: 40 / 60 };
    }
    // MangaDex official API has 4 req/sec limit
    if (host === 'api.mangadex.org') {
      return { capacity: 4, refillPerSec: 4 };
    }
    // Default rate limit: 4 req/sec
    return { capacity: 4, refillPerSec: 4 };
  }

  async acquire(url: string): Promise<void> {
    let hostname = 'default';
    try {
      hostname = new URL(url).hostname;
    } catch {
      // Relative or invalid URL
    }

    const config = this.getConfig(hostname);
    const now = Date.now();
    let bucket = this.buckets.get(hostname);

    if (!bucket) {
      bucket = { tokens: config.capacity, lastRefill: now };
      this.buckets.set(hostname, bucket);
    } else {
      const elapsedSec = (now - bucket.lastRefill) / 1000;
      bucket.tokens = Math.min(
        config.capacity,
        bucket.tokens + elapsedSec * config.refillPerSec,
      );
      bucket.lastRefill = now;
    }

    if (bucket.tokens >= 1) {
      bucket.tokens -= 1;
      return;
    }

    // Must wait for bucket to refill
    const needed = 1 - bucket.tokens;
    const waitMs = Math.ceil((needed / config.refillPerSec) * 1000);

    return new Promise((resolve) => {
      setTimeout(async () => {
        await this.acquire(url);
        resolve();
      }, Math.max( waitMs, 25 ));
    });
  }
}

export const rateLimiter = new TokenBucketLimiter();

export interface NetFetchOptions extends RequestInit {
  retries?: number;
}

/**
 * Universal net client that supports:
 * - Tauri native HTTP plugin or Web proxy routing
 * - Host-specific token bucket rate limiting
 * - Exponential backoff with jitter on 429/5xx and Retry-After support
 */
// Hosts verified to send Access-Control-Allow-Origin, so a browser may call them
// directly. gutenberg.org and standardebooks.org do NOT (checked 2026-10-13): in a
// browser their files are only reachable through the signed-in server proxy.
const DIRECT_CORS_HOSTS = new Set([
  'gutendex.com',
  'api.mangadex.org',
  'uploads.mangadex.org',
  'openlibrary.org',
  'covers.openlibrary.org',
  'archive.org',
  'raw.githubusercontent.com',
  'cdn.jsdelivr.net',
  'localhost',
  '127.0.0.1',
]);

/**
 * True when a localhost/127.0.0.1 URL can be fetched directly without a proxy.
 * In a non-browser environment (Node/tests), localhost is always reachable.
 * In a browser, only same-origin localhost (same port) avoids CORS issues.
 */
function isLocalhostDirectFetch(targetUrl: string): boolean {
  try {
    const target = new URL(targetUrl);
    const targetHost = target.hostname.toLowerCase();
    if (targetHost !== 'localhost' && targetHost !== '127.0.0.1') return false;
    const current = typeof window !== 'undefined' ? window.location : undefined;
    // Non-browser (Node/tests/SSR): no CORS, fetch directly
    if (!current || !current.port) return true;
    // Browser: only direct-fetch if same origin (same port + protocol)
    return target.port === current.port && target.protocol === current.protocol;
  } catch {
    return false;
  }
}

function corsBlockedError(host: string): Error {
  return new Error(
    `${host} doesn't allow downloads directly from a web browser. Sign in to your Verso ` +
      'server (Settings → Account & Sync) so it can fetch the file for you, or use the ' +
      'desktop or Android app.',
  );
}

export async function netFetch(
  input: string | URL,
  init: NetFetchOptions = {},
): Promise<Response> {
  const urlStr = typeof input === 'string' ? input : input.toString();
  const maxRetries = init.retries ?? 3;

  await rateLimiter.acquire(urlStr);

  const isTauri =
    typeof window !== 'undefined' &&
    ('__TAURI__' in window || '__TAURI_INTERNALS__' in window);

  const isExternalHttp =
    urlStr.startsWith('http://') || urlStr.startsWith('https://');

  let fetchFn: typeof fetch = fetch;
  if (isTauri) {
    try {
      const tauriHttp = await import('@tauri-apps/plugin-http');
      if (tauriHttp && typeof tauriHttp.fetch === 'function') {
        fetchFn = tauriHttp.fetch as typeof fetch;
      }
    } catch {
      fetchFn = fetch;
    }
  }

  let targetUrl = urlStr;
  const headers = new Headers(init.headers);

  let targetHost = '';
  try {
    targetHost = new URL(urlStr).hostname.toLowerCase();
  } catch {
    // Ignore URL parse errors
  }

  if (
    targetHost.endsWith('readdetectiveconan.com') ||
    targetHost.endsWith('mangapill.com')
  ) {
    if (!headers.has('Referer')) {
      headers.set('Referer', 'https://mangapill.com/');
    }
  }

  if (!isTauri && isExternalHttp) {
    const serverUrl = await getServerUrl();
    let serverOrigin = '';
    try {
      const su = new URL(serverUrl);
      serverOrigin = su.origin; // e.g. "http://localhost:8080"
    } catch {
      // Ignore URL parse errors
    }

    let targetOrigin = '';
    try {
      const tu = new URL(urlStr);
      targetOrigin = tu.origin; // e.g. "http://localhost:4567"
    } catch {
      // Ignore URL parse errors
    }

    let token = getAccessToken();
    if (!token) {
      try {
        token = await executeTokenRefresh();
      } catch {
        // Proceed without auth
      }
    }

    // Route through /proxy when:
    // - The target is not the Verso server itself (different origin)
    // - AND target is not a direct CORS host or localhost server
    // (Direct CORS hosts and local servers like Suwayomi/Komga natively allow CORS from the browser)
    const isTargetingServer = serverOrigin && targetOrigin && serverOrigin === targetOrigin;
    const isDirect = DIRECT_CORS_HOSTS.has(targetHost) || isLocalhostDirectFetch(urlStr);
    if (serverOrigin && !isTargetingServer && !isDirect) {
      targetUrl = `${serverUrl}/proxy?url=${encodeURIComponent(urlStr)}`;
      if (token) {
        headers.set('Authorization', `Bearer ${token}`);
      }
    }
  }

  let attempt = 0;
  while (true) {
    try {
      const response = await fetchFn(targetUrl, {
        ...init,
        headers,
      });

      // If proxy failed with 401/403/502/404, fallback to direct fetch for external targets
      if (!response.ok && targetUrl !== urlStr && isExternalHttp) {
        try {
          const directRes = await fetchFn(urlStr, { ...init, headers: init.headers });
          if (directRes.ok) {
            return directRes;
          }
        } catch {
          // ignore fallback error and continue retry loop on response
        }
      }

      if (
        (response.status === 429 || response.status >= 500) &&
        attempt < maxRetries
      ) {
        attempt++;
        let delayMs = Math.pow(2, attempt) * 500 + Math.random() * 200;

        const retryAfter = response.headers.get('Retry-After');
        if (retryAfter) {
          const parsedSec = parseFloat(retryAfter);
          if (!isNaN(parsedSec)) {
            delayMs = parsedSec * 1000;
          } else {
            const dateParsed = Date.parse(retryAfter);
            if (!isNaN(dateParsed)) {
              delayMs = Math.max(0, dateParsed - Date.now());
            }
          }
        }

        await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, delayMs));
        continue;
      }

      return response;
    } catch (err) {
      // If network fetch failed on proxy URL, attempt direct fetch as fallback
      if (targetUrl !== urlStr && isExternalHttp) {
        try {
          const directRes = await fetchFn(urlStr, { ...init, headers: init.headers });
          if (directRes.ok) {
            return directRes;
          }
        } catch {
          // continue to retry
        }
      }

      if ((err as Error)?.name === 'AbortError') throw err;
      // A browser fetch to a host without CORS fails identically every time; retrying
      // only added ~7s of backoff before the same "Failed to fetch".
      if (!isTauri && isExternalHttp && targetUrl === urlStr) {
        const host = new URL(urlStr).hostname.toLowerCase();
        if (!DIRECT_CORS_HOSTS.has(host) && !isLocalhostDirectFetch(urlStr)) throw corsBlockedError(host);
      }

      if (attempt < maxRetries) {
        attempt++;
        const delayMs = Math.pow(2, attempt) * 500 + Math.random() * 200;
        await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, delayMs));
        continue;
      }
      throw err;
    }
  }
}

export interface DownloadOptions {
  onProgress?: (fraction: number, received: number, total?: number) => void;
  signal?: AbortSignal;
  /** How many times to resume after the connection drops (default 8). */
  maxResumes?: number;
  headers?: HeadersInit;
}

/**
 * Downloads a file, resuming with HTTP Range requests when the connection drops.
 * Some hosts (gutenberg.org from parts of Asia, for example) stream at a few KB/s
 * and reset long transfers; restarting from zero meant large books never finished.
 */
export async function netDownload(url: string, opts: DownloadOptions = {}): Promise<Blob> {
  const chunks: Uint8Array[] = [];
  let received = 0;
  let total: number | undefined;
  let contentType = '';
  let failures = 0;
  const maxResumes = opts.maxResumes ?? 8;

  for (;;) {
    if (opts.signal?.aborted) throw new DOMException('Download cancelled', 'AbortError');
    const headers = new Headers(opts.headers);
    if (received > 0) headers.set('Range', `bytes=${received}-`);
    try {
      const res = await netFetch(url, { headers, signal: opts.signal, retries: 1 });
      if (received > 0 && res.status === 200) {
        // Server ignored the Range header: start over.
        chunks.length = 0;
        received = 0;
      } else if (!res.ok && res.status !== 206) {
        throw new Error(`Download failed: HTTP ${res.status} ${res.statusText}`.trim());
      }
      contentType ||= res.headers.get('content-type') ?? '';
      const range = res.headers.get('content-range');
      const len = Number(res.headers.get('content-length')) || undefined;
      total = range ? Number(range.split('/')[1]) || total : len !== undefined ? received + len : total;

      if (!res.body) {
        const buf = new Uint8Array(await res.arrayBuffer());
        chunks.push(buf);
        received += buf.byteLength;
      } else {
        const reader = res.body.getReader();
        for (;;) {
          const { value, done } = await reader.read();
          if (done) break;
          chunks.push(value);
          received += value.byteLength;
          opts.onProgress?.(total ? received / total : 0, received, total);
        }
      }
      if (total === undefined || received >= total) break;
      // Stream ended early without an error: resume from where it stopped.
      throw new Error('connection closed early');
    } catch (err) {
      if ((err as Error)?.name === 'AbortError' || opts.signal?.aborted) throw err;
      if (++failures > maxResumes) {
        throw new Error(
          `Download kept failing after ${maxResumes} retries ` +
            `(${Math.round(received / 1024)} KB of ${total ? Math.round(total / 1024) + ' KB' : 'unknown'} received): ` +
            ((err as Error)?.message || String(err)),
          { cause: err },
        );
      }
      await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, Math.min(8000, 500 * 2 ** failures)));
    }
  }
  opts.onProgress?.(1, received, total);
  return new Blob(chunks as BlobPart[], { type: contentType.split(';')[0] || undefined });
}

/** Size in bytes of a remote file without downloading it (undefined if unknown). */
export async function remoteSize(url: string, signal?: AbortSignal): Promise<number | undefined> {
  try {
    const res = await netFetch(url, { headers: { Range: 'bytes=0-0' }, signal, retries: 0 });
    const range = res.headers.get('content-range');
    await res.body?.cancel().catch(() => {});
    if (range) return Number(range.split('/')[1]) || undefined;
    return Number(res.headers.get('content-length')) || undefined;
  } catch {
    return undefined;
  }
}

/** Rewrite image URLs that reject third-party referers (like MangaPill CDN) to the local proxy */
export function proxyImageUrl(url?: string): string | undefined {
  if (!url) return undefined;
  if (url.startsWith('blob:') || url.startsWith('data:')) return url;
  try {
    const parsed = new URL(url);
    const host = parsed.hostname.toLowerCase();
    if (host.endsWith('readdetectiveconan.com') || host.endsWith('mangapill.com')) {
      return `${DEFAULT_SERVER_URL}/proxy?url=${encodeURIComponent(url)}`;
    }
  } catch {
    // Return original if invalid URL
  }
  return url;
}

/**
 * Loads a protected image (e.g. MangaPill CDN requiring anti-hotlink Referer)
 * via netFetch and returns a local blob URL.
 */
export async function loadProtectedImage(url: string): Promise<string> {
  if (!url || url.startsWith('blob:') || url.startsWith('data:')) return url;
  let target = url;
  if (url.includes('/proxy?url=')) {
    const match = url.match(/[?&]url=([^&]+)/);
    if (match) {
      target = decodeURIComponent(match[1]);
    }
  }
  const res = await netFetch(target, {
    headers: {
      Referer: 'https://mangapill.com/',
      'User-Agent':
        'Mozilla/5.0 (Windows NT 10.0; Win64; x64) AppleWebKit/537.36 (KHTML, like Gecko) Chrome/120.0.0.0 Safari/537.36',
    },
  });
  if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`Image fetch failed: ${res.status}`);
  const blob = await res.blob();
  return URL.createObjectURL(blob);
}

export const net = {
  fetch: netFetch,
  download: netDownload,
  size: remoteSize,
  limiter: rateLimiter,
  proxyImageUrl,
  loadProtectedImage,
};

const DAY = 864e5;

function readingStats(sessions: ReadingSession[]) {
  const key = (t: number) => new Date(t).toDateString();
  const byDay = new Map<string, number>();
  for (const s of sessions) {
    if (s.deleted_at) continue;
    byDay.set(key(s.started_at), (byDay.get(key(s.started_at)) ?? 0) + s.duration_sec);
  }
  const today = Math.round((byDay.get(key(Date.now())) ?? 0) / 60);
  let week = 0;
  for (let i = 0; i < 7; i++) week += byDay.get(key(Date.now() - i * DAY)) ?? 0;
  let streak = 0;
  const d = new Date();
  if (!byDay.has(d.toDateString())) d.setDate(d.getDate() - 1);
  while (byDay.has(d.toDateString())) {
    streak++;
    d.setDate(d.getDate() - 1);
  }
  return { today, week: Math.round(week / 60), streak };
}

export default function Home() {
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const { openImport, openSettings } = useOutletContext<ShellContext>();
  const { setActiveCoverUrl } = useTheme();
  const [showSyncPrompt, setShowSyncPrompt] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    void (async () => {
      const dismissed = await db.kv.get('dismissed_sync_prompt');
      const user = await getAuthUser();
      if (!user && dismissed?.value !== true) {
        setShowSyncPrompt(true);
      }
    })();
  }, []);

  const handleDismissPrompt = async () => {
    setShowSyncPrompt(false);
    await db.kv.put({ key: 'dismissed_sync_prompt', value: true });
  };

  const books = useLiveQuery(() => db.books.filter((b) => !b.deleted_at).toArray(), [], [] as Book[]);
  const progressRows = useLiveQuery(() => db.progress.toArray(), [], [] as Progress[]);
  const sessions = useLiveQuery(
    () => db.reading_sessions.where('started_at').above(Date.now() - 60 * DAY).toArray(),
    [],
    [] as ReadingSession[],
  );
  // Manga reading history: every series read (followed or not), newest first,
  // with the chapter to resume.
  const mangaHistory = useLiveQuery(
    async () => {
      const series = (await db.series.filter((s) => !s.deleted_at && !!s.last_read_at).toArray()).sort(
        (a, b) => (b.last_read_at ?? 0) - (a.last_read_at ?? 0),
      );
      const progress = await db.chapter_progress.filter((p) => !p.deleted_at).toArray();
      const latest = new Map<string, (typeof progress)[number]>();
      for (const p of progress) {
        const cur = latest.get(p.series_id);
        if (!cur || p.updated_at > cur.updated_at) latest.set(p.series_id, p);
      }
      return series.slice(0, 20).map((s) => ({ series: s, last: latest.get(s.id) }));
    },
    [],
    [],
  );
  const favIds = useFavoriteIds();

  const progress = useMemo(() => new Map(progressRows.map((p) => [p.id, p])), [progressRows]);
  const recent = useMemo(
    () =>
      books
        .filter((b) => b.last_opened_at)
        .sort((a, b) => (b.last_opened_at ?? 0) - (a.last_opened_at ?? 0))
        .slice(0, 5),
    [books],
  );
  const favorites = useMemo(() => books.filter((b) => favIds.has(b.id)).slice(0, 10), [books, favIds]);
  const added = useMemo(() => [...books].sort((a, b) => b.added_at - a.added_at).slice(0, 12), [books]);
  const stats = useMemo(() => readingStats(sessions), [sessions]);
  const { goals } = useReadingGoals();
  const finishedBooksCount = useMemo(
    () => books.filter((b) => !!b.meta?.finished_at).length,
    [books],
  );
  const dailyGoalPercent = Math.min(
    100,
    Math.round((stats.today / Math.max(1, goals.dailyMinutes)) * 100),
  );

  const hero = recent[0];
  const heroCoverUrl = useCoverBlobUrl(hero?.id ?? '', hero?.cover_hash ?? null);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (heroCoverUrl) {
      setActiveCoverUrl(heroCoverUrl);
    } else {
      setActiveCoverUrl(null);
    }
  }, [heroCoverUrl, setActiveCoverUrl]);

  const open = (b: Book) => navigate(`/read/${b.id}`);
  const pct = (b: Book) => Math.round((progress.get(b.id)?.percent ?? 0) * 100);

  if (!books.length) {
    return (
      <div className="flex-1 flex flex-col items-center justify-center p-6 max-w-xl mx-auto w-full gap-6">
        {showSyncPrompt && (
          <div className="w-full p-4 rounded-[var(--r-lg)] bg-[var(--layer-2)] border border-[var(--stroke)] flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-3 animate-in fade-in-0 duration-200">
            <div className="flex items-start gap-3">
              <Sparkles className="w-5 h-5 text-[var(--accent)] shrink-0 mt-0.5" />
              <div>
                <div className="font-semibold text-sm text-[var(--text-1)]">Sync across devices?</div>
                <div className="text-xs text-[var(--text-2)] mt-0.5">
                  Verso lets you sync books, notes, and reading progress across all your devices.
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2 self-end sm:self-auto shrink-0">
              <Button size="sm" variant="subtle" onClick={handleDismissPrompt}>
                Not now
              </Button>
              <Button
                size="sm"
                variant="accent"
                onClick={() => {
                  void handleDismissPrompt();
                  openSettings?.('account');
                }}
              >
                Sign in
              </Button>
            </div>
          </div>
        )}
        <EmptyState
          icon={<BookOpen className="w-8 h-8" />}
          title="Welcome to Verso"
          description="Add EPUB, MOBI, AZW3, FB2, CBZ or PDF files to get started. You can also drag & drop them directly into this window."
          action={
            <Button variant="accent" size="lg" icon={<Plus className="w-4 h-4" />} onClick={openImport}>
              Add Books
            </Button>
          }
        />
      </div>
    );
  }

  const getContextSections = (b: Book) => {
    const fav = favIds.has(b.id);
    return [
      {
        items: [
          {
            key: 'open',
            label: 'Open reader',
            icon: <BookOpen className="w-4 h-4" />,
            onSelect: () => open(b),
          },
          {
            key: 'fav',
            label: fav ? 'Remove favourite' : 'Add favourite',
            icon: <Heart className="w-4 h-4" />,
            onSelect: () => void toggleFavorite(b.id),
          },
          {
            key: 'finish',
            label: pct(b) >= 99 ? 'Mark unread' : 'Mark finished',
            icon: <CheckCircle className="w-4 h-4" />,
            onSelect: async () => {
              const newPercent = pct(b) >= 99 ? 0 : 1;
              await db.progress.put({
                id: b.id,
                cfi: '',
                percent: newPercent,
                chapter: null,
                device_id: deviceId(),
                deleted_at: null,
                ...dirty(),
              });
            },
          },
        ],
      },
    ];
  };

  return (
    // The scroll container spans the full width (the centred column used to be the
    // scroller, so the wheel did nothing over the side margins and the scrollbar
    // floated mid-window); content stays centred inside it.
    <div className="flex-1 min-h-0 overflow-y-auto">
    <div className="px-4 md:px-8 py-6 space-y-8 max-w-7xl mx-auto w-full">
      {/* First-run sync prompt */}
      {showSyncPrompt && (
        <div className="p-4 rounded-[var(--r-lg)] bg-[var(--layer-2)] border border-[var(--stroke)] flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-3 animate-in fade-in-0 duration-200">
          <div className="flex items-start gap-3">
            <Sparkles className="w-5 h-5 text-[var(--accent)] shrink-0 mt-0.5" />
            <div>
              <div className="font-semibold text-sm text-[var(--text-1)]">Sync across devices?</div>
              <div className="text-xs text-[var(--text-2)] mt-0.5">
                Verso lets you sync books, notes, and reading progress across all your devices.
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2 self-end sm:self-auto shrink-0">
            <Button size="sm" variant="subtle" onClick={handleDismissPrompt}>
              Not now
            </Button>
            <Button
              size="sm"
              variant="accent"
              onClick={() => {
                void handleDismissPrompt();
                openSettings?.('account');
              }}
            >
              Sign in
            </Button>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
      {/* Top Split: Recent Reads & Hero + Stats Chips */}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-12 gap-8 items-start">
        {/* Left / Hero Column */}
        <section className="lg:col-span-7 flex flex-col gap-4">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
            <h2 className="text-xl font-bold tracking-tight text-[var(--text-1)]">Recent reads</h2>
            <Link
              to="/library"
              className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 text-xs font-semibold text-[var(--accent)] hover:underline"
            >
              Open library <ChevronRight className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
            </Link>
          </div>

          {hero ? (
            <div className="flex flex-row gap-4 sm:gap-6 p-4 sm:p-5 rounded-[var(--r-xl)] bg-[var(--layer-2)] border border-[var(--stroke)] shadow-sm">
              {/* Hero Cover (300px wide on desktop) */}
              <div className="w-28 sm:w-[200px] md:w-[240px] xl:w-[280px] shrink-0">
                <ContextMenu sections={getContextSections(hero)}>
                  <div>
                    <BookCover
                      id={hero.id}
                      title={hero.title}
                      author={hero.author}
                      coverHash={hero.cover_hash}
                      progressPercent={pct(hero)}
                      isFavourite={favIds.has(hero.id)}
                      onToggleFavourite={() => void toggleFavorite(hero.id)}
                      onClick={() => open(hero)}
                    />
                  </div>
                </ContextMenu>
              </div>

              {/* Hero Metadata & Resume */}
              <div className="flex flex-col justify-between flex-1 min-w-0 py-1 gap-4">
                <div className="space-y-2">
                  <span className="text-[11px] font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-[var(--accent)]">
                    Continue reading
                  </span>
                  <h3 className="font-display text-lg sm:text-2xl font-bold text-[var(--text-1)] leading-tight line-clamp-3 sm:line-clamp-2 [overflow-wrap:anywhere]">
                    {hero.title}
                  </h3>
                  <p className="text-sm text-[var(--text-2)] font-medium line-clamp-1 [overflow-wrap:anywhere]">
                    {hero.author}
                  </p>
                  {progress.get(hero.id)?.chapter && (
                    <p className="text-xs text-[var(--text-3)] line-clamp-1 italic">
                      {progress.get(hero.id)?.chapter}
                    </p>
                  )}
                </div>

                <div className="space-y-3 pt-2">
                  <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-xs text-[var(--text-2)] font-medium">
                    <span>{pct(hero)}% completed</span>
                  </div>
                  <div className="w-full h-1.5 bg-[var(--layer-3)] rounded-full overflow-hidden">
                    <div
                      className="h-full bg-[var(--accent)] rounded-full transition-all duration-[var(--d-fast)]"
                      style={{ width: `${pct(hero)}%` }}
                    />
                  </div>

                  <div className="pt-1">
                    <Button
                      variant="accent"
                      size="md"
                      icon={<Play className="w-4 h-4 fill-current" />}
                      onClick={() => open(hero)}
                    >
                      Resume
                    </Button>
                  </div>
                </div>

                {/* Additional Recent Mini-Covers */}
                {recent.length > 1 && (
                  <div className="hidden sm:flex items-center gap-3 pt-3 border-t border-[var(--stroke)] min-w-0">
                    <span className="text-xs text-[var(--text-3)] shrink-0">More recent:</span>
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-2 overflow-x-auto no-scrollbar py-1">

                        <div key={b.id} className="w-11 shrink-0">
                          <BookCover
                            id={b.id}
                            title={b.title}
                            author={b.author}
                            coverHash={b.cover_hash}
                            progressPercent={pct(b)}
                            onClick={() => open(b)}
                          />
                        </div>
                      ))}
                    </div>
                  </div>
                )}

            </div>

            <div className="p-8 rounded-[var(--r-xl)] bg-[var(--layer-2)] border border-[var(--stroke)] text-center text-sm text-[var(--text-2)]">

          )}

            to="/stats"

                  {stats.today} / {goals.dailyMinutes} min read
                </p>
                <p className="text-[11px] text-[var(--text-3)]">
                  {finishedBooksCount} of {goals.yearlyBooks} books this year
                </p>
              </div>
            </div>
            <div className="flex items-center text-xs text-[var(--text-3)] group-hover:text-[var(--text-1)] transition-colors">
              <span>View stats</span>
              <ChevronRight className="w-4 h-4 ml-0.5" />
            </div>
          </Link>

          {/* Stats Chips */}
          <div className="grid grid-cols-3 gap-3">
            <div className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center p-3.5 rounded-[var(--r-lg)] bg-[var(--layer-2)] border border-[var(--stroke)] shadow-xs">
              <Clock className="w-4 h-4 text-[var(--accent)] mb-1" />
              <span className="text-base font-bold text-[var(--text-1)]">{stats.today}m</span>
              <span className="text-[11px] text-[var(--text-3)] font-medium">Today</span>
            </div>
            <div className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center p-3.5 rounded-[var(--r-lg)] bg-[var(--layer-2)] border border-[var(--stroke)] shadow-xs">
              <CalendarDays className="w-4 h-4 text-[var(--accent)] mb-1" />
              <span className="text-base font-bold text-[var(--text-1)]">{stats.week}m</span>
              <span className="text-[11px] text-[var(--text-3)] font-medium">This week</span>
            </div>
            <div className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center p-3.5 rounded-[var(--r-lg)] bg-[var(--layer-2)] border border-[var(--stroke)] shadow-xs">
              <Flame className="w-4 h-4 text-[var(--warning)] mb-1" />
              <span className="text-base font-bold text-[var(--text-1)]">
                {stats.streak} {stats.streak === 1 ? 'day' : 'days'}
              </span>
              <span className="text-[11px] text-[var(--text-3)] font-medium">Streak</span>
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Shelves on Right Column */}
          <div className="space-y-6">

              title="Favourite books"
              action={

                  to="/library?filter=favorites"
                  className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 hover:underline text-xs"
                >
                  See more <ChevronRight className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                </Link>
              }
            >
              {favorites.length ? (
                favorites.map((b) => (
                  <ContextMenu key={b.id} sections={getContextSections(b)}>
                    <div className="w-[120px] shrink-0">
                      <BookCard
                        id={b.id}
                        title={b.title}
                        author={b.author}
                        coverHash={b.cover_hash}
                        progressPercent={pct(b)}
                        isFavourite={true}
                        onToggleFavourite={() => void toggleFavorite(b.id)}
                        onClick={() => open(b)}
                      />
                    </div>
                  </ContextMenu>
                ))
              ) : (
                <div className="text-xs text-[var(--text-3)] p-4">
                  Tap the heart on any book to pin it to your favorites.
                </div>
              )}
            </Shelf>
          </div>
        </section>
      </div>

      {/* Full-width Recently Added Shelf */}
      <section className="pt-4 border-t border-[var(--stroke)]">
        <Shelf
          title="Recently added"
          action={
            <Link
              to="/library?sort=added"
              className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 hover:underline text-xs"
            >
              See more <ChevronRight className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
            </Link>
          }
        >
          {added.map((b) => (
            <ContextMenu key={b.id} sections={getContextSections(b)}>
              <div className="w-[130px] shrink-0">
                <BookCard
                  id={b.id}
                  title={b.title}
                  author={b.author}
                  coverHash={b.cover_hash}
                  progressPercent={pct(b)}
                  isFavourite={favIds.has(b.id)}
                  onToggleFavourite={() => void toggleFavorite(b.id)}
                  onClick={() => open(b)}
                />
              </div>
            </ContextMenu>
          ))}
        </Shelf>
      </section>

      {/* Continue Manga Shelf */}
      {mangaHistory.length > 0 && (
        <section className="pt-4 border-t border-[var(--stroke)]">
          <Shelf
            title="Continue manga"
            action={
              <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-3">
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={async () => {
                    if (await confirmAction('Clear manga reading history? Series in your library stay there.')) {
                      await clearMangaHistory();
                    }
                  }}
                  className="text-xs text-[var(--text-3)] hover:text-[var(--danger)] hover:underline cursor-pointer"
                >
                  Clear history
                </button>
                <Link
                  to="/library?format=manga"
                  className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 hover:underline text-xs"
                >
                  Manga library <ChevronRight className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                </Link>
              </span>
            }
          >
            {mangaHistory.map(({ series: s, last }) => {
              const resume = () =>
                navigate(
                  last
                    ? `/read/manga/${encodeURIComponent(s.id)}/${encodeURIComponent(last.chapter_id)}`
                    : `/series/${encodeURIComponent(s.id)}`,
                );
              return (
                <div key={s.id} className="relative w-[130px] shrink-0 cursor-pointer group/hist">
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    aria-label={`Remove ${s.title} from history`}
                    title="Remove from history"
                    onClick={(e) => {
                      e.stopPropagation();
                      void removeFromMangaHistory(s.id);
                    }}
                    className="absolute top-1.5 right-1.5 z-30 w-6 h-6 rounded-full bg-black/65 text-white flex items-center justify-center opacity-100 md:opacity-0 md:group-hover/hist:opacity-100 focus-visible:opacity-100 transition-opacity cursor-pointer"
                  >
                    <X className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                  </button>
                  <BookCard
                    id={s.id}
                    title={s.title}
                    author={
                      last
                        ? `${last.title || (last.chapter_number != null ? `Ch. ${last.chapter_number}` : 'Chapter')}${
                            last.total_pages ? ` · p. ${last.page}/${last.total_pages}` : ''
                          }`
                        : s.author || 'Unknown'
                    }
                    coverUrl={s.cover_url}
                    onClick={resume}
                  />
                </div>
              );
            })}
          </Shelf>
        </section>
      )}
    </div>
    </div>
  );
}

import { useMemo, useState, useCallback, useRef, useEffect, Fragment } from 'react';
import { useNavigate, useOutletContext, useSearchParams } from 'react-router-dom';
import { useLiveQuery } from 'dexie-react-hooks';
import {
